	public class ColumnChooserWindow : Window, IComponentConnector
	{
		private readonly DataTable _dataTable;

		private readonly List<DataColumn> _initialVisibleColumns;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ItemsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Items", typeof(IEnumerable<ColumnChooserItem>), typeof(ColumnChooserWindow));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsAllCheckedProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsAllChecked", typeof(bool), typeof(ColumnChooserWindow), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, OnIsAllCheckedPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ReservedCommandProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ReservedCommand", typeof(Tuple<string, ICommand>), typeof(ColumnChooserWindow), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnReservedCommandPropertyChanged));

		private int _frozenColumnsCount;

		private int _silentlyChangeIsAllChecked;

		internal Button buttonReserved;

		internal Button buttonOK;

		internal Button buttonCancel;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public int helpId => 200;

		public string helpFile => "eoconf.chm";

		public IEnumerable<ColumnChooserItem> Items
		{
			get
			{
				return (IEnumerable<ColumnChooserItem>)GetValue(ItemsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ItemsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsAllChecked
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsAllCheckedProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsAllCheckedProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public Tuple<string, ICommand> ReservedCommand
		{
			get
			{
				return (Tuple<string, ICommand>)GetValue(ReservedCommandProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ReservedCommandProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public ColumnChooserWindow(DataTable dataTable)
		{
			if (dataTable == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("dataTable");
			}
			_dataTable = dataTable;
			_initialVisibleColumns = dataTable.Columns.Where((DataColumn x) => x.Visibility == Visibility.Visible).ToList();
			InitializeComponent();
		}

		protected override void OnInitialized(System.EventArgs e)
		{
			try
			{
				_silentlyChangeIsAllChecked++;
				List<ColumnChooserItem> list = new List<ColumnChooserItem>();
				foreach (DataColumn column in _dataTable.Columns)
				{
					list.Add(new ColumnChooserItem(this)
					{
						Column = column,
						IsChecked = (column.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
					});
				}
				Items = list;
				IsAllChecked = !Items.Any((ColumnChooserItem x) => !x.IsChecked);
			}
			finally
			{
				_silentlyChangeIsAllChecked--;
			}
			base.OnInitialized(e);
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsAllCheckedChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			if (_silentlyChangeIsAllChecked > 0 || Items == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (ColumnChooserItem item in Items)
			{
				if (item.Column != null && item.Column.CanChangeVisibility)
				{
					item.IsChecked = newValue;
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnReservedCommandChanged(Tuple<string, ICommand> oldValue, Tuple<string, ICommand> newValue)
		{
			if (newValue != null && newValue.Item1 != null && newValue.Item2 != null)
			{
				buttonReserved.Content = newValue.Item1;
				buttonReserved.Command = newValue.Item2;
				buttonReserved.CommandParameter = this;
				buttonReserved.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
			}
			else
			{
				buttonReserved.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
		}

		internal void OnColumnItemCheckedStatusChanged(ColumnChooserItem cci)
		{
			try
			{
				_silentlyChangeIsAllChecked++;
				IsAllChecked = Items != null && !Items.Any((ColumnChooserItem x) => !x.IsChecked);
			}
			finally
			{
				_silentlyChangeIsAllChecked--;
			}
		}

		private void buttonOK_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (Items != null)
			{
				foreach (ColumnChooserItem item in Items)
				{
					if (item.IsChecked)
					{
						item.Column.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					}
					else
					{
						item.Column.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
				}
				List<DataColumn> list = (from x in Items
					where x.IsChecked
					select x.Column).ToList();
				if (list.Except(_initialVisibleColumns).Count() > 0 || _initialVisibleColumns.Except(list).Count() > 0)
				{
					int num = 0;
					foreach (DataColumn item2 in list)
					{
						item2.DisplayIndex = num++;
					}
					_dataTable.OnColumnsVisibilityChanged();
				}
			}
			base.DialogResult = true;
			Close();
		}

		private static void OnIsAllCheckedPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as ColumnChooserWindow).OnIsAllCheckedChanged((bool)e.OldValue, (bool)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnReservedCommandPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as ColumnChooserWindow).OnReservedCommandChanged((Tuple<string, ICommand>)e.OldValue, (Tuple<string, ICommand>)e.NewValue);
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls;component/columnchooserwindow.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				buttonReserved = (Button)target;
				break;
			case 2:
				buttonOK = (Button)target;
				buttonOK.Click += buttonOK_Click;
				break;
			case 3:
				buttonCancel = (Button)target;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	public class ColumnChooserItem : DependencyObject
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsCheckedProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsChecked", typeof(bool), typeof(ColumnChooserItem), new PropertyMetadata(false, OnIsCheckedPropertyChanged));

		private readonly ColumnChooserWindow _parentChooser;

		public bool IsChecked
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsCheckedProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsCheckedProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public DataColumn Column { get; set; }

		public ColumnChooserItem(ColumnChooserWindow parentChooser)
		{
			_parentChooser = parentChooser;
		}

		private static void OnIsCheckedPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ColumnChooserItem columnChooserItem = d as ColumnChooserItem;
			columnChooserItem._parentChooser.OnColumnItemCheckedStatusChanged(columnChooserItem);
		}
	}
	public struct DataCellInfo : IEquatable<DataCellInfo>
	{
		public DataRow Row { get; set; }

		public DataColumn Column { get; set; }

		public IDataCell Data
		{
			get
			{
				if (IsEmpty)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return Row[Column];
			}
		}

		public bool IsEmpty
		{
			get
			{
				if (Row != null)
				{
					return Column == null;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public DataCellInfo(DataRow row, DataColumn column)
		{
			this = default(DataCellInfo);
			if (row == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("row");
			}
			if (column == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("column");
			}
			Row = row;
			Column = column;
		}

		public void UpdateIsCurrent(bool isCurrent)
		{
			if (!IsEmpty && Data is IDataCellInternal dataCellInternal)
			{
				dataCellInternal.IsCurrent = isCurrent;
			}
		}

		public bool IsSelectedInGrid(GridControl gc)
		{
			if (gc == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("gc");
			}
			if (gc.DataGrid == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (Column.IsColumnSelected)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (gc.DataGrid.SelectedItems.Contains(Row))
			{
				return true;
			}
			foreach (DataGridCellInfo selectedCell in gc.DataGrid.SelectedCells)
			{
				if (selectedCell.Item != Row)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (selectedCell.Column is GridControlColumn)
				{
					if ((selectedCell.Column as GridControlColumn).DataColumn == Column)
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				else if ((selectedCell.Column as PlaceholderColumn).ContainsColumn(Column.Index))
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool Equals(DataCellInfo other)
		{
			if (Row == other.Row)
			{
				return Column == other.Column;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (!(obj is DataCellInfo))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return Equals((DataCellInfo)obj);
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			if (Row != null && Column != null)
			{
				return Row.GetHashCode() | Column.GetHashCode();
			}
			return base.GetHashCode();
		}

		public static bool operator ==(DataCellInfo first, DataCellInfo second)
		{
			return first.Equals(second);
		}

		public static bool operator !=(DataCellInfo first, DataCellInfo second)
		{
			return !first.Equals(second);
		}
	}
	public class DataColumnHeaderControl : Control
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty MouseOverBrushProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ColumnSelectionBrushProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ColumnProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsHoveredProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey LeaderColumnPropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty LeaderColumnProperty;

		public Brush MouseOverBrush
		{
			get
			{
				return (Brush)GetValue(MouseOverBrushProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(MouseOverBrushProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public Brush ColumnSelectionBrush
		{
			get
			{
				return (Brush)GetValue(ColumnSelectionBrushProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ColumnSelectionBrushProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public DataColumn Column
		{
			get
			{
				return (DataColumn)GetValue(ColumnProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ColumnProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public DataColumn LeaderColumn
		{
			get
			{
				return (DataColumn)GetValue(LeaderColumnProperty);
			}
			private set
			{
				SetValue(LeaderColumnPropertyKey, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsHovered
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsHoveredProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsHoveredProperty, value);
			}
		}

		internal HeaderControlDragAndDropHelper DragAndDropHelper { get; private set; }

		private GridControl GridControlOwner
		{
			get
			{
				if (Column != null && Column.TargetGridColumn != null)
				{
					return Column.TargetGridColumn.GridControlOwner;
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		static DataColumnHeaderControl()
		{
			MouseOverBrushProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MouseOverBrush", typeof(Brush), typeof(DataColumnHeaderControl));
			ColumnSelectionBrushProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ColumnSelectionBrush", typeof(Brush), typeof(DataColumnHeaderControl));
			ColumnProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Column", typeof(DataColumn), typeof(DataColumnHeaderControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnColumnPropertyChanged));
			IsHoveredProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsHovered", typeof(bool), typeof(DataColumnHeaderControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsRender));
			LeaderColumnPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("LeaderColumn", typeof(DataColumn), typeof(DataColumnHeaderControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata());
			LeaderColumnProperty = LeaderColumnPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			Control.IsTabStopProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(DataColumnHeaderControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
		}

		public DataColumnHeaderControl()
		{
			DragAndDropHelper = new HeaderControlDragAndDropHelper(this);
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size constraint)
		{
			if (base.Template == null)
			{
				return HeaderControlHelper.GetSizeToRenderText(this, new Size(constraint.Width, 0.0));
			}
			return base.MeasureOverride(constraint);
		}

		public void InvalidateTemplate()
		{
			InternalInvalidateTemplate();
		}

		private void InternalInvalidateTemplate()
		{
			GridControlTemplates.UpdateColumnHeaderTemplate(this);
			InvalidateVisual();
			InvalidateColumnHeaderMargin();
		}

		protected override void OnGotKeyboardFocus(KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControlOwner?.ScrollIntoView(Column);
			base.OnGotKeyboardFocus(e);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			DragAndDropHelper.OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			DragAndDropHelper.OnPreviewMouseMove(e);
			base.OnMouseMove(e);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			DragAndDropHelper.OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseRightButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			DragAndDropHelper.OnPreviewMouseRightButtonDown(e);
			base.OnPreviewMouseRightButtonDown(e);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseDoubleClick(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			DragAndDropHelper.OnPreviewMouseDoubleClick(e);
			base.OnPreviewMouseDoubleClick(e);
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size arrangeBounds)
		{
			Size result = base.ArrangeOverride(arrangeBounds);
			if (result.Height > 0.0 && VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(this) is FrameworkElement frameworkElement && frameworkElement.ActualHeight != result.Height)
			{
				frameworkElement.InvalidateMeasure();
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected override void OnRenderSizeChanged(SizeChangedInfo sizeInfo)
		{
			if (sizeInfo.WidthChanged)
			{
				InvalidateColumnHeaderMargin();
				if (GridControlOwner != null && GridControlOwner.InternalFreezingHelper != null)
				{
					GridControlOwner.InternalFreezingHelper.UpdateColumnFrozenLine();
				}
			}
			base.OnRenderSizeChanged(sizeInfo);
		}

		public void InvalidateColumnHeaderMargin()
		{
			GridControlOwner?.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.ProcessColumnSpanHeaderWidth(Column);
		}

		protected override void OnRender(DrawingContext drawingContext)
		{
			GridControl gridControlOwner = GridControlOwner;
			if (gridControlOwner != null && (!gridControlOwner.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.HasColumnSpan || (!gridControlOwner.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.IsColumnSpanLeader(Column) && (!gridControlOwner.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.IsColumnSpanSlaver(Column) || gridControlOwner.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.IsLastColumnSpanSlaver(Column)))))
			{
				if (Column != null && Column.Filter.Visibility != Visibility.Visible)
				{
					HeaderControlHelper.RenderBackground(this, drawingContext);
				}
				base.OnRender(drawingContext);
				if (base.Template == null)
				{
					HeaderControlHelper.RenderText(this, drawingContext);
				}
				if (Column != null && Column.Filter.Visibility != Visibility.Visible)
				{
					HeaderControlHelper.RenderSortDirectionFlag(this, drawingContext);
					HeaderControlHelper.RenderFilterFlag(this, drawingContext);
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnColumnChanged(DataColumn oldValue, DataColumn newValue)
		{
			if (oldValue != null)
			{
				oldValue.HeaderControl = null;
			}
			if (newValue != null)
			{
				newValue.HeaderControl = this;
			}
			LeaderColumn = null;
			if (newValue != null)
			{
				GridControl gridControlOwner = GridControlOwner;
				if (gridControlOwner != null && gridControlOwner.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.HasColumnSpan && gridControlOwner.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.IsColumnSpanSlaver(newValue))
				{
					LeaderColumn = gridControlOwner.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.GetColumnSpanLeader(newValue);
				}
			}
			InvalidateTemplate();
		}

		private static void OnColumnPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataColumnHeaderControl).OnColumnChanged((DataColumn)e.OldValue, (DataColumn)e.NewValue);
		}
	}
	public static class DispatcherExtensions
	{
		private static readonly Dictionary<Action, DispatcherOperation> _operations = new Dictionary<Action, DispatcherOperation>();

		public static void BeginInvokeOnce(this Dispatcher dispatcher, Action action)
		{
			if (dispatcher == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("dispatcher");
			}
			if (action == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("action");
			}
			dispatcher.BeginInvokeOnce(action, DispatcherPriority.Normal);
		}

		public static void BeginInvokeOnce(this Dispatcher dispatcher, Action action, DispatcherPriority priority)
		{
			if (dispatcher == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("dispatcher");
			}
			if (action == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("action");
			}
			if (_operations.ContainsKey(action))
			{
				return;
			}
			_operations[action] = dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action<Action>)delegate(Action act)
			{
				act();
				if (_operations.ContainsKey(act))
				{
					_operations.Remove(act);
				}
			}, priority, action);
		}
	}
	public static class DataRowExtensions
	{
		public static GridControlDataGridRow GetContainerControl(this DataRow row)
		{
			if (row == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("row");
			}
			GridControlDataGrid gridControlDataGrid = row.DataTable?.GridControl?.DataGrid;
			if (gridControlDataGrid != null)
			{
				return gridControlDataGrid.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(row) as GridControlDataGridRow;
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static GridControlCell GetCellControl(this DataRow row, DataColumn column)
		{
			if (row == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("row");
			}
			if (column == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("column");
			}
			if (column.TargetGridColumn != null)
			{
				GridControlDataGridRow containerControl = row.GetContainerControl();
				if (containerControl != null && containerControl.InternalCellsPresenter != null)
				{
					int num = row.DataTable.GridControl.DataGrid.Columns.IndexOf(column.TargetGridColumn);
					if (num >= 0)
					{
						return containerControl.InternalCellsPresenter.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromIndex(num) as GridControlCell;
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public class GridControlCell : DataGridCell
	{
		private class CellPopupManager
		{
			private GridControlCell OwnerCell { get; set; }

			public string SelectedUnits { get; private set; }

			public Popup Popup { get; private set; }

			public CellPopupManager(GridControlCell cell)
			{
				if (cell == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException("cell");
				}
				OwnerCell = cell;
			}

			public void UpdatePopupStatusIfPossible()
			{
				if (OwnerCell.IsInEditMode)
				{
					if (OwnerCell.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
					{
						OpenPopupIfPossible();
					}
					else if (Popup != null && !Popup.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
					{
						ClosePopupIfPossible();
					}
				}
				else
				{
					ClosePopupIfPossible();
				}
			}

			private void OpenPopupIfPossible()
			{
				if (!OwnerCell.IsInEditMode || (Popup != null && Popup.IsOpen))
				{
					return;
				}
				GridControlCellPopupEventArgs e = OwnerCell.OnPopupOpening();
				if (e.Content != null)
				{
					if (Popup == null)
					{
						Popup = new Popup
						{
							StaysOpen = true
						};
						Popup.Placement = PlacementMode.Right;
						Popup.PlacementTarget = OwnerCell;
						Popup.IsKeyboardFocusWithinChanged += Popup_IsKeyboardFocusWithinChanged;
						Popup.Opened += Popup_Opened;
						Popup.Closed += Popup_Closed;
					}
					Window window = Window.GetWindow(OwnerCell);
					if (window != null)
					{
						window.LocationChanged += window_LocationChanged;
					}
					Popup.Child = new Border
					{
						Child = new ContentControl
						{
							Content = e.Content
						},
						Background = Brushes.White
					};
					Popup.IsOpen = true;
				}
			}

			private void window_LocationChanged(object sender, System.EventArgs e)
			{
				if (Popup != null && Popup.IsOpen)
				{
					OwnerCell.EndEditModeIfPossible(commitChange: true);
				}
			}

			private void ClosePopupIfPossible()
			{
				if (Popup != null)
				{
					if (Popup.IsOpen)
					{
						Popup.IsOpen = false;
					}
					Popup = null;
				}
			}

			public void Initialize(GridControlCellPopupEventArgs e)
			{
				IDataCell cell = OwnerCell.Cell;
				if (cell != null && cell.UnitsSource != null && cell.UnitsSource.Count() > 1 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(cell.CurrentUnits) && cell.UnitsSource.Contains(cell.CurrentUnits))
				{
					ComboBox comboBox = new ComboBox
					{
						ItemsSource = cell.UnitsSource
					};
					string selectedItem = (SelectedUnits = cell.CurrentUnits);
					comboBox.SelectedItem = selectedItem;
					comboBox.PreviewKeyDown += combo_PreviewKeyDown;
					comboBox.SelectionChanged += combo_SelectionChanged;
					e.Content = comboBox;
				}
			}

			public void TryFocus()
			{
				if (((Popup.Child as Border).Child as ContentControl).Content is ComboBox comboBox)
				{
					comboBox.IsDropDownOpen = true;
					comboBox.Focus();
				}
			}

			private void combo_PreviewKeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
			{
				if (e.Key != Key.Return)
				{
					return;
				}
				ComboBox comboBox = sender as ComboBox;
				if (comboBox.IsDropDownOpen)
				{
					comboBox.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
					{
						OwnerCell.EndEditModeIfPossible(commitChange: true);
						OwnerCell.Focus();
					});
				}
			}

			private void combo_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				ComboBox comboBox = sender as ComboBox;
				SelectedUnits = comboBox.SelectedItem as string;
				TryUpdateCellCurrentUnits();
				if (Popup != null && Popup.IsOpen)
				{
					OwnerCell.EndEditModeIfPossible(commitChange: true);
				}
			}

			private void Popup_IsKeyboardFocusWithinChanged(object sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				if (Popup != null && !Popup.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
				{
					UpdatePopupStatusIfPossible();
					if (!OwnerCell.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
					{
						OwnerCell.EndEditModeIfPossible(commitChange: true);
					}
				}
			}

			private void Popup_Opened(object sender, System.EventArgs e)
			{
				Popup popup = sender as Popup;
				if (popup.Child is FrameworkElement frameworkElement && !(frameworkElement.ActualHeight >= OwnerCell.ActualHeight))
				{
					popup.VerticalOffset = (OwnerCell.ActualHeight - frameworkElement.ActualHeight) / 2.0;
				}
			}

			private void Popup_Closed(object sender, System.EventArgs e)
			{
				Popup obj = sender as Popup;
				obj.Opened -= Popup_Opened;
				obj.Closed -= Popup_Closed;
				Popup = null;
				Window window = Window.GetWindow(OwnerCell);
				if (window != null)
				{
					window.LocationChanged -= window_LocationChanged;
				}
			}

			private void TryUpdateCellCurrentUnits()
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(SelectedUnits))
				{
					IDataCell cell = OwnerCell.Cell;
					if (cell != null && cell.UnitsSource != null && !string.Equals(SelectedUnits, cell.CurrentUnits, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
					{
						cell.CurrentUnits = SelectedUnits;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private class ValueChangeReporter
		{
			private object _valueBeforeReport;

			private bool _stackedTag;

			private GridControlCell OwnerCell { get; set; }

			public ValueChangeReporter(GridControlCell cell)
			{
				if (cell == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException("cell");
				}
				OwnerCell = cell;
			}

			public void OnEnteringEditMode()
			{
				if (OwnerCell.SupressImmediateChangeReport && !_stackedTag)
				{
					_stackedTag = true;
					_valueBeforeReport = OwnerCell.Cell.Value;
				}
			}

			public void OnLeavingEditMode()
			{
				if (_stackedTag)
				{
					_stackedTag = false;
					TryReportValueChanged();
				}
			}

			public void TryReportValueChanged()
			{
				IDataCell cell = OwnerCell.Cell;
				if (cell == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				object value = cell.Value;
				if (!object.Equals(_valueBeforeReport, value))
				{
					if (cell.Row != null && cell.Row.DataTable != null && cell.Row.DataTable.IsCellValueChangedReportEnabled)
					{
						cell.Row.DataTable.CallCellValueChanged(cell, _valueBeforeReport, value);
					}
					_valueBeforeReport = value;
				}
			}
		}

		private class IsCurrentConverter : IValueConverter
		{
			public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				if (value is DataCellInfo dataCellInfo && parameter is GridControlCell { Column: not null } gridControlCell)
				{
					return gridControlCell.Column == dataCellInfo.Column?.TargetGridColumn && gridControlCell.DataContext == dataCellInfo.Row;
				}
				return false;
			}

			public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				throw new NotImplementedException();
			}
		}

		private const string PART_FocusSite = "PART_FocusSite";

		public static readonly string DoubleMask;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty CellProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey IsInEditModePropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsInEditModeProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsCurrentProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsDropDownOpenProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsColumnSelectedProperty;

		private CellPopupManager _popupManager;

		private ValueChangeReporter _changeReporter;

		private bool _isInEditModeFlag;

		private bool? _commitChangeFlag;

		public IDataCell Cell
		{
			get
			{
				return (IDataCell)GetValue(CellProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(CellProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsInEditMode
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsInEditModeProperty);
			}
			private set
			{
				SetValue(IsInEditModePropertyKey, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsCurrent
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsCurrentProperty);
			}
			private set
			{
				SetValue(IsCurrentProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsDropDownOpen
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsDropDownOpenProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsDropDownOpenProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsColumnSelected
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsColumnSelectedProperty);
			}
			private set
			{
				SetValue(IsColumnSelectedProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsEditModeCombo
		{
			get
			{
				if (Cell != null)
				{
					return CellControlTemplates.IsEditableCombo(Cell);
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public bool ShouldEnterEditModeWhenFocused
		{
			get
			{
				if (!IsEditModeCombo)
				{
					if (Cell != null)
					{
						return Cell.ShouldEnterEditModeWhenFocused;
					}
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public GridControlColumn GridColumn => base.Column as GridControlColumn;

		public bool IsPlaceHolder => base.Column is PlaceholderColumn;

		private CellPopupManager PopupManager
		{
			get
			{
				if (_popupManager == null)
				{
					_popupManager = new CellPopupManager(this);
				}
				return _popupManager;
			}
		}

		private ValueChangeReporter ChangeReporter
		{
			get
			{
				if (_changeReporter == null)
				{
					_changeReporter = new ValueChangeReporter(this);
				}
				return _changeReporter;
			}
		}

		public bool HasPopup => PopupManager.Popup != null;

		public GridControl GridControlOwner
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.Column is GridControlColumn)
				{
					return ((GridControlColumn)base.Column).GridControlOwner;
				}
				if (base.Column is PlaceholderColumn)
				{
					return ((PlaceholderColumn)base.Column).GridControlOwner;
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		internal bool SupressImmediateChangeReport
		{
			get
			{
				if (HasPopup)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (Cell != null && Cell.ItemsSource != null && Cell.IsComboEditable)
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public bool ResolvedIsCurrent
		{
			get
			{
				if (IsCurrent)
				{
					return true;
				}
				GridControl gridControlOwner = GridControlOwner;
				if (gridControlOwner != null && gridControlOwner.CurrentCell.Data?.HostControl == this)
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public static string GetSystemDecimalSymbol()
		{
			return 0.1.ToString().Replace("0", string.Empty).Replace("1", string.Empty);
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			if (!AutomationPeerHelpers.UseCustomAutomationPeers)
			{
				return base.OnCreateAutomationPeer();
			}
			return new GridControlCellAutomationPeer(this);
		}

		public void SetCellValue(string value)
		{
			try
			{
				Cell.Parse(value);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		static GridControlCell()
		{
			DoubleMask = "{char:16:-+0-9eE" + GetSystemDecimalSymbol() + "}";
			CellProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Cell", typeof(IDataCell), typeof(GridControlCell), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnCellPropertyChanged));
			IsInEditModePropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("IsInEditMode", typeof(bool), typeof(GridControlCell), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnIsInEditModePropertyChanged));
			IsInEditModeProperty = IsInEditModePropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			IsCurrentProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsCurrent", typeof(bool), typeof(GridControlCell), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsRender, OnIsCurrentPropertyChanged));
			IsDropDownOpenProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsDropDownOpen", typeof(bool), typeof(GridControlCell), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnIsDropDownOpenPropertyChanged));
			IsColumnSelectedProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsColumnSelected", typeof(bool), typeof(GridControlCell));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(GridControlCell), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(GridControlCell)));
		}

		protected override void OnColumnChanged(DataGridColumn oldColumn, DataGridColumn newColumn)
		{
			UpdateIsColumnSelected();
			SetBinding(IsCurrentProperty, new Binding
			{
				Source = GridControlOwner,
				Path = new PropertyPath(GridControl.CurrentCellProperty),
				Converter = new IsCurrentConverter(),
				ConverterParameter = this,
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				FallbackValue = false
			});
			base.OnColumnChanged(oldColumn, newColumn);
		}

		private void UpdateIsColumnSelected()
		{
			if (IsPlaceHolder)
			{
				IsColumnSelected = false;
				return;
			}
			SetBinding(IsColumnSelectedProperty, new Binding("DataColumn.IsColumnSelected")
			{
				Source = base.Column,
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
		}

		public bool BeginEditModeIfPossible(bool syncTemplateImmediately)
		{
			if (Cell != null && Cell.CanHaveEditMode && !IsInEditMode)
			{
				IsInEditMode = true;
				if (syncTemplateImmediately && !IsEditModeCombo)
				{
					base.Dispatcher.Invoke(delegate
					{
						UIElement uIElement = GetTemplateChild("PART_FocusSite") as UIElement;
						uIElement?.Focus();
						if (uIElement is XamMaskedEditor)
						{
							XamMaskedEditor xamMaskedEditor = uIElement as XamMaskedEditor;
							if (!xamMaskedEditor.IsInEditMode)
							{
								xamMaskedEditor.IsInEditMode = true;
							}
							xamMaskedEditor.SelectAll();
						}
						else if (uIElement is TextBox)
						{
							TextBox obj = uIElement as TextBox;
							obj.Focus();
							obj.SelectAll();
						}
						else if (Utilities.GetDescendantFromType(this, typeof(XamMaskedEditor), allowSubclassOfType: true) is XamMaskedEditor { Visibility: Visibility.Visible } xamMaskedEditor2)
						{
							if (!xamMaskedEditor2.IsInEditMode)
							{
								xamMaskedEditor2.IsInEditMode = true;
							}
							xamMaskedEditor2.SelectAll();
						}
						else if (Utilities.GetDescendantFromType(this, typeof(TextBox), allowSubclassOfType: true) is TextBox { Visibility: Visibility.Visible } textBox)
						{
							textBox.Focus();
							textBox.SelectAll();
						}
					}, DispatcherPriority.Render);
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void EndEditModeIfPossible(bool commitChange)
		{
			EndEditModeIfPossible(commitChange, null);
		}

		internal void EndEditModeIfPossible(bool commitChange, Action editModeEnding)
		{
			if (IsInEditMode && _isInEditModeFlag)
			{
				_isInEditModeFlag = false;
				editModeEnding?.Invoke();
				_commitChangeFlag = commitChange;
				try
				{
					IsInEditMode = false;
				}
				finally
				{
					_commitChangeFlag = null;
				}
			}
		}

		public void TryFocusInternalEditor()
		{
			if (!IsInEditMode || !(GetTemplateChild("PART_FocusSite") is UIElement uIElement))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (uIElement.Focusable)
			{
				uIElement.Focus();
			}
			else if (uIElement is ContentPresenter reference && VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(reference) > 0)
			{
				UIElement uIElement2 = VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(reference, 0) as UIElement;
				if (uIElement2.Focusable)
				{
					uIElement2.Focus();
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnTemplateChanged(ControlTemplate oldTemplate, ControlTemplate newTemplate)
		{
			if (!IsPlaceHolder)
			{
				InvalidateVisual();
				base.OnTemplateChanged(oldTemplate, newTemplate);
			}
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size constraint)
		{
			if (IsPlaceHolder)
			{
				return base.MeasureOverride(constraint);
			}
			Size result = base.MeasureOverride(constraint);
			if (!IsInEditMode && base.Template == null)
			{
				if (base.Column != null && base.Column.Width.IsAuto)
				{
					Size size = GridControlCellHelper.MeasureText(this);
					result = new Size(Math.Max(result.Width, size.Width), Math.Max(result.Height, size.Height));
				}
				if (GridColumn != null && GridColumn.GridControlOwner != null && GridColumn.GridControlOwner.IsMultilineAllowedInCell)
				{
					double desiredHeightToRenderText = GridControlCellHelper.GetDesiredHeightToRenderText(this);
					return new Size(result.Width, Math.Max(result.Height, desiredHeightToRenderText));
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected override void OnRender(DrawingContext drawingContext)
		{
			if (IsPlaceHolder)
			{
				return;
			}
			GridControlCellHelper.RenderBorderAndBackground(this, drawingContext);
			base.OnRender(drawingContext);
			if (!IsInEditMode && base.Template == null)
			{
				if (!base.IsSelected && !IsColumnSelected)
				{
					GridControlCellHelper.RenderCellBackground(this, drawingContext);
				}
				if (Cell != null && Cell.UIType != CellUIType.CheckBox && (Cell.UIType == CellUIType.ComboBox || !(Cell.ValueType == DataRow.NullableBooleanType)))
				{
					GridControlCellHelper.RenderText(this, drawingContext);
				}
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnCellValueAsStringChanged()
		{
			InvalidateVisual();
		}

		protected virtual void OnCellChanged(IDataCell oldValue, IDataCell newValue)
		{
			if (oldValue != null && oldValue.HostControl == this)
			{
				((IDataCellInternal)oldValue).HostControl = null;
			}
			if (newValue != null)
			{
				((IDataCellInternal)newValue).HostControl = this;
			}
			CellControlTemplates.UpdateTemplates(this);
			InvalidateVisual();
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsInEditModeChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			_isInEditModeFlag = newValue;
			bool flag = !_commitChangeFlag.HasValue || _commitChangeFlag.Value;
			if (oldValue && !newValue && flag)
			{
				if (!base.IsKeyboardFocused && base.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
				{
					Focus();
				}
				else if (!IsEditModeCombo)
				{
					FrameworkElement editingControl = GetTemplateChild("PART_FocusSite") as FrameworkElement;
					if (Cell != null && Cell.Row != null && Cell.Row.DataTable != null)
					{
						Cell.Row.DataTable.CommitEditingValue(Cell, editingControl);
					}
				}
			}
			CellControlTemplates.UpdateTemplates(this);
			PopupManager.UpdatePopupStatusIfPossible();
			if (newValue)
			{
				ChangeReporter.OnEnteringEditMode();
			}
			else
			{
				ChangeReporter.OnLeavingEditMode();
			}
			if (Cell != null && Cell.Column != null && Cell.Column.TargetGridColumn != null)
			{
				GridColumn.GridControlOwner.OnCellControlEditModeChanged(this);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsCurrentChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			if (!IsPlaceHolder)
			{
				if (!newValue && IsInEditMode)
				{
					EndEditModeIfPossible(commitChange: true);
				}
				else
				{
					TryUpdateEditModeIfPossible();
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsDropDownOpenChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			if (newValue)
			{
				TryLoadItemsSourceIfAny();
				if (GridControlOwner != null)
				{
					GridControlOwner.OnCellDropDownOpening(new GridControlCellEventArgs(this, GridControl.CellDropDownOpeningEvent, GridColumn.GridControlOwner));
				}
			}
			else if (SupressImmediateChangeReport)
			{
				ChangeReporter.TryReportValueChanged();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnIsKeyboardFocusWithinChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (IsPlaceHolder || Mouse.RightButton == MouseButtonState.Pressed)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (Keyboard.FocusedElement is ContextMenu { PlacementTarget: var placementTarget } contextMenu)
			{
				if (placementTarget != null && (placementTarget == this || IsAncestorOf(placementTarget)))
				{
					return;
				}
				Point point = contextMenu.TranslatePoint(new Point(0.0, 0.0), this);
				if (point.X >= 0.0 && point.Y >= 0.0 && point.X <= base.ActualWidth && point.Y <= base.ActualHeight)
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				if (base.IsKeyboardFocusWithin && ResolvedIsCurrent && !IsInEditMode && ShouldEnterEditModeWhenFocused)
				{
					BeginEditModeIfPossible(syncTemplateImmediately: false);
				}
				else if (!base.IsKeyboardFocusWithin && IsInEditMode && (PopupManager.Popup == null || !PopupManager.Popup.IsKeyboardFocusWithin))
				{
					EndEditModeIfPossible(commitChange: true);
				}
			});
			PopupManager.UpdatePopupStatusIfPossible();
			base.OnIsKeyboardFocusWithinChanged(e);
		}

		protected virtual GridControlCellPopupEventArgs OnPopupOpening()
		{
			GridControlCellPopupEventArgs e = new GridControlCellPopupEventArgs(this, GridControl.CellPopupOpeningEvent, GridColumn.GridControlOwner);
			GridColumn.GridControlOwner.OnCellPopupOpening(e);
			if (e.Content == null)
			{
				PopupManager.Initialize(e);
			}
			return e;
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewKeyDown(KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!IsPlaceHolder)
			{
				if (e.Key == Key.Space && PopupManager.Popup != null)
				{
					PopupManager.TryFocus();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				base.OnPreviewKeyDown(e);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!IsPlaceHolder)
			{
				if (Keyboard.Modifiers.HasFlag(ModifierKeys.Control) && Keyboard.Modifiers.HasFlag(ModifierKeys.Shift))
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				base.OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseEnter(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!IsPlaceHolder)
			{
				CheckTootip();
				base.OnMouseEnter(e);
			}
		}

		private void CheckTootip()
		{
			if (base.ToolTip != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (GridControlOwner != null)
			{
				GridControlCellEventArgs e = new GridControlCellEventArgs(this, GridControl.CellTooltipCheckingEvent, GridControlOwner);
				GridControlOwner.OnCellTooltipChecking(e);
				if (e.Handled)
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			if (BindingOperations.GetBindingExpression(this, FrameworkElement.ToolTipProperty) == null)
			{
				SetBinding(FrameworkElement.ToolTipProperty, new Binding("Cell.Tooltip")
				{
					Source = this,
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
			}
		}

		private void TryLoadItemsSourceIfAny()
		{
			if (Cell != null && Cell.UIType == CellUIType.ComboBox)
			{
				Cell.EnsureItemsSource();
			}
		}

		private void TryUpdateEditModeIfPossible()
		{
			if (ShouldEnterEditModeWhenFocused)
			{
				if (ResolvedIsCurrent)
				{
					BeginEditModeIfPossible(syncTemplateImmediately: false);
				}
				else
				{
					EndEditModeIfPossible(commitChange: false);
				}
			}
		}

		internal bool ProcessMouseDownEventForGroup(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (Cell != null && Cell.Row != null && Cell.Row.GroupingState != RowGroupingState.None && Cell.Column != null && Cell.Column == Cell.Row.DataTable.Columns.FirstOrDefault() && base.Template != null && GetTemplateChild("PART_GroupBorder") is Border { IsMouseOver: not false })
			{
				if (Cell.Row.GroupingState == RowGroupingState.Expanded)
				{
					Cell.Row.GroupingState = RowGroupingState.Collapsed;
					e.Handled = true;
					return true;
				}
				if (Cell.Row.GroupingState == RowGroupingState.Collapsed)
				{
					Cell.Row.GroupingState = RowGroupingState.Expanded;
					e.Handled = true;
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private static void OnCellPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as GridControlCell).OnCellChanged((IDataCell)e.OldValue, (IDataCell)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnIsInEditModePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as GridControlCell).OnIsInEditModeChanged((bool)e.OldValue, (bool)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnIsCurrentPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as GridControlCell).OnIsCurrentChanged((bool)e.OldValue, (bool)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnIsDropDownOpenPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as GridControlCell).OnIsDropDownOpenChanged((bool)e.OldValue, (bool)e.NewValue);
		}
	}
	public class GridControlCellsPresenter : DataGridCellsPresenter
	{
		private static readonly Binding IsTabStopBinding = new Binding("Cell.IsReadOnly")
		{
			Converter = new BooleanInvertConverter(),
			Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
			RelativeSource = RelativeSource.Self
		};

		public GridControlCellsPresenter()
		{
			base.ItemsPanel = GridControlDataGridCellsPanel.RowItemsPanelTemplate;
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			GridControlDataGridRow ancestor = this.GetAncestor<GridControlDataGridRow>();
			if (ancestor != null)
			{
				ancestor.InternalCellsPresenter = this;
			}
		}

		protected override DependencyObject GetContainerForItemOverride()
		{
			GridControlCell gridControlCell = new GridControlCell();
			SetIsTabStop(gridControlCell);
			return gridControlCell;
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			Size result = base.MeasureOverride(availableSize);
			if (result.Width <= 0.0 && Utilities.GetAncestorFromType(this, typeof(DataGridRowsPresenter), allowSubclassOfType: false) is DataGridRowsPresenter parent && Utilities.GetDescendantFromType(parent, typeof(GridControlCellsPresenter), allowSubclassOfType: false) is GridControlCellsPresenter gridControlCellsPresenter)
			{
				if (result.Height > 0.0)
				{
					return new Size(gridControlCellsPresenter.DesiredSize.Width, result.Height);
				}
				return gridControlCellsPresenter.DesiredSize;
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected override void PrepareContainerForItemOverride(DependencyObject element, object item)
		{
			try
			{
				base.PrepareContainerForItemOverride(element, item);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		private void SetIsTabStop(GridControlCell cell)
		{
			if (base.Item is DataRow { DataTable: not null } dataRow && dataRow.DataTable.GridControl != null && dataRow.DataTable.GridControl.SkipReadonlyCellsWhenNavigation)
			{
				cell.SetBinding(Control.IsTabStopProperty, IsTabStopBinding);
			}
		}
	}
	public class GridControlDataGridCellsPanel : DataGridCellsPanel
	{
		private class MeasuredHeightRecord
		{
			public double Height { get; }

			public DateTime MeasuredTime { get; }

			public bool IsTimeValid => (DateTime.Now - MeasuredTime).TotalMilliseconds <= 100.0;

			public MeasuredHeightRecord(double height)
			{
				Height = height;
				MeasuredTime = DateTime.Now;
			}
		}

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsUsedByColumnHeaderProperty;

		private DataGridColumnHeadersPresenter _parentHeaderPresenter;

		private MeasuredHeightRecord _lastMesuredHeight;

		public static ItemsPanelTemplate RowItemsPanelTemplate { get; }

		public static ItemsPanelTemplate HeaderItemsPanelTemplate { get; }

		public bool IsUsedByColumnHeader
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsUsedByColumnHeaderProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsUsedByColumnHeaderProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private DataGridColumnHeadersPresenter ParentHeaderPresenter
		{
			get
			{
				if (_parentHeaderPresenter == null && IsUsedByColumnHeader)
				{
					_parentHeaderPresenter = this.GetAncestor<DataGridColumnHeadersPresenter>();
				}
				return _parentHeaderPresenter;
			}
		}

		static GridControlDataGridCellsPanel()
		{
			IsUsedByColumnHeaderProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsUsedByColumnHeader", typeof(bool), typeof(GridControlDataGridCellsPanel));
			RowItemsPanelTemplate = new ItemsPanelTemplate(new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(GridControlDataGridCellsPanel)));
			FrameworkElementFactory frameworkElementFactory = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(GridControlDataGridCellsPanel));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(IsUsedByColumnHeaderProperty, true);
			HeaderItemsPanelTemplate = new ItemsPanelTemplate(frameworkElementFactory);
		}

		protected override void OnRenderSizeChanged(SizeChangedInfo sizeInfo)
		{
			if (IsUsedByColumnHeader)
			{
				EnsureColumnHeaderHeightUpdated();
			}
			base.OnRenderSizeChanged(sizeInfo);
		}

		private void EnsureColumnHeaderHeightUpdated()
		{
			if (ParentHeaderPresenter == null || ParentHeaderPresenter.ActualHeight == base.ActualHeight)
			{
				return;
			}
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvokeOnce(delegate
			{
				if (ParentHeaderPresenter.ActualHeight != base.ActualHeight)
				{
					ParentHeaderPresenter.Height = base.ActualHeight;
					ParentHeaderPresenter.UpdateLayout();
					ParentHeaderPresenter.ClearValue(FrameworkElement.HeightProperty);
				}
			}, DispatcherPriority.Loaded);
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size constraint)
		{
			Size result = base.MeasureOverride(constraint);
			if (IsUsedByColumnHeader)
			{
				if (_lastMesuredHeight != null && _lastMesuredHeight.IsTimeValid && _lastMesuredHeight.Height > result.Height)
				{
					result = new Size(result.Width, _lastMesuredHeight.Height);
				}
				_lastMesuredHeight = new MeasuredHeightRecord(result.Height);
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size arrangeSize)
		{
			try
			{
				return base.ArrangeOverride(arrangeSize);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return arrangeSize;
			}
		}
	}
	public enum GridControlClipboardCopyMode
	{
		None,
		ExcludeHeader,
		IncludeHeader,
		IncludeHeaderOnlyWhenColumnSelected
	}
	public class GridControlDataGrid : DataGrid
	{
		internal GridControl ParentGridControl { get; set; }

		internal bool IgnoreSelectionChanged { get; private set; }

		public new IEnumerable<DataRow> ItemsSource
		{
			get
			{
				if (!(base.ItemsSource is IEnumerable<DataRow> enumerable) || !enumerable.Any() || enumerable.First().IsFakeRow)
				{
					return new DataRow[0];
				}
				return enumerable;
			}
			internal set
			{
				base.ItemsSource = value;
			}
		}

		internal IEnumerable<DataRow> BaseItemsSource => base.ItemsSource as IEnumerable<DataRow>;

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			if (!AutomationPeerHelpers.UseCustomAutomationPeers)
			{
				return base.OnCreateAutomationPeer();
			}
			return new GridControlDataGridAutomationPeer(this);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			ParentGridControl?.InternalScrollHelper.OnDataGridApplyTemplate();
		}

		protected override DependencyObject GetContainerForItemOverride()
		{
			return new GridControlDataGridRow();
		}

		protected override void OnMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			try
			{
				base.OnMouseMove(e);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		protected override void OnContextMenuOpening(ContextMenuEventArgs e)
		{
			if (ParentGridControl != null && ParentGridControl.InternalSelectionHelper.IsAllSelected && (e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject).GetAncestor<DataGridRowHeader>(this) != null)
			{
				IgnoreSelectionChanged = true;
			}
			try
			{
				base.OnContextMenuOpening(e);
			}
			finally
			{
				if (IgnoreSelectionChanged)
				{
					IgnoreSelectionChanged = false;
				}
			}
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			ParentGridControl?.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(ParentGridControl.CheckMeasuredSize));
			return base.MeasureOverride(availableSize);
		}
	}
	public class GridControlDataGridRow : DataGridRow
	{
		public GridControlCellsPresenter InternalCellsPresenter { get; internal set; }

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			if (!AutomationPeerHelpers.UseCustomAutomationPeers)
			{
				return base.OnCreateAutomationPeer();
			}
			return new GridControlDGRowAutomationPeer(this);
		}

		protected override void OnTemplateChanged(ControlTemplate oldTemplate, ControlTemplate newTemplate)
		{
			base.OnTemplateChanged(oldTemplate, newTemplate);
			InternalCellsPresenter = null;
		}
	}
	public class DataColumnSortingItem
	{
		public DataColumn Column { get; private set; }

		public ListSortDirection SortDirection { get; set; }

		public DataColumnSortingItem(DataColumn column, ListSortDirection sortDirection)
		{
			if (column == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("column");
			}
			Column = column;
			SortDirection = sortDirection;
		}
	}
	public class GridControlSortingInfo
	{
		private readonly List<DataColumnSortingItem> _sortedItems;

		private readonly GridControl _gridControl;

		private int _suppressUpdateFlag;

		public ReadOnlyCollection<DataColumnSortingItem> SortedItems { get; private set; }

		public GridControlSortingInfo(GridControl gridControl)
		{
			if (gridControl == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("gridControl");
			}
			_gridControl = gridControl;
			_sortedItems = new List<DataColumnSortingItem>();
			SortedItems = new ReadOnlyCollection<DataColumnSortingItem>(_sortedItems);
		}

		public ListSortDirection? GetSortDirection(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (column == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("column");
			}
			return _sortedItems.FirstOrDefault((DataColumnSortingItem x) => x.Column == column)?.SortDirection;
		}

		public void SetSort(DataColumn column, ListSortDirection sortDirection)
		{
			if (column == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("column");
			}
			if (_gridControl.DataSource == null || !_gridControl.DataSource.Columns.Contains(column))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("Specified column is not in the columns collection of the target GridControl's DataSource.");
			}
			DataColumnSortingItem dataColumnSortingItem = _sortedItems.FirstOrDefault((DataColumnSortingItem x) => x.Column == column);
			if (dataColumnSortingItem != null)
			{
				if (dataColumnSortingItem.SortDirection != sortDirection)
				{
					ListSortDirection sortDirection2 = dataColumnSortingItem.SortDirection;
					dataColumnSortingItem.SortDirection = sortDirection;
					OnSortingInfoChanged();
					NotifyColumn(dataColumnSortingItem.Column, sortDirection2, sortDirection);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				_sortedItems.Add(new DataColumnSortingItem(column, sortDirection));
				OnSortingInfoChanged();
				NotifyColumn(column, null, sortDirection);
			}
		}

		public void RemoveSort(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (column == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("column");
			}
			DataColumnSortingItem dataColumnSortingItem = _sortedItems.FirstOrDefault((DataColumnSortingItem x) => x.Column == column);
			if (dataColumnSortingItem != null)
			{
				_sortedItems.Remove(dataColumnSortingItem);
				OnSortingInfoChanged();
				NotifyColumn(column, dataColumnSortingItem.SortDirection, null);
			}
		}

		public void ClearSorts()
		{
			if (_sortedItems.Count > 0)
			{
				ClearSortsWithoutEvents();
				OnSortingInfoChanged();
			}
		}

		public void ClearSortsWithoutEvents()
		{
			if (_sortedItems.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (DataColumnSortingItem sortedItem in _sortedItems)
			{
				NotifyColumn(sortedItem.Column, sortedItem.SortDirection, null);
			}
			_sortedItems.Clear();
		}

		public void LockNotification()
		{
			_suppressUpdateFlag++;
		}

		public void UnlockNotification(bool notify)
		{
			_suppressUpdateFlag--;
			if (notify && _suppressUpdateFlag == 0)
			{
				OnSortingInfoChanged();
			}
		}

		private void NotifyColumn(DataColumn column, ListSortDirection? oldDirection, ListSortDirection? newDirection)
		{
			column.OnSortDirectionChanged(oldDirection, newDirection);
		}

		private void OnSortingInfoChanged()
		{
			if (_suppressUpdateFlag == 0 && _gridControl.DataSource != null)
			{
				_gridControl.DataSource.OnSortingInfoChanged();
			}
		}
	}
	public class DataRowHeaderControl : FrameworkElement
	{
