		private static Pen BorderPen { get; set; }

		private RowHeaderControlHelper ControlHelper { get; set; }

		static DataRowHeaderControl()
		{
			ActiveBackgroundProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ActiveBackground", typeof(Brush), typeof(DataRowHeaderControl));
			InactiveBackgroundProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("InactiveBackground", typeof(Brush), typeof(DataRowHeaderControl));
			TriangleActiveBrushProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("TriangleActiveBrush", typeof(Brush), typeof(DataRowHeaderControl));
			TriangleInactiveBrushProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("TriangleInactiveBrush", typeof(Brush), typeof(DataRowHeaderControl));
			NewActiveBrushProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("NewActiveBrush", typeof(Brush), typeof(DataRowHeaderControl));
			NewInactiveBrushProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("NewInactiveBrush", typeof(Brush), typeof(DataRowHeaderControl));
			MinusActiveBrushProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MinusActiveBrush", typeof(Brush), typeof(DataRowHeaderControl));
			MinusInactiveBrushProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MinusInactiveBrush", typeof(Brush), typeof(DataRowHeaderControl));
			IsHoveredProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsHovered", typeof(bool), typeof(DataRowHeaderControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsRender));
			IsCurrentProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsCurrent", typeof(bool), typeof(DataRowHeaderControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsRender));
			HasRowSelectionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("HasRowSelection", typeof(bool), typeof(DataRowHeaderControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsRender));
			IsNewlyAddedProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsNewlyAdded", typeof(bool), typeof(DataRowHeaderControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsRender));
			GroupingStateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("GroupingState", typeof(RowGroupingState), typeof(DataRowHeaderControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(RowGroupingState.None, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsRender));
			BorderPen = new Pen(Brushes.LightGray, 1.0);
			BorderPen.Freeze();
		}

		public DataRowHeaderControl()
		{
			ControlHelper = new RowHeaderControlHelper(this);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			ControlHelper.OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
			base.OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			ControlHelper.OnPreviewMouseMove(e);
			base.OnPreviewMouseMove(e);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			ControlHelper.OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
			base.OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseRightButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			ControlHelper.OnPreviewMouseRightButtonUp(e);
			base.OnPreviewMouseRightButtonUp(e);
		}

		protected override void OnDragOver(DragEventArgs e)
		{
			ControlHelper.OnDragOver(e);
			base.OnDragOver(e);
		}

		protected override void OnDragLeave(DragEventArgs e)
		{
			ControlHelper.OnDragLeave(e);
			base.OnDragLeave(e);
		}

		protected override void OnDrop(DragEventArgs e)
		{
			ControlHelper.OnDrop(e);
			base.OnDrop(e);
		}

		protected override void OnRender(DrawingContext drawingContext)
		{
			if (IsCurrent || HasRowSelection)
			{
				DrawBackground(drawingContext, ActiveBackground);
			}
			else
			{
				DrawBackground(drawingContext, InactiveBackground);
			}
			if (IsNewlyAdded)
			{
				if (IsHovered || IsCurrent)
				{
					DrawBackground(drawingContext, NewActiveBrush);
				}
				else
				{
					DrawBackground(drawingContext, NewInactiveBrush);
				}
			}
			else if (IsHovered || IsCurrent)
			{
				DrawBackground(drawingContext, TriangleActiveBrush);
			}
			else
			{
				DrawBackground(drawingContext, TriangleInactiveBrush);
			}
			base.OnRender(drawingContext);
		}

		private void DrawDoubleBackground(DrawingContext dc, Brush background, Brush foreground)
		{
			DrawBackground(dc, background);
			DrawBackground(dc, foreground);
		}

		private void DrawBackground(DrawingContext dc, Brush brush)
		{
			if (brush != null)
			{
				if (brush.CanFreeze && !brush.IsFrozen)
				{
					brush.Freeze();
				}
				dc.DrawRectangle(brush, BorderPen, new Rect(base.RenderSize));
			}
		}
	}
	public class GridControlColumn : DataGridColumn
	{
		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey IsViewGeneratedPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("IsViewGenerated", typeof(bool), typeof(GridControlColumn), new PropertyMetadata(OnIsViewGeneratedPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsViewGeneratedProperty = IsViewGeneratedPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		private BindingBase _binding;

		private double? _virtualizingWidth;

		public bool IsViewGenerated
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsViewGeneratedProperty);
			}
			private set
			{
				SetValue(IsViewGeneratedPropertyKey, value);
			}
		}

		public GridControl GridControlOwner { get; private set; }

		public DataColumn DataColumn { get; private set; }

		public BindingBase Binding
		{
			get
			{
				if (_binding == null)
				{
					_binding = new Binding
					{
						Path = new PropertyPath($"[{DataColumn.Index}]"),
						Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
					};
				}
				return _binding;
			}
		}

		internal double? VirtualizingWidth
		{
			get
			{
				return _virtualizingWidth;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_virtualizingWidth != value)
				{
					_virtualizingWidth = value;
					if (_virtualizingWidth.HasValue)
					{
						base.Width = new DataGridLength(_virtualizingWidth.Value);
					}
					else if (DataColumn.Width.HasValue && !AreClose(DataColumn.Width.Value, GridControlOwner.DefaultColumnWidth))
					{
						base.Width = new DataGridLength(DataColumn.Width.Value);
					}
					else
					{
						base.Width = default(DataGridLength);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public Visibility OriginalVisibility { get; private set; }

		public double? OriginalWidth { get; private set; }

		private static void OnIsViewGeneratedPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControlColumn gridControlColumn = d as GridControlColumn;
			if ((bool)e.NewValue)
			{
				gridControlColumn.OnViewGenerated();
				gridControlColumn.GridControlOwner.OnColumnViewGenerated(gridControlColumn.DataColumn);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnViewGenerated()
		{
		}

		public GridControlColumn(GridControl owner, DataColumn column)
		{
			if (owner == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("owner");
			}
			if (column == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("column");
			}
			column.TargetGridColumn = this;
			GridControlOwner = owner;
			DataColumn = column;
			base.Header = column;
			OriginalVisibility = column.Visibility;
			base.IsReadOnly = true;
			OriginalWidth = column.Width;
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, DataGridColumn.MinWidthProperty, new Binding
			{
				Source = column,
				Path = new PropertyPath(DataColumn.MinWidthProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, DataGridColumn.MaxWidthProperty, new Binding
			{
				Source = column,
				Path = new PropertyPath(DataColumn.MaxWidthProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, DataGridColumn.WidthProperty, new MultiBinding
			{
				Bindings = 
				{
					(BindingBase)new Binding
					{
						Source = column,
						Path = new PropertyPath(DataColumn.IsStarWidthProperty),
						Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
					},
					(BindingBase)new Binding
					{
						Source = column,
						Path = new PropertyPath(DataColumn.WidthProperty),
						Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
					}
				},
				Converter = new DataColumnWidthToDataGridLengthConverter()
			});
		}

		protected override void OnPropertyChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Property == DataGridColumn.ActualWidthProperty)
			{
				GridControlOwner.OnColumnActualWidthChanged(DataColumn);
			}
			base.OnPropertyChanged(e);
		}

		protected override FrameworkElement GenerateEditingElement(DataGridCell cell, object dataItem)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		protected override FrameworkElement GenerateElement(DataGridCell cell, object dataItem)
		{
			TimingDebugger.BeingTimingBlock("GenerateElement");
			if (!IsViewGenerated)
			{
				IsViewGenerated = true;
			}
			if (cell is GridControlCell gridControlCell)
			{
				gridControlCell.SetBinding(GridControlCell.CellProperty, Binding);
			}
			TimingDebugger.EndTimingBlock();
			return null;
		}

		public static bool AreClose(double x, double y)
		{
			return Math.Abs(x - y) < 1.0;
		}
	}
	internal class PlaceholderColumn : DataGridColumn
	{
		private readonly GridControl _gc;

		private bool _hasSelection;

		private bool _isColumnSelected;

		public GridControl GridControlOwner => _gc;

		public int LeftIndex { get; private set; }

		public int RightIndex { get; private set; }

		public int IncludedColumnsCount => RightIndex - LeftIndex + 1;

		public bool HasSelection
		{
			get
			{
				return _hasSelection;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_hasSelection == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				_hasSelection = value;
				foreach (DataColumn includedColumn in GetIncludedColumns())
				{
					includedColumn.HasSelection = _hasSelection;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsColumnSelected
		{
			get
			{
				return _isColumnSelected;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_isColumnSelected == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				_isColumnSelected = value;
				foreach (DataColumn includedColumn in GetIncludedColumns())
				{
					includedColumn.IsColumnSelected = _isColumnSelected;
				}
			}
		}

		public PlaceholderColumn(GridControl gc, int leftIndex, int rightIndex)
		{
			base.IsReadOnly = true;
			_gc = gc;
			LeftIndex = leftIndex;
			RightIndex = rightIndex;
		}

		protected override FrameworkElement GenerateEditingElement(DataGridCell cell, object dataItem)
		{
			return null;
		}

		protected override FrameworkElement GenerateElement(DataGridCell cell, object dataItem)
		{
			return null;
		}

		public IEnumerable<DataColumn> GetIncludedColumns()
		{
			DataTable table = _gc.DataSource;
			if (table != null)
			{
				for (int i = LeftIndex; i <= RightIndex && i < table.Columns.Count; i++)
				{
					yield return table.Columns[i];
				}
			}
		}

		public bool ContainsColumn(int columnIndex)
		{
			if (columnIndex >= LeftIndex)
			{
				return columnIndex <= RightIndex;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public class DataColumnWidthToDataGridLengthConverter : IMultiValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (values != null && values.Length == 2)
			{
				bool num = (values[0] as bool?) ?? false;
				double? num2 = values[1] as double?;
				if (num)
				{
					return new DataGridLength(num2 ?? 1.0, DataGridLengthUnitType.Star);
				}
				if (num2.HasValue)
				{
					return new DataGridLength(num2.Value);
				}
			}
			return default(DataGridLength);
		}

		public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public sealed class GridControlTemplates : ResourceDictionary, IComponentConnector
	{
		private static readonly GridControlTemplates Default = new GridControlTemplates();

		public static readonly ControlTemplate TextColumnHeaderTemplate = Default.FindDataTemplate("textColumnHeaderTemplate");

		public static readonly ControlTemplate ComboColumnHeaderTemplate = Default.FindDataTemplate("comboColumnHeaderTemplate");

		public static readonly ControlTemplate GenericColumnHeaderTemplate = Default.FindDataTemplate("genericColumnHeaderTemplate");

		public static readonly ControlTemplate TextColumnHeaderTemplateWithFilter = Default.FindDataTemplate("textColumnHeaderTemplateWithFilter");

		public static readonly ControlTemplate ComboColumnHeaderTemplateWithFilter = Default.FindDataTemplate("comboColumnHeaderTemplateWithFilter");

		public static readonly ControlTemplate GenericColumnHeaderTemplateWithFilter = Default.FindDataTemplate("genericColumnHeaderTemplateWithFilter");

		public static readonly ControlTemplate TextColumnHeaderTemplateForColumnSpan = Default.FindDataTemplate("textColumnHeaderTemplateForColumnSpan");

		public static readonly ControlTemplate ComboColumnHeaderTemplateForColumnSpan = Default.FindDataTemplate("comboColumnHeaderTemplateForColumnSpan");

		public static readonly ControlTemplate GenericColumnHeaderTemplateForColumnSpan = Default.FindDataTemplate("genericColumnHeaderTemplateForColumnSpan");

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		private GridControlTemplates()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
		}

		public static void UpdateColumnHeaderTemplate(DataColumnHeaderControl control)
		{
			if (control == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("control");
			}
			control.Template = GetColumnHeaderTemplate(control);
		}

		private static ControlTemplate GetColumnHeaderTemplate(DataColumnHeaderControl control)
		{
			DataColumn column = control.Column;
			if (column == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (column.HeaderContentTemplate != null)
			{
				if (IsLastColumnSpanSlaverHeader(control))
				{
					return GenericColumnHeaderTemplateForColumnSpan;
				}
				if (column.Filter.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					return GenericColumnHeaderTemplateWithFilter;
				}
				return GenericColumnHeaderTemplate;
			}
			if (column.HeaderItems != null)
			{
				if (IsLastColumnSpanSlaverHeader(control))
				{
					return ComboColumnHeaderTemplateForColumnSpan;
				}
				if (column.Filter.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					return ComboColumnHeaderTemplateWithFilter;
				}
				return ComboColumnHeaderTemplate;
			}
			if (IsLastColumnSpanSlaverHeader(control))
			{
				return TextColumnHeaderTemplateForColumnSpan;
			}
			if (column.Filter.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
			{
				return TextColumnHeaderTemplateWithFilter;
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static bool IsLastColumnSpanSlaverHeader(DataColumnHeaderControl control)
		{
			if (control.Column != null && control.LeaderColumn != null && control.Column.TargetGridColumn != null && control.Column.TargetGridColumn.GridControlOwner.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.IsLastColumnSpanSlaver(control.Column))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private ControlTemplate FindDataTemplate(string key)
		{
			return base[key] as ControlTemplate;
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls;component/gridcontroltemplates.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			_contentLoaded = true;
		}
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "PART_TextBox", Type = typeof(TextBox))]
	public class NumberInputBox : Control
	{
		private const string PART_TextBox = "PART_TextBox";

		private static readonly string SystemDecimalSymbol;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty TextProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SupportsDecimalProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty DisplayTextConverterProperty;

		private TextBox _templatedTextBox;

		private bool _isUpdatingTextProperty;

		public string Text
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(TextProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(TextProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool SupportsDecimal
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(SupportsDecimalProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(SupportsDecimalProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public IValueConverter DisplayTextConverter
		{
			get
			{
				return (IValueConverter)GetValue(DisplayTextConverterProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DisplayTextConverterProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static NumberInputBox()
		{
			SystemDecimalSymbol = GetSystemDecimalSymbol();
			TextProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Text", typeof(string), typeof(NumberInputBox));
			SupportsDecimalProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SupportsDecimal", typeof(bool), typeof(NumberInputBox), new UIPropertyMetadata(false));
			DisplayTextConverterProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DisplayTextConverter", typeof(IValueConverter), typeof(NumberInputBox), new UIPropertyMetadata(null, OnDisplayTextConverterPropertyChanged));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(NumberInputBox), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(NumberInputBox)));
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			_templatedTextBox = GetTemplateChild("PART_TextBox") as TextBox;
			if (_templatedTextBox != null)
			{
				_templatedTextBox.LostKeyboardFocus += _templatedTextBox_LostKeyboardFocus;
				_templatedTextBox.PreviewKeyDown += _templatedTextBox_PreviewKeyDown;
				_templatedTextBox.PreviewTextInput += _templatedTextBox_PreviewTextInput;
				_templatedTextBox.PreviewDragOver += _templatedTextBox_PreviewDragOver;
				_templatedTextBox.PreviewDrop += _templatedTextBox_PreviewDrop;
				_templatedTextBox.Loaded += _templatedTextBox_Loaded;
				_templatedTextBox.Unloaded += _templatedTextBox_Unloaded;
				UpdateDisplayTextBinding();
			}
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
		}

		public void UpdateTextProperty(bool silently)
		{
			if (_isUpdatingTextProperty)
			{
				return;
			}
			_isUpdatingTextProperty = true;
			try
			{
				bool flag = true;
				if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_templatedTextBox.Text) && !IsNumber(_templatedTextBox.Text, SupportsDecimal))
				{
					if (!silently)
					{
						MessageBox.Show(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.NumberInputBox_InvalidDialogMessage, AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.NumberInputBox_InvalidDialogTitle, MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Exclamation);
					}
					flag = false;
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (DisplayTextConverter == null)
				{
					Text = _templatedTextBox.Text;
					return;
				}
				CultureInfo cultureInfo = null;
				cultureInfo = ((base.Language == null) ? Thread.CurrentThread.CurrentCulture : base.Language.GetEquivalentCulture());
				object obj = DisplayTextConverter.ConvertBack(_templatedTextBox.Text, DataRow.StringType, null, cultureInfo);
				if (obj == null)
				{
					Text = null;
				}
				else if (obj is string)
				{
					Text = obj as string;
				}
				else
				{
					Text = obj.ToString();
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				_isUpdatingTextProperty = false;
			}
		}

		private void _templatedTextBox_LostKeyboardFocus(object sender, KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateTextProperty(silently: false);
		}

		private void _templatedTextBox_PreviewKeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Decimal && SystemDecimalSymbol == ",")
			{
				_templatedTextBox.RaiseEvent(new TextCompositionEventArgs(e.Device, new TextComposition(InputManager.Current, _templatedTextBox, ","))
				{
					RoutedEvent = UIElement.TextInputEvent
				});
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private void _templatedTextBox_PreviewTextInput(object sender, TextCompositionEventArgs e)
		{
			if (string.Equals(e.Text, "E", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) || string.Equals(e.Text, "+", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) || string.Equals(e.Text, "-", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (SupportsDecimal)
			{
				if (e.Text != SystemDecimalSymbol)
				{
					double result = 0.0;
					if (!double.TryParse(e.Text, out result))
					{
						e.Handled = true;
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				int result2 = 0;
				if (!int.TryParse(e.Text, out result2))
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private void _templatedTextBox_PreviewDragOver(object sender, DragEventArgs e)
		{
			string draggingString = GetDraggingString(e.Data);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(draggingString) && IsNumber(draggingString, SupportsDecimal))
			{
				e.Effects = DragDropEffects.Copy;
			}
			else
			{
				e.Effects = DragDropEffects.None;
			}
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private void _templatedTextBox_PreviewDrop(object sender, DragEventArgs e)
		{
			string draggingString = GetDraggingString(e.Data);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(draggingString) || !IsNumber(draggingString, SupportsDecimal))
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private void _templatedTextBox_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			CommandManager.AddPreviewCanExecuteHandler(_templatedTextBox, _templatedTextBox_CanExecute);
		}

		private void _templatedTextBox_Unloaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			CommandManager.RemovePreviewCanExecuteHandler(_templatedTextBox, _templatedTextBox_CanExecute);
		}

		private void _templatedTextBox_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Command == ApplicationCommands.Paste)
			{
				string clipboardText = GetClipboardText();
				e.CanExecute = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(clipboardText) && IsNumber(clipboardText, SupportsDecimal);
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateDisplayTextBinding()
		{
			if (_templatedTextBox != null)
			{
				Binding binding = new Binding
				{
					Source = this,
					Path = new PropertyPath(TextProperty),
					Converter = DisplayTextConverter,
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				};
				_templatedTextBox.SetBinding(TextBox.TextProperty, binding);
			}
		}

		private static bool IsNumber(string text, bool supportsDecimal)
		{
			if (supportsDecimal)
			{
				double result = 0.0;
				return double.TryParse(text, out result);
			}
			int result2 = 0;
			return int.TryParse(text, out result2);
		}

		private static string GetClipboardText()
		{
			string result = null;
			for (int i = 1; i <= 2; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					result = Clipboard.GetText();
				}
				catch
				{
					if (i < 2)
					{
						Thread.Sleep(0);
					}
					continue;
				}
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static string GetDraggingString(IDataObject dataObject)
		{
			if (dataObject != null)
			{
				string text = null;
				if (dataObject.GetDataPresent(DataFormats.UnicodeText))
				{
					text = dataObject.GetData(DataFormats.UnicodeText) as string;
				}
				if (text == null && dataObject.GetDataPresent(DataFormats.Text))
				{
					text = dataObject.GetData(DataFormats.Text) as string;
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
				{
					int num = text.IndexOf('\t');
					if (num > 0)
					{
						text = text.Substring(0, num);
					}
					int num2 = text.IndexOf(Environment.NewLine);
					if (num2 > 0)
					{
						text = text.Substring(0, num2);
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
					{
						return text;
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static string GetSystemDecimalSymbol()
		{
			return 0.1.ToString().Replace("0", string.Empty).Replace("1", string.Empty);
		}

		private static void OnDisplayTextConverterPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as NumberInputBox).UpdateDisplayTextBinding();
		}
	}
	public enum SortingMode
	{
		NotAllowed,
		AllowSingleColumnSorting,
		AllowMultipleColumnsSorting
	}
	public sealed class CellControlTemplates : ResourceDictionary, IComponentConnector
	{
		private static readonly CellControlTemplates Default = new CellControlTemplates();

		private static readonly ControlTemplate PlainTextTemplateWithTooltip = Default.FindTemplate("plainTextTemplateWithTooltip");

		private static readonly ControlTemplate PlainTextTemplateWithPrefixImage = Default.FindTemplate("plainTextTemplateWithPrefixImage");

		private static readonly ControlTemplate PlainTextTemplateWithAll = Default.FindTemplate("plainTextTemplateWithAll");

		private static readonly ControlTemplate CheckBoxTemplate = Default.FindTemplate("checkBoxTemplate");

		private static readonly ControlTemplate ComboBoxTemplateEditable = Default.FindTemplate("comboBoxTemplateEditable");

		private static readonly ControlTemplate ComboBoxTemplateNonEditable = Default.FindTemplate("comboBoxTemplateNonEditable");

		private static readonly ControlTemplate IntegerEditorTemplate = Default.FindTemplate("integerEditorTemplate");

		private static readonly ControlTemplate DoubleEditorTemplate = Default.FindTemplate("doubleEditorTemplate");

		private static readonly ControlTemplate TextEditorTemplate = Default.FindTemplate("textEditorTemplate");

		private static readonly ControlTemplate GenericTemplate = Default.FindTemplate("genericTemplate");

		private static readonly ControlTemplate GenericEditTemplate = Default.FindTemplate("genericEditTemplate");

		private static readonly ControlTemplate GroupingReadonlyCellTemplate = Default.FindTemplate("groupingReadonlyCellTemplate");

		public static readonly VisualBrush PlusSigh = Default["plusSign"] as VisualBrush;

		public static readonly VisualBrush MinusSigh = Default["minusSign"] as VisualBrush;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		private CellControlTemplates()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
		}

		public static void UpdateTemplates(GridControlCell control)
		{
			if (control != null && control.Cell != null)
			{
				control.Template = FindTemplateForCellData(control.Cell, control.IsInEditMode);
			}
		}

		public static bool IsEditableCombo(IDataCell cell)
		{
			if (cell.IsReadOnly)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (cell.UIType == CellUIType.PlainText)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (cell.UIType == CellUIType.CheckBox)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (cell.UIType == CellUIType.ComboBox)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (cell.ValueType == DataRow.NullableBooleanType)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (cell.ItemsSource == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private static ControlTemplate FindTemplateForCellData(IDataCell cell, bool isInEditMode)
		{
			if (!isInEditMode && cell.Template != null)
			{
				return GenericTemplate;
			}
			if (cell.Row.DataTable.HasGroupingRows && cell.Column.LogicalDisplayIndex == 0)
			{
				return GroupingReadonlyCellTemplate;
			}
			if (cell.UIType == CellUIType.PlainText)
			{
				return GetResolvedPlainTextTemplate(cell);
			}
			if (cell.UIType == CellUIType.CheckBox)
			{
				return CheckBoxTemplate;
			}
			if (cell.UIType == CellUIType.ComboBox)
			{
				if (cell.IsReadOnly)
				{
					return GetResolvedPlainTextTemplate(cell);
				}
				if (isInEditMode)
				{
					if (cell.EditTemplate != null)
					{
						return GenericEditTemplate;
					}
					return GetComboBoxTemplate(cell);
				}
				return GetResolvedPlainTextTemplate(cell);
			}
			if (cell.UIType == CellUIType.Unknown)
			{
				if (cell is DataCellBoolean)
				{
					return CheckBoxTemplate;
				}
				if (cell is DataCellInteger)
				{
					if (cell.IsReadOnly)
					{
						return GetResolvedPlainTextTemplate(cell);
					}
					if (!isInEditMode)
					{
						return GetResolvedPlainTextTemplate(cell);
					}
					if (cell.EditTemplate != null)
					{
						return GenericEditTemplate;
					}
					if (cell.ItemsSource == null)
					{
						return IntegerEditorTemplate;
					}
					return GetComboBoxTemplate(cell);
				}
				if (cell is DataCellDouble)
				{
					if (cell.IsReadOnly)
					{
						return GetResolvedPlainTextTemplate(cell);
					}
					if (!isInEditMode)
					{
						return GetResolvedPlainTextTemplate(cell);
					}
					if (cell.EditTemplate != null)
					{
						return GenericEditTemplate;
					}
					if (cell.ItemsSource == null)
					{
						return DoubleEditorTemplate;
					}
					return GetComboBoxTemplate(cell);
				}
				if (cell is DataCellText)
				{
					if (cell.IsReadOnly)
					{
						return GetResolvedPlainTextTemplate(cell);
					}
					if (!isInEditMode)
					{
						return GetResolvedPlainTextTemplate(cell);
					}
					if (cell.EditTemplate != null)
					{
						return GenericEditTemplate;
					}
					if (cell.ItemsSource == null)
					{
						return TextEditorTemplate;
					}
					return GetComboBoxTemplate(cell);
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static ControlTemplate GetResolvedPlainTextTemplate(IDataCell cell)
		{
			if (cell == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			bool flag = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(cell.TopRightWarningTooltip) || !string.IsNullOrEmpty(cell.BottomRightWarningTooltip);
			bool flag2 = cell.PrefixImage != null;
			if (flag && flag2)
			{
				return PlainTextTemplateWithAll;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				return PlainTextTemplateWithTooltip;
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				return PlainTextTemplateWithPrefixImage;
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static ControlTemplate GetComboBoxTemplate(IDataCell cell)
		{
			if (cell.IsComboEditable)
			{
				return ComboBoxTemplateEditable;
			}
			return ComboBoxTemplateNonEditable;
		}

		private ControlTemplate FindTemplate(string key)
		{
			return base[key] as ControlTemplate;
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls;component/cellcontroltemplates.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			_contentLoaded = true;
		}
	}
	public enum CellUIType
	{
		Unknown,
		PlainText,
		CheckBox,
		ComboBox
	}
	public abstract class DataCell<T> : NotifiableObjectBase, IDataCell, IDataCellInternal

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ValueAsStringProperty = ValueAsStringPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty MarginProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Margin", typeof(Thickness), typeof(DataCell<T>));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty PrefixImageProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("PrefixImage", typeof(ImageSource), typeof(DataCell<T>));

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey IsCurrentPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("IsCurrent", typeof(bool), typeof(DataCell<T>), new PropertyMetadata(OnIsCurrentPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsCurrentProperty = IsCurrentPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty NoValueDisplayTextProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("NoValueDisplayText", typeof(string), typeof(DataCell<T>), new PropertyMetadata(OnNoValueDisplayTextPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty TemplateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Template", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(DataCell<T>));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty EditTemplateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("EditTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(DataCell<T>));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ShouldEnterEditModeWhenFocusedProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ShouldEnterEditModeWhenFocused", typeof(bool), typeof(DataCell<T>), new PropertyMetadata(false));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty NumberToTextConverterProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("NumberToTextConverter", typeof(IValueConverter), typeof(DataCell<T>));

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey RowPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("Row", typeof(DataRow), typeof(DataCell<T>), new PropertyMetadata(OnRowPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty RowProperty = RowPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey ColumnPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("Column", typeof(DataColumn), typeof(DataCell<T>), new PropertyMetadata(OnColumnPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ColumnProperty = ColumnPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey HostControlPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("HostControl", typeof(GridControlCell), typeof(DataCell<T>), new PropertyMetadata(OnHostControlPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty HostControlProperty = HostControlPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey IsLoadedPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("IsLoaded", typeof(bool), typeof(DataCell<T>), new PropertyMetadata(OnIsLoadedPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsLoadedProperty = IsLoadedPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AllowDragProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowDrag", typeof(bool), typeof(DataCell<T>), new PropertyMetadata(true));

		public bool CanHaveEditMode
		{
			get
			{
				if (UIType == CellUIType.PlainText)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (UIType == CellUIType.CheckBox)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (IsReadOnly)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (UIType == CellUIType.ComboBox)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (ValueType == DataRow.NullableBooleanType)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		protected virtual T DefaultValue => default(T);

		public bool IsItemsSourceLoaded { get; private set; }

		object IDataCell.Value
		{
			get
			{
				return Value;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != null && value != DBNull.Value)
				{
					if (ValueType.IsAssignableFrom(value.GetType()))
					{
						T value2 = (T)value;
						Value = value2;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					Value = DefaultValue;
				}
			}
		}

		internal DataCell()
		{
			InvalidateDisplayText();
		}

		internal DataCell(T value)
			: this()
		{
			Value = value;
		}

		internal DataCell(T value, CellUIType uiType)
			: this(value)
		{
			UIType = uiType;
		}

		protected override void OnPropertyChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (HostControl != null && (e.Property == BackgroundProperty || e.Property == ForegroundProperty || e.Property == FontStyleProperty || e.Property == FontWeightProperty))
			{
				HostControl.InvalidateVisual();
			}
			base.OnPropertyChanged(e);
		}

		protected virtual void OnMaskChanged(string oldValue, string newValue)
		{
			InvalidateDisplayText();
		}

		protected virtual void OnValueChanged(T oldValue, T newValue)
		{
			if (!object.Equals(oldValue, newValue))
			{
				if (HostControl == null || !HostControl.SupressImmediateChangeReport)
				{
					ReportValueChanged(oldValue, newValue);
				}
				InvalidateDisplayText();
				InitializeFakeItemsSourceIfNeeded();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnUITypeChanged(CellUIType oldValue, CellUIType newValue)
		{
			InitializeFakeItemsSourceIfNeeded();
		}

		private void InitializeFakeItemsSourceIfNeeded()
		{
			if (UIType == CellUIType.ComboBox && !IsItemsSourceLoaded && Value != null)
			{
				ItemsSource = new T[1] { Value };
				IsItemsSourceLoaded = false;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnCurrentUnitsChanged(string oldValue, string newValue)
		{
			ReportUnitsChanged(oldValue, newValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemsSourceChanged(IEnumerable<T> oldValue, IEnumerable<T> newValue)
		{
			IsItemsSourceLoaded = newValue != null;
		}

		public virtual void EnsureItemsSource()
		{
			if (UIType == CellUIType.ComboBox && !IsItemsSourceLoaded && Row != null && Row.DataTable != null)
			{
				Row.DataTable.OnCellItemsSourceQueried(this);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsCurrentChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnHasValueChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			InvalidateDisplayText();
		}

		protected virtual void OnNoValueDisplayTextChanged(string oldValue, string newValue)
		{
			InvalidateDisplayText();
		}

		protected virtual void OnRowChanged(DataRow oldValue, DataRow newValue)
		{
			InvalidateDisplayText();
		}

		protected virtual void OnColumnChanged(DataColumn oldValue, DataColumn newValue)
		{
			InvalidateDisplayText();
		}

		protected virtual void OnHostControlChanged(GridControlCell oldValue, GridControlCell newValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsLoadedChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			if (newValue && HostControl != null && HostControl.GridControlOwner != null)
			{
				HostControl.GridControlOwner.OnCellValueLoaded(new CellValueLoadedEventArgs(this));
			}
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return ValueAsString;
		}

		protected abstract bool CanParseInternal(string value);

		protected abstract void ParseInternal(string value);

		public bool CanParse(string value)
		{
			return CanParseInternal(value);
		}

		public void Parse(string value)
		{
			ParseInternal(value);
		}

		protected void RefreshFormattedText()
		{
			if (!HasValue && NoValueDisplayText != null)
			{
				FormattedText = NoValueDisplayText;
				return;
			}
			if (Value == null)
			{
				FormattedText = null;
				return;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(ResolvedMask))
			{
				FormattedText = Value.ToString();
				return;
			}
			string format = "{0:" + ResolvedMask + "}";
			FormattedText = string.Format(format, Value);
		}

		protected void RefreshValueAsString()
		{
			if (!HasValue && NoValueDisplayText != null)
			{
				ValueAsString = FixValueAsString(NoValueDisplayText);
				return;
			}
			if (Value == null)
			{
				ValueAsString = FixValueAsString(null);
			}
			else if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(FormattedText))
			{
				ValueAsString = FixValueAsString(FormattedText);
			}
			else
			{
				ValueAsString = FixValueAsString(Value.ToString());
			}
			if (HostControl != null)
			{
				HostControl.OnCellValueAsStringChanged();
			}
		}

		private string FixValueAsString(string text)
		{
			if (Row != null && Column != null && Row.DataTable != null)
			{
				return Row.DataTable.FixCellDisplayText(this, text);
			}
			return text;
		}

		private void ReportValueChanged(T oldValue, T newValue)
		{
			if (!_suppressValueChangedReport && !object.Equals(oldValue, newValue) && Row != null && Row.DataTable != null && Row.DataTable.IsCellValueChangedReportEnabled)
			{
				Row.DataTable.CallCellValueChanged(this, oldValue, newValue);
			}
		}

		private void ReportUnitsChanged(string oldUnits, string newUnits)
		{
			if (!string.Equals(oldUnits, newUnits, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase) && Row != null && Row.DataTable != null && Row.DataTable.IsCellValueChangedReportEnabled)
			{
				Row.DataTable.CallCellUnitsChanged(this, oldUnits, newUnits);
			}
		}

		public void ClearValue()
		{
			T value = Value;
			_suppressValueChangedReport = true;
			try
			{
				Value = DefaultValue;
				if (Row != null && Row.DataTable != null)
				{
					Row.DataTable.OnCellValueCleared(this);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				_suppressValueChangedReport = false;
			}
			T value2 = Value;
			ReportValueChanged(value, value2);
			InvalidateDisplayText();
		}

		public BindingBase GetBindingBase(string propertyName)
		{
			if (propertyName == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("propertyName");
			}
			BindingBase binding = null;
			if (TryGetBindingBase(propertyName, ValueProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingBase(propertyName, TagProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingBase(propertyName, BackgroundProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingBase(propertyName, ForegroundProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingBase(propertyName, FontStyleProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingBase(propertyName, FontWeightProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingBase(propertyName, TooltipProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingBase(propertyName, IsReadOnlyProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingBase(propertyName, BottomRightWarningTooltipProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingBase(propertyName, ContentHorizontalAlignmentProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingBase(propertyName, EditTemplateProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingBase(propertyName, ItemsSourceProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingBase(propertyName, MarginProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingBase(propertyName, MaskProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingBase(propertyName, NoValueDisplayTextProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingBase(propertyName, PrefixImageProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingBase(propertyName, TooltipIndicatorBrushProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingBase(propertyName, TopRightWarningTooltipProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			TryGetBindingBase(propertyName, UITypeProperty, out binding);
			return binding;
		}

		public BindingExpression GetBindingExpression(string propertyName)
		{
			if (propertyName == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("propertyName");
			}
			BindingExpression binding = null;
			if (TryGetBindingExpression(propertyName, ValueProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingExpression(propertyName, TagProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingExpression(propertyName, BackgroundProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingExpression(propertyName, ForegroundProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingExpression(propertyName, FontStyleProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingExpression(propertyName, FontWeightProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingExpression(propertyName, TooltipProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingExpression(propertyName, IsReadOnlyProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingExpression(propertyName, BottomRightWarningTooltipProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingExpression(propertyName, ContentHorizontalAlignmentProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingExpression(propertyName, EditTemplateProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingExpression(propertyName, ItemsSourceProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingExpression(propertyName, MarginProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingExpression(propertyName, MaskProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingExpression(propertyName, NoValueDisplayTextProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingExpression(propertyName, PrefixImageProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingExpression(propertyName, TooltipIndicatorBrushProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			if (TryGetBindingExpression(propertyName, TopRightWarningTooltipProperty, out binding))
			{
				return binding;
			}
			TryGetBindingExpression(propertyName, UITypeProperty, out binding);
			return binding;
		}

		public void SetBinding(string propertyName, BindingBase binding)
		{
			if (propertyName == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("propertyName");
			}
			if (binding == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("binding");
			}
			if (!TryBindProperty(propertyName, ValueProperty, binding) && !TryBindProperty(propertyName, TagProperty, binding) && !TryBindProperty(propertyName, BackgroundProperty, binding) && !TryBindProperty(propertyName, ForegroundProperty, binding) && !TryBindProperty(propertyName, FontStyleProperty, binding) && !TryBindProperty(propertyName, FontWeightProperty, binding) && !TryBindProperty(propertyName, TooltipProperty, binding) && !TryBindProperty(propertyName, IsReadOnlyProperty, binding) && !TryBindProperty(propertyName, BottomRightWarningTooltipProperty, binding) && !TryBindProperty(propertyName, ContentHorizontalAlignmentProperty, binding) && !TryBindProperty(propertyName, EditTemplateProperty, binding) && !TryBindProperty(propertyName, ItemsSourceProperty, binding) && !TryBindProperty(propertyName, MarginProperty, binding) && !TryBindProperty(propertyName, MaskProperty, binding) && !TryBindProperty(propertyName, NoValueDisplayTextProperty, binding) && !TryBindProperty(propertyName, PrefixImageProperty, binding) && !TryBindProperty(propertyName, TooltipIndicatorBrushProperty, binding) && !TryBindProperty(propertyName, TopRightWarningTooltipProperty, binding))
			{
				TryBindProperty(propertyName, UITypeProperty, binding);
			}
		}

		public void InvalidateDisplayText()
		{
			RefreshFormattedText();
			RefreshValueAsString();
		}

		private bool TryBindProperty(string propertyName, DependencyProperty dp, BindingBase binding)
		{
			if (string.Equals(propertyName, dp.Name))
			{
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, dp, binding);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool TryGetBindingBase(string propertyName, DependencyProperty dp, out BindingBase binding)
		{
			binding = null;
			if (string.Equals(propertyName, dp.Name))
			{
				binding = BindingOperations.GetBindingBase(this, dp);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool TryGetBindingExpression(string propertyName, DependencyProperty dp, out BindingExpression binding)
		{
			binding = null;
			if (string.Equals(propertyName, dp.Name))
			{
				binding = BindingOperations.GetBindingExpression(this, dp);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		bool IDataCell.CanParse(string value)
		{
			return CanParse(value);
		}

		void IDataCell.Parse(string value)
		{
			Parse(value);
		}

		void IDataCell.EnsureItemsSource()
		{
			EnsureItemsSource();
		}

		void IDataCell.ClearValue()
		{
			ClearValue();
		}

		void IDataCell.SetBinding(string propertyName, BindingBase binding)
		{
			SetBinding(propertyName, binding);
		}

		BindingBase IDataCell.GetBindingBase(string propertyName)
		{
			return GetBindingBase(propertyName);
		}

		BindingExpression IDataCell.GetBindingExpression(string propertyName)
		{
			return GetBindingExpression(propertyName);
		}

		void IDataCell.InvalidateDisplayText()
		{
			InvalidateDisplayText();
		}

		public static int Compares(IDataCell x, IDataCell y)
		{
			if (x == null && y == null)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			if (x == null)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			if (y == null)
			{
				return 1;
			}
			IComparable comparable = x.Value as IComparable;
			IComparable comparable2 = y.Value as IComparable;
			if (comparable == null && comparable2 == null)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			if (comparable == null)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			if (comparable2 == null)
			{
				return 1;
			}
			return comparable.CompareTo(comparable2);
		}

		private static void OnMaskPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataCell<T>).OnMaskChanged((string)e.OldValue, (string)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnValuePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataCell<T>).OnValueChanged((T)e.OldValue, (T)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnUITypePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataCell<T>).OnUITypeChanged((CellUIType)e.OldValue, (CellUIType)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnCurrentUnitsPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataCell<T>).OnCurrentUnitsChanged((string)e.OldValue, (string)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnItemsSourcePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataCell<T>).OnItemsSourceChanged((IEnumerable<T>)e.OldValue, (IEnumerable<T>)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnIsCurrentPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataCell<T>).OnIsCurrentChanged((bool)e.OldValue, (bool)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnHasValuePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataCell<T>).OnHasValueChanged((bool)e.OldValue, (bool)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnNoValueDisplayTextPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataCell<T>).OnNoValueDisplayTextChanged((string)e.OldValue, (string)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnRowPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataCell<T>).OnRowChanged((DataRow)e.OldValue, (DataRow)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnColumnPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataCell<T>).OnColumnChanged((DataColumn)e.OldValue, (DataColumn)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnHostControlPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			DataCell<T> obj = d as DataCell<T>;
			obj.OnHostControlChanged((GridControlCell)e.OldValue, (GridControlCell)e.NewValue);
			obj.IsLoaded = obj.HostControl != null;
		}

		private static void OnIsLoadedPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataCell<T>).OnIsLoadedChanged((bool)e.OldValue, (bool)e.NewValue);
		}

	public sealed class DataCellBoolean : DataCell<bool?>
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsThreeStateProperty;

		public bool IsThreeState
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsThreeStateProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsThreeStateProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected override bool? DefaultValue
		{
			get
			{
				if (!IsThreeState)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		static DataCellBoolean()
		{
			IsThreeStateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsThreeState", typeof(bool), typeof(DataCellBoolean), new PropertyMetadata(false));
			DataCell<bool?>.ValueProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(DataCellBoolean), new PropertyMetadata(false));
			DataCell<bool?>.ContentHorizontalAlignmentProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(DataCellBoolean), new PropertyMetadata(HorizontalAlignment.Center));
		}

		public DataCellBoolean()
		{
		}

		public DataCellBoolean(bool? value)
			: base(value)
		{
		}

		public DataCellBoolean(bool value, CellUIType uiType)
			: base((bool?)value, uiType)
		{
		}

		protected override bool CanParseInternal(string value)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
			{
				return true;
			}
			bool result;
			return bool.TryParse(value, out result);
		}

		protected override void ParseInternal(string value)
		{
			bool result;
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
			{
				base.Value = DefaultValue;
			}
			else if (bool.TryParse(value, out result))
			{
				base.Value = result;
			}
			else
			{
				base.Value = DefaultValue;
			}
		}

		public override void EnsureItemsSource()
		{
			if (base.UIType == CellUIType.ComboBox && !base.IsItemsSourceLoaded)
			{
				if (IsThreeState)
				{
					base.ItemsSource = new bool?[3] { true, false, null };
				}
				else
				{
					base.ItemsSource = new bool?[2] { true, false };
				}
			}
			else
			{
				base.EnsureItemsSource();
			}
		}
	}
	public sealed class DataCellDouble : DataCell<double?>
	{
		private const string MaskG6 = "g6";

		protected override string ResolvedMask
		{
			get
			{
				string resolvedMask = base.ResolvedMask;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(resolvedMask))
				{
					return resolvedMask;
				}
				return "g6";
			}
		}

		static DataCellDouble()
		{
			DataCell<double?>.ContentHorizontalAlignmentProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(DataCellDouble), new PropertyMetadata(HorizontalAlignment.Right));
		}

		public DataCellDouble()
		{
		}

		public DataCellDouble(double? value, bool useValueAsTooltip = false)
			: base(value)
		{
			if (useValueAsTooltip)
			{
				base.Tooltip = base.Value;
			}
		}

		public DataCellDouble(double? value, CellUIType uiType)
			: base(value, uiType)
		{
		}

		protected override bool CanParseInternal(string value)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
			{
				return true;
			}
			double result;
			bool flag = double.TryParse(value, out result);
			if (flag && base.ItemsSource != null)
			{
				EnsureItemsSource();
				if (base.ItemsSource != null)
				{
					return base.ItemsSource.Contains(result);
				}
			}
			return flag;
		}

		protected override void ParseInternal(string value)
		{
			if (double.TryParse(value, out var result))
			{
				base.Value = result;
			}
			else
			{
				base.Value = null;
			}
		}
	}
	public sealed class DataCellInteger : DataCell<int?>
	{
		private const string MaskG6 = "g6";

		protected override string ResolvedMask
		{
			get
			{
				string resolvedMask = base.ResolvedMask;

			DataCell<int?>.ContentHorizontalAlignmentProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(DataCellInteger), new PropertyMetadata(HorizontalAlignment.Right));

		{
		}

			: base(value)
		{
		}

		public DataCellInteger(int? value, CellUIType uiType)
			: base(value, uiType)
		{
		}

		protected override bool CanParseInternal(string value)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
			{
				return true;
			}
			int result;
			bool flag = int.TryParse(value, out result);
			if (flag && base.ItemsSource != null)
			{
				EnsureItemsSource();
				if (base.ItemsSource != null)
				{
					return base.ItemsSource.Contains(result);
				}
			}
			return flag;
		}

		protected override void ParseInternal(string value)
		{
			if (int.TryParse(value, out var result))
			{
				base.Value = result;
			}
			else
			{
				base.Value = null;
			}
		}
	}
	public sealed class DataCellText : DataCell<string>
	{
		public DataCellText()
		{
		}

		public DataCellText(string value)
			: base(value)
		{
		}

		public DataCellText(string value, CellUIType uiType)
			: base(value, uiType)
		{
		}

		protected override bool CanParseInternal(string value)
		{
			if (base.ItemsSource != null)
			{
				EnsureItemsSource();
			}
			if (base.ItemsSource != null && !base.IsComboEditable)
			{
				return base.ItemsSource.Contains(value, StringComparer.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase);
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected override void ParseInternal(string value)
		{
			if (base.ItemsSource != null)
			{
				string text = base.ItemsSource.FirstOrDefault((string x) => string.Equals(x, value, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase));
				if (text != null)
				{
					base.Value = text;
				}
				else if (base.IsComboEditable)
				{
					base.Value = value;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				base.Value = value;
			}
		}
	}

		public Visibility Visibility

		public double RenderedWidth
		{
			get
			{
				if (TargetGridColumn != null)
				{
					return TargetGridColumn.ActualWidth;
				}
				if (Width.HasValue)
				{
					return Width.Value;
				}
				return 0.0;
			}
		}

		public DataColumnFilter Filter { get; private set; }

		public int DisplayIndex
		{
			get
			{
				if (_targetGridColumn != null)
				{
					return _targetGridColumn.DisplayIndex;
				}
				return 9999999;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_targetGridColumn != null)
				{
					_targetGridColumn.DisplayIndex = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public int LogicalDisplayIndex { get; internal set; }

		internal GridControlColumn TargetGridColumn
		{
			get
			{
				return _targetGridColumn;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_targetGridColumn != value)
				{
					_targetGridColumn = value;
					BindingOperations.SetBinding(_targetGridColumn, DataGridColumn.VisibilityProperty, new Binding
					{
						Source = this,
						Path = new PropertyPath(VisibilityProperty),
						Mode = BindingMode.TwoWay
					});
				}
			}
		}

		internal DataColumnHeaderControl HeaderControl { get; set; }

		public DataColumn()
		{
			Filter = new DataColumnFilter(this);
		}

		public DataColumn(string header)
			: this()
		{
			Header = header;
		}

		protected virtual void OnHeaderChanged(string oldValue, string newValue)
		{
			if (TargetGridColumn != null && TargetGridColumn.GridControlOwner != null && TargetGridColumn.GridControlOwner.DataSource != null)
			{
				TargetGridColumn.GridControlOwner.DataSource.OnColumnHeaderChanged(this, oldValue, newValue);
			}
			InvalidateHeaderControlTemplate();
		}

		protected virtual void OnHeaderItemsChanged(IEnumerable<string> oldValue, IEnumerable<string> newValue)
		{
			InvalidateHeaderControlTemplate();
		}

		protected virtual void OnHeaderContentTemplateChanged(DataTemplate oldValue, DataTemplate newValue)
		{
			InvalidateHeaderControlTemplate();
		}

		protected virtual void OnVisibilityChanged(Visibility oldValue, Visibility newValue)
		{
			if (TargetGridColumn != null)
			{
				TargetGridColumn.GridControlOwner.InternalFreezingHelper.UpdateColumnFrozenLine();
			}
			if (TargetGridColumn != null && TargetGridColumn.GridControlOwner != null)
			{
				TargetGridColumn.GridControlOwner.InternalColumnsVirtualizationHelper.InvalidateLogicalDisplayIndexes();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnDefaultDataTypeChanged(Type oldValue, Type newValue)
		{
			if (newValue == DataRow.StringType)
			{
				Filter.SelectedOperator = DataColumnFilterOperator.StartsWith;
			}
			else
			{
				Filter.SelectedOperator = DataColumnFilterOperator.Equal;
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnSortDirectionChanged(ListSortDirection? oldValue, ListSortDirection? newValue)
		{
			SortDirection = newValue;
			if (HeaderControl != null)
			{
				HeaderControl.InvalidateVisual();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnHasSelectionChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			if (HeaderControl != null)
			{
				HeaderControl.InvalidateVisual();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsColumnSelectedChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			if (TargetGridColumn != null)
			{
				TargetGridColumn.GridControlOwner.TryInvokeSelectionChanged();
			}
		}

		public void InvalidateHeaderControlTemplate()
		{
			if (HeaderControl != null)
			{
				HeaderControl.InvalidateTemplate();
			}
		}

		[Obsolete]
		public void ApplyVisibilityChange()
		{
		}

		public static object ConvertStringToObject(string valueAsString, Type targetType)
		{
			if (targetType == DataRow.NullableBooleanType || targetType == DataRow.BooleanType)
			{
				if (string.Equals(valueAsString, "1", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (string.Equals(valueAsString, "0", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
				{
					return false;
				}
				bool result = false;
				if (bool.TryParse(valueAsString, out result))
				{
					return result;
				}
			}
			else if (targetType == DataRow.NullableIntType || targetType == DataRow.Int32Type)
			{
				int result2 = 0;
				if (int.TryParse(valueAsString, out result2))
				{
					return result2;
				}
			}
			else if (targetType == DataRow.NullableDoubleType || targetType == DataRow.DoubleType)
			{
				double result3 = 0.0;
				if (double.TryParse(valueAsString, out result3))
				{
					return result3;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (!(targetType == DataRow.StringType))
				{
					return valueAsString;
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(valueAsString))
				{
					return valueAsString.Trim();
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static void OnIndexPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataColumn)._name = null;
		}

		private static void OnHeaderPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataColumn).OnHeaderChanged((string)e.OldValue, (string)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnHeaderItemsPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataColumn).OnHeaderItemsChanged((IEnumerable<string>)e.OldValue, (IEnumerable<string>)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnVisibilityPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataColumn).OnVisibilityChanged((Visibility)e.OldValue, (Visibility)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnDefaultDataTypePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataColumn).OnDefaultDataTypeChanged((Type)e.OldValue, (Type)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnHasSelectionPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataColumn).OnHasSelectionChanged((bool)e.OldValue, (bool)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnIsColumnSelectedPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataColumn).OnIsColumnSelectedChanged((bool)e.OldValue, (bool)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnHeaderContentTemplatePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataColumn).OnHeaderContentTemplateChanged((DataTemplate)e.OldValue, (DataTemplate)e.NewValue);
		}
	}
	public class DataRow : NotifiableObjectBase
	{
		private const double UnitGroupingIndent = 20.0;

		private static readonly ReadOnlyCollection<DataColumn> EmptyColumns = new ReadOnlyCollection<DataColumn>(new List<DataColumn>());

		private readonly ReadOnlyCollection<DataColumn> _availableColumns;

		private readonly Dictionary<DataColumn, IDataCell> _cellDatas = new Dictionary<DataColumn, IDataCell>();

		public static readonly Type StringType = typeof(string);

		public static readonly Type BooleanType = typeof(bool);

		public static readonly Type NullableIntType = typeof(int?);

		public static readonly Type NullableDoubleType = typeof(double?);

		public static readonly Type NullableBooleanType = typeof(bool?);

		public static readonly Type Int32Type = typeof(int);

		public static readonly Type Int16Type = typeof(short);

		public static readonly Type Int64Type = typeof(long);

		public static readonly Type DoubleType = typeof(double);

		public static readonly Type FloatType = typeof(float);

		public static readonly Type DecimalType = typeof(decimal);

		public static readonly DependencyProperty TagProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Tag", typeof(object), typeof(DataRow));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty VisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Visibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(DataRow), new PropertyMetadata(Visibility.Visible, OnVisibilityPropertyChanged));

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey IsFilteredOutPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("IsFilteredOut", typeof(bool), typeof(DataRow), new PropertyMetadata(false, OnIsFilteredOutPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsFilteredOutProperty = IsFilteredOutPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey ResolvedVisibilityPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("ResolvedVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(DataRow), new PropertyMetadata(Visibility.Visible, OnResolvedVisibilityPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ResolvedVisibilityProperty = ResolvedVisibilityPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey HasSelectionPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("HasSelection", typeof(bool), typeof(DataRow), new PropertyMetadata());

		public static readonly DependencyProperty HasSelectionProperty = HasSelectionPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey IsCurrentPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("IsCurrent", typeof(bool), typeof(DataRow), new PropertyMetadata());

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsCurrentProperty = IsCurrentPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsNewlyAddedProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsNewlyAdded", typeof(bool), typeof(DataRow));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty GroupingStateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("GroupingState", typeof(RowGroupingState), typeof(DataRow), new PropertyMetadata(OnGroupingStatePropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty GroupingRowsCountProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("GroupingRowsCount", typeof(int), typeof(DataRow), new PropertyMetadata(OnGroupingRowsCountPropertyChanged));

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey GroupingIndentPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("GroupingIndent", typeof(double), typeof(DataRow), new PropertyMetadata(0.0, OnGroupingIndentPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty GroupingIndentProperty = GroupingIndentPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey IsParentGroupCollapsedPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("IsParentGroupCollapsed", typeof(bool), typeof(DataRow), new PropertyMetadata(false, OnIsParentGroupCollapsedPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsParentGroupCollapsedProperty = IsParentGroupCollapsedPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		private readonly bool _setParentTableInCtor;

		public int AvailableColumnsCount => AvailableColumns.Count;

		private ReadOnlyCollection<DataColumn> AvailableColumns
		{
			get
			{
				if (DataTable != null)
				{
					return DataTable.Columns ?? EmptyColumns;
				}
				return _availableColumns ?? EmptyColumns;
			}
		}
