		protected IEnumerable<DataColumn> Columns => AvailableColumns;

		public DataTable DataTable { get; private set; }

		internal bool IsFakeRow { get; set; }

		public bool HasData => _cellDatas.Count > 0;

		public IDataCell this[string index]
		{
			get
			{
				return this[GetColumn(index)];
			}
			set
			{
				this[GetColumn(index)] = value;
			}
		}

		public IDataCell this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				return this[GetColumn(index)];
			}
			set
			{
				this[GetColumn(index)] = value;
			}
		}

		public IDataCell this[DataColumn index]
		{
			get
			{
				if (IsFakeRow)
				{
					return null;
				}
				if (index == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				if (_cellDatas.ContainsKey(index))
				{
					return _cellDatas[index];
				}
				if (DataTable != null)
				{
					TimingDebugger.BeingTimingBlock("DataTable.OnCellValueQueried");
					IDataCell dataCell = DataTable.CallCellValueQueried(this, index);
					TimingDebugger.EndTimingBlock();
					if (dataCell != null)
					{
						InternalSetData(index, dataCell);
					}
					return dataCell;
				}
				return null;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!IsFakeRow && index != null && AvailableColumns.Contains(index))
				{
					InternalSetData(index, value);
					base.Dispatcher.BeginInvokeOnce(base.RaiseIndexerPropertyChanged);
				}
			}
		}

		[DefaultValue(false)]
		public bool SuppressAutoClearWhenRefresh { get; set; }

		internal void InternalSetDataTable(DataTable parenTable)
		{
			if (!_setParentTableInCtor)
			{
				DataTable = parenTable;
			}
		}

		[Obsolete]
		public DataRow(IEnumerable<DataColumn> columns)
		{
			if (columns == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("columns");
			}
			_availableColumns = new ReadOnlyCollection<DataColumn>(columns.ToList());
			_setParentTableInCtor = false;
		}

		public DataRow(DataTable parentDataTable)
		{
			if (parentDataTable == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("parentDataTable");
			}
			DataTable = parentDataTable;
			_setParentTableInCtor = true;
		}

		[Obsolete]
		public DataRow(DataTable parentDataTable, IEnumerable<DataColumn> columns)
			: this(parentDataTable)
		{
		}

		public bool IsDataLoaded(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (column == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("column");
			}
			return _cellDatas.ContainsKey(column);
		}

		private DataColumn GetColumn(string fieldName)
		{
			if (int.TryParse(fieldName, out var result))
			{
				return GetColumn(result);
			}
			return AvailableColumns.FirstOrDefault((DataColumn x) => string.Equals(x.Name, fieldName, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase));
		}

		private DataColumn GetColumn(int fieldIndex)
		{
			if (fieldIndex < 0 || fieldIndex >= AvailableColumns.Count)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return AvailableColumns[fieldIndex];
		}

		protected virtual void InternalSetData(DataColumn column, IDataCell dataCell)
		{
			if (dataCell == null)
			{
				if (_cellDatas.ContainsKey(column))
				{
					_cellDatas.Remove(column);
				}
				return;
			}
			_cellDatas[column] = dataCell;
			if (column.IsReadOnly)
			{
				dataCell.IsReadOnly = true;
			}
			if (dataCell.ItemsSource == null && column.CellsItemsSource != null)
			{
				dataCell.ItemsSource = column.CellsItemsSource;
			}
			if (dataCell.Background == null && column.CellsBackground != null)
			{
				dataCell.Background = column.CellsBackground;
			}
			if (dataCell.UIType == CellUIType.Unknown && column.UseComboBoxForBoolValue && dataCell is DataCellBoolean)
			{
				dataCell.UIType = CellUIType.ComboBox;
			}
			if (dataCell is DataCellDouble && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(column.ValueDisplayFormat))
			{
				dataCell.Mask = column.ValueDisplayFormat;
			}
			if (column.CellContentHorizontalAlignment.HasValue)
			{
				dataCell.ContentHorizontalAlignment = column.CellContentHorizontalAlignment.Value;
			}
			if (column.PrefixImage != null && dataCell.PrefixImage == null)
			{
				dataCell.PrefixImage = column.PrefixImage;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(column.NoValueDisplayText) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(dataCell.NoValueDisplayText))
			{
				dataCell.NoValueDisplayText = column.NoValueDisplayText;
			}
			if (dataCell.Template == null && column.Template != null)
			{
				dataCell.Template = column.Template;
			}
			if (dataCell.EditTemplate == null && column.EditTemplate != null)
			{
				dataCell.EditTemplate = column.EditTemplate;
			}
			if (column.AllowCellDrag.HasValue)
			{
				dataCell.AllowDrag = column.AllowCellDrag.Value;
			}
			if (dataCell is IDataCellInternal dataCellInternal)
			{
				dataCellInternal.Row = this;
				dataCellInternal.Column = column;
			}
		}

		public void RefreshLoadedData()
		{
			if (DataTable == null || _cellDatas.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_cellDatas.Keys.Any((DataColumn x) => !AvailableColumns.Contains(x)))
			{
				_cellDatas.Clear();
			}
			else
			{
				foreach (DataColumn item in _cellDatas.Keys.ToList())
				{
					IDataCell dataCell = _cellDatas[item];
					IDataCell dataCell2 = DataTable.CallCellValueQueried(this, item);
					if (dataCell == null)
					{
						if (dataCell2 != null)
						{
							this[item] = dataCell2;
						}
						continue;
					}
					if (dataCell2 == null)
					{
						this[item] = null;
						DataTable.OnCellValueReloaded(dataCell, null, reuseOriginalCellValue: false);
						continue;
					}
					if (dataCell2.ValueType != dataCell.ValueType)
					{
						this[item] = dataCell2;
						DataTable.OnCellValueReloaded(dataCell, dataCell2, reuseOriginalCellValue: false);
						continue;
					}
					if (dataCell.HostControl != null && !dataCell.HostControl.IsPlaceHolder && dataCell.HostControl.GridColumn.DataColumn != item)
					{
						this[item] = dataCell2;
						DataTable.OnCellValueReloaded(dataCell, dataCell2, reuseOriginalCellValue: false);
						continue;
					}
					CopyCellData(dataCell2, dataCell, out var templateNeedsUpdate);
					InternalSetData(item, dataCell);
					DataTable.OnCellValueReloaded(dataCell, dataCell2, reuseOriginalCellValue: true);
					dataCell.InvalidateDisplayText();
					if (templateNeedsUpdate && dataCell.HostControl != null)
					{
						CellControlTemplates.UpdateTemplates(dataCell.HostControl);
					}
				}
			}
			RaiseIndexerPropertyChanged();
		}

		public void ClearData()
		{
			_cellDatas.Clear();
		}

		public void ClearData(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (column == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("column");
			}
			if (_cellDatas.ContainsKey(column))
			{
				_cellDatas.Remove(column);
			}
		}

		private static void CopyCellData(IDataCell source, IDataCell destination, out bool templateNeedsUpdate)
		{
			templateNeedsUpdate = false;
			if (source != null && destination != null && !(source.ValueType != destination.ValueType))
			{
				destination.Value = source.Value;
				destination.Mask = source.Mask;
				destination.UIType = source.UIType;
				destination.IsReadOnly = source.IsReadOnly;
				destination.CurrentUnits = source.CurrentUnits;
				destination.UnitsSource = source.UnitsSource;
				if (source.IsItemsSourceLoaded)
				{
					destination.ItemsSource = source.ItemsSource;
				}
				destination.IsComboEditable = source.IsComboEditable;
				destination.Tag = source.Tag;
				destination.Background = source.Background;
				destination.Foreground = source.Foreground;
				destination.FontStyle = source.FontStyle;
				destination.FontWeight = source.FontWeight;
				destination.Tooltip = source.Tooltip;
				if ((destination.TopRightWarningTooltip == null && source.TopRightWarningTooltip != null) || (destination.TopRightWarningTooltip != null && source.TopRightWarningTooltip == null))
				{
					destination.TopRightWarningTooltip = source.TopRightWarningTooltip;
					templateNeedsUpdate = true;
				}
				if ((destination.BottomRightWarningTooltip == null && source.BottomRightWarningTooltip != null) || (destination.BottomRightWarningTooltip != null && source.BottomRightWarningTooltip == null))
				{
					destination.BottomRightWarningTooltip = source.BottomRightWarningTooltip;
					templateNeedsUpdate = true;
				}
				if ((destination.PrefixImage == null && source.PrefixImage != null) || (destination.PrefixImage != null && source.PrefixImage == null))
				{
					destination.PrefixImage = source.PrefixImage;
					templateNeedsUpdate = true;
				}
				destination.ContentHorizontalAlignment = source.ContentHorizontalAlignment;
				destination.Margin = source.Margin;
				destination.NoValueDisplayText = source.NoValueDisplayText;
				destination.Template = source.Template;
				destination.EditTemplate = source.EditTemplate;
				destination.ShouldEnterEditModeWhenFocused = source.ShouldEnterEditModeWhenFocused;
				destination.AllowDrag = source.AllowDrag;
				destination.Tag = source.Tag;
			}
		}

		public static IDataCell CreateDataCell(object value, bool isReadOnly = false, IEnumerable itemsSource = null, CellUIType editorType = CellUIType.Unknown, object tag = null, Brush background = null, Type valueType = null, string topRightWarningTooltip = null, string bottomRightWarningTooltip = null, HorizontalAlignment? contentHorizontalAlignment = null, string mask = null, Thickness? margin = null, Brush foreground = null, bool showValueAsTooltipForDoubleTypes = false)
		{
			IDataCell dataCell = CreateCellDataInternal(value, valueType, itemsSource, showValueAsTooltipForDoubleTypes);
			if (dataCell is DataCellText)
			{
				dataCell.ItemsSource = ConvertEnumerable<string>(itemsSource);
			}
			else if (dataCell is DataCellInteger)
			{
				dataCell.ItemsSource = ConvertEnumerable<int>(itemsSource);
			}
			else if (dataCell is DataCellDouble)
			{
				dataCell.ItemsSource = ConvertEnumerable<double>(itemsSource);
			}
			dataCell.IsReadOnly = isReadOnly;
			dataCell.UIType = editorType;
			dataCell.Tag = tag;
			dataCell.Background = background;
			dataCell.Foreground = foreground;
			dataCell.TopRightWarningTooltip = topRightWarningTooltip;
			dataCell.BottomRightWarningTooltip = bottomRightWarningTooltip;
			if (contentHorizontalAlignment.HasValue)
			{
				dataCell.ContentHorizontalAlignment = contentHorizontalAlignment.Value;
			}
			dataCell.Mask = mask;
			if (margin.HasValue)
			{
				dataCell.Margin = margin.Value;
			}
			return dataCell;
		}

		private static IDataCell CreateCellDataInternal(object value, Type valueType, IEnumerable itemsSource, bool showValueAsToolTipForDoubleTypes = false)
		{
			if (value == null || value == DBNull.Value)
			{
				if (valueType == BooleanType || valueType == NullableBooleanType)
				{
					return new DataCellBoolean();
				}
				if (valueType == NullableIntType || valueType == Int32Type || valueType == Int16Type || valueType == Int64Type)
				{
					return new DataCellInteger();
				}
				if (valueType == NullableDoubleType || valueType == DoubleType || valueType == FloatType || valueType == DecimalType)
				{
					return new DataCellDouble();
				}
				return new DataCellText();
			}
			Type type = value.GetType();
			if (type == BooleanType)
			{
				return new DataCellBoolean((bool?)value);
			}
			if (type == Int32Type || type == Int16Type || type == Int64Type)
			{
				if (type == Int32Type)
				{
					return new DataCellInteger((int?)value);
				}
				if (type == Int16Type)
				{
					return new DataCellInteger((short)value);
				}
				return new DataCellInteger((int)(long)value);
			}
			if (type == DoubleType || valueType == FloatType || valueType == DecimalType)
			{
				if (type == DoubleType)
				{
					return new DataCellDouble((double)value, showValueAsToolTipForDoubleTypes);
				}
				if (type == FloatType)
				{
					return new DataCellDouble((float)value, showValueAsToolTipForDoubleTypes);
				}
				return new DataCellDouble((double)(decimal)value, showValueAsToolTipForDoubleTypes);
			}
			Type type2 = valueType;
			string text = value.ToString().Trim();
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
			{
				if (type2 == NullableBooleanType || type2 == BooleanType)
				{
					return new DataCellBoolean();
				}
				if (type2 == NullableIntType || type2 == Int32Type || type2 == Int16Type || type2 == Int64Type)
				{
					return new DataCellInteger();
				}
				if (type2 == NullableDoubleType || type2 == DoubleType || valueType == FloatType || valueType == DecimalType)
				{
					return new DataCellDouble();
				}
				return new DataCellText();
			}
			if (itemsSource != null)
			{
				foreach (object item in itemsSource)
				{
					if (item != null)
					{
						type2 = item.GetType();
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (type2 == NullableBooleanType || type2 == BooleanType)
			{
				if (bool.TryParse(text, out var result))
				{
					return new DataCellBoolean(result);
				}
				return new DataCellText(text);
			}
			if (type2 == NullableIntType || type2 == Int32Type || type2 == Int16Type || type2 == Int64Type)
			{
				int result2 = 0;
				if (int.TryParse(text, out result2))
				{
					return new DataCellInteger(result2);
				}
				return new DataCellText(text);
			}
			if (type2 == NullableDoubleType || type2 == DoubleType || valueType == FloatType || valueType == DecimalType)
			{
				double result3 = 0.0;
				if (double.TryParse(text, out result3))
				{
					return new DataCellDouble(result3);
				}
				return new DataCellText(text);
			}
			return new DataCellText(text);
		}

		private static IEnumerable<T> ConvertEnumerable<T>(IEnumerable source)
		{
			return source?.OfType<T>();
		}

		private static void OnVisibilityPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataRow).OnVisibilityChanged((Visibility)e.OldValue, (Visibility)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnIsFilteredOutPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataRow).OnIsFilteredOutChanged((bool)e.OldValue, (bool)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnResolvedVisibilityPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataRow).OnResolvedVisibilityChanged((Visibility)e.OldValue, (Visibility)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnGroupingStatePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataRow).OnGroupingStateChanged((RowGroupingState)e.OldValue, (RowGroupingState)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnGroupingRowsCountPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataRow).OnGroupingRowsCountChanged((int)e.OldValue, (int)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnGroupingIndentPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataRow).OnGroupingIndentChanged((double)e.OldValue, (double)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnIsParentGroupCollapsedPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataRow).RefreshResolvedVisibility();
		}

		public void UpdateGroupingStatus()
		{
			if (GroupingRowsCount > 0)
			{
				UpdateGroupingRows();
				UpdateSubGroupingRowsControls(GroupingRowsCount);
				UpdateSubGroupingRowsIndents(GroupingRowsCount);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnVisibilityChanged(Visibility oldValue, Visibility newValue)
		{
			RefreshResolvedVisibility();
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsFilteredOutChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			RefreshResolvedVisibility();
		}

		private void RefreshResolvedVisibility()
		{
			if (Visibility == Visibility.Visible && !IsParentGroupCollapsed && !IsFilteredOut)
			{
				ResolvedVisibility = Visibility.Visible;
			}
			else
			{
				ResolvedVisibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnResolvedVisibilityChanged(Visibility oldValue, Visibility newValue)
		{
			if (DataTable != null && DataTable.GridControl != null)
			{
				DataTable.GridControl.OnDataSourceRowResolvedVisibilityChanged();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnGroupingStateChanged(RowGroupingState oldValue, RowGroupingState newValue)
		{
			UpdateGroupingRows();
		}

		protected virtual void OnGroupingRowsCountChanged(int oldValue, int newValue)
		{
			if (newValue > 0 && GroupingState == RowGroupingState.None)
			{
				GroupingState = RowGroupingState.Expanded;
			}
			else if (newValue <= 0 && GroupingState != RowGroupingState.None)
			{
				GroupingState = RowGroupingState.None;
			}
			int affectedRows = Math.Max(oldValue, newValue);
			UpdateSubGroupingRowsIndents(affectedRows);
			UpdateSubGroupingRowsControls(affectedRows);
			if (DataTable != null)
			{
				DataTable.ResetHasGroupingRows();
			}
		}

		private void OnGroupingIndentChanged(double oldValue, double newValue)
		{
			UpdateSubGroupingRowsIndents(GroupingRowsCount);
		}

		private void UpdateGroupingRows()
		{
			if (GroupingRowsCount <= 0 || DataTable == null || !DataTable.Rows.Contains(this))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (GroupingState == RowGroupingState.Collapsed)
			{
				int num = DataTable.Rows.IndexOf(this);
				for (int i = 0; i < GroupingRowsCount; i++)
				{
					int num2 = num + i + 1;
					if (num2 < DataTable.Rows.Count)
					{
						DataRow dataRow = DataTable.Rows[num2];
						if (dataRow.GroupingRowsCount > 0)
						{
							dataRow.GroupingState = RowGroupingState.Collapsed;
						}
						dataRow.IsParentGroupCollapsed = true;
						continue;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (GroupingState != RowGroupingState.Expanded)
				{
					return;
				}
				int num3 = DataTable.Rows.IndexOf(this);
				for (int j = 0; j < GroupingRowsCount; j++)
				{
					int num4 = num3 + j + 1;
					if (num4 < DataTable.Rows.Count)
					{
						DataRow dataRow2 = DataTable.Rows[num4];
						dataRow2.IsParentGroupCollapsed = false;
						if (dataRow2.GroupingRowsCount > 0)
						{
							dataRow2.UpdateGroupingRows();
							j += dataRow2.GroupingRowsCount;
						}
						continue;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateSubGroupingRowsControls(int affectedRows)
		{
			if (DataTable == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			DataColumn dataColumn = DataTable.Columns.FirstOrDefault();
			if (dataColumn == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = DataTable.Rows.IndexOf(this);
			if (num < 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i <= affectedRows; i++)
			{
				int num2 = num + i;
				if (num2 >= DataTable.Rows.Count)
				{
					break;
				}
				DataRow dataRow = DataTable.Rows[num2];
				if (dataRow.IsDataLoaded(dataColumn))
				{
					GridControlCell hostControl = dataRow[dataColumn].HostControl;
					if (hostControl != null)
					{
						CellControlTemplates.UpdateTemplates(hostControl);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateSubGroupingRowsIndents(int affectedRows)
		{
			if (DataTable == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = DataTable.Rows.IndexOf(this);
			if (num < 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num2 = 0;
			while (num2 < affectedRows)
			{
				int num3 = num + num2 + 1;
				if (num3 < DataTable.Rows.Count)
				{
					DataRow dataRow = DataTable.Rows[num3];
					if (num2 < GroupingRowsCount)
					{
						dataRow.GroupingIndent = GroupingIndent + 20.0;
					}
					else
					{
						dataRow.GroupingIndent = GroupingIndent;
					}
					num2 += dataRow.GroupingRowsCount;
					num2++;
					continue;
				}
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	public enum RowGroupingState
	{
		None,
		Collapsed,
		Expanded
	}
	public class DataTable : NotifiableObjectBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty TagProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Tag", typeof(object), typeof(DataTable));

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey ColumnsPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("Columns", typeof(ReadOnlyCollection<DataColumn>), typeof(DataTable), new PropertyMetadata());

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ColumnsProperty = ColumnsPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey RowsPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("Rows", typeof(ObservableCollection<DataRow>), typeof(DataTable), new PropertyMetadata());

		public static readonly DependencyProperty RowsProperty = RowsPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		private IEnumerable<DataRow> _fakeRowsForHorizontalScrollBar;

		private bool? _hasGroupingRows;

		private bool _suppressRowsChangedNotification;

		private bool _invalidateRowsGroupingStatusFlag;

		private int _cellValueChangedReportLock;

		public object Tag
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(TagProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(TagProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public ReadOnlyCollection<DataColumn> Columns
		{
			get
			{
				return (ReadOnlyCollection<DataColumn>)GetValue(ColumnsProperty);
			}
			protected set
			{
				SetValue(ColumnsPropertyKey, value);
			}
		}

		public ObservableCollection<DataRow> Rows
		{
			get
			{
				return (ObservableCollection<DataRow>)GetValue(RowsProperty);
			}
			private set
			{
				SetValue(RowsPropertyKey, value);
			}
		}

		internal IEnumerable<DataRow> FakeRowsForHorizontalScrollBar
		{
			get
			{
				if (_fakeRowsForHorizontalScrollBar == null)
				{
					DataRow dataRow = CreateRow();
					dataRow.IsFakeRow = true;
					_fakeRowsForHorizontalScrollBar = new List<DataRow> { dataRow };
				}
				return _fakeRowsForHorizontalScrollBar;
			}
		}

		internal bool HasGroupingRows
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_hasGroupingRows.HasValue)
				{
					_hasGroupingRows = Rows.Any((DataRow x) => x.GroupingRowsCount > 0);
				}
				return _hasGroupingRows == true;
			}
		}

		public GridControl GridControl { get; internal set; }

		public Func<DataCellInfo, string, DataColumnFilterOperator, bool> FilterFunction { get; set; }

		internal bool IsCellValueChangedReportEnabled => _cellValueChangedReportLock <= 0;

		internal void ResetHasGroupingRows()
		{
			_hasGroupingRows = null;
		}

		public DataTable(IEnumerable<DataColumn> columns = null)
		{
			TimingDebugger.BeingTimingBlock("DataTable.Construction");
			if (columns != null)
			{
				Columns = new ReadOnlyCollection<DataColumn>(new List<DataColumn>(columns));
			}
			else
			{
				Columns = new ReadOnlyCollection<DataColumn>(new List<DataColumn>());
			}
			Rows = new ObservableCollection<DataRow>();
			Rows.CollectionChanged += Rows_CollectionChanged;
			TimingDebugger.EndTimingBlock();
		}

		private void Rows_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add && e.NewItems != null)
			{
				foreach (DataRow newItem in e.NewItems)
				{
					newItem.InternalSetDataTable(this);
				}
			}
			if (!_suppressRowsChangedNotification)
			{
				NotifyRowsChanged();
			}
		}

		private void NotifyRowsChanged()
		{
			InvalidateRowsGroupingStatus();
			GridControl?.OnDataSourceRowsChanged();
		}

		private void InvalidateRowsGroupingStatus()
		{
			if (!_invalidateRowsGroupingStatusFlag)
			{
				_invalidateRowsGroupingStatusFlag = true;
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					UpdateRowsGroupingStatus();
					_invalidateRowsGroupingStatusFlag = false;
				}, DispatcherPriority.DataBind);
			}
		}

		public void UpdateRowsGroupingStatus()
		{
			int num;
			for (num = 0; num < Rows.Count; num++)
			{
				DataRow dataRow = Rows[num];
				dataRow.UpdateGroupingStatus();
				num += dataRow.GroupingRowsCount;
			}
		}

		public void UpdateRows(IEnumerable<DataRow> rows)
		{
			if (rows == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("rows");
			}
			TimingDebugger.BeingTimingBlock("DataTable.UpdateRows");
			try
			{
				_suppressRowsChangedNotification = true;
				Rows.Clear();
				foreach (DataRow row in rows)
				{
					Rows.Add(row);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				_suppressRowsChangedNotification = false;
				NotifyRowsChanged();
			}
			TimingDebugger.EndTimingBlock();
		}

		public void UpdateRows(int updatedRowsCount, Func<int, DataRow> generator)
		{
			if (generator == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("rows");
			}
			try
			{
				_suppressRowsChangedNotification = true;
				int count = Rows.Count;
				int num = updatedRowsCount - count;
				if (num < 0)
				{
					while (Rows.Count > updatedRowsCount)
					{
						Rows.RemoveAt(Rows.Count - 1);
					}
				}
				else if (num > 0)
				{
					for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
					{
						DataRow dataRow = generator(count + i);
						if (dataRow != null)
						{
							Rows.Add(dataRow);
						}
					}
				}
				if (count > 0)
				{
					RefreshData();
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				_suppressRowsChangedNotification = false;
				NotifyRowsChanged();
			}
		}

		public void UpdateDataRange(int count)
		{
			List<DataRow> list = new List<DataRow>();
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				list.Add(CreateRow());
			}
			UpdateRows(list);
		}

		public virtual void RefreshData()
		{
			try
			{
				SuspendCellValueChangedReport();
				DataCellInfo currentCell = default(DataCellInfo);
				if (GridControl != null && Rows.Count > 0)
				{
					currentCell = GridControl.CurrentCell;
				}
				if (GridControl != null)
				{
					List<DataRow> list = GridControl.ViewportRows.ToList();
					foreach (DataRow row in Rows)
					{
						if (row.HasData)
						{
							if (list.Contains(row))
							{
								row.RefreshLoadedData();
								continue;
							}
							row.ClearData();
							row.RaiseIndexerPropertyChanged();
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					foreach (DataRow row2 in Rows)
					{
						if (row2.HasData)
						{
							row2.ClearData();
						}
					}
				}
				if (GridControl != null)
				{
					if (!currentCell.IsEmpty)
					{
						GridControl.CurrentCell = currentCell;
					}
					if (GridControl.InternalFilterHelper.HasFiltering || GridControl.InternalSortHelper.SortingInfo.SortedItems.Count > 0)
					{
						GridControl.InvalidateSorting();
					}
					GridControl.InternalFilterHelper.ResetFilterOperands();
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				ResumeCellValueChangedReport();
			}
		}

		public virtual DataRow CreateRow()
		{
			return new DataRow(this);
		}

		public void EnsureAllRowsData()
		{
			TimingDebugger.BeingTimingBlock("DataTable.EnsureAllRowsData");
			foreach (DataRow row in Rows)
			{
				if (!row.HasData)
				{
					OnRowValuesQueried(row);
				}
			}
			TimingDebugger.EndTimingBlock();
		}

		public virtual void Reset()
		{
			Rows.Clear();
			Columns = new ReadOnlyCollection<DataColumn>(new List<DataColumn>());
			if (GridControl != null)
			{
				GridControl.RefreshDataInternal(clearRowsData: true);
			}
		}

		public void SuspendCellValueChangedReport()
		{
			_cellValueChangedReportLock++;
		}

		public void ResumeCellValueChangedReport()
		{
			_cellValueChangedReportLock--;
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnBoundToGridControl()
		{
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnColumnHeaderChanged(DataColumn column, string oldHeader, string newHeader)
		{
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnSortingInfoChanged()
		{
			if (GridControl != null)
			{
				GridControl.InvalidateSorting();
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnFilteringChanged()
		{
			if (GridControl != null)
			{
				GridControl.InternalFilterHelper.DoDefaultFiltering();
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual object GetDirectCellValue(DataRow row, DataColumn column)
		{
			return row[column]?.Value;
		}

		protected internal virtual string FixCellDisplayText(IDataCell cell, string text)
		{
			return text;
		}

		protected internal virtual IList<string> OnFilterOperandsQueried(DataColumn column)
		{
			return null;
		}

		public virtual bool CanCellPassFilter(DataCellInfo cell, string filterOperand, DataColumnFilterOperator filterOperator)
		{
			if (FilterFunction != null)
			{
				return FilterFunction(cell, filterOperand, filterOperator);
			}
			if (GridControl != null)
			{
				return GridControl.InternalFilterHelper.CanCellPassFilter(cell, filterOperand, filterOperator);
			}
			IDataCell data = cell.Data;
			if (data != null)
			{
				return FilterHelper.CanPassFilter(data.Value, data.ValueAsString, filterOperand, filterOperator, data.Column.ResolvedValueComparer);
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnRowValuesQueried(DataRow row)
		{
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnColumnsVisibilityChanged()
		{
			if (GridControl != null)
			{
				GridControl.OnDataSourceColumnsVisibilityChanged();
			}
		}

		internal void CallPreparingCellValues(PreparingCellValuesEventArgs e)
		{
			OnPreparingCellValues(e);
			if (GridControl != null)
			{
				GridControl.OnPreparingCellValues(e);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnPreparingCellValues(PreparingCellValuesEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		internal IDataCell CallCellValueQueried(DataRow row, DataColumn column)
		{
			IDataCell dataCell = OnCellValueQueried(row, column);
			if (GridControl == null)
			{
				return dataCell;
			}
			CellValueQueriedEventArgs e = new CellValueQueriedEventArgs(row, column)
			{
				Data = dataCell
			};
			GridControl.OnCellValueQueried(e);
			return e.Data;
		}

		protected virtual IDataCell OnCellValueQueried(DataRow row, DataColumn column)
		{
			return null;
		}

		internal void CallCellValueChanged(IDataCell cell, object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			OnCellValueChanged(cell, oldValue, newValue);
			if (GridControl != null)
			{
				GridControl.OnCellValueChanged(new CellValueChangedEventArgs(cell, oldValue, newValue));
			}
			if (cell.Column != null)
			{
				cell.Column.Filter.ResetAvailableOperands();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnCellValueChanged(IDataCell cell, object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnCellValueReloaded(IDataCell targetCellValue, IDataCell feedCellValue, bool reuseOriginalCellValue)
		{
			if (targetCellValue.IsLoaded && GridControl != null)
			{
				GridControl.OnCellValueLoaded(new CellValueLoadedEventArgs(targetCellValue));
			}
		}

		internal void CallCellUnitsChanged(IDataCell cell, string oldUnits, string newUnits)
		{
			OnCellUnitsChanged(cell, oldUnits, newUnits);
			if (GridControl != null)
			{
				GridControl.OnCellUnitsChanged(new CellUnitsChangedEventArgs(cell, oldUnits, newUnits));
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnCellUnitsChanged(IDataCell cell, string oldUnits, string newUnits)
		{
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnCellItemsSourceQueried(IDataCell cell)
		{
			if (cell != null && !cell.IsItemsSourceLoaded && cell.Column != null && cell.Column.CellsItemsSource != null)
			{
				cell.ItemsSource = cell.Column.CellsItemsSource;
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnCellValueCleared(IDataCell cell)
		{
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnCellDropDownOpening(IDataCell cell)
		{
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnCellTooltipChecking(IDataCell cell)
		{
		}

		protected internal virtual void CommitEditingValue(IDataCell cell, FrameworkElement editingControl)
		{
			if (editingControl is NumberInputBox)
			{
				NumberInputBox numberInputBox = editingControl as NumberInputBox;
				numberInputBox.UpdateTextProperty(silently: true);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(numberInputBox.Text))
				{
					cell.ClearValue();
				}
				else
				{
					TryUpdateBindingSource(numberInputBox, NumberInputBox.TextProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (editingControl is TextBox)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(((TextBox)editingControl).Text))
				{
					cell.ClearValue();
				}
				else
				{
					TryUpdateBindingSource(editingControl, TextBox.TextProperty);
				}
			}
			else if (editingControl is ValueEditor)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(((ValueEditor)editingControl).Text))
				{
					cell.ClearValue();
				}
				else
				{
					TryUpdateBindingSource(editingControl, ValueEditor.ValueProperty);
				}
			}
		}

		private static void TryUpdateBindingSource(FrameworkElement targetElement, DependencyProperty targetProperty)
		{
			BindingExpression bindingExpression = targetElement.GetBindingExpression(targetProperty);
			if (bindingExpression != null && bindingExpression.Status == BindingStatus.Active)
			{
				bindingExpression.UpdateSource();
			}
		}
	}
	[ContentProperty("GridControls")]
	public class GridControlPanel : Control
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ActiveGridControlProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty HeaderContextMenuProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ZoomFactorProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty PostTabItemTextProperty;

		public static readonly RoutedUICommand AddNewGridCommand;

		public static readonly RoutedUICommand CloneCurrentGridCommand;

		public static readonly RoutedUICommand RenameCurrentGridCommand;

		public static readonly RoutedUICommand RemoveCurrentGridCommand;

		public static readonly RoutedEvent ActiveGridControlChangedEvent;

		public static readonly RoutedEvent GridControlAddedManuallyEvent;

		public static readonly RoutedEvent GridControlClonedEvent;

		public static readonly RoutedEvent GridControlRenamedEvent;

		public static readonly RoutedEvent GridControlRemovingManuallyEvent;

		public static readonly RoutedEvent GridControlRemovedManuallyEvent;

		public static readonly RoutedEvent CommandExecutingEvent;

		private INameProvider _defaultNameProvider;

		private readonly TabControlHelper _tabHelper;

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public ObservableCollection<GridControl> GridControls { get; private set; }

		public GridControl ActiveGridControl
		{
			get
			{
				return (GridControl)GetValue(ActiveGridControlProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ActiveGridControlProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public ContextMenu HeaderContextMenu
		{
			get
			{
				return (ContextMenu)GetValue(HeaderContextMenuProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(HeaderContextMenuProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public double ZoomFactor
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(ZoomFactorProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ZoomFactorProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public string PostTabItemText
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(PostTabItemTextProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(PostTabItemTextProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public INameProvider NameProvider { get; set; }

		public INameProvider ResolvedNameProvider
		{
			get
			{
				if (NameProvider == null)
				{
					if (_defaultNameProvider == null)
					{
						_defaultNameProvider = new DefaultNameProvider(this);
					}
					return _defaultNameProvider;
				}
				return NameProvider;
			}
		}

		public Func<GridControl> ChildGridControlGenerator { get; set; }

		public event RoutedPropertyChangedEventHandler<GridControl> ActiveGridControlChanged
		{
			add
			{
				AddHandler(ActiveGridControlChangedEvent, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				RemoveHandler(ActiveGridControlChangedEvent, value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<GridControlEventArgs> GridControlAddedManually
		{
			add
			{
				AddHandler(GridControlAddedManuallyEvent, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				RemoveHandler(GridControlAddedManuallyEvent, value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<GridControlClonedEventArgs> GridControlCloned
		{
			add
			{
				AddHandler(GridControlClonedEvent, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				RemoveHandler(GridControlClonedEvent, value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<GridControlRenamedEventArgs> GridControlRenamed
		{
			add
			{
				AddHandler(GridControlRenamedEvent, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				RemoveHandler(GridControlRenamedEvent, value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<GridControlRemovingEventArgs> GridControlRemovingManually
		{
			add
			{
				AddHandler(GridControlRemovingManuallyEvent, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				RemoveHandler(GridControlRemovingManuallyEvent, value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<GridControlEventArgs> GridControlRemovedManually
		{
			add
			{
				AddHandler(GridControlRemovedManuallyEvent, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				RemoveHandler(GridControlRemovedManuallyEvent, value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<CommandExecutingEventArgs> CommandExecuting
		{
			add
			{
				AddHandler(CommandExecutingEvent, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				RemoveHandler(CommandExecutingEvent, value);
			}
		}

		static GridControlPanel()
		{
			ActiveGridControlProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ActiveGridControl", typeof(GridControl), typeof(GridControlPanel), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnActiveGridControlPropertyChanged));
			HeaderContextMenuProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("HeaderContextMenu", typeof(ContextMenu), typeof(GridControlPanel));
			ZoomFactorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ZoomFactor", typeof(double), typeof(GridControlPanel), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(1.0));
			PostTabItemTextProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("PostTabItemText", typeof(string), typeof(GridControlPanel));
			AddNewGridCommand = new RoutedUICommand("Add", "AddNewGrid", typeof(GridControlPanel));
			CloneCurrentGridCommand = new RoutedUICommand("Clone", "CloneCurrentGrid", typeof(GridControlPanel));
			RenameCurrentGridCommand = new RoutedUICommand("Rename", "RenameCurrentGrid", typeof(GridControlPanel));
			RemoveCurrentGridCommand = new RoutedUICommand("Remove", "RemoveCurrentGrid", typeof(GridControlPanel));
			ActiveGridControlChangedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("ActiveGridControlChanged", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(RoutedPropertyChangedEventHandler<GridControl>), typeof(GridControlPanel));
			GridControlAddedManuallyEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("GridControlAddedManually", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<GridControlEventArgs>), typeof(GridControlPanel));
			GridControlClonedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("GridControlCloned", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<GridControlClonedEventArgs>), typeof(GridControlPanel));
			GridControlRenamedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("GridControlRenamed", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<GridControlRenamedEventArgs>), typeof(GridControlPanel));
			GridControlRemovingManuallyEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("GridControlRemovingManually", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<GridControlRemovingEventArgs>), typeof(GridControlPanel));
			GridControlRemovedManuallyEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("GridControlRemovedManually", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<GridControlEventArgs>), typeof(GridControlPanel));
			CommandExecutingEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("CommandExecuting", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<CommandExecutingEventArgs>), typeof(GridControlPanel));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(GridControlPanel), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(GridControlPanel)));
			Control.IsTabStopProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(GridControlPanel), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(GridControlPanel), new CommandBinding(AddNewGridCommand, AddNewGridCommand_Executed, AddNewGridCommand_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(GridControlPanel), new CommandBinding(CloneCurrentGridCommand, CloneCurrentGridCommand_Executed, CloneCurrentGridCommand_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(GridControlPanel), new CommandBinding(RenameCurrentGridCommand, RenameCurrentGridCommand_Executed, RenameCurrentGridCommand_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(GridControlPanel), new CommandBinding(RemoveCurrentGridCommand, RemoveCurrentGridCommand_Executed, RemoveCurrentGridCommand_CanExecute));
		}

		public GridControlPanel()
		{
			_tabHelper = new TabControlHelper(this);
			GridControls = new ObservableCollection<GridControl>();
			GridControls.CollectionChanged += GridControls_CollectionChanged;
		}

		private void GridControls_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.NewItems == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (GridControl newItem in e.NewItems)
			{
				newItem.SetBinding(GridControl.ZoomFactorProperty, new Binding
				{
					Source = this,
					Path = new PropertyPath(ZoomFactorProperty),
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnActiveGridControlChanged(GridControl oldValue, GridControl newValue)
		{
			RaiseEvent(new RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<GridControl>(oldValue, newValue, ActiveGridControlChangedEvent)
			{
				Source = this
			});
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			_tabHelper.InitializeOnTemplateApplied();
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
		}

		protected virtual bool OnCommandExecuting(ICommand command)
		{
			if (command == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("command");
			}
			CommandExecutingEventArgs e = new CommandExecutingEventArgs(command, CommandExecutingEvent, this);
			RaiseEvent(e);
			return !e.Cancel;
		}

		protected virtual void OnGridControlAddedManually(GridControl GridControl)
		{
			if (GridControl == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("GridControl");
			}
			RaiseEvent(new GridControlEventArgs(GridControl, GridControlAddedManuallyEvent, this));
		}

		protected virtual void OnGridControlCloned(GridControl basedGridControl, GridControl clonedGridControl)
		{
			if (basedGridControl == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("basedGridControl");
			}
			if (clonedGridControl == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("clonedGridControl");
			}
			RaiseEvent(new GridControlClonedEventArgs(basedGridControl, clonedGridControl, GridControlClonedEvent, this));
		}

		protected virtual void OnGridControlRenamed(string oldTitle, string newTitle, GridControl GridControl)
		{
			if (oldTitle == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("oldTitle");
			}
			if (newTitle == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("newTitle");
			}
			if (GridControl == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("GridControl");
			}
			GridControlRenamedEventArgs e = new GridControlRenamedEventArgs(oldTitle, newTitle, GridControl, GridControlRenamedEvent, this);
			RaiseEvent(e);
			if (!e.Cancel)
			{
				GridControl.Title = e.NewTitle;
			}
		}

		protected virtual bool OnGridControlRemoving(GridControl GridControl)
		{
			if (GridControl == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("GridControl");
			}
			GridControlRemovingEventArgs e = new GridControlRemovingEventArgs(GridControl, GridControlRemovingManuallyEvent, this);
			RaiseEvent(e);
			return !e.Cancel;
		}

		protected virtual void OnGridControlRemovedManually(GridControl GridControl)
		{
			if (GridControl == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("GridControl");
			}
			RaiseEvent(new GridControlEventArgs(GridControl, GridControlRemovedManuallyEvent, this));
		}

		private GridControl CreateChildGridControl(string title)
		{
			if (ChildGridControlGenerator != null)
			{
				GridControl gridControl = ChildGridControlGenerator();
				gridControl.Title = title;
				return gridControl;
			}
			return new GridControl
			{
				Title = title
			};
		}

		private static void OnActiveGridControlPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as GridControlPanel).OnActiveGridControlChanged((GridControl)e.OldValue, (GridControl)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void AddNewGridCommand_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControlPanel gridControlPanel = sender as GridControlPanel;
			string text = gridControlPanel.ResolvedNameProvider.GenerateNewNameForAdding();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				GridControl gridControl = gridControlPanel.CreateChildGridControl(text);
				gridControlPanel.GridControls.Add(gridControl);
				gridControlPanel.ActiveGridControl = gridControl;
				gridControlPanel.OnGridControlAddedManually(gridControl);
			}
		}

		private static void AddNewGridCommand_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControlPanel gridControlPanel = sender as GridControlPanel;
			e.CanExecute = gridControlPanel.ActiveGridControl != null && gridControlPanel.OnCommandExecuting(AddNewGridCommand);
		}

		private static void CloneCurrentGridCommand_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControlPanel gridControlPanel = sender as GridControlPanel;
			GridControl activeGridControl = gridControlPanel.ActiveGridControl;
			string text = gridControlPanel.ResolvedNameProvider.GenereteNewNameForCloning(activeGridControl.Title);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				GridControl gridControl = gridControlPanel.CreateChildGridControl(text);
				gridControlPanel.GridControls.Add(gridControl);
				gridControlPanel.ActiveGridControl = gridControl;
				gridControlPanel.OnGridControlCloned(activeGridControl, gridControl);
			}
		}

		private static void CloneCurrentGridCommand_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControlPanel gridControlPanel = sender as GridControlPanel;
			e.CanExecute = gridControlPanel.ActiveGridControl != null && gridControlPanel.OnCommandExecuting(CloneCurrentGridCommand);
		}

		private static void RenameCurrentGridCommand_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControlPanel gridControlPanel = sender as GridControlPanel;
			GridControl activeGridControl = gridControlPanel.ActiveGridControl;
			string title = activeGridControl.Title;
			string text = gridControlPanel.ResolvedNameProvider.GenerateNewNameForRenaming(activeGridControl.Title);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				activeGridControl.Title = text;
				gridControlPanel.OnGridControlRenamed(title, text, activeGridControl);
			}
		}

		private static void RenameCurrentGridCommand_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControlPanel gridControlPanel = sender as GridControlPanel;
			e.CanExecute = gridControlPanel.ActiveGridControl != null && gridControlPanel.OnCommandExecuting(RenameCurrentGridCommand);
		}

		private static void RemoveCurrentGridCommand_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControlPanel gridControlPanel = sender as GridControlPanel;
			GridControl activeGridControl = gridControlPanel.ActiveGridControl;
			if (gridControlPanel.OnGridControlRemoving(activeGridControl))
			{
				gridControlPanel.GridControls.Remove(activeGridControl);
				gridControlPanel.OnGridControlRemovedManually(activeGridControl);
			}
		}

		private static void RemoveCurrentGridCommand_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControlPanel gridControlPanel = sender as GridControlPanel;
			e.CanExecute = gridControlPanel.ActiveGridControl != null && gridControlPanel.OnCommandExecuting(RemoveCurrentGridCommand);
		}
	}

		bool IsComboEditable { get; set; }

		Brush Foreground { get; set; }

		string BottomRightWarningTooltip { get; set; }

		bool IsItemsSourceLoaded { get; }

		bool IsLoaded { get; }

		bool IsCurrent { get; }

		bool HasValue { get; }

		string NoValueDisplayText { get; set; }

		DataTemplate Template { get; set; }

		DataTemplate EditTemplate { get; set; }

		IValueConverter NumberToTextConverter { get; set; }

		bool ShouldEnterEditModeWhenFocused { get; set; }

		bool AllowDrag { get; set; }

		bool CanParse(string value);

		void Parse(string value);

		void EnsureItemsSource();

		void ClearValue();

		void SetBinding(string propertyName, BindingBase binding);

		BindingBase GetBindingBase(string propertyName);

		BindingExpression GetBindingExpression(string propertyName);

		void InvalidateDisplayText();
	}

		public Size DataGridControlSize
		{
			get
			{
				Size result = default(Size);
				double num = 0.0;
				double num2 = 0.0;
				foreach (DataGridColumn column in DataGrid.Columns)
				{
					if (column.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
					{
						num += column.ActualWidth;
					}
				}
				num += DataGrid.RowHeaderWidth + 12.0;
				num2 = (double)DataGrid.Items.Count * DataGrid.RowHeight + 60.0;
				result.Width = num;
				result.Height = num2;
				return result;
			}
		}

		public GridControlDataGrid DataGridControlInstance => DataGrid;

		public IEnumerable<DataRow> BoundRows
		{
			get
			{
				if (DataSource != null)
				{
					if (DataGrid != null)
					{
						FlushUpdateForDataGrid();
						return DataGrid.ItemsSource;
					}
					return DataSource.Rows.Where((DataRow x) => x.ResolvedVisibility == Visibility.Visible);
				}
				return new DataRow[0];
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<DataRow> ViewportRows
		{
			get
			{
				if (DataGrid != null && InternalScrollHelper.ScrollViewer != null)
				{
					IEnumerable<DataRow> boundRows = BoundRows;
					if (boundRows != null)
					{
						int num = (int)InternalScrollHelper.ScrollViewer.VerticalOffset;
						int num2 = Math.Min((int)InternalScrollHelper.ScrollViewer.ViewportHeight, boundRows.Count() - num);
						if (num2 > 0)
						{
							return boundRows.Skip(num).Take(num2);
						}
					}
				}
				return new DataRow[0];
			}
		}
