		public void AddFromDummy()
		{
			if (GridControlPanel.AddNewGridCommand.CanExecute(null, this))
			{
				GridControlPanel.AddNewGridCommand.Execute(null, this);
			}
		}

		static GridControl()
		{
			TitleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Title", typeof(string), typeof(GridControl));
			DataSourceProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DataSource", typeof(DataTable), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnDataSourcePropertyChanged));
			CoverTextProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("CoverText", typeof(string), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnCoverTextPropertyChanged));
			CurrentCellProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("CurrentCell", typeof(DataCellInfo), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnCurrentCellPropertyChanged));
			CurrentDataRowProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("CurrentDataRow", typeof(DataRow), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnCurrentDataRowPropertyChanged));
			SelectionUnitProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SelectionUnit", typeof(DataGridSelectionUnit), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(DataGridSelectionUnit.CellOrRowHeader));
			SelectionModeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SelectionMode", typeof(DataGridSelectionMode), typeof(GridControl), new UIPropertyMetadata(DataGridSelectionMode.Extended));
			DefaultColumnWidthProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DefaultColumnWidth", typeof(double), typeof(GridControl), new PropertyMetadata(120.0));
			MinRowHeightProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MinRowHeight", typeof(double), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(25.0));
			AlternatingRowBackgroundProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AlternatingRowBackground", typeof(Brush), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(new BrushConverter().ConvertFromString("#F4F9FD") as Brush));
			RowBackgroundProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("RowBackground", typeof(Brush), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(new SolidColorBrush(Colors.White)));
			SortingModeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SortingMode", typeof(SortingMode), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(SortingMode.AllowMultipleColumnsSorting));
			AllowRecordFilteringProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowRecordFiltering", typeof(bool), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true, OnAllowRecordFilteringPropertyChanged));
			AllowColumnChooserProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowColumnChooser", typeof(bool), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			AllowColumnReorderProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowColumnReorder", typeof(bool), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			AllowRowReorderProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowRowReorder", typeof(bool), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			AllowSelectAllProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowSelectAll", typeof(bool), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			AllowSelectColumnProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowSelectColumn", typeof(bool), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			AllowSelectRowProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowSelectRow", typeof(bool), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			AllowColumnFreezeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowColumnFreeze", typeof(bool), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			ClipboardCopyModeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ClipboardCopyMode", typeof(GridControlClipboardCopyMode), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(GridControlClipboardCopyMode.IncludeHeaderOnlyWhenColumnSelected));
			ReservedColumnChooserCommandProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ReservedColumnChooserCommand", typeof(Tuple<string, ICommand>), typeof(GridControl));
			FilterHeaderVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("FilterHeaderVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(Visibility.Collapsed, OnFilterHeaderVisibilityPropertyChanged));
			IsHorizontalScrollDeferredProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsHorizontalScrollDeferred", typeof(bool), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			IsVerticalScrollDeferredProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsVerticalScrollDeferred", typeof(bool), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			ZoomFactorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ZoomFactor", typeof(double), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(1.0, OnZoomFactorPropertyChanged, OnZoomFactorPropertyCoerced));
			MinimumZoomFactorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MinimumZoomFactor", typeof(double), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0.1));
			MaximumZoomFactorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MaximumZoomFactor", typeof(double), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(10.0));
			SupressSelectionChangedEventProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SupressSelectionChangedEvent", typeof(bool), typeof(GridControl));
			HorizontalScrollBarVisibilityProperty = ScrollViewer.HorizontalScrollBarVisibilityProperty.AddOwner(typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(ScrollBarVisibility.Auto));
			VerticalScrollBarVisibilityProperty = ScrollViewer.VerticalScrollBarVisibilityProperty.AddOwner(typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(ScrollBarVisibility.Auto));
			ShowLastRowAsNewlyAddedProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ShowLastRowAsNewlyAdded", typeof(bool), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, OnShowLastRowAsNewlyAddedPropertyChanged));
			SkipReadonlyCellsWhenNavigationProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SkipReadonlyCellsWhenNavigation", typeof(bool), typeof(GridControl));
			HeadersVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("HeadersVisibility", typeof(DataGridHeadersVisibility), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(DataGridHeadersVisibility.All));
			IsMultilineAllowedInCellProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsMultilineAllowedInCell", typeof(bool), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, OnIsMultilineAllowedInCellPropertyChanged));
			VerticalScrollUpdateLayoutProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("VerticalScrollUpdateLayout", typeof(bool), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			IgnoreCellNativeClipboardActionsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IgnoreCellNativeClipboardActions", typeof(bool), typeof(GridControl));
			ColumnHeaderHeightProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ColumnHeaderHeight", typeof(double), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(double.NaN, OnColumnHeaderHeightPropertyChanged));
			FullDisplayDesiredHeightPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("FullDisplayDesiredHeight", typeof(double), typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata());
			FullDisplayDesiredHeightProperty = FullDisplayDesiredHeightPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			FirstTimeGridLoadedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("FirstTimeGridLoaded", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(RoutedEventHandler), typeof(GridControl));
			DefaultContextMenuOpeningEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("DefaultContextMenuOpening", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<DefaultContextMenuEventArgs>), typeof(GridControl));
			CommandExecutingEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("CommandExecuting", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<CommandExecutingEventArgs>), typeof(GridControl));
			SelectionChangedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("SelectionChanged", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(RoutedEventHandler), typeof(GridControl));
			CellControlEditModeChangedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("CellControlEditModeChanged", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<GridControlCellEventArgs>), typeof(GridControl));
			DataCopyingEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("DataCopying", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<GridControlCopyEventArgs>), typeof(GridControl));
			DataPastingEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("DataPasting", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<GridControlPastingEventArgs>), typeof(GridControl));
			DataDraggingEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("DataDragging", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<GridControlDataDraggingEventArgs>), typeof(GridControl));
			DataDroppingEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("DataDropping", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<GridControlDataDroppingEventArgs>), typeof(GridControl));
			DataSortingEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("DataSorting", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<GridControlDataSortingEventArgs>), typeof(GridControl));
			ScrollChangedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("ScrollChanged", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<GridControlScrollChangedEventArgs>), typeof(GridControl));
			FilterOperatorsQueriedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("FilterOperatorsQueried", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<FilterOperatorsQueriedEventArgs>), typeof(GridControl));
			CanPasteCheckingEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("CanPasteChecking", RoutingStrategy.Direct, typeof(EventHandler<GridControlCanPasteEventArgs>), typeof(GridControl));
			CellPopupOpeningEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("CellPopupOpening", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<GridControlCellPopupEventArgs>), typeof(GridControl));
			CellDropDownOpeningEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("CellDropDownOpening", RoutingStrategy.Direct, typeof(EventHandler<GridControlCellEventArgs>), typeof(GridControl));
			CellTooltipCheckingEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("CellTooltipChecking", RoutingStrategy.Direct, typeof(EventHandler<GridControlCellEventArgs>), typeof(GridControl));
			SelectAllCommand = new RoutedUICommand(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridControl_ContextMenu_SelectAll, "SelectAll", typeof(GridControl));
			SelectColumnCommand = new RoutedUICommand(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridControl_ContextMenu_SelectColumn, "SelectColumn", typeof(GridControl));
			SelectRowCommand = new RoutedUICommand(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridControl_ContextMenu_SelectRow, "SelectRow", typeof(GridControl));
			DisplayColumnChooserCommand = new RoutedUICommand(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridControl_ContextMenu_ChooseColumn, "DisplayColumnChooser", typeof(GridControl));
			SortAscendingCommand = new RoutedUICommand(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridControl_ContextMenu_SortAscending, "SortAscending", typeof(GridControl));
			SortDescendingCommand = new RoutedUICommand(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridControl_ContextMenu_SortDescending, "SortDescending", typeof(GridControl));
			RemoveSortingCommand = new RoutedUICommand(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridControl_ContextMenu_RemoveSorting, "RemoveSorting", typeof(GridControl));
			ClearSortingsCommand = new RoutedUICommand(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridControl_ContextMenu_ClearSortings, "ClearSortings", typeof(GridControl));
			FreezeColumnCommand = new RoutedUICommand(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridControl_ContextMenu_FreezeColumn, "FreezeColumn", typeof(GridControl));
			UnfreezeColumnCommand = new RoutedUICommand(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridControl_ContextMenu_UnfreezeColumn, "UnfreezeColumn", typeof(GridControl));
			FilterHeaderCommand = new RoutedUICommand(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridControl_ContextMenu_ShowRecordFilterLine, "FilterHeader", typeof(GridControl));
			ClearCellFilterCommand = new RoutedCommand("ClearCellFilter", typeof(GridControl));
			ClearAllFiltersCommand = new RoutedCommand("ClearAllFilters", typeof(GridControl));
			ExpandAllCommand = new RoutedUICommand(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridControl_ContextMenu_ExpandAll, "ExpandAll", typeof(GridControl));
			CollapseAllCommand = new RoutedUICommand(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridControl_ContextMenu_CollapseAll, "CollapseAll", typeof(GridControl));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(GridControl)));
			Control.IsTabStopProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(GridControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(GridControl), new CommandBinding(SelectAllCommand, SelectAllCommand_Executed, SelectAllCommand_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(GridControl), new CommandBinding(SelectColumnCommand, SelectColumnCommand_Executed, SelectColumnCommand_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(GridControl), new CommandBinding(SelectRowCommand, SelectRowCommand_Executed, SelectRowCommand_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(GridControl), new CommandBinding(DisplayColumnChooserCommand, DisplayColumnChooserCommand_Executed, DisplayColumnChooserCommand_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(GridControl), new CommandBinding(SortAscendingCommand, SortAscendingCommand_Executed, SortAscendingCommand_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(GridControl), new CommandBinding(SortDescendingCommand, SortDescendingCommand_Executed, SortDescendingCommand_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(GridControl), new CommandBinding(RemoveSortingCommand, RemoveSortingCommand_Executed, RemoveSortingCommand_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(GridControl), new CommandBinding(ClearSortingsCommand, ClearSortingsCommand_Executed, ClearSortingsCommand_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(GridControl), new CommandBinding(FreezeColumnCommand, FreezeColumnCommand_Executed, FreezeColumnCommand_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(GridControl), new CommandBinding(UnfreezeColumnCommand, UnfreezeColumnCommand_Executed, UnfreezeColumnCommand_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(GridControl), new CommandBinding(FilterHeaderCommand, FilterHeaderCommand_Executed, FilterHeaderCommand_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(GridControl), new CommandBinding(ClearCellFilterCommand, ClearCellFilterCommand_Executed, ClearCellFilterCommand_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(GridControl), new CommandBinding(ClearAllFiltersCommand, ClearAllFiltersCommand_Executed, ClearAllFiltersCommand_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(GridControl), new CommandBinding(ExpandAllCommand, ExpandAllCommand_Executed, ExpandAllCommand_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(GridControl), new CommandBinding(CollapseAllCommand, CollapseAllCommand_Executed, CollapseAllCommand_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassInputBinding(typeof(GridControl), new InputBinding(SelectAllCommand, new KeyGesture(Key.A, ModifierKeys.Control)));
			ApplicationCommands.Copy.Text = AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridControl_ContextMenu_Copy;
			ApplicationCommands.Paste.Text = AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridControl_ContextMenu_Paste;
			CommandManager.RegisterClassInputBinding(typeof(GridControl), new InputBinding(ApplicationCommands.Cut, new KeyGesture(Key.X, ModifierKeys.Control)));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassInputBinding(typeof(GridControl), new InputBinding(ApplicationCommands.Copy, new KeyGesture(Key.C, ModifierKeys.Control)));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassInputBinding(typeof(GridControl), new InputBinding(ApplicationCommands.Paste, new KeyGesture(Key.V, ModifierKeys.Control)));
		}

		public GridControl()
		{
			TimingDebugger.RegisterTimingReportKey(this);
			TimingDebugger.Reset();
			TimingDebugger.BeingTimingBlock("GridControl.Construction");
			InternalScrollHelper = new ScrollHelper(this);
			InternalSortHelper = new SortingHelper(this);
			InternalSelectionHelper = new SelectionHelper(this);
			InternalFreezingHelper = new FreezingHelper(this);
			InternalFilterHelper = new FilterHelper(this);
			InternalCellValueHelper = new CellValueHelper(this);
			InternalDragAndDropHelper = new DragAndDropHelper(this);
			InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper = new HeaderColumnSpanHelper(this);
			InternalColumnsVirtualizationHelper = new ColumnsVirtualizationHelper(this);
			InternalLayoutPersistenceHelper = new LayoutPersistenceHelper(this);
			CommandManager.AddPreviewCanExecuteHandler(this, CommandManager_PreviewCanExecute);
			CommandManager.AddPreviewExecutedHandler(this, CommandManager_PreviewExecuted);
			TimingDebugger.EndTimingBlock();
		}

		private void CommandManager_PreviewCanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Command == ApplicationCommands.Copy)
			{
				if (!IgnoreClipboardActions())
				{
					e.CanExecute = GridClipboardHelper.CanCopy(this);
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
			else if (e.Command == ApplicationCommands.Paste)
			{
				if (!IgnoreClipboardActions(isPaste: true))
				{
					e.CanExecute = CanPasteData();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
			else if (e.Command == ApplicationCommands.Cut && !IgnoreClipboardActions())
			{
				e.CanExecute = CanCutData();
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private void CommandManager_PreviewExecuted(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Command == ApplicationCommands.Copy)
			{
				if (!IgnoreClipboardActions())
				{
					GridControlCopyEventArgs e2 = new GridControlCopyEventArgs(this, DataCopyingEvent, this);
					RaiseEvent(e2);
					if (!e2.Handled)
					{
						GridClipboardHelper.DoDefaultCopy(e2);
					}
					OnDataCopying(e2);
					e2.SendToClipboard();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
			else if (e.Command == ApplicationCommands.Paste)
			{
				if (IgnoreClipboardActions(isPaste: true))
				{
					return;
				}
				IList<IList<string>> plainTextsFromClipboard = GridClipboardHelper.GetPlainTextsFromClipboard();
				NumberSelectionInfo selectionInfo = PasteTargetAreaInfo.GetSelectionInfo(this);
				GridControlPastingEventArgs e3 = new GridControlPastingEventArgs(plainTextsFromClipboard, this, DataPastingEvent, selectionInfo);
				OnDataPasting(e3);
				if (!e3.Cancel && !e3.Handled)
				{
					if (e3.PastingData.Count <= 0)
					{
						return;
					}
					PasteTargetAreaInfo pasteInfo = new PasteTargetAreaInfo
					{
						ClipboardTexts = e3.PastingData,
						StartRowIndex = selectionInfo.StartRowIndex,
						StartColumnIndex = selectionInfo.StartColumnIndex,
						SelectedRowsCount = selectionInfo.SelectedRowsCount,
						SelectedColumnsCount = selectionInfo.SelectedColumnsCount
					};
					if (e3.ShouldDelayPasting)
					{
						if (e3.TryOnceMoreAfterRender)
						{
							GridClipboardHelper.DoDefaultPaste(this, pasteInfo);
							base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
							{
								GridClipboardHelper.DoDefaultPasteOneByOne(this, pasteInfo);
							}, DispatcherPriority.Render);
						}
						else
						{
							GridClipboardHelper.DoDefaultPasteOneByOne(this, pasteInfo);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						GridClipboardHelper.DoDefaultPaste(this, pasteInfo);
						if (e3.TryOnceMoreAfterRender)
						{
							base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
							{
								GridClipboardHelper.DoDefaultPaste(this, pasteInfo);
							}, DispatcherPriority.Render);
						}
					}
					e3.Handled = true;
				}
				e.Handled = e3.Handled;
			}
			else if (e.Command == ApplicationCommands.Cut && !IgnoreClipboardActions(isPaste: true))
			{
				GridControlCopyEventArgs e4 = new GridControlCopyEventArgs(this, DataCopyingEvent, this);
				RaiseEvent(e4);
				if (!e4.Handled)
				{
					GridClipboardHelper.DoDefaultCopy(e4);
				}
				OnDataCopying(e4);
				e4.SendToClipboard();
				OnDataClearAfterCopyForCut();
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private bool IgnoreClipboardActions(bool isPaste = false)
		{
			if (IgnoreCellNativeClipboardActions)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (isPaste && SelectedCells.Count() == 1)
			{
				DataCellInfo dataCellInfo = SelectedCells.First();
				if (dataCellInfo.Data != null && dataCellInfo.Data.HostControl != null && dataCellInfo.Data.HostControl.IsInEditMode && dataCellInfo.Data.ItemsSource == null)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.GetCellControl(this) != null)
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			else if (!base.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
			{
				Focus();
			}
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
		}

		protected virtual bool OnCommandExecuting(ICommand command)
		{
			if (command == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("command");
			}
			CommandExecutingEventArgs e = new CommandExecutingEventArgs(command, CommandExecutingEvent, this);
			RaiseEvent(e);
			return !e.Cancel;
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			_outlineBorder = GetTemplateChild("PART_OutlineBorder") as Border;
			_outlineGrid = GetTemplateChild("PART_OutlineGrid") as Grid;
			DataGrid = GetTemplateChild("PART_DataGrid") as GridControlDataGrid;
			_gridContainer = GetTemplateChild("PART_GridContainer") as Grid;
			_columnFrozenLine = GetTemplateChild("PART_ColumnFrozenLine") as Border;
			_rowIndicatorLine = GetTemplateChild("PART_DataDraggingLine") as Line;
			if (DataGrid != null)
			{
				DataGrid.ParentGridControl = this;
				DataGrid.SetBinding(System.Windows.Controls.DataGrid.ColumnWidthProperty, new Binding
				{
					Source = this,
					Path = new PropertyPath(DefaultColumnWidthProperty),
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				DataGrid.Loaded += _dataGrid_Loaded;
				DataGrid.LoadingRow += _dataGrid_LoadingRow;
				DataGrid.SelectedCellsChanged += _dataGrid_SelectedCellsChanged;
				DataGrid.CurrentCellChanged += _dataGrid_CurrentCellChanged;
				DataGrid.ColumnReordered += _dataGrid_ColumnReordered;
				RefreshDataInternal(clearRowsData: false);
				UpdateInnerGirdVisibility();
				InternalScrollHelper.InitializeScrollBars();
				if (ZoomFactor != 1.0)
				{
					ApplyZoomFactor();
				}
				InternalFreezingHelper.UpdateColumnFrozenLine();
			}
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
		}

		public void RefreshData()
		{
			RefreshDataInternal(clearRowsData: true);
		}

		internal void RefreshDataInternal(bool clearRowsData)
		{
			TimingDebugger.BeingTimingBlock("GridControl.RefreshData");
			if (DataGrid != null)
			{
				InternalSelectionHelper.SaveSelection();
				if (DataSource != null)
				{
					bool needReinitialize = InternalColumnsVirtualizationHelper.NeedReinitialize;
					if (needReinitialize)
					{
						DataGrid.ItemsSource = null;
						InternalColumnsVirtualizationHelper.ReInitializeColumns();
					}
					if (clearRowsData)
					{
						foreach (DataRow row in DataSource.Rows)
						{
							if (row.HasData)
							{
								row.ClearData();
								row.RaiseIndexerPropertyChanged();
							}
						}
					}
					RebindData();
					if (needReinitialize)
					{
						EnsureColumnsReconstructed();
					}
					InternalLayoutPersistenceHelper.FlushCachedLayoutSettings();
				}
				else
				{
					DataGrid.ItemsSource = null;
					DataGrid.Columns.Clear();
				}
				InternalSelectionHelper.RestoreSelection(raiseSelectionChanged: true);
			}
			TimingDebugger.EndTimingBlock();
		}

		private void EnsureColumnsReconstructed()
		{
			if (_columnsReconstructionOperation != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			_columnsReconstructionOperation = base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				try
				{
					if (DataGrid != null)
					{
						int frozenColumnCount = DataGrid.FrozenColumnCount;
						if (frozenColumnCount > 0)
						{
							DataGrid.FrozenColumnCount = 0;
						}
						else if (DataGrid.Columns.Count > 0)
						{
							DataGrid.FrozenColumnCount = 1;
						}
						DataGrid.FrozenColumnCount = frozenColumnCount;
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					_columnsReconstructionOperation = null;
				}
			}, DispatcherPriority.Render);
		}

		public void RebindData()
		{
			if (DataSource != null && DataGrid != null)
			{
				TimingDebugger.BeingTimingBlock("GridControl.RebindData");
				if (DataGrid.Columns.Count == 0 && InternalColumnsVirtualizationHelper.NeedReinitialize)
				{
					DataGrid.ItemsSource = null;
					InternalColumnsVirtualizationHelper.ReInitializeColumns();
				}
				DirectlySetItemsSource(DataSource.Rows);
				if (DataSource != null)
				{
					DataSource.OnBoundToGridControl();
				}
				TimingDebugger.BeingTimingBlock("this.InternalFreezingHelper.Initialize");
				InternalFreezingHelper.Initialize();
				TimingDebugger.EndTimingBlock();
				if (_hasDataBoundedEver)
				{
					InternalSelectionHelper.ResetSelection();
					InternalFilterHelper.ClearColumnsFilterOperands();
					InternalSortHelper.InvalidateSorting();
				}
				_hasDataBoundedEver = true;
				TimingDebugger.EndTimingBlock();
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnDataSourceColumnsVisibilityChanged()
		{
			EnsureColumnsReconstructed();
			InternalFreezingHelper.UpdateColumnFrozenLine();
			InternalColumnsVirtualizationHelper.OnColumnsVisibilityChanged();
			if (this.ColumnsVisibilityChanged != null)
			{
				this.ColumnsVisibilityChanged(this, System.EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		public void DirectlySetItemsSource(IEnumerable<DataRow> rows)
		{
			InvalidateDataGridItemsSource(rows);
		}

		internal void OnDataSourceRowsChanged()
		{
			InvalidateDataGridItemsSource(DataSource.Rows);
			UpdateLastRow();
			if (!CurrentCell.IsEmpty)
			{
				InvalidateCurrentCell();
			}
		}

		private void UpdateCurrentCellIndexes()
		{
			_currentCellRowIndex = -1;
			_currentCellColumnIndex = -1;
			FlushUpdateForDataGrid();
			if (CurrentCell.IsEmpty || DataGrid == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = 0;
			DataRow row = CurrentCell.Row;
			foreach (DataRow item in DataGrid.ItemsSource)
			{
				if (row == item)
				{
					_currentCellRowIndex = num;
					break;
				}
				num++;
			}
			_currentCellColumnIndex = CurrentCell.Column.LogicalDisplayIndex;
		}

		private void InvalidateCurrentCell()
		{
			if (_invalidateCurrentCellOperation != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			_invalidateCurrentCellOperation = base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				try
				{
					UpdateCurrentCell();
				}
				finally
				{
					_invalidateCurrentCellOperation = null;
				}
			}, DispatcherPriority.DataBind);
		}

		private void UpdateCurrentCell()
		{
			if (DataSource == null || _currentCellRowIndex == -1 || _currentCellColumnIndex == -1 || CurrentCell.IsEmpty)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			DataRow dataRow = BoundRows.ElementAtOrDefault(_currentCellRowIndex);
			if (dataRow != null)
			{
				DataColumn dataColumn = DataSource.Columns.FirstOrDefault((DataColumn x) => x.LogicalDisplayIndex == _currentCellColumnIndex);
				if (dataColumn != null)
				{
					CurrentCell = new DataCellInfo(dataRow, dataColumn);
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				CurrentCell = default(DataCellInfo);
			}
		}

		internal void OnDataSourceRowResolvedVisibilityChanged()
		{
			InvalidateDataGridItemsSource(_latestRowsForItemsSource);
		}

		private void InvalidateDataGridItemsSource(IEnumerable<DataRow> rows)
		{
			_latestRowsForItemsSource = rows;
			if (!_invalidateDataGridItemsSourceFlag)
			{
				_invalidateDataGridItemsSourceFlag = true;
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(FlushUpdateForDataGrid));
			}
		}

		private void FlushUpdateForDataGrid()
		{
			if (_invalidateDataGridItemsSourceFlag)
			{
				try
				{
					UpdateDataGridItemsSource();
				}
				finally
				{
					_invalidateDataGridItemsSourceFlag = false;
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateDataGridItemsSource()
		{
			if (DataGrid == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			IEnumerable<DataRow> latestRowsForItemsSource = _latestRowsForItemsSource;
			IEnumerable<DataRow> baseItemsSource = DataGrid.BaseItemsSource;
			bool raiseSelectionChanged = false;
			InternalSelectionHelper.SaveSelection();
			if (latestRowsForItemsSource == null || !latestRowsForItemsSource.Any((DataRow x) => x.ResolvedVisibility == Visibility.Visible))
			{
				IEnumerable<DataRow> enumerable = ((DataSource == null) ? null : DataSource.FakeRowsForHorizontalScrollBar);
				if (enumerable != baseItemsSource)
				{
					DataGrid.ItemsSource = enumerable;
					raiseSelectionChanged = true;
				}
				DataGrid.MinRowHeight = 0.0;
				DataGrid.RowHeight = 0.0;
			}
			else
			{
				IEnumerable<DataRow> enumerable2 = baseItemsSource;
				List<DataRow> list = latestRowsForItemsSource.Where((DataRow x) => x.ResolvedVisibility == Visibility.Visible).ToList();
				if (enumerable2 == null || !IsItemsSourceEqual(enumerable2, list))
				{
					DataGrid.ItemsSource = list;
					raiseSelectionChanged = true;
				}
				DataGrid.MinRowHeight = MinRowHeight;
				UpdateRowHeight();
			}
			InternalSelectionHelper.RestoreSelection(raiseSelectionChanged);
			UpdateFullDisplayDesiredHeight();
			InternalFilterHelper.ResetFilterOperands();
			static bool IsItemsSourceEqual(IEnumerable<DataRow> oldItemsSource, IEnumerable<DataRow> newItemsSource)
			{
				if (oldItemsSource == null)
				{
					return newItemsSource == null;
				}
				if (newItemsSource == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (oldItemsSource == newItemsSource)
				{
					return true;
				}
				return oldItemsSource.SequenceEqual(newItemsSource);
			}
		}

		public Point? GetCurrentActiveCellAsPoint()
		{
			if (DataGrid != null)
			{
				_ = DataGrid.CurrentCell;
				return new Point(DataGrid.ItemsSource.ToList().IndexOf(DataGrid.CurrentCell.Item as DataRow), DataGrid.Columns.IndexOf(DataGrid.CurrentCell.Column));
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void SetCurrentActiveCellInPoint(Point activeCellPoint)
		{
			if (activeCellPoint.X >= 0.0 && activeCellPoint.Y >= 0.0 && DataGrid != null && DataSource != null && (double)DataSource.Rows.Count > activeCellPoint.X && (double)DataGrid.Columns.Count > activeCellPoint.Y)
			{
				DataRow item = DataSource.Rows[(int)activeCellPoint.X];
				DataGridColumn column = DataGrid.Columns[(int)activeCellPoint.Y];
				DataGridCellInfo dataGridCellInfo = new DataGridCellInfo(item, column);
				DataGrid.CurrentCell = dataGridCellInfo;
				if (SelectionUnit != DataGridSelectionUnit.FullRow)
				{
					DataGrid.UnselectAllCells();
					DataGrid.SelectedCells.Add(dataGridCellInfo);
				}
			}
		}

		internal void UpdateLastRow()
		{
			if (_cachedLastRow != null)
			{
				_cachedLastRow.IsNewlyAdded = false;
				_cachedLastRow = null;
			}
			if (ShowLastRowAsNewlyAdded && DataSource != null)
			{
				DataRow dataRow = DataSource.Rows.LastOrDefault();
				if (dataRow != null)
				{
					dataRow.IsNewlyAdded = true;
					_cachedLastRow = dataRow;
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateInnerGirdVisibility()
		{
			if (DataGrid != null)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(CoverText))
				{
					DataGrid.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				else
				{
					DataGrid.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				}
				InternalFreezingHelper.UpdateColumnFrozenLine();
			}
		}

		private void InitializeAfterLoaded()
		{
			ScrollViewer scrollViewer = InternalScrollHelper.ScrollViewer;
			if (scrollViewer != null && VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(scrollViewer, 0) is Grid grid && grid.Children.Count > 0 && grid.Children[0] is Button button)
			{
				button.Command = SelectAllCommand;
			}
			InternalFreezingHelper.UpdateColumnFrozenLine();
		}

		private void ApplyZoomFactor()
		{
			if (ZoomFactor > 0.0 && _gridContainer != null)
			{
				if (ZoomFactor != 1.0)
				{
					_gridContainer.LayoutTransform = new ScaleTransform(ZoomFactor, ZoomFactor);
				}
				else
				{
					_gridContainer.LayoutTransform = null;
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateRowHeight()
		{
			if (DataGrid != null)
			{
				if (IsMultilineAllowedInCell)
				{
					DataGrid.ClearValue(System.Windows.Controls.DataGrid.RowHeightProperty);
				}
				else
				{
					DataGrid.RowHeight = MinRowHeight;
				}
			}
		}

		internal void CheckMeasuredSize()
		{
			if (_outlineBorder != null && _outlineGrid != null)
			{
				Size desiredSize = _outlineGrid.DesiredSize;
				Size size = new Size(desiredSize.Width + base.BorderThickness.Left + base.BorderThickness.Right, desiredSize.Height + base.BorderThickness.Top + base.BorderThickness.Bottom);
				if (base.DesiredSize != size)
				{
					InvalidateMeasure();
				}
			}
		}

		public void SelectAll()
		{
			InternalSelectionHelper.SelectAll();
		}

		public void DeselectAll()
		{
			InternalSelectionHelper.DeselectAll();
		}

		public void SelectColumn(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (column == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("column");
			}
			InternalSelectionHelper.SelectColumn(column);
		}

		public void DeselectColumn(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (column == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("column");
			}
			InternalSelectionHelper.DeselectColumn(column);
		}

		public void SelectRow(DataRow row)
		{
			if (row == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("row");
			}
			InternalSelectionHelper.SelectRow(row);
		}

		public void DeselectRow(DataRow row)
		{
			if (row == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("row");
			}
			InternalSelectionHelper.DeselectRow(row);
		}

		public void SelectCell(DataCellInfo cell, bool deselectOthers = false, bool setCurrent = false)
		{
			if (deselectOthers)
			{
				if (!(TotalSelectedCells.FirstOrDefault() == cell) || TotalSelectedCells.Skip(1).Any())
				{
					InternalSelectionHelper.LockSelectionChanged();
					try
					{
						InternalSelectionHelper.DeselectAll();
						InternalSelectionHelper.SelectCell(cell);
					}
					finally
					{
						InternalSelectionHelper.UnlockSelectionChanged(raiseEvent: true);
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				InternalSelectionHelper.SelectCell(cell);
			}
			if (setCurrent)
			{
				CurrentCell = cell;
			}
		}

		public void DeselectCell(DataCellInfo cell)
		{
			InternalSelectionHelper.DeselectCell(cell);
		}

		public void InvalidateData()
		{
			if (!_invalidateDataFlag)
			{
				_invalidateDataFlag = true;
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					RefreshData();
					_invalidateDataFlag = false;
				}, DispatcherPriority.Normal);
			}
		}

		public void InvalidateGridWidth()
		{
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				if (DataGrid != null && DataGrid.ActualWidth > 1.0)
				{
					DataGrid.Width = DataGrid.ActualWidth - 0.01;
					DataGrid.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
					{
						DataGrid.ClearValue(FrameworkElement.WidthProperty);
					}, DispatcherPriority.Loaded);
				}
			}, DispatcherPriority.Normal);
		}

		public DataColumn ColumnFromDisplayIndex(int displayIndex)
		{
			if (DataGrid != null && DataSource != null && displayIndex >= 0 && displayIndex < DataSource.Columns.Count)
			{
				if (InternalColumnsVirtualizationHelper.UseVirtualizationLoading)
				{
					return InternalColumnsVirtualizationHelper.GetDisplayedColumnsInOrder().BisectionFind((DataColumn x) => x.LogicalDisplayIndex, displayIndex);
				}
				if (DataGrid.ColumnFromDisplayIndex(displayIndex) is GridControlColumn gridControlColumn)
				{
					return gridControlColumn.DataColumn;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void InvalidateSorting()
		{
			InternalSortHelper.InvalidateSorting();
		}

		}

		public void Print(string title, string description)
		{
			try
			{
				PageSettings defaultPageSettings = PrintHelper.DefaultPageSettings;
				Thickness value = PrintHelper.ConvertMargins(defaultPageSettings.Margins);
				PrintDialog printDialog = new PrintDialog();
				PrintQueue printQueue = printDialog.PrintQueue;
				PrintTicket printTicket = printDialog.PrintTicket;
				if (defaultPageSettings.PaperSize != null)
				{
					printTicket.PageMediaSize = PrintHelper.ConvertPaperSizeToPageMediaSize(defaultPageSettings.PaperSize, printQueue.GetPrintCapabilities(printTicket).PageMediaSizeCapability);
				}
				printTicket.PageOrientation = (defaultPageSettings.Landscape ? PageOrientation.Landscape : PageOrientation.Portrait);
				if (printDialog.ShowDialog() == true)
				{
					bool flag = printDialog.PrintTicket.PageOrientation == PageOrientation.Landscape;
					Size size = (flag ? new Size(printDialog.PrintableAreaHeight, printDialog.PrintableAreaWidth) : new Size(printDialog.PrintableAreaWidth, printDialog.PrintableAreaHeight));
					Rect printableArea = new Rect(size);
					FixedDocument fixedDocument = PrintHelper.CreateFixedDocument(this, printableArea, size, value, flag, title);
					printDialog.PrintDocument(fixedDocument.DocumentPaginator, description);
				}
			}
			catch (InvalidOperationException)
			{
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> GenerateGenericPrintPages(Size availablePrintSize, int frozenRows = 0, int frozenColumns = 0, string title = null, string additionalFooter = null, bool ignoreLastColumn = false, bool ignoreLastRow = false, Func<DataColumn, string> columnHeaderTextFunc = null)
		{
			return PrintingHelper.GenerateGenericPrintPages(this, availablePrintSize, frozenRows, frozenColumns, title, additionalFooter, ignoreLastColumn, ignoreLastRow, columnHeaderTextFunc);
		}

		public void ScrollIntoView(DataRow row)
		{
			if (row == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("row");
			}
			if (DataGrid != null && BoundRows.Contains(row))
			{
				DataGrid.ScrollIntoView(row);
			}
		}

		public void ScrollIntoView(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (column == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("column");
			}
			if (DataGrid == null || DataSource == null || !DataSource.Columns.Contains(column) || column.TargetGridColumn == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			IEnumerable<DataRow> viewportRows = ViewportRows;
			if (viewportRows == null)
			{
				DataRow dataRow = DataGrid.ItemsSource.FirstOrDefault();
				if (dataRow != null)
				{
					InternalDataGridScrollIntoView(dataRow, column);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				DataRow dataRow2 = viewportRows.FirstOrDefault();
				if (dataRow2 != null)
				{
					InternalDataGridScrollIntoView(dataRow2, column);
				}
			}
		}

		public void ScrollIntoView(DataCellInfo cell)
		{
			if (cell.IsEmpty)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("Parameter cell cannot be empty.");
			}
			InternalDataGridScrollIntoView(cell.Row, cell.Column);
		}

		private void InternalDataGridScrollIntoView(DataRow row, DataColumn column)
		{
			if (DataGrid != null && column.TargetGridColumn != null && column.TargetGridColumn.DataColumn == column && DataGrid.Columns.Contains(column.TargetGridColumn))
			{
				try
				{
					DataGrid.ScrollIntoView(row, column.TargetGridColumn);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
			}
		}

		public void SyncColumnsFilterVisibility()
		{
			if (DataSource == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (DataColumn column in DataSource.Columns)
			{
				column.Filter.Visibility = FilterHeaderVisibility;
			}
		}

		public void UpdateRowIndicatorLine(Visibility visibility, Func<IInputElement, Point> mousePositionCalculator = null)
		{
			if (_rowIndicatorLine == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (visibility == Visibility.Visible)
			{
				IEnumerable<DataRow> viewportRows = ViewportRows;
				if (viewportRows == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				{
					foreach (DataRow item in viewportRows.ToList())
					{
						if (!(DataGrid.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(item) is DataGridRow dataGridRow))
						{
							continue;
						}
						Point point = default(Point);
						point = mousePositionCalculator?.Invoke(dataGridRow) ?? Mouse.GetPosition(dataGridRow);
						if (point.X >= 0.0 && point.X <= dataGridRow.ActualWidth && point.Y >= 0.0 && point.Y < dataGridRow.ActualHeight)
						{
							if (point.Y <= dataGridRow.ActualHeight / 2.0)
							{
								Point point2 = dataGridRow.TranslatePoint(new Point(0.0, 0.0), this);
								_rowIndicatorLine.Margin = new Thickness(0.0, point2.Y - 0.5, 0.0, 0.0);
								_rowIndicatorLine.Tag = new KeyValuePair<DataRow, bool>(item, value: true);
							}
							else
							{
								Point point3 = dataGridRow.TranslatePoint(new Point(0.0, dataGridRow.ActualHeight), this);
								_rowIndicatorLine.Margin = new Thickness(0.0, point3.Y - 0.5, 0.0, 0.0);
								_rowIndicatorLine.Tag = new KeyValuePair<DataRow, bool>(item, value: false);
							}
							_rowIndicatorLine.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
							break;
						}
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			_rowIndicatorLine.Margin = new Thickness(0.0);
			_rowIndicatorLine.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
		}

		public int GetRowIndexOfRowIndicator()
		{
			if (_rowIndicatorLine == null)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			if (_rowIndicatorLine.Visibility != Visibility.Visible)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			if (_rowIndicatorLine.Tag == null)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			if (!(_rowIndicatorLine.Tag is KeyValuePair<DataRow, bool>))
			{
				return -1;
			}
			KeyValuePair<DataRow, bool> keyValuePair = (KeyValuePair<DataRow, bool>)_rowIndicatorLine.Tag;
			int num = DataSource.Rows.IndexOf(keyValuePair.Key);
			if (!keyValuePair.Value)
			{
				return num + 1;
			}
			return num;
		}

		public void BeginBatchCellDataUpdate()
		{
			if (this.BatchCellDataUpdateStarted != null)
			{
				this.BatchCellDataUpdateStarted(this, System.EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		public void EndBatchCellDataUpdate()

			{
				this.BarchCellDataUpdateEnded(this, System.EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		internal void UpdateFullDisplayDesiredHeight()
		{
			double num = (double.IsNaN(ColumnHeaderHeight) ? 0.0 : ColumnHeaderHeight);
			if (DataGrid == null)
			{
				FullDisplayDesiredHeight = num;
				return;
			}
			int num2 = DataGrid.ItemsSource.Count();
			FullDisplayDesiredHeight = num + (double)num2 * DataGrid.MinRowHeight;
		}

		private void _dataGrid_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_initializedAfterLoaded)
			{
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(InitializeAfterLoaded), DispatcherPriority.Input);
				_initializedAfterLoaded = true;
				OnFirstTimeGridLoaded();
				RaiseEvent(new RoutedEventArgs(FirstTimeGridLoadedEvent, this));
			}
		}

		private void _dataGrid_LoadingRow(object sender, DataGridRowEventArgs e)
		{
			TimingDebugger.BeingTimingBlock("DataTable._dataGrid_LoadingRow");
			if (e.Row.Item is DataRow dataRow)
			{
				if (!dataRow.HasData)
				{
					TimingDebugger.BeingTimingBlock("DataTable.OnRowValuesQueried");
					if (!dataRow.IsFakeRow)
					{
						DataSource.OnRowValuesQueried(dataRow);
					}
					TimingDebugger.EndTimingBlock();
					if (dataRow.HasData)
					{
						dataRow.RaiseIndexerPropertyChanged();
					}
				}
				if (!dataRow.IsFakeRow)
				{
					OnRowLoading(new DataRowEventArgs(dataRow));
				}
			}
			TimingDebugger.EndTimingBlock();
		}

		private void _dataGrid_SelectedCellsChanged(object sender, SelectedCellsChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!DataGrid.IgnoreSelectionChanged)
			{
				InternalSelectionHelper.RemoveColumnSelectionFlagsSilently();
				TryInvokeSelectionChanged();
			}
		}

		private void _dataGrid_CurrentCellChanged(object sender, System.EventArgs e)
		{
			DataGridCellInfo currentCell = DataGrid.CurrentCell;
			if (currentCell.IsValid)
			{
				CurrentCell = currentCell.GetDataCellInfo();
			}
		}

		private void _dataGrid_ColumnReordered(object sender, DataGridColumnEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Column is GridControlColumn { DataColumn: not null } gridControlColumn)
			{
				OnColumnDisplayIndexChanged(gridControlColumn.DataColumn);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnCoverTextChanged(string oldValue, string newValue)
		{
			UpdateInnerGirdVisibility();
		}

		protected virtual void OnCurrentCellChanged(DataCellInfo oldValue, DataCellInfo newValue)
		{
			if (DataGrid != null && !newValue.IsEmpty && newValue.Column.TargetGridColumn != null)
			{
				DataRow row = newValue.Row;
				GridControlColumn targetGridColumn = newValue.Column.TargetGridColumn;
				if (DataGrid.Columns.Contains(targetGridColumn))
				{
					DataGridCellInfo dataGridCellInfo = new DataGridCellInfo(row, targetGridColumn);
					if (DataGrid.CurrentCell != dataGridCellInfo)
					{
						DataGrid.CurrentCell = dataGridCellInfo;
					}
				}
			}
			if (!oldValue.IsEmpty)
			{
				oldValue.UpdateIsCurrent(isCurrent: false);
			}
			if (!newValue.IsEmpty)
			{
				newValue.UpdateIsCurrent(isCurrent: true);
				UpdateCurrentCellIndexes();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnCurrentDataRowChanged(DataRow oldValue, DataRow newValue)
		{
			if (oldValue != null)
			{
				oldValue.IsCurrent = false;
			}
			if (newValue != null)
			{
				newValue.IsCurrent = true;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnAllowRecordFilteringChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			if (DataSource == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (DataColumn column in DataSource.Columns)
			{
				column.InvalidateHeaderControlTemplate();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnFilterHeaderVisibilityChanged(Visibility oldValue, Visibility newValue)

		}

		protected virtual void OnZoomFactorChanged(double oldValue, double newValue)

			ApplyZoomFactor();
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnDataDragging(IDataObject dataObject)
		{
			dataObject.SetData(DataFormats.Text, InternalDragAndDropHelper.CreateDraggingText());
			GridControlDataDraggingEventArgs e = new GridControlDataDraggingEventArgs(dataObject, this, DataDraggingEvent, this);
			RaiseEvent(e);
		}

		protected override void OnDragOver(DragEventArgs e)
		{
			if (InternalScrollHelper.ScrollViewer != null && InternalScrollHelper.ScrollViewer.Content is FrameworkElement frameworkElement)
			{
				double num = 16.0;
				double y = e.GetPosition(frameworkElement).Y;
				if (y < num && y > 0.0)
				{
					InternalScrollHelper.ScrollVertically(up: true);
				}
				else if (frameworkElement.ActualHeight - y < num)
				{
					InternalScrollHelper.ScrollVertically(up: false);
				}
			}
			base.OnDragOver(e);
		}

		protected override void OnDrop(DragEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!e.Handled && DataSource != null && base.AllowDrop)
			{
				string text = null;
				if (e.Data.GetDataPresent(DataFormats.UnicodeText))
				{
					text = e.Data.GetData(DataFormats.UnicodeText) as string;
				}
				if (text == null && e.Data.GetDataPresent(DataFormats.Text))
				{
					text = e.Data.GetData(DataFormats.Text) as string;
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
				{
					IList<IList<string>> list = GridClipboardHelper.SplitIntoStringTable(text);
					GridControlCell ancestor = (e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject).GetAncestor<GridControlCell>(this);
					NumberSelectionInfo dropTargetInfo = DragAndDropHelper.GetDropTargetInfo(list, ancestor, this);
					GridControlDataDroppingEventArgs e2 = new GridControlDataDroppingEventArgs(list, dropTargetInfo, this, DataDroppingEvent, e.Data);
					OnDataDropping(e2);
					if (!e2.Cancel && !e2.Handled)
					{
						PasteTargetAreaInfo pasteInfo = new PasteTargetAreaInfo
						{
							ClipboardTexts = list,
							StartRowIndex = dropTargetInfo.StartRowIndex,
							StartColumnIndex = dropTargetInfo.StartColumnIndex,
							SelectedRowsCount = dropTargetInfo.SelectedRowsCount,
							SelectedColumnsCount = dropTargetInfo.SelectedColumnsCount
						};
						if (e2.ShouldDelayPasting)
						{
							GridClipboardHelper.DoDefaultPasteOneByOne(this, pasteInfo);
						}
						else
						{
							GridClipboardHelper.DoDefaultPaste(this, pasteInfo);
						}
						if (ancestor != null && ancestor.Cell != null)
						{
							CurrentCell = new DataCellInfo(ancestor.Cell.Row, ancestor.Cell.Column);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			base.OnDrop(e);
		}

		protected virtual void OnDataDropping(GridControlDataDroppingEventArgs e)
		{
			RaiseEvent(e);
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnDataSorting(GridControlDataSortingEventArgs e)

		internal void TryInvokeSelectionChanged()
		{
			if (InternalSelectionHelper.IsReady && !InternalSelectionHelper.ResolvedSuppressSelectionChangedEvent)
			{
				if (!InternalSelectionHelper.HasSelection)
				{
					DataGrid.CurrentCell = default(DataGridCellInfo);
				}
				InternalSelectionHelper.UpdateRowAndColumnSelectionStatus();
				OnSelectionChanged();
				RaiseEvent(new RoutedEventArgs(SelectionChangedEvent, this));
			}
		}

		protected virtual bool CanPasteData()
		{
			GridControlCanPasteEventArgs e = new GridControlCanPasteEventArgs(GridClipboardHelper.GetPlainTextsFromClipboard(), this, CanPasteCheckingEvent)
			{
				CanPaste = true
			};
			OnCanPasteChecking(e);
			if (e.Handled)
			{
				return e.CanPaste;
			}
			if (e.CanPaste)
			{
				return GridClipboardHelper.CanPaste(this, e.PastingData);
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual void OnColumnDisplayIndexChanged(DataColumn column)
		{
			InternalColumnsVirtualizationHelper.OnColumnDisplayIndexChanged(column);
			if (this.ColumnDisplayIndexChanged != null)
			{
				this.ColumnDisplayIndexChanged(this, new DataColumnEventArgs(column));
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnColumnActualWidthChanged(DataColumn column)
		{
			InternalColumnsVirtualizationHelper.OnColumnWidthChanged(column);
			if (this.ColumnActualWidthChanged != null)
			{
				this.ColumnActualWidthChanged(this, new DataColumnEventArgs(column));
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnColumnViewGenerated(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (this.ColumnViewGenerated != null)
			{
				this.ColumnViewGenerated(this, new DataColumnEventArgs(column));
			}
		}

		private static void OnDataSourcePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControl gridControl = d as GridControl;
			if (e.OldValue is DataTable dataTable)
			{
				dataTable.GridControl = null;
			}
			if (e.NewValue is DataTable dataTable2)
			{
				dataTable2.GridControl = gridControl;
			}
			gridControl.RefreshDataInternal(clearRowsData: false);
			gridControl.UpdateLastRow();
			gridControl.UpdateFullDisplayDesiredHeight();
		}

		private static void OnCoverTextPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as GridControl).OnCoverTextChanged((string)e.OldValue, (string)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnCurrentCellPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as GridControl).OnCurrentCellChanged((DataCellInfo)e.OldValue, (DataCellInfo)e.NewValue);
		}

		}

		private static object OnZoomFactorPropertyCoerced(DependencyObject d, object value)
		{
			GridControl gridControl = d as GridControl;
			double num = (double)value;
			if (num < gridControl.MinimumZoomFactor)
			{
				return gridControl.MinimumZoomFactor;
			}
			if (num > gridControl.MaximumZoomFactor)
			{
				return gridControl.MaximumZoomFactor;
			}
			return num;
		}

		{
			(d as GridControl).UpdateLastRow();
		}

		{
			(d as GridControl).UpdateRowHeight();

		private static void SelectAllCommand_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControl gridControl = sender as GridControl;
			if (gridControl.InternalSelectionHelper.IsAllSelected)
			{
				gridControl.InternalSelectionHelper.DeselectAll();
				return;
			}
			gridControl.InternalSelectionHelper.SelectAll();
			gridControl.InternalSelectionHelper.FocusOnTopFirstCell();
		}

		private static void SelectAllCommand_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControl gridControl = sender as GridControl;
			e.CanExecute = gridControl.InternalSelectionHelper.IsReady && gridControl.OnCommandExecuting(SelectAllCommand);
		}

		private static void SelectColumnCommand_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControl gridControl = sender as GridControl;
			DataColumn column = e.Parameter as DataColumn;
			if (gridControl.InternalSelectionHelper.IsColumnSelected(column))
			{
				gridControl.InternalSelectionHelper.DeselectColumn(column);
				return;
			}
			gridControl.InternalSelectionHelper.DeselectAll();
			gridControl.InternalSelectionHelper.SelectColumn(column);
			gridControl.InternalSelectionHelper.FocusOnTopFirstCell(column);
		}

		private static void SelectColumnCommand_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControl gridControl = sender as GridControl;
			DataColumn dataColumn = e.Parameter as DataColumn;
			e.CanExecute = dataColumn != null && gridControl.InternalSelectionHelper.IsReady && gridControl.OnCommandExecuting(SelectColumnCommand);
		}

		private static void SelectRowCommand_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControl gridControl = sender as GridControl;
			DataRow row = e.Parameter as DataRow;
			if (gridControl.InternalSelectionHelper.IsRowSelected(row))
			{
				gridControl.InternalSelectionHelper.DeselectRow(row);
				return;
			}
			gridControl.InternalSelectionHelper.DeselectAll();
			gridControl.InternalSelectionHelper.SelectRow(row);
		}

		private static void SelectRowCommand_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControl gridControl = sender as GridControl;
			DataRow dataRow = e.Parameter as DataRow;
			e.CanExecute = dataRow != null && gridControl.InternalSelectionHelper.IsReady && gridControl.OnCommandExecuting(SelectRowCommand);
		}

		private static void DisplayColumnChooserCommand_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControl gridControl = sender as GridControl;
			ColumnChooserWindow columnChooserWindow = new ColumnChooserWindow(gridControl.DataSource);
			columnChooserWindow.Owner = Window.GetWindow(gridControl);
			columnChooserWindow.ReservedCommand = gridControl.ReservedColumnChooserCommand;
			columnChooserWindow.ShowDialog();
		}

		}

		private static void SortAscendingCommand_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControl gridControl = sender as GridControl;
			DataColumn column = e.Parameter as DataColumn;
			if (gridControl.SortingMode == SortingMode.AllowSingleColumnSorting || Keyboard.Modifiers != ModifierKeys.Control)
			{
				gridControl.SortingInfo.ClearSorts();
			}
			gridControl.SortingInfo.SetSort(column, ListSortDirection.Ascending);
		}

		private static void SortAscendingCommand_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControl gridControl = sender as GridControl;
			DataColumn dataColumn = e.Parameter as DataColumn;
			if (gridControl.SortingMode != SortingMode.NotAllowed && dataColumn != null && dataColumn.CanSorting)
			{
				ListSortDirection? sortDirection = gridControl.SortingInfo.GetSortDirection(dataColumn);
				if (!sortDirection.HasValue || sortDirection.Value == ListSortDirection.Descending)
				{
					e.CanExecute = gridControl.OnCommandExecuting(SortAscendingCommand);
				}
			}
		}

		private static void SortDescendingCommand_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControl gridControl = sender as GridControl;
			DataColumn column = e.Parameter as DataColumn;
			if (gridControl.SortingMode == SortingMode.AllowSingleColumnSorting || Keyboard.Modifiers != ModifierKeys.Control)
			{

			}
			gridControl.SortingInfo.SetSort(column, ListSortDirection.Descending);
		}

				}
			}
		}

		private static void FilterHeaderCommand_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControl gridControl = sender as GridControl;
			if (gridControl.FilterHeaderVisibility == Visibility.Visible)
			{
				gridControl.FilterHeaderVisibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			else
			{
				gridControl.FilterHeaderVisibility = Visibility.Visible;
			}
		}

		private static void ExpandAllCommand_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControl gridControl = sender as GridControl;
			if (gridControl.DataSource == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (DataRow row in gridControl.DataSource.Rows)
			{
				if (row.GroupingRowsCount > 0 && row.GroupingState != RowGroupingState.Expanded)
				{
					row.GroupingState = RowGroupingState.Expanded;
				}
			}
		}

		private static void ExpandAllCommand_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControl gridControl = sender as GridControl;
			if (gridControl.DataSource != null)
			{
				e.CanExecute = gridControl.DataSource.Rows.Any((DataRow x) => x.GroupingRowsCount > 0 && x.GroupingState == RowGroupingState.Collapsed);
			}
			else
			{
				e.CanExecute = false;
			}
		}

		private static void CollapseAllCommand_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControl gridControl = sender as GridControl;
			if (gridControl.DataSource == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (DataRow row in gridControl.DataSource.Rows)
			{
				if (row.GroupingRowsCount > 0 && row.GroupingState != RowGroupingState.Collapsed)
				{
					row.GroupingState = RowGroupingState.Collapsed;
				}
			}
		}

		private static void CollapseAllCommand_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControl gridControl = sender as GridControl;
			if (gridControl.DataSource != null)
			{
				e.CanExecute = gridControl.DataSource.Rows.Any((DataRow x) => x.GroupingRowsCount > 0 && x.GroupingState == RowGroupingState.Expanded);
			}
			else
			{
				e.CanExecute = false;
			}
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return new GridControlAutomationPeer(this);
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size constraint)
		{
			Size size = base.MeasureOverride(constraint);
			if (_outlineBorder != null && _outlineGrid != null)
			{
				Size desiredSize = _outlineGrid.DesiredSize;
				Size size2 = new Size(desiredSize.Width + base.BorderThickness.Left + base.BorderThickness.Right, desiredSize.Height + base.BorderThickness.Top + base.BorderThickness.Bottom);
				if (size != size2)
				{
					return size2;
				}
			}
			return size;
		}
	}
	public class NotifiableObjectBase : DependencyObject, INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		protected virtual void OnPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
			}
		}

		protected virtual void InternalUpdateProperty<T>(string propertyName, ref T propertyField, T value)
		{
			propertyField = value;
			OnPropertyChanged(propertyName);
		}

		public void RaiseIndexerPropertyChanged()
		{
			OnPropertyChanged("Item[]");
		}
	}
