namespace AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Virtualization
{
	internal class ColumnsVirtualizationHelper
	{
		public const int RegularInitializationMaxColumns = 100;

		private readonly GridControl _gc;

		private readonly RegularInitializer _regularInitializer;

		private readonly VirtualInitializer _virtualInitializer;

		private DispatcherOperation _invalidateLogicalDisplayIndexesOperation;

		private bool IsReady
		{
			get
			{
				if (_gc.DataSource != null && _gc.DataSource.Columns != null)
				{
					return _gc.DataGrid != null;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public bool UseVirtualizationLoading { get; private set; }

		public bool NeedReinitialize
		{
			get
			{
				if (IsReady)
				{
					if (_gc.DataSource.Columns.Count > 100)
					{
						return _virtualInitializer.NeedReinitialize;
					}
					return _regularInitializer.NeedReinitialize;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public ColumnsVirtualizationHelper(GridControl gc)
		{
			if (gc == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("gc");
			}
			_gc = gc;
			_regularInitializer = new RegularInitializer(gc);
			_virtualInitializer = new VirtualInitializer(gc);
		}

		public void ReInitializeColumns()
		{
			if (IsReady)
			{
				if (_gc.DataSource.Columns.Count > 100)
				{
					UseVirtualizationLoading = true;
					_virtualInitializer.ReInitializeColumns();
				}
				else
				{
					UseVirtualizationLoading = false;
					_regularInitializer.ReInitializeColumns();
				}
				InvalidateLogicalDisplayIndexes();
			}
		}

		public void OnHorizontalScrollChanged()
		{
			if (IsReady && UseVirtualizationLoading && _gc.InternalScrollHelper.ScrollViewer != null)
			{
				_virtualInitializer.InvalidateLoadedColumns(updateImmediately: true);
			}
		}

		public void OnColumnsVisibilityChanged()
		{
			if (IsReady)
			{
				if (UseVirtualizationLoading)
				{
					_virtualInitializer.InvalidateLoadedColumns();
					_virtualInitializer.InvalidateColumnsOffsets();
				}
				InvalidateLogicalDisplayIndexes();
			}
		}

		public void OnColumnWidthChanged(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (IsReady && UseVirtualizationLoading)
			{
				_virtualInitializer.InvalidateLoadedColumns();
				_virtualInitializer.InvalidateColumnsOffsets();
			}
		}

		public void OnColumnDisplayIndexChanged(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (IsReady)
			{
				InvalidateLogicalDisplayIndexes();
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<DataColumn> GetDisplayedColumnsInOrder()
		{
			if (!IsReady)
			{
				yield break;
			}
			foreach (DataGridColumn item in _gc.DataGrid.Columns.OrderBy((DataGridColumn x) => x.DisplayIndex))
			{
				if (item.Visibility != Visibility.Visible)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (item is PlaceholderColumn placeholderColumn)
				{
					foreach (DataColumn includedColumn in placeholderColumn.GetIncludedColumns())
					{
						yield return includedColumn;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					yield return ((GridControlColumn)item).DataColumn;
				}
			}
		}

		public void InvalidateLogicalDisplayIndexes()
		{
			if (_invalidateLogicalDisplayIndexesOperation != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			_invalidateLogicalDisplayIndexesOperation = _gc.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				try
				{
					UpdateLogicalDisplayIndexes();
				}
				finally
				{
					_invalidateLogicalDisplayIndexesOperation = null;
				}
			});
		}

		private void UpdateLogicalDisplayIndexes()
		{
			TimingDebugger.BeingTimingBlock("UpdateLogicalDisplayIndexes");
			ReadOnlyCollection<DataColumn> columns = _gc.DataSource.Columns;
			for (int i = 0; i < columns.Count; i++)
			{
				columns[i].LogicalDisplayIndex = -1;
			}
			int num = 0;
			foreach (DataColumn item in GetDisplayedColumnsInOrder())
			{
				item.LogicalDisplayIndex = num++;
			}
			TimingDebugger.EndTimingBlock();
		}
	}
	internal class RegularInitializer
	{
		private readonly GridControl _gc;

		private List<DataColumn> _lastInitializedColumns;

		public bool NeedReinitialize
		{
			get
			{
				ReadOnlyCollection<DataColumn> readOnlyCollection = _gc.DataSource?.Columns;
				if (_lastInitializedColumns == null)
				{
					return readOnlyCollection != null;
				}
				if (readOnlyCollection == null)
				{
					return true;
				}
				return !_lastInitializedColumns.SequenceEqual(readOnlyCollection);
			}
		}

		public RegularInitializer(GridControl gc)
		{
			if (gc == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("gc");
			}
			_gc = gc;
		}

		public void ReInitializeColumns()
		{
			_gc.DataGrid.Columns.Clear();
			int num = 0;
			bool flag = false;
			_lastInitializedColumns = _gc.DataSource.Columns.ToList();
			foreach (DataColumn column in _gc.DataSource.Columns)
			{
				if (!flag && column.HeaderColumnSpan > 1)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				column.Index = num++;
				_gc.DataGrid.Columns.Add(new GridControlColumn(_gc, column));
			}
			_gc.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.PrepareColumnSpanInfomation(flag);
		}
	}
	internal class VirtualInitializer
	{
		private const int MarginColumnsCount = 2;

		private readonly GridControl _gc;

		private readonly List<bool> _loadedStates = new List<bool>();

		private readonly List<double> _offsets = new List<double>();

		private List<DataColumn> _lastInitializedColumns;

		private DispatcherOperation _updateColumnsOperation;

		private DispatcherOperation _updateColumnsOffsetsOperation;

		public bool NeedReinitialize
		{
			get
			{
				ReadOnlyCollection<DataColumn> readOnlyCollection = _gc.DataSource?.Columns;
				if (_lastInitializedColumns == null)
				{
					return readOnlyCollection != null;
				}
				if (readOnlyCollection == null)
				{
					return true;
				}
				return !_lastInitializedColumns.SequenceEqual(readOnlyCollection);
			}
		}

		public VirtualInitializer(GridControl gc)
		{
			if (gc == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("gc");
			}
			_gc = gc;
		}

		public void ReInitializeColumns()
		{
			TimingDebugger.BeingTimingBlock("VirtualInitializer.ReInitializeColumns");
			_gc.DataGrid.Columns.Clear();
			int num = 0;
			int count = _gc.DataSource.Columns.Count;
			int num2 = count - 1;
			double num3 = 0.0;
			double defaultColumnWidth = _gc.DefaultColumnWidth;
			_lastInitializedColumns = _gc.DataSource.Columns.ToList();
			_loadedStates.Clear();
			_offsets.Clear();
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				DataColumn dataColumn = _gc.DataSource.Columns[i];
				dataColumn.Index = i;
				if (dataColumn.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					if (num++ < 100 || i == num2)
					{
						_gc.DataGrid.Columns.Add(CreateView(dataColumn));
						_loadedStates.Add(item: true);
					}
					else
					{
						_loadedStates.Add(item: false);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					_gc.DataGrid.Columns.Add(CreateView(dataColumn));
					_loadedStates.Add(item: true);
				}
				_offsets.Add(num3);
				if (dataColumn.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					num3 += (dataColumn.Width.HasValue ? dataColumn.Width.Value : defaultColumnWidth);
				}
			}
			TimingDebugger.BeingTimingBlock("VirtualInitializer.AppendPlaceHolderColumns");
			AppendPlaceHolderColumns(fromInitialization: true);
			TimingDebugger.EndTimingBlock();
			TimingDebugger.EndTimingBlock();
		}

		public void InvalidateLoadedColumns(bool updateImmediately = false)
		{
			if (updateImmediately)
			{
				try
				{
					UpdateLoadedColumns();
					return;
				}
				finally
				{
					_updateColumnsOperation = null;
				}
			}
			if (_updateColumnsOperation != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			_updateColumnsOperation = _gc.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				try
				{
					UpdateLoadedColumns();
				}
				finally
				{
					_updateColumnsOperation = null;
				}
			});
		}

		private void UpdateLoadedColumns()
		{
			ScrollViewer scrollViewer = _gc.InternalScrollHelper.ScrollViewer;
			if (scrollViewer == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			int fromIndex = 0;
			int toIndex = 0;
			if (!GetColumnsToLoad(scrollViewer.HorizontalOffset, scrollViewer.HorizontalOffset + scrollViewer.ViewportWidth, out fromIndex, out toIndex))
			{
				return;
			}
			List<int> list = new List<int>();
			for (int i = fromIndex; i <= toIndex; i++)
			{
				if (!_loadedStates[i])
				{
					list.Add(i);
				}
			}
			if (list.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			ClearPlaceHolderColumns();
			int num = list[0];
			int num2 = 0;
			for (int j = 0; j < num; j++)
			{
				if (_loadedStates[j])
				{
					num2++;
				}
			}
			foreach (int item in list)
			{
				DataColumn column = _gc.DataSource.Columns[item];
				int index = item - num + num2;
				_gc.DataGrid.Columns.Insert(index, CreateView(column));
				_loadedStates[item] = true;
			}
			AppendPlaceHolderColumns(fromInitialization: false);
		}

		public void InvalidateColumnsOffsets(bool updateImmediately = false)
		{
			if (_updateColumnsOffsetsOperation != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			_updateColumnsOffsetsOperation = _gc.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				try
				{
					UpdateColumnsOffsets();
				}
				finally
				{
					_updateColumnsOffsetsOperation = null;
				}
			});
		}

		private void UpdateColumnsOffsets()
		{
			_offsets.Clear();
			double num = 0.0;
			ReadOnlyCollection<DataColumn> columns = _gc.DataSource.Columns;
			int count = columns.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count - 1; i++)
			{
				_offsets.Add(num);
				DataColumn dataColumn = columns[i];
				if (dataColumn.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					num += GetColumnWidth(dataColumn, _gc.DefaultColumnWidth);
				}
			}
		}

		private bool GetColumnsToLoad(double leftOffset, double rightOffset, out int fromIndex, out int toIndex)
		{
			fromIndex = (toIndex = 0);
			int count = _gc.DataSource.Columns.Count;
			double defaultColumnWidth = _gc.DefaultColumnWidth;
			int num = _offsets.BisectionFindPositionInsert((double x) => x, leftOffset);
			if (num >= count - 1)
			{
				return false;
			}
			int? num2 = null;
			double num3 = 0.0;
			double num4 = rightOffset - leftOffset;
			for (int num5 = num + 1; num5 < count; num5++)
			{
				DataColumn dataColumn = _gc.DataSource.Columns[num5];
				if (dataColumn.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					GetColumnWidth(dataColumn, defaultColumnWidth);
					if (num3 > num4)
					{
						num2 = num5 + 1;
						break;
					}
					num3 += GetColumnWidth(dataColumn, defaultColumnWidth);
				}
			}
			if (!num2.HasValue)
			{
				num2 = count - 1;
			}
			fromIndex = num;
			toIndex = Math.Min(count - 1, num2.Value);
			return true;
		}

		private double GetColumnWidth(DataColumn column, double defaultWidth)
		{
			if (column.TargetGridColumn != null)
			{
				return column.TargetGridColumn.ActualWidth;
			}
			if (column.Width.HasValue)
			{
				return column.Width.Value;
			}
			return defaultWidth;
		}

		private void ClearPlaceHolderColumns()
		{
			foreach (DataGridColumn item in _gc.DataGrid.Columns.Where((DataGridColumn x) => x is PlaceholderColumn).ToList())
			{
				_gc.DataGrid.Columns.Remove(item);
			}
		}

		private void AppendPlaceHolderColumns(bool fromInitialization)
		{
			int count = _gc.DataSource.Columns.Count;
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = count - 1;
			int? num3 = null;
			int? num4 = null;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (_loadedStates[i])
				{
					if (num3.HasValue && num4.HasValue)
					{
						AppendPlaceHolderColumn(num, num3.Value, num4.Value, fromInitialization);
						num4 = null;
						num3 = num4;
						num++;
					}
					num++;
					continue;
				}
				if (!num3.HasValue)
				{
					num3 = i;
				}
				num4 = i;
				if (i == num2)
				{
					AppendPlaceHolderColumn(num, num3.Value, num4.Value, fromInitialization);
					num4 = null;
					num3 = num4;
					num++;
				}
			}
		}

		private GridControlColumn CreateView(DataColumn column)
		{
			return new GridControlColumn(_gc, column);
		}

		private double CalculatePlaceHolderColumnWidth(int fromIndex, int toIndex, bool fromInitialization)
		{
			if (fromInitialization)
			{
				double num = _offsets[toIndex] - _offsets[fromIndex];
				DataColumn dataColumn = _gc.DataSource.Columns[toIndex];
				double num2 = (dataColumn.Width.HasValue ? dataColumn.Width.Value : _gc.DefaultColumnWidth);
				return num + num2;
			}
			double num3 = 0.0;
			double defaultColumnWidth = _gc.DefaultColumnWidth;
			ReadOnlyCollection<DataColumn> columns = _gc.DataSource.Columns;
			for (int i = fromIndex; i <= toIndex; i++)
			{
				DataColumn dataColumn2 = columns[i];
				num3 = ((!dataColumn2.Width.HasValue) ? (num3 + defaultColumnWidth) : (num3 + dataColumn2.Width.Value));
			}
			return num3;
		}

		private void AppendPlaceHolderColumn(int index, int leftColumnIndex, int rightColumnIndex, bool fromInitialization)
		{
			double pixels = CalculatePlaceHolderColumnWidth(leftColumnIndex, rightColumnIndex, fromInitialization);
			PlaceholderColumn item = new PlaceholderColumn(_gc, leftColumnIndex, rightColumnIndex)
			{
				Width = new DataGridLength(pixels)
			};
			_gc.DataGrid.Columns.Insert(index, item);
		}
	}
}
namespace AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Transposed
{
	public class TransposedDataColumn : DataColumn
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty DataItemProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DataItem", typeof(object), typeof(TransposedDataColumn), new PropertyMetadata(OnDataItemPropertyChanged));

		public object DataItem
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(DataItemProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DataItemProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public TransposedDataColumn()
		{
			base.CanSorting = false;
		}

		protected virtual void OnDataItemChanged(object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
		}

		private static void OnDataItemPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as TransposedDataColumn).OnDataItemChanged(e.OldValue, e.NewValue);
		}
	}
	public class TransposedDataRow : DataRow
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty LabelProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Label", typeof(object), typeof(TransposedDataRow), new PropertyMetadata(OnLabelPropertyChanged));

		public object Label
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(LabelProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(LabelProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public TransposedDataRow(TransposedDataTable dataTable, IEnumerable<DataColumn> columns)
			: base(dataTable, columns)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnLabelChanged(object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			RaiseIndexerPropertyChanged();
		}

		private static void OnLabelPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as TransposedDataRow).OnLabelChanged(e.OldValue, e.NewValue);
		}
	}
	public class TransposedDataTable : DataTable
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ItemsSourceProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemsSource", typeof(IEnumerable), typeof(TransposedDataTable), new PropertyMetadata(OnItemsSourcePropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty LabelsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Labels", typeof(IEnumerable), typeof(TransposedDataTable), new PropertyMetadata(OnLabelsPropertyChanged));

		private ObservableCollection<DataColumn> _columnsList;

		public IEnumerable ItemsSource
		{
			get
			{
				return (IEnumerable)GetValue(ItemsSourceProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ItemsSourceProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable Labels
		{
			get
			{
				return (IEnumerable)GetValue(LabelsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(LabelsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemsSourceChanged(IEnumerable oldValue, IEnumerable newValue)
		{
			if (oldValue is INotifyCollectionChanged notifyCollectionChanged)
			{
				notifyCollectionChanged.CollectionChanged -= ItemsSource_CollectionChanged;
			}
			if (newValue is INotifyCollectionChanged notifyCollectionChanged2)
			{
				notifyCollectionChanged2.CollectionChanged += ItemsSource_CollectionChanged;
			}
			ResetColumns();
			ResetRows();
			RefreshGrid();
		}

		private void ItemsSource_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			switch (e.Action)
			{
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add:
				if (e.NewItems == null)
				{
					break;
				}
				foreach (object newItem in e.NewItems)
				{
					_columnsList.Add(new TransposedDataColumn
					{
						DataItem = newItem
					});
				}
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Remove:
				if (_columnsList.Count > e.OldStartingIndex)
				{
					_columnsList.RemoveAt(e.OldStartingIndex);
				}
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Move:
				if (_columnsList.Count > e.OldStartingIndex && _columnsList.Count > e.NewStartingIndex)
				{
					_columnsList.Move(e.OldStartingIndex, e.NewStartingIndex);
				}
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace:
				if (_columnsList.Count > e.OldStartingIndex && e.NewItems != null && e.NewItems.Count > 0)
				{
					((TransposedDataColumn)_columnsList[e.OldStartingIndex]).DataItem = e.NewItems[0];
				}
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset:
				ResetColumns();
				ResetRows();
				break;
			}
			RefreshGrid();
		}

		protected virtual void OnLabelsChanged(IEnumerable oldValue, IEnumerable newValue)
		{
			if (oldValue is INotifyCollectionChanged notifyCollectionChanged)
			{
				notifyCollectionChanged.CollectionChanged -= Labels_CollectionChanged;
			}
			if (newValue is INotifyCollectionChanged notifyCollectionChanged2)
			{
				notifyCollectionChanged2.CollectionChanged += Labels_CollectionChanged;
			}
			ResetRows();
			RefreshGrid();
		}

		private void Labels_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			switch (e.Action)
			{
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add:
				if (e.NewItems == null)
				{
					break;
				}
				foreach (object newItem in e.NewItems)
				{
					base.Rows.Add(GenerateTransposedDataRow(newItem));
				}
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Remove:
				if (base.Rows.Count > e.OldStartingIndex)
				{
					base.Rows.RemoveAt(e.OldStartingIndex);
				}
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Move:
				if (base.Rows.Count > e.OldStartingIndex && base.Rows.Count > e.NewStartingIndex)
				{
					base.Rows.Move(e.OldStartingIndex, e.NewStartingIndex);
				}
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace:
				if (base.Rows.Count > e.OldStartingIndex && e.NewItems != null && e.NewItems.Count > 0)
				{
					((TransposedDataRow)base.Rows[e.OldStartingIndex]).Label = e.NewItems[0];
				}
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset:
				ResetRows();
				break;
			}
			RefreshGrid();
		}

		private void ResetColumns()
		{
			ObservableCollection<DataColumn> observableCollection = CreateColumns();
			if (_columnsList != null)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < observableCollection.Count && i < _columnsList.Count; i++)
				{
					if (_columnsList[i].RenderedWidth > 0.0)
					{
						observableCollection[i].Width = _columnsList[i].RenderedWidth;
					}
					observableCollection[i].Visibility = _columnsList[i].Visibility;
				}
			}
			_columnsList = observableCollection;
			base.Columns = new ReadOnlyCollection<DataColumn>(_columnsList);
			if (base.Columns.Count > 0)
			{
				base.Columns[0].IsFrozen = true;
			}
		}

		private void ResetRows()
		{
			base.Rows.Clear();
			foreach (TransposedDataRow item in CreateRows())
			{
				base.Rows.Add(item);
			}
		}

		private void RefreshGrid()
		{
			if (base.GridControl != null)
			{
				base.GridControl.RefreshDataInternal(clearRowsData: true);
				base.GridControl.InvalidateGridWidth();
			}
		}

		protected virtual ObservableCollection<DataColumn> CreateColumns()
		{
			ObservableCollection<DataColumn> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<DataColumn>();
			observableCollection.Add(new TransposedDataColumn
			{
				IsReadOnly = true,
				CellsBackground = SystemColors.ControlBrush,
				CanSorting = false
			});
			if (ItemsSource != null)
			{
				foreach (object item in ItemsSource)
				{
					TransposedDataColumn transposedDataColumn = GenerateTransposedDataColumn(item);
					if (transposedDataColumn != null)
					{
						observableCollection.Add(transposedDataColumn);
					}
				}
			}
			return observableCollection;
		}

		protected virtual IEnumerable<TransposedDataRow> CreateRows()
		{
			if (Labels == null)
			{
				yield break;
			}
			foreach (object label in Labels)
			{
				yield return GenerateTransposedDataRow(label);
			}
		}

		protected virtual TransposedDataColumn GenerateTransposedDataColumn(object data)
		{
			return new TransposedDataColumn
			{
				DataItem = data
			};
		}

		protected virtual TransposedDataRow GenerateTransposedDataRow(object label)
		{
			return new TransposedDataRow(this, base.Columns)
			{
				Label = label
			};
		}

		protected sealed override IDataCell OnCellValueQueried(DataRow row, DataColumn column)
		{
			TransposedDataRow transposedDataRow = row as TransposedDataRow;
			TransposedDataColumn transposedDataColumn = column as TransposedDataColumn;
			if (transposedDataRow != null && transposedDataColumn != null)
			{
				if (transposedDataColumn.DataItem == null)
				{
					return CreateLabelCell(transposedDataRow, transposedDataColumn);
				}
				return CreateDataCell(transposedDataRow, transposedDataColumn);
			}
			return base.OnCellValueQueried(row, column);
		}

		protected virtual IDataCell CreateLabelCell(TransposedDataRow row, TransposedDataColumn column)
		{
			DataCellText dataCellText = new DataCellText();
			if (row.Label != null)
			{
				dataCellText.Value = row.Label.ToString();
			}
			return dataCellText;
		}

		protected virtual IDataCell CreateDataCell(TransposedDataRow row, TransposedDataColumn column)
		{
			return null;
		}

		private static void OnItemsSourcePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as TransposedDataTable).OnItemsSourceChanged(e.OldValue as IEnumerable, e.NewValue as IEnumerable);
		}

		private static void OnLabelsPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as TransposedDataTable).OnLabelsChanged(e.OldValue as IEnumerable, e.NewValue as IEnumerable);
		}
	}
}
namespace AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Printing
{
	internal static class PrintHelper
	{
		private const double MinimumMagin = 15.0;

		public static PageSettings DefaultPageSettings => new PageSettings(new PrinterSettings());

		public static PageMediaSize ConvertPaperSizeToPageMediaSize(PaperSize paperSize, IEnumerable<PageMediaSize> pageMediaSizeCapability)
		{
			if (paperSize == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("paperSize");
			}
			if (pageMediaSizeCapability == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("pageMediaSizeCapability");
			}
			double y = ConvertDotsToPixels(paperSize.Width);
			double y2 = ConvertDotsToPixels(paperSize.Height);
			foreach (PageMediaSize item in pageMediaSizeCapability)
			{
				if (item.Width.HasValue && item.Height.HasValue && IsRoughlyEqual(item.Width.Value, y) && IsRoughlyEqual(item.Height.Value, y2))
				{
					return item;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static FixedDocument CreateFixedDocument(GridControl gc, Rect printableArea, Size paperSize, Thickness? margin, bool landscape, string title)
		{
			Size size = (landscape ? new Size(paperSize.Height, paperSize.Width) : new Size(paperSize.Width, paperSize.Height));
			Rect adjustedPrintableArea = (landscape ? new Rect(printableArea.Top, printableArea.Left, printableArea.Height, printableArea.Width) : printableArea);
			FixedDocument fixedDocument = new FixedDocument();
			fixedDocument.DocumentPaginator.PageSize = size;
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in GeneratePrintingElements(gc, size, adjustedPrintableArea, margin, title))
			{
				FixedPage child = new FixedPage
				{
					Width = size.Width,
					Height = size.Height,
					Children = { (UIElement)item }
				};
				fixedDocument.Pages.Add(new PageContent
				{
					Child = child
				});
			}
			return fixedDocument;
		}

		private static IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> GeneratePrintingElements(GridControl gc, Size adjustedPaperSize, Rect adjustedPrintableArea, Thickness? margin, string title)
		{
			Thickness borderPadding = CalculateBorderPadding(adjustedPrintableArea, adjustedPaperSize, margin);
			borderPadding = FixThickness(borderPadding);
			Size availablePrintSize = new Size(adjustedPaperSize.Width - borderPadding.Left - borderPadding.Right, adjustedPaperSize.Height - borderPadding.Top - borderPadding.Bottom);
			IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> enumerable = gc.GenerateGenericPrintPages(availablePrintSize, 0, 0, title);
			if (enumerable == null)
			{
				yield break;
			}
			foreach (FrameworkElement item in enumerable)
			{
				item.HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left;
				item.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top;
				yield return new Border
				{
					Width = adjustedPaperSize.Width,
					Height = adjustedPaperSize.Height,
					Padding = borderPadding,
					Child = item,
					Background = Brushes.White
				};
			}
		}

		private static Thickness CalculateBorderPadding(Rect adjustedPrintableArea, Size adjustedPaperSize, Thickness? margin)
		{
			double num = (double.IsNaN(adjustedPrintableArea.Left) ? 0.0 : adjustedPrintableArea.Left);
			double num2 = (double.IsNaN(adjustedPrintableArea.Top) ? 0.0 : adjustedPrintableArea.Top);
			double num3 = (double.IsNaN(adjustedPrintableArea.Width) ? adjustedPaperSize.Width : adjustedPrintableArea.Width);
			double num4 = (double.IsNaN(adjustedPrintableArea.Height) ? adjustedPaperSize.Height : adjustedPrintableArea.Height);
			double num5 = Math.Max(num, 15.0);
			double num6 = Math.Max(num2, 15.0);
			double num7 = Math.Max(adjustedPaperSize.Width - num3 - num, 15.0);
			double num8 = Math.Max(adjustedPaperSize.Height - num4 - num2, 15.0);
			if (margin.HasValue)
			{
				Thickness value = margin.Value;
				double num9 = Math.Max(num5, value.Left);
				double num10 = Math.Max(num6, value.Top);
				double num11 = Math.Max(num7, value.Right);
				double num12 = Math.Max(num8, value.Bottom);
				if (num9 + num11 >= adjustedPaperSize.Width)
				{
					num9 = num5;
					num11 = num7;
				}
				if (num10 + num12 >= adjustedPaperSize.Height)
				{
					num10 = num6;
					num12 = num8;
				}
				return new Thickness(num9, num10, num11, num12);
			}
			return new Thickness(num5, num6, num7, num8);
		}

		public static double ConvertDotsToPixels(double dots)
		{
			return dots * 0.96;
		}

		public static Thickness ConvertMargins(Margins mgs)
		{
			return new Thickness(ConvertDotsToPixels(mgs.Left), ConvertDotsToPixels(mgs.Top), ConvertDotsToPixels(mgs.Right), ConvertDotsToPixels(mgs.Bottom));
		}

		private static bool IsRoughlyEqual(double x, double y)
		{
			return Math.Abs(x - y) <= 1.0;
		}

		private static Thickness FixThickness(Thickness tn)
		{
			Thickness result = tn;
			if (double.IsInfinity(tn.Left) || double.IsNaN(tn.Left))
			{
				result.Left = 0.0;
			}
			if (double.IsInfinity(tn.Right) || double.IsNaN(tn.Right))
			{
				result.Right = 0.0;
			}
			if (double.IsInfinity(tn.Top) || double.IsNaN(tn.Top))
			{
				result.Top = 0.0;
			}
			if (double.IsInfinity(tn.Bottom) || double.IsNaN(tn.Bottom))
			{
				result.Bottom = 0.0;
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
}

	public class GridNameDialog : Window, IComponentConnector
	{

		{

				return;

	public interface INameProvider
	{
		string GenerateNewNameForAdding();

		string GenereteNewNameForCloning(string baseName);

		string GenerateNewNameForRenaming(string nameToChange);
	}
	public class DefaultNameProvider : INameProvider
	{
		private readonly GridControlPanel _panel;

		private string _newGridNamePattern;

		public string NewGridNamePattern
		{
			get
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_newGridNamePattern))
				{
					return AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridControlPanel_NewGrid_DefaultName;
				}
				return _newGridNamePattern;
			}
			set
			{
				_newGridNamePattern = value;
			}
		}

		public string DescriptionLabel { get; set; }

		public DefaultNameProvider(GridControlPanel panel)
		{
			if (panel == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("panel");
			}
			_panel = panel;
		}

		public string GenerateNewNameForAdding()
		{
			return QueryForNewGridName(GetNewGridName());
		}

		public string GenereteNewNameForCloning(string baseName)
		{
			return QueryForNewGridName(GetClonedGridName(baseName + " 1"));
		}

		public string GenerateNewNameForRenaming(string nameToChange)
		{
			return QueryForNewGridName(nameToChange, nameToChange);
		}

		private string GetNewGridName()
		{
			int num = 1;
			string newName;
			while (true)
			{
				newName = string.Format(NewGridNamePattern, num);
				if (_panel.GridControls.FirstOrDefault((GridControl x) => string.Equals(x.Title, newName, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase)) == null)
				{
					break;
				}
				num++;
			}
			return newName;
		}

		private string GetClonedGridName(string testGridName)
		{
			if (_panel.GridControls.FirstOrDefault((GridControl x) => string.Equals(x.Title, testGridName, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase)) == null)
			{
				return testGridName;
			}
			int num = 1;
			string newGridName;
			while (true)
			{
				newGridName = testGridName + num.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
				if (_panel.GridControls.FirstOrDefault((GridControl x) => string.Equals(x.Title, newGridName, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase)) == null)
				{
					break;
				}
				num++;
			}
			return newGridName;
		}

		private string QueryForNewGridName(string defaultName, string nameToChange = null)
		{
			GridNameDialog gridNameDialog = new GridNameDialog((string newName) => CheckNewGridName(newName, nameToChange))
			{
				Owner = Window.GetWindow(_panel),
				NewGridName = defaultName,
				DescriptionLabel = DescriptionLabel
			};
			bool? flag = gridNameDialog.ShowDialog();
			if (flag.HasValue && flag.Value)
			{
				return gridNameDialog.NewGridName;
			}
			return null;
		}

		private string CheckNewGridName(string newGridName, string nameToChange)
		{
			if (newGridName.Length > 21)
			{
				return AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridNameDialog_NameTooLong_Message;
			}
			if (string.Equals(newGridName, nameToChange, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (_panel.GridControls.Where((GridControl grid) => string.Equals(grid.Title, newGridName, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase)).Count() > 0)
			{
				return AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridNameDialog_InvalidName_Message;
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
}

	public class CellDraggingAnchor : Control
	{
		static CellDraggingAnchor()
		{
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(CellDraggingAnchor), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(CellDraggingAnchor)));
		}

		public static bool IsInDraggingAnchor(object eventSource)
		{
			if (eventSource == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			for (DependencyObject dependencyObject = eventSource as DependencyObject; dependencyObject != null; dependencyObject = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject))
			{
				if (dependencyObject is CellDraggingAnchor)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public static class GridClipboardHelper
	{
		private static class InternalClipboardWrapper
		{
			private const int DefaultTryTimes = 2;

			private const int DefaultRetryInterval = 0;

			public static void Clear()
			{
				TimesExecution.TryExecute(Clipboard.Clear);
			}

			public static bool ContainsText()
			{
				return TimesExecution.TryExecute(() => Clipboard.ContainsText());
			}

			public static bool ContainsText(TextDataFormat format)
			{
				return TimesExecution.TryExecute(() => Clipboard.ContainsText(format));

			{
				return TimesExecution.TryExecute(() => Clipboard.ContainsData(format));

		internal static bool CanCopy(GridControl gc)
		{
			return gc.HasSelection;
		}

		internal static void DoDefaultCopy(GridControlCopyEventArgs e)
		{
			e.ClipboardContents.Add(DataFormats.Text, e.GenerateTextToCopy());
		}

		internal static bool CanPaste(GridControl gc, IList<IList<string>> data)
		{
			if (gc.HasSelection && CanPasteFromClipboard(gc.DataSource, gc.SelectedRows, gc.SelectedColumns, gc.TotalOrderedSelectedCells, data))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal static void DoDefaultPaste(GridControl gc, PasteTargetAreaInfo pasteInfo)
		{
			IEnumerable<PasteCellInfo> enumerable = pasteInfo.ConvertPastingTarget(gc);
			foreach (PasteCellInfo item in enumerable)
			{
				if (CanPasteToCell(item.CellInfo, item.Text))
				{
					PasteTextToCell(gc, item.CellInfo.Data, item.Text, item.RowIndex == pasteInfo.StartRowIndex + pasteInfo.ClipboardTexts.Count - 1);
				}
			}
			if (gc.SelectionUnit == DataGridSelectionUnit.FullRow)
			{
				return;
			}
			gc.InternalSelectionHelper.LockSelectionChanged();
			gc.InternalSelectionHelper.DeselectAll();
			foreach (PasteCellInfo item2 in enumerable)
			{
				gc.SelectCell(item2.CellInfo);
			}
			gc.InternalSelectionHelper.UnlockSelectionChanged(raiseEvent: true);
		}

		internal static void DoDefaultPasteOneByOne(GridControl gc, PasteTargetAreaInfo pasteInfo)
		{
			IEnumerable<PasteCellInfo> enumerable = pasteInfo.ConvertPastingTarget(gc);
			foreach (PasteCellInfo item in enumerable)
			{
				if (item.RowIndex >= gc.DataSource.Rows.Count || item.ColumnIndex >= gc.DataSource.Columns.Count)
				{
					continue;
				}
				DataRow dataRow = gc.BoundRows.ElementAt(item.RowIndex);
				if (dataRow == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				DataColumn dataColumn = gc.ColumnFromDisplayIndex(item.ColumnIndex);
				if (dataColumn == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				DataCellInfo dci = new DataCellInfo(dataRow, dataColumn);
				if (CanPasteToCell(dci, item.Text))
				{
					object value = dci.Data.Value;
					PasteTextToCell(gc, dci.Data, item.Text);
					if (object.Equals(value, dci.Data.Value))
					{
						dci.Data.InvalidateDisplayText();
					}
				}
				gc.Dispatcher.Invoke(delegate
				{
				}, DispatcherPriority.DataBind);
			}
			gc.InternalSelectionHelper.LockSelectionChanged();
			gc.InternalSelectionHelper.DeselectAll();
			foreach (PasteCellInfo item2 in enumerable)
			{
				if (item2.RowIndex >= gc.DataSource.Rows.Count || item2.ColumnIndex >= gc.DataSource.Columns.Count)
				{
					continue;
				}
				DataRow dataRow2 = gc.BoundRows.ElementAt(item2.RowIndex);
				if (dataRow2 != null)
				{
					DataColumn dataColumn2 = gc.ColumnFromDisplayIndex(item2.ColumnIndex);
					if (dataColumn2 != null)
					{
						DataCellInfo cell = new DataCellInfo(dataRow2, dataColumn2);
						gc.SelectCell(cell);
					}
				}
			}
			gc.InternalSelectionHelper.UnlockSelectionChanged(raiseEvent: true);
		}

		private static void PasteTextToCell(GridControl gc, IDataCell cell, string text, bool isLastRowInClipboard = false)
		{
			CellValuePastingEventArgs e = new CellValuePastingEventArgs(cell, text, isLastRowInClipboard);
			gc.OnCellValuePasting(e);
			if (!e.Handled)
			{
				cell.Parse(text);
			}
			else
			{
				gc.InvalidateData();
			}
		}

		internal static void ProcessClipboardKeyDown(this IInputElement target, KeyEventArgs keyEventArgs, RoutedCommand copyCommand, RoutedCommand pasteCommand = null)
		{
			if (Keyboard.Modifiers == ModifierKeys.Control)
			{
				if (keyEventArgs.Key == Key.C && copyCommand.CanExecute(null, target))
				{
					copyCommand.Execute(null, target);
				}
				if (pasteCommand != null && keyEventArgs.Key == Key.V && pasteCommand.CanExecute(null, target))
				{
					pasteCommand.Execute(null, target);
				}
			}
		}

		public static string GenerateTextToCopy(DataTable table, IEnumerable<DataRow> rows, IEnumerable<DataColumn> columns, IEnumerable<DataCellInfo> cells, bool includeHeader, Func<DataColumn, string> columnHeaderTextFunc = null, Dictionary<DataColumn, string> columnPrefixes = null, List<IGridCellData> cellData = null, bool removeLastRow = false)
		{
			if (table == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("table");
			}
			if (rows == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("rows");
			}
			if (columns == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("columns");
			}
			if (cells == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("cells");
			}
			List<DataRow> list = rows.ToList();
			List<DataColumn> selectedColumns = columns.ToList();
			List<DataCellInfo> list2 = cells.ToList();
			List<DataColumn> orderedIncludedColumns = GetOrderedIncludedColumns(table, list, selectedColumns, list2);
			List<DataRow> orderedIncludedRows = GetOrderedIncludedRows(table, list, selectedColumns, list2);
			if (removeLastRow)
			{
				orderedIncludedRows.RemoveAt(orderedIncludedRows.Count - 1);
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			if (includeHeader)
			{
				AppendColumnHeaders(stringBuilder, orderedIncludedColumns, columnHeaderTextFunc, columnPrefixes);
			}
			List<DataCellInfo> list3 = new List<DataCellInfo>();
			for (int i = 0; i < orderedIncludedRows.Count; i++)
			{
				DataRow dataRow = orderedIncludedRows[i];
				bool flag = list.Contains(dataRow);
				for (int j = 0; j < orderedIncludedColumns.Count; j++)
				{
					DataColumn dataColumn = orderedIncludedColumns[j];
					DataCellInfo item = new DataCellInfo(dataRow, dataColumn);
					if (flag || dataColumn.IsColumnSelected || list2.Contains(item))
					{
						list3.Add(item);
					}
				}
			}
			PreparingCellValuesEventArgs e = new PreparingCellValuesEventArgs(list3);
			table.CallPreparingCellValues(e);
			int num = list2.Count;
			if (num == 0)
			{
				num = orderedIncludedColumns.Count * orderedIncludedRows.Count;
			}
			int k = 0;
			if (cellData != null && cellData.Count > 0 && num < cellData.Count)
			{
				for (; cellData.Count > k && cellData[k].RowIndex == 0; k++)
				{
				}
				if (e.Cells.Count > cellData.Count - k)
				{
					cellData = null;
				}
			}
			List<DataRow> list4 = new List<DataRow>();
			if (table.GridControl != null && table.GridControl.BoundRows != null)
			{
				list4 = table.GridControl.BoundRows.ToList();
			}
			for (int l = 0; l < orderedIncludedRows.Count; l++)
			{
				DataRow dataRow2 = orderedIncludedRows[l];
				bool flag2 = list.Contains(dataRow2);
				bool flag3 = true;
				StringBuilder stringBuilder2 = new StringBuilder();
				if (l > 0 || (l == 0 && stringBuilder.Length > 0))
				{
					if (l > 0 && list4.Any())
					{
						int num2 = list4.IndexOf(orderedIncludedRows[l]);
						int num3 = list4.IndexOf(orderedIncludedRows[l - 1]);
						int num4 = num2 - num3;
						if (num2 > 0 && num3 >= 0 && num4 > 1)
						{
							for (int m = 0; m < num4; m++)
							{
								stringBuilder2.AppendLine();
							}
						}
						else
						{
							stringBuilder2.AppendLine();
						}
					}
					else
					{
						stringBuilder2.AppendLine();
					}
				}
				for (int n = 0; n < orderedIncludedColumns.Count; n++)
				{
					DataColumn dataColumn2 = orderedIncludedColumns[n];
					DataCellInfo dataCellInfo = new DataCellInfo(dataRow2, dataColumn2);
					if (flag2 || dataColumn2.IsColumnSelected || list2.Contains(dataCellInfo))
					{
						if (cellData == null)
						{
							object obj = (e.Values.ContainsKey(dataCellInfo) ? e.Values[dataCellInfo] : GridControlHelper.GetDirectValueAt(dataRow2, dataColumn2));
							if (obj != null)
							{
								if (columnPrefixes != null && columnPrefixes.ContainsKey(dataColumn2))
								{
									stringBuilder2.Append(columnPrefixes[dataColumn2]);
								}
								stringBuilder2.Append(GetFormattedText(obj.ToString()));
								flag3 = false;
							}
						}
						else
						{
							string textValue = cellData[k].TextValue;
							if (columnPrefixes != null && columnPrefixes.ContainsKey(dataColumn2))
							{
								stringBuilder2.Append(columnPrefixes[dataColumn2]);
							}
							stringBuilder2.Append(GetFormattedText(textValue));
							flag3 = false;
							k++;
						}
					}
					if (n >= orderedIncludedColumns.Count - 1)
					{
						continue;
					}
					int num5 = orderedIncludedColumns[n + 1].Index - orderedIncludedColumns[n].Index;
					if (num5 > 1)
					{
						int num6 = 0;
						int index = orderedIncludedColumns[n].Index;
						for (int num7 = 1; num7 < num5; num7++)
						{
							int tabColumnIndex = index + num7;
							DataColumn dataColumn3 = table.Columns.FirstOrDefault((DataColumn p) => p.Index == tabColumnIndex);
							if ((dataColumn3 != null && dataColumn3.Visibility == Visibility.Collapsed) || dataColumn3.Visibility == Visibility.Hidden)
							{
								num6++;
							}
						}
						num5 -= num6;
						for (int num8 = 0; num8 < num5; num8++)
						{
							stringBuilder2.Append('\t');
						}
					}
					else
					{
						stringBuilder2.Append('\t');
					}
				}
				if (l < orderedIncludedRows.Count - 1 || !flag3)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(stringBuilder2);
				}
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		public static string GenerateTextToCopyAndRemoveEmptyRowsColumns(DataTable table, IEnumerable<DataRow> rows, IEnumerable<DataColumn> columns, IEnumerable<DataCellInfo> cells, bool includeHeader, Func<DataColumn, string> columnHeaderTextFunc = null, Dictionary<DataColumn, string> columnPrefixes = null)
		{
			if (table == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("table");
			}
			if (rows == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("rows");
			}
			if (columns == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("columns");
			}
			if (cells == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("cells");
			}
			List<DataRow> list = rows.ToList();
			List<DataColumn> selectedColumns = columns.ToList();
			List<DataCellInfo> list2 = cells.ToList();
			List<DataColumn> orderedIncludedColumns = GetOrderedIncludedColumns(table, list, selectedColumns, list2);
			List<DataRow> orderedIncludedRows = GetOrderedIncludedRows(table, list, selectedColumns, list2);
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			if (includeHeader)
			{
				AppendColumnHeaders(stringBuilder, orderedIncludedColumns, columnHeaderTextFunc, columnPrefixes);
			}
			List<DataCellInfo> list3 = new List<DataCellInfo>();
			for (int i = 0; i < orderedIncludedRows.Count; i++)
			{
				DataRow dataRow = orderedIncludedRows[i];
				bool flag = list.Contains(dataRow);
				for (int j = 0; j < orderedIncludedColumns.Count; j++)
				{
					DataColumn dataColumn = orderedIncludedColumns[j];
					DataCellInfo item = new DataCellInfo(dataRow, dataColumn);
					if (flag || dataColumn.IsColumnSelected || list2.Contains(item))
					{
						list3.Add(item);
					}
				}
			}
			PreparingCellValuesEventArgs e = new PreparingCellValuesEventArgs(list3);
			table.CallPreparingCellValues(e);
			for (int k = 0; k < orderedIncludedRows.Count; k++)
			{
				DataRow dataRow2 = orderedIncludedRows[k];
				bool flag2 = list.Contains(dataRow2);
				bool flag3 = true;
				StringBuilder stringBuilder2 = new StringBuilder();
				if (k > 0 || (k == 0 && stringBuilder.Length > 0))
				{
					stringBuilder2.AppendLine();
				}
				for (int l = 0; l < orderedIncludedColumns.Count; l++)
				{
					DataColumn dataColumn2 = orderedIncludedColumns[l];
					DataCellInfo dataCellInfo = new DataCellInfo(dataRow2, dataColumn2);
					if (flag2 || dataColumn2.IsColumnSelected || list2.Contains(dataCellInfo))
					{
						object obj = (e.Values.ContainsKey(dataCellInfo) ? e.Values[dataCellInfo] : GridControlHelper.GetDirectValueAt(dataRow2, dataColumn2));
						if (obj != null)
						{
							if (columnPrefixes != null && columnPrefixes.ContainsKey(dataColumn2))
							{
								stringBuilder2.Append(columnPrefixes[dataColumn2]);
							}
							stringBuilder2.Append(GetFormattedText(obj.ToString()));
							flag3 = false;
						}
					}
					if (l < orderedIncludedColumns.Count - 1)
					{
						stringBuilder2.Append('\t');
					}
				}
				if (k >= orderedIncludedRows.Count - 1 && flag3)
				{
					continue;
				}
				string text = stringBuilder2.ToString();
				if (text.Replace("\r\n", string.Empty).Replace("\t", string.Empty).Length > 0)
				{
					if (text.EndsWith("\t") && text.Length > 1)
					{
						text = text.Substring(0, text.Length - 1);
					}
					stringBuilder.Append(text);
				}
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		internal static List<DataColumn> GetOrderedIncludedColumns(DataTable table, List<DataRow> selectedRows, List<DataColumn> selectedColumns, List<DataCellInfo> selectedCells)
		{
			if (table == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("table");
			}
			if (selectedRows == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("selectedRows");
			}
			if (selectedColumns == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("selectedColumns");
			}
			if (selectedCells == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("selectedCells");
			}
			if (selectedRows.Count > 0)
			{
				return (from x in table.Columns
					where x.Visibility == Visibility.Visible
					orderby x.LogicalDisplayIndex
					select x).ToList();
			}
			List<DataColumn> list = new List<DataColumn>(selectedColumns);
			foreach (DataCellInfo selectedCell in selectedCells)
			{
				if (!list.Contains(selectedCell.Column))
				{
					list.Add(selectedCell.Column);
				}
			}
			return list.OrderBy((DataColumn x) => x.LogicalDisplayIndex).ToList();
		}

		internal static List<DataRow> GetOrderedIncludedRows(DataTable table, List<DataRow> selectedRows, List<DataColumn> selectedColumns, List<DataCellInfo> selectedCells)
		{
			if (table == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("table");
			}
			if (selectedRows == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("selectedRows");
			}
			if (selectedColumns == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("selectedColumns");
			}
			if (selectedCells == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("selectedCells");
			}
			if (selectedColumns.Count > 0)
			{
				if (table.GridControl != null)
				{
					if (table.GridControl.BoundRows == null)
					{
						return new List<DataRow>();
					}
					return table.GridControl.BoundRows.ToList();
				}
				return table.Rows.ToList();
			}
			List<DataRow> list = new List<DataRow>(selectedRows);
			foreach (DataCellInfo selectedCell in selectedCells)
			{
				if (!list.Contains(selectedCell.Row))
				{
					list.Add(selectedCell.Row);
				}
			}
			if (table.GridControl != null)
			{
				List<DataRow> boundRows = table.GridControl.BoundRows.ToList();
				return list.OrderBy((DataRow x) => boundRows.IndexOf(x)).ToList();
			}
			return list.OrderBy((DataRow x) => table.Rows.IndexOf(x)).ToList();
		}

		private static void AppendColumnHeaders(StringBuilder sb, IList<DataColumn> selectedColumns, Func<DataColumn, string> columnHeaderTextFunc, Dictionary<DataColumn, string> columnPrefixes)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < selectedColumns.Count; i++)
			{
				DataColumn dataColumn = selectedColumns[i];
				string text = dataColumn.Header;
				if (columnHeaderTextFunc != null)
				{
					text = columnHeaderTextFunc(dataColumn);
				}
				if (columnPrefixes != null && columnPrefixes.ContainsKey(dataColumn) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
				{
					sb.Append(columnPrefixes[dataColumn]);
				}
				sb.Append(GetFormattedText(text));
				if (i < selectedColumns.Count - 1)
				{
					sb.Append('\t');
				}
			}
		}

		private static string GetFormattedText(string text)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && (text.Contains('\r') || text.Contains('\n')))
			{
				if (text.Contains("\""))
				{
					text = text.Replace("\"", "\"\"");
				}
				return "\"" + text + "\"";
			}
			return text;
		}

		private static bool CanPasteFromClipboard(DataTable table, IEnumerable<DataRow> selectedRows, IEnumerable<DataColumn> selectedColumns, IEnumerable<DataCellInfo> totalOrderedSelectedCells, IList<IList<string>> data)
		{
			if (data.Count > 0)
			{
				if (!table.Columns.Any((DataColumn x) => !x.IsReadOnly))
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (selectedRows.Any())
				{
					return table.Columns.Any((DataColumn x) => !x.IsReadOnly);
				}
				if (selectedColumns.Any((DataColumn x) => !x.IsReadOnly))
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (data.Count == 1 && data[0].Count == 1)
				{
					string text = data[0][0];
					foreach (DataCellInfo totalOrderedSelectedCell in totalOrderedSelectedCells)
					{
						if (CanPasteToCell(totalOrderedSelectedCell, text))
						{
							return true;
						}
					}
					return false;
				}
				IEnumerable<PasteCellInfo> enumerable = PasteTargetAreaInfo.Create(table.GridControl, data).ConvertPastingTarget(table.GridControl);
				Stopwatch stopwatch = new Stopwatch();
				stopwatch.Start();
				foreach (PasteCellInfo item in enumerable)
				{
					if (CanPasteToCell(item.CellInfo, item.Text) || stopwatch.ElapsedMilliseconds > 500)
					{
						stopwatch.Stop();
						return true;
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static IList<IList<string>> GetPlainTextsFromClipboard()
		{
			string text = InternalClipboardWrapper.GetText(TextDataFormat.Text);
			if (text != null)
			{
				if (text.LastOrDefault() == '\n')
				{
					text = text.Substring(0, text.Length - 1);
				}
				if (text.LastOrDefault() == '\r')
				{
					text = text.Substring(0, text.Length - 1);
				}
			}
			int clipboardHeaderLinesCount = GetClipboardHeaderLinesCount();
			IList<IList<string>> list = SplitIntoStringTable(text);
			if (clipboardHeaderLinesCount > 0 && list.Count > clipboardHeaderLinesCount)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < clipboardHeaderLinesCount; i++)
				{
					list.RemoveAt(0);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		private static int GetClipboardHeaderLinesCount()
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			object data = InternalClipboardWrapper.GetData("DatagridMetadata");
			if (data is string text2)
			{
				text = text2;
			}
			else if (data is Stream stream)
			{
				using StreamReader streamReader = new StreamReader(stream);
				text = streamReader.ReadToEnd();
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				int num = text.IndexOf('=');
				if (num > 0 && num < text.Length - 1 && int.TryParse(text.Substring(num + 1), out var result))
				{
					return result;
				}
			}
			return 0;
		}

		public static IList<IList<string>> SplitIntoStringTable(string input)
		{
			IList<IList<string>> list = new List<IList<string>>();
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(input))
			{
				string[] array = input.Split('\n');
				foreach (string text in array)
				{
					if (text == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					IList<string> list2 = new List<string>();
					string[] array2 = text.Split('\t');
					foreach (string text2 in array2)
					{
						if (text2 == null)
						{
							list2.Add(text2);
						}
						else
						{
							list2.Add(text2.Trim());
						}
					}
					list.Add(list2);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		private static bool CanPasteToCell(DataCellInfo dci, string text)
		{
			IDataCell data = dci.Data;
			if (data != null && !data.IsReadOnly)
			{
				return data.CanParse(text);
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal static bool CanCut(GridControl gc)
		{
			if (gc.HasSelection)
			{
				if (!gc.DataSource.Columns.Any((DataColumn x) => !x.IsReadOnly))
				{
					return false;
				}
				foreach (DataCellInfo totalSelectedCell in gc.TotalSelectedCells)
				{
					if (!totalSelectedCell.Column.IsReadOnly)
					{
						IDataCell data = totalSelectedCell.Data;
						if (data != null && !data.IsReadOnly && data.ValueType != DataRow.NullableBooleanType && (data.ItemsSource == null || data.IsComboEditable))
						{
							return true;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal static void DefaultClearDataAfterCopyForCut(GridControl gc)
		{
			foreach (DataCellInfo item in gc.TotalOrderedSelectedCells.ToList())
			{
				IDataCell data = item.Data;
				if (data != null && !data.IsReadOnly && data.ValueType != DataRow.NullableBooleanType && (data.ItemsSource == null || data.IsComboEditable))
				{
					data.ClearValue();
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public static class TimesExecution
	{
		public static T TryExecute<T>(Func<T> func)
		{
			T result = default(T);
			TryExecute(delegate
			{
				result = func();
			});
			return result;
		}

		public static void TryExecute(Action action, int tryTimes = 2)
		{
			if (action == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = Math.Max(1, tryTimes);
			for (int i = 1; i <= num; i++)
			{
				try
				{
					action();
					break;
				}
				catch
				{
					if (i < num)
					{
						Thread.Sleep(0);
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	internal class PasteCellInfo
	{
		public int RowIndex { get; set; }

		public int ColumnIndex { get; set; }

		public DataCellInfo CellInfo { get; set; }

		public string Text { get; set; }
	}
	public class NumberSelectionInfo
	{
		public int StartRowIndex { get; set; }

		public int StartColumnIndex { get; set; }

		public int SelectedRowsCount { get; set; }

		public int SelectedColumnsCount { get; set; }
	}
	internal class PasteTargetAreaInfo
	{
		public int StartRowIndex { get; set; }

		public int StartColumnIndex { get; set; }

		public int SelectedRowsCount { get; set; }

		public int SelectedColumnsCount { get; set; }

		public IList<IList<string>> ClipboardTexts { get; set; }

		public IEnumerable<PasteCellInfo> ConvertPastingTarget(GridControl gc)
		{
			List<PasteCellInfo> list = new List<PasteCellInfo>();
			int count = ClipboardTexts.Count;
			int num = ((count > 0) ? ClipboardTexts[0].Count : 0);
			if (count > 0 && num > 0)
			{
				int num2 = CalculateTargetPastingNumber(count, SelectedRowsCount);
				int num3 = CalculateTargetPastingNumber(num, SelectedColumnsCount);
				List<DataRow> list2 = ((gc.BoundRows != null) ? gc.BoundRows.ToList() : new List<DataRow>());
				List<DataColumn> list3 = (from x in gc.DataSource.Columns
					where x.Visibility == Visibility.Visible
					orderby x.LogicalDisplayIndex
					select x).ToList();
				for (int num4 = 0; num4 < num2 && StartRowIndex + num4 <= list2.Count - 1; num4++)
				{
					IList<string> list4 = ClipboardTexts[num4 % count];
					DataRow row = list2[StartRowIndex + num4];
					for (int num5 = 0; num5 < num3 && StartColumnIndex + num5 <= list3.Count - 1; num5++)
					{
						if (num5 % num < list4.Count)
						{
							string text = list4[num5 % num];
							DataColumn column = list3[StartColumnIndex + num5];
							list.Add(new PasteCellInfo
							{
								RowIndex = StartRowIndex + num4,
								ColumnIndex = StartColumnIndex + num5,
								CellInfo = new DataCellInfo(row, column),
								Text = text
							});
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		public static PasteTargetAreaInfo Create(GridControl gc, IList<IList<string>> clipboardTexts)
		{
			if (!gc.HasSelection)
			{
				return CreateEmpty(clipboardTexts);
			}
			List<DataColumn> list = new List<DataColumn>();
			List<DataRow> list2 = new List<DataRow>();
			foreach (DataCellInfo totalOrderedSelectedCell in gc.TotalOrderedSelectedCells)
			{
				if (!list.Contains(totalOrderedSelectedCell.Column))
				{
					list.Add(totalOrderedSelectedCell.Column);
				}
				if (!list2.Contains(totalOrderedSelectedCell.Row))
				{
					list2.Add(totalOrderedSelectedCell.Row);
				}
			}
			if (list.Count == 0)
			{
				return CreateEmpty(clipboardTexts);
			}
			int num = gc.BoundRows.ToList().IndexOf(list2[0]);
			if (num < 0)
			{
				return CreateEmpty(clipboardTexts);
			}
			return new PasteTargetAreaInfo
			{
				StartRowIndex = num,
				StartColumnIndex = list[0].LogicalDisplayIndex,
				SelectedRowsCount = list2.Count,
				SelectedColumnsCount = list.Count,
				ClipboardTexts = clipboardTexts
			};
		}

		public static NumberSelectionInfo GetSelectionInfo(GridControl gc)
		{
			if (!gc.HasSelection)
			{
				return new NumberSelectionInfo
				{
					StartRowIndex = 0,
					StartColumnIndex = 0,
					SelectedRowsCount = 0,
					SelectedColumnsCount = 0
				};
			}
			List<DataColumn> list = new List<DataColumn>();
			List<DataRow> list2 = new List<DataRow>();
			foreach (DataCellInfo totalOrderedSelectedCell in gc.TotalOrderedSelectedCells)
			{
				if (!list.Contains(totalOrderedSelectedCell.Column))
				{
					list.Add(totalOrderedSelectedCell.Column);
				}
				if (!list2.Contains(totalOrderedSelectedCell.Row))
				{
					list2.Add(totalOrderedSelectedCell.Row);
				}
			}
			return new NumberSelectionInfo
			{
				StartRowIndex = gc.BoundRows.ToList().IndexOf(list2[0]),
				StartColumnIndex = list[0].LogicalDisplayIndex,
				SelectedRowsCount = list2.Count,
				SelectedColumnsCount = list.Count
			};
		}

		private static int CalculateTargetPastingNumber(int textCount, int selectedCount)
		{
			if (selectedCount <= textCount)
			{
				return textCount;
			}
			return selectedCount - selectedCount % textCount;
		}

		private static PasteTargetAreaInfo CreateEmpty(IList<IList<string>> texts)
		{
			return new PasteTargetAreaInfo
			{
				StartRowIndex = 0,
				StartColumnIndex = 0,
				SelectedRowsCount = 0,
				SelectedColumnsCount = 0,
				ClipboardTexts = texts
			};
		}
	}
	public interface IGridCellData
	{
		string Name { get; set; }

		string Moniker { get; set; }

		int RowIndex { get; set; }

		int ColumnIndex { get; set; }

		string TextValue { get; set; }
	}
	internal static class GridControlCellHelper
	{
		private class CellProperties : DependencyObject
		{
			public static readonly DependencyProperty MultilineRenderHeightProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("MultilineRenderHeight", typeof(double), typeof(CellProperties), new PropertyMetadata(0.0));

			public static void SetMultilineRenderHeight(DependencyObject target, double value)
			{
				target.SetValue(MultilineRenderHeightProperty, value);
			}

			public static double GetMultilineRenderHeight(DependencyObject target)
			{
				return (double)target.GetValue(MultilineRenderHeightProperty);
			}
		}

		private static double? _elementHeight;

		public static Size MeasureText(GridControlCell cc)
		{
			if (cc.Cell != null)
			{
				string valueAsString = cc.Cell.ValueAsString;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(valueAsString))
				{
					Brush foreground = cc.Cell.Foreground ?? cc.Foreground;
					FontStyle style = cc.FontStyle;
					if (cc.Cell.FontStyle.HasValue)
					{
						style = cc.Cell.FontStyle.Value;
					}
					FontWeight weight = cc.FontWeight;
					if (cc.Cell.FontWeight.HasValue)
					{
						weight = cc.Cell.FontWeight.Value;
					}
					FormattedText formattedText = new FormattedText(valueAsString, Thread.CurrentThread.CurrentCulture, FlowDirection.LeftToRight, new Typeface(cc.FontFamily, style, weight, cc.FontStretch), cc.FontSize, foreground);
					Thickness margin = cc.Cell.Margin;
					return new Size(formattedText.Width + margin.Left + margin.Right + 16.0, formattedText.Height + margin.Top + margin.Bottom);
				}
			}
			return new Size(1.0, 1.0);
		}

		public static void RenderText(GridControlCell cc, DrawingContext dc)
		{
			if (cc.Cell == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string valueAsString = cc.Cell.ValueAsString;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(valueAsString))
			{
				return;
			}
			double num = cc.ActualWidth - cc.Cell.Margin.Left - cc.Cell.Margin.Right - 16.0;
			if (num > 0.0)
			{
				Brush foreground = cc.Cell.Foreground ?? cc.Foreground;
				FontStyle style = cc.FontStyle;
				if (cc.Cell.FontStyle.HasValue)
				{
					style = cc.Cell.FontStyle.Value;
				}
				FontWeight weight = cc.FontWeight;
				if (cc.Cell.FontWeight.HasValue)
				{
					weight = cc.Cell.FontWeight.Value;
				}
				FormattedText formattedText = new FormattedText(valueAsString, Thread.CurrentThread.CurrentCulture, FlowDirection.LeftToRight, new Typeface(cc.FontFamily, style, weight, cc.FontStretch), cc.FontSize, foreground);
				formattedText.MaxTextWidth = num;
				formattedText.Trimming = TextTrimming.CharacterEllipsis;
				if (cc.GridColumn != null && !cc.GridColumn.GridControlOwner.IsMultilineAllowedInCell)
				{
					formattedText.MaxLineCount = 1;
				}
				if (cc.Cell.ContentHorizontalAlignment == HorizontalAlignment.Right)
				{
					formattedText.TextAlignment = TextAlignment.Right;
				}
				dc.DrawText(formattedText, GetTextRenderPoint(cc, formattedText));
				if (cc.GridColumn != null && cc.GridColumn.GridControlOwner.IsMultilineAllowedInCell && cc.DesiredSize.Height > 0.0 && formattedText.Height > cc.DesiredSize.Height)
				{
					CellProperties.SetMultilineRenderHeight(cc, formattedText.Height + 4.0);
					cc.InvalidateMeasure();
				}
			}
		}

		private static Point GetTextRenderPoint(GridControlCell cc, FormattedText ft)
		{
			double y = (cc.ActualHeight - ft.Height) / 2.0;
			if (cc.Cell.ContentHorizontalAlignment == HorizontalAlignment.Center)
			{
				return new Point((cc.ActualWidth - ft.Width) / 2.0, y);
			}
			if (cc.Cell.ContentHorizontalAlignment == HorizontalAlignment.Right)
			{
				double val = 4.0 + cc.Cell.Margin.Right;
				return new Point(Math.Max(0.0, val), y);
			}
			return new Point(4.0 + cc.Cell.Margin.Left, y);
		}

		public static void RenderBorderAndBackground(GridControlCell cc, DrawingContext dc)
		{
			if (cc.ResolvedIsCurrent)
			{
				dc.DrawRectangle(new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(byte.MaxValue, 239, 143)), new Pen(cc.BorderBrush, 1.0), new Rect(0.0, 0.0, Math.Max(1.0, cc.RenderSize.Width - 1.0), Math.Max(1.0, cc.RenderSize.Height - 1.0)));
			}
			else
			{
				dc.DrawRectangle(cc.Background, null, new Rect(cc.RenderSize));
			}
		}

		public static void RenderCellBackground(GridControlCell cc, DrawingContext dc)
		{
			if (cc.Cell != null && cc.Cell.Background != null && cc.Cell.Background != Brushes.Transparent)
			{
				double num = cc.ActualWidth - 2.0;
				double num2 = cc.ActualHeight - 2.0;
				if (num > 0.0 && num2 > 0.0)
				{
					dc.DrawRectangle(cc.Cell.Background, null, new Rect(1.0, 1.0, num, num2));
				}
			}
		}

		public static double GetDesiredHeightToRenderText(GridControlCell cc)
		{
			if (cc.Cell != null)
			{
				string valueAsString = cc.Cell.ValueAsString;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(valueAsString))
				{
					double multilineRenderHeight = CellProperties.GetMultilineRenderHeight(cc);
					if (multilineRenderHeight > 0.0)
					{
						return multilineRenderHeight;
					}
					if (!_elementHeight.HasValue)
					{
						_elementHeight = GetDesiredHeightForSingleLine(cc);
					}
					int num = valueAsString.Count((char c) => c == '\n') + 1;
					return _elementHeight.Value * (double)num + 10.0;
				}
			}
			return 0.0;
		}

		private static double GetDesiredHeightForSingleLine(GridControlCell cc)
		{
			Brush foreground = cc.Cell.Foreground ?? cc.Foreground;
			FontStyle style = cc.FontStyle;
			if (cc.Cell.FontStyle.HasValue)
			{
				style = cc.Cell.FontStyle.Value;
			}
			FontWeight weight = cc.FontWeight;
			if (cc.Cell.FontWeight.HasValue)
			{
				weight = cc.Cell.FontWeight.Value;
			}
			return new FormattedText("A", Thread.CurrentThread.CurrentCulture, FlowDirection.LeftToRight, new Typeface(cc.FontFamily, style, weight, cc.FontStretch), cc.FontSize, foreground).Height + 3.0;
		}
	}
	internal static class PrintingHelper
	{
		private const double ContentVerticalMargin = 90.0;

		private const double MaxColumnWidth = 250.0;

		private const double DefaultRowHeight = 25.0;

		public static IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> GenerateGenericPrintPages(GridControl dg, Size availablePrintSize, int frozenRows, int frozenColumns, string title, string additionalFooter, bool ignoreLastColumn, bool ignoreLastRow, Func<DataColumn, string> columnHeaderTextFunc)
		{
			if (dg == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("dg");
			}
			if (dg.DataSource == null || dg.DataGrid == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			List<DataColumn> list = (from x in dg.DataSource.Columns
				where x.Visibility == Visibility.Visible
				orderby x.LogicalDisplayIndex
				select x).ToList();
			if (ignoreLastColumn)
			{
				list = list.Take(list.Count - 1).ToList();
			}
			List<DataRow> list2 = dg.DataGrid.ItemsSource.ToList();
			if (ignoreLastRow)
			{
				list2.RemoveAt(list2.Count - 1);
			}
			double num = 25.0;
			double headerHeight = num + 5.0;
			Size size = new Size(availablePrintSize.Width, availablePrintSize.Height - 90.0);
			List<List<DataColumn>> list3 = GenerateDataColumnGroups(list, size.Width, frozenColumns);
			List<List<DataRow>> list4 = GenerateRowGroups(list2, size.Height, num, headerHeight, frozenRows);
			int pageCount = list3.Count * list4.Count;
			DateTime now = DateTime.Now;
			List<FrameworkElement> list5 = new List<FrameworkElement>();
			for (int num2 = 0; num2 < list4.Count; num2++)
			{
				List<DataRow> rows = list4[num2];
				for (int num3 = 0; num3 < list3.Count; num3++)
				{
					int pageIndex = num2 * list3.Count + num3 + 1;
					FrameworkElement frameworkElement = CreateGenericPrintingPage(rows, list3[num3], headerHeight, num, title, additionalFooter, pageIndex, pageCount, now, columnHeaderTextFunc);
					frameworkElement.Width = availablePrintSize.Width;
					frameworkElement.Height = availablePrintSize.Height;
					list5.Add(frameworkElement);
				}
			}
			return list5;
		}

		private static FrameworkElement CreateGenericPrintingPage(List<DataRow> rows, List<DataColumn> columns, double headerHeight, double rowHeight, string title, string additionalFooter, int pageIndex, int pageCount, DateTime printTime, Func<DataColumn, string> columnHeaderTextFunc = null)
		{
			Border element = new Border
			{
				Child = new TextBlock
				{
					HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Center,
					VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center,
					FontWeight = FontWeights.Bold,
					Text = title
				},
				Height = 40.0,
				BorderBrush = Brushes.Gray,
				BorderThickness = new Thickness(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0)
			};
			Border element2 = new Border
			{
				Child = CreateGenericPrintingPageBody(rows, columns, headerHeight, rowHeight, columnHeaderTextFunc),
				Padding = new Thickness(0.0, 10.0, 0.0, 10.0)
			};
			Grid.SetRow(element2, 1);
			TextBlock textBlock = new TextBlock
			{
				HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Center,
				VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center,
				MinWidth = 80.0,
				Text = $"Page {pageIndex}/{pageCount}"
			};
			TextBlock element3 = new TextBlock
			{
				HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right,
				VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center,
				TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap,
				Text = printTime.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd hh:mm:ss")
			};
			Grid grid = new Grid();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(additionalFooter))
			{
				grid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition
				{
					Width = new GridLength(50.0, GridUnitType.Star)
				});
				grid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition
				{
					Width = new GridLength(10.0, GridUnitType.Auto)
				});
				grid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition
				{
					Width = new GridLength(50.0, GridUnitType.Star)
				});
				TextBlock element4 = new TextBlock
				{
					HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left,
					VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center,
					TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap,
					Text = additionalFooter
				};
				grid.Children.Add(element4);
				Grid.SetColumn(textBlock, 1);
				grid.Children.Add(textBlock);
				Grid.SetColumn(element3, 2);
				grid.Children.Add(element3);
			}
			else
			{
				grid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition
				{
					Width = new GridLength(50.0, GridUnitType.Star)
				});
				grid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition
				{
					Width = new GridLength(50.0, GridUnitType.Star)
				});
				textBlock.HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left;
				Grid.SetColumn(textBlock, 0);
				grid.Children.Add(textBlock);
				Grid.SetColumn(element3, 1);
				grid.Children.Add(element3);
			}
			Border element5 = new Border
			{
				Child = grid,
				Height = 30.0,
				BorderBrush = Brushes.Gray,
				BorderThickness = new Thickness(0.0, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0)
			};
			Grid.SetRow(element5, 2);
			return new Grid
			{
				RowDefinitions = 
				{
					new RowDefinition
					{
						Height = new GridLength(0.0, GridUnitType.Auto)
					},
					new RowDefinition
					{
						Height = new GridLength(100.0, GridUnitType.Star)
					},
					new RowDefinition
					{
						Height = new GridLength(0.0, GridUnitType.Auto)
					}
				},
				Children = 
				{
					(UIElement)element,
					(UIElement)element2,
					(UIElement)element5
				}
			};
		}

		private static FrameworkElement CreateGenericPrintingPageBody(List<DataRow> rows, List<DataColumn> columns, double headerHeight, double rowHeight, Func<DataColumn, string> columnLabelFunc)
		{
			Grid grid = new Grid
			{
				VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top,
				HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left
			};
			foreach (DataColumn column in columns)
			{
				grid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition
				{
					Width = new GridLength(GetDataColumnWidth(column))
				});
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < rows.Count + 1; i++)
			{
				grid.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition());
			}
			foreach (DataColumn column2 in columns)
			{
				grid.Children.Add(CreateGenericPrintingCellElement(GetDataColumnLabelText(column2, columnLabelFunc), headerHeight, HorizontalAlignment.Left, 0, columns.IndexOf(column2)));
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < rows.Count; j++)
			{
				DataRow row = rows[j];
				for (int k = 0; k < columns.Count; k++)
				{
					string text = ConvertToPrintingText(GridControlHelper.GetDirectValueAt(row, columns[k]));
					grid.Children.Add(CreateGenericPrintingCellElement(text, rowHeight, HorizontalAlignment.Left, j + 1, k));
				}
			}
			return grid;
		}

		private static UIElement CreateGenericPrintingCellElement(string text, double height, HorizontalAlignment horizontalAlignment, int rowIndex, int columnIndex)
		{
			TextBlock obj = new TextBlock
			{
				Text = text,
				Height = height,
				HorizontalAlignment = horizontalAlignment
			};
			Grid.SetRow(obj, rowIndex);
			Grid.SetColumn(obj, columnIndex);
			return obj;
		}

		private static string GetDataColumnLabelText(DataColumn column, Func<DataColumn, string> columnLabelFunc)
		{
			if (columnLabelFunc != null)
			{
				return columnLabelFunc(column);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(column.Header))
			{
				return column.Header;
			}
			return string.Empty;
		}

		private static double GetDataColumnWidth(DataColumn column)
		{
			return column.RenderedWidth;
		}

		private static List<DataColumn> GetFrozenDataColumns(IList<DataColumn> columns, double unitWidth, int frozenColumns, out double frozenWidth)
		{
			frozenWidth = 0.0;
			List<DataColumn> list = new List<DataColumn>();
			for (int i = 0; i < frozenColumns && i < columns.Count - 1; i++)
			{
				DataColumn dataColumn = columns[i];
				double dataColumnWidth = GetDataColumnWidth(dataColumn);
				double dataColumnWidth2 = GetDataColumnWidth(columns[i + 1]);
				if (frozenWidth + dataColumnWidth + dataColumnWidth2 > unitWidth)
				{
					break;
				}
				frozenWidth += dataColumnWidth;
				list.Add(dataColumn);
			}
			return list;
		}

		private static List<DataRow> GetFrozenRecords(IList<DataRow> rows, double unitHeight, double rowHeight, double headerHeight, int frozenRows, out double frozenHeight)
		{
			frozenHeight = headerHeight;
			List<DataRow> list = new List<DataRow>();
			for (int i = 0; i < frozenRows && i < rows.Count - 1; i++)
			{
				if (frozenHeight + rowHeight * 2.0 > unitHeight)
				{
					break;
				}
				frozenHeight += rowHeight;
				list.Add(rows[i]);
			}
			return list;
		}

		private static List<List<DataColumn>> GenerateDataColumnGroups(IList<DataColumn> columns, double unitWidth, int frozenColumns)
		{
			double frozenWidth = 0.0;
			List<DataColumn> frozenDataColumns = GetFrozenDataColumns(columns, unitWidth, frozenColumns, out frozenWidth);
			double num = frozenWidth;
			List<List<DataColumn>> list = new List<List<DataColumn>>();
			List<DataColumn> list2 = null;
			for (int i = frozenDataColumns.Count; i < columns.Count; i++)
			{
				DataColumn dataColumn = columns[i];
				if (list2 == null)
				{
					list2 = new List<DataColumn>(frozenDataColumns);
				}
				list2.Add(dataColumn);
				num += GetDataColumnWidth(dataColumn);
				bool flag = i == columns.Count - 1;
				if (!flag)
				{
					DataColumn column = columns[i + 1];
					if (num + GetDataColumnWidth(column) > unitWidth)
					{
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					list.Add(list2);
					list2 = null;
					num = frozenWidth;
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		private static List<List<DataRow>> GenerateRowGroups(IList<DataRow> rows, double unitHeight, double rowHeight, double headerHeight, int frozenRows)
		{
			double frozenHeight = 0.0;
			List<DataRow> frozenRecords = GetFrozenRecords(rows, unitHeight, rowHeight, headerHeight, frozenRows, out frozenHeight);
			List<List<DataRow>> list = new List<List<DataRow>>();
			double num = frozenHeight;
			List<DataRow> list2 = null;
			for (int i = frozenRecords.Count; i < rows.Count; i++)
			{
				DataRow item = rows[i];
				if (list2 == null)
				{
					list2 = new List<DataRow>(frozenRecords);
				}
				list2.Add(item);
				num += rowHeight;
				bool flag = i == rows.Count - 1;
				if (!flag && num + rowHeight > unitHeight)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					list.Add(list2);
					list2 = null;
					num = frozenHeight;
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		private static string ConvertToPrintingText(object cellValue)
		{
			if (cellValue == null)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			if (cellValue is double num)
			{
				return num.ToString("G6");
			}
			return cellValue.ToString();
		}
	}
	internal class RowHeaderControlHelper
	{
		private const double StartDraggingMargin = 4.0;

		private const string RowDraggingDataFormat = "AP_GridControl_RowDragging";

		private GridControl _gc;

		private Point? _downPosition;

		private DataRowHeaderControl RowHeader { get; set; }

		private GridControl GridControlOwner
		{
			get
			{
				if (_gc == null)
				{
					_gc = RowHeader.GetAncestor<GridControl>();
				}
				return _gc;
			}
		}

		private bool IsReady
		{
			get
			{
				if (GridControlOwner != null)
				{
					return GridControlOwner.AllowRowReorder;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public RowHeaderControlHelper(DataRowHeaderControl rowHeader)
		{
			if (rowHeader == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("rowHeader");
			}
			RowHeader = rowHeader;
		}

		public void OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!ProcessGroupingState(e) && IsReady)
			{
				_downPosition = e.GetPosition(RowHeader);
			}
		}

		public void OnPreviewMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (IsReady && _downPosition.HasValue && e.LeftButton == MouseButtonState.Pressed && (e.GetPosition(RowHeader) - _downPosition.Value).Length >= 4.0)
			{
				DataObject dataObject = new DataObject();
				dataObject.SetData("AP_GridControl_RowDragging", this);
				DragDrop.DoDragDrop(RowHeader, dataObject, DragDropEffects.Move);
				_downPosition = null;
			}
		}

		public void OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			_downPosition = null;
		}

		public void OnPreviewMouseRightButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (RowHeader.DataContext is DataRow value && GridControlOwner.SelectedRows.Contains(value))
			{
				if (GridControlOwner.ContextMenu != null)
				{
					GridControlOwner.ContextMenu.IsOpen = true;
				}
				else
				{
					ContextMenuHelper.DoDefaultContextMenu(GridControlOwner, e);
				}
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		public void OnDragOver(DragEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!IsReady)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!e.Data.GetDataPresent("AP_GridControl_RowDragging"))
			{
				e.Effects = DragDropEffects.None;
			}
			else if (e.Data.GetData("AP_GridControl_RowDragging") is RowHeaderControlHelper rowHeaderControlHelper && rowHeaderControlHelper.RowHeader != RowHeader && rowHeaderControlHelper.GridControlOwner == GridControlOwner)
			{
				e.Effects = DragDropEffects.Move;
				GridControlOwner.UpdateRowIndicatorLine(Visibility.Visible, (IInputElement x) => e.GetPosition(x));
			}
			else
			{
				e.Effects = DragDropEffects.None;
			}
		}

		public void OnDragLeave(DragEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControlOwner.UpdateRowIndicatorLine(Visibility.Collapsed);
		}

		public void OnDrop(DragEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!IsReady || !e.Data.GetDataPresent("AP_GridControl_RowDragging"))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (e.Data.GetData("AP_GridControl_RowDragging") is RowHeaderControlHelper rowHeaderControlHelper && rowHeaderControlHelper.RowHeader != RowHeader && rowHeaderControlHelper.GridControlOwner == GridControlOwner)
			{
				DataRow dataRow = rowHeaderControlHelper.RowHeader.DataContext as DataRow;
				DataRow dataRow2 = RowHeader.DataContext as DataRow;
				DataTable dataSource = GridControlOwner.DataSource;
				if (dataSource != null && dataRow != null && dataRow2 != null && dataSource.Rows.Contains(dataRow) && dataSource.Rows.Contains(dataRow2))
				{
					int num = dataSource.Rows.IndexOf(dataRow);
					int num2 = dataSource.Rows.IndexOf(dataRow2);
					if (IsOnTopHalf(e.GetPosition(RowHeader)))
					{
						if (num > num2)
						{
							dataSource.Rows.Move(num, num2);
						}
						else if (num != num2 - 1)
						{
							dataSource.Rows.Move(num, num2 - 1);
						}
					}
					else if (num < num2)
					{
						dataSource.Rows.Move(num, num2);
					}
					else if (num != num2 + 1)
					{
						dataSource.Rows.Move(num, num2 + 1);
					}
				}
			}
			GridControlOwner.UpdateRowIndicatorLine(Visibility.Collapsed);
		}
