		private bool ProcessGroupingState(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			return false;
		}

		private bool IsOnTopHalf(Point mousePosition)
		{
			return mousePosition.Y <= RowHeader.ActualHeight / 2.0;
		}
	}
	internal class CellValueHelper
	{
		private readonly GridControl _gc;

		public CellValueHelper(GridControl gc)
		{
			if (gc == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("gc");
			}
			_gc = gc;
		}

		public object GetCellValue(DataRow row, DataColumn column)
		{
			if (_gc.DataSource == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (row.IsDataLoaded(column))
			{
				return row[column].Value;
			}
			object directCellValue = _gc.DataSource.GetDirectCellValue(row, column);
			if (directCellValue == null || directCellValue == DBNull.Value)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return directCellValue;
		}
	}
	internal static class ContextMenuHelper
	{
		public static void DoDefaultContextMenu(GridControl gc, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (gc.DataGrid == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject;
			DefaultContextMenuEventArgs e2 = new DefaultContextMenuEventArgs(gc, new ContextMenu(), e, GridControl.DefaultContextMenuOpeningEvent, gc);
			if (!e2.IsOpenFromScrollBar)
			{
				DataColumnHeaderControl ancestor = dependencyObject.GetAncestor<DataColumnHeaderControl>(gc);
				if (ancestor != null)
				{
					InternalOpenDefaultContextMenu(gc, e2, ancestor);
				}
				else
				{
					InternalOpenDefaultContextMenu(gc, e2, dependencyObject as UIElement);
				}
			}
		}

		private static void InternalOpenDefaultContextMenu(GridControl gc, DefaultContextMenuEventArgs args, UIElement placementTarget)
		{
			PrepareDefaultContextMenu(gc, args);
			gc.OnDefaultContextMenuOpening(args);
			if (args.DefaultContextMenu.HasItems)
			{
				if (placementTarget != null)
				{
					args.DefaultContextMenu.PlacementTarget = placementTarget;
				}
				args.DefaultContextMenu.IsOpen = true;
				args.OriginalEventArgs.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private static void PrepareDefaultContextMenu(GridControl gc, DefaultContextMenuEventArgs args)
		{
			if (gc != null && args != null && gc.DataGrid != null && gc.DataSource != null)
			{
				if (args.IsOpenFromHeaderPrefixArea)
				{
					AddOnHeaderPrefixArea(gc, args);
				}
				else if (args.IsOpenFromColumnHeader)
				{
					AddOnColumnHeader(gc, args);
				}
				else if (args.IsOpenFromRowHeader)
				{
					AddOnRowHeader(gc, args);
				}
				else if (args.IsOpenFromGridCell)
				{
					AddOnCell(gc, args);
				}
			}
		}

		private static void AddOnHeaderPrefixArea(GridControl gc, DefaultContextMenuEventArgs args)
		{
			if (gc.AllowSelectAll)
			{
				args.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
				{
					IsCheckable = true,
					IsChecked = gc.InternalSelectionHelper.IsAllSelected,
					Command = GridControl.SelectAllCommand,
					CommandTarget = gc
				});
			}
			if (gc.AllowColumnChooser)
			{
				if (args.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Count > 0)
				{
					args.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(new Separator());
				}
				args.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
				{
					Icon = new Image
					{
						Source = ImageSourceHelper.FieldChooser16
					},
					Command = GridControl.DisplayColumnChooserCommand,
					CommandTarget = gc
				});
			}
		}

		private static void AddOnColumnHeader(GridControl gc, DefaultContextMenuEventArgs args)
		{
			DataColumn dataColumn = ((GridControlColumn)args.SourceColumnHeader.Column).DataColumn;
			if (gc.AllowSelectColumn)
			{
				args.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
				{
					IsCheckable = true,
					IsChecked = gc.InternalSelectionHelper.IsColumnSelected(dataColumn),
					Command = GridControl.SelectColumnCommand,
					CommandTarget = gc,
					CommandParameter = dataColumn
				});
			}
			if (gc.SortingMode != SortingMode.NotAllowed && dataColumn.CanSorting)
			{
				if (args.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Count > 0)
				{
					args.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(new Separator());
				}
				args.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
				{
					Icon = new Image
					{
						Source = ImageSourceHelper.SortAscend16
					},
					Command = GridControl.SortAscendingCommand,
					CommandTarget = gc,
					CommandParameter = dataColumn
				});
				args.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
				{
					Icon = new Image
					{
						Source = ImageSourceHelper.SortDescend16
					},
					Command = GridControl.SortDescendingCommand,
					CommandTarget = gc,
					CommandParameter = dataColumn
				});
				args.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
				{
					Icon = new Image
					{
						Source = ImageSourceHelper.SortClear16
					},
					Command = GridControl.RemoveSortingCommand,
					CommandTarget = gc,
					CommandParameter = dataColumn
				});
				if (gc.SortingMode == SortingMode.AllowMultipleColumnsSorting)
				{
					args.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
					{
						Command = GridControl.ClearSortingsCommand,
						CommandTarget = gc,
						CommandParameter = dataColumn
					});
				}
			}
			if (gc.AllowColumnFreeze)
			{
				if (args.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Count > 0)
				{
					args.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(new Separator());
				}
				args.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
				{
					Icon = new Image
					{
						Source = ImageSourceHelper.RecordsFreezeColumns16
					},
					Command = GridControl.FreezeColumnCommand,
					CommandTarget = gc,
					CommandParameter = dataColumn
				});
				args.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
				{
					Command = GridControl.UnfreezeColumnCommand,
					CommandTarget = gc,
					CommandParameter = dataColumn
				});
			}
			if (gc.AllowColumnChooser)
			{
				if (args.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Count > 0)
				{
					args.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(new Separator());
				}
				args.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
				{
					Icon = new Image
					{
						Source = ImageSourceHelper.FieldChooser16
					},
					Command = GridControl.DisplayColumnChooserCommand,
					CommandTarget = gc
				});
			}
			if (gc.AllowRecordFiltering)
			{
				args.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(new Separator());
				args.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
				{
					Header = ((gc.FilterHeaderVisibility == Visibility.Visible) ? AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridControl_ContextMenu_HideRecordFilterLine : AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridControl_ContextMenu_ShowRecordFilterLine),
					Command = GridControl.FilterHeaderCommand,
					CommandTarget = gc
				});
				args.DefaultContextMenu.Items.Add(new MenuItem
				{
					Header = AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridControl_ContextMenu_ClearFilterings,
					Command = GridControl.ClearAllFiltersCommand,
					CommandTarget = gc
				});
			}
		}

		private static void AddOnRowHeader(GridControl gc, DefaultContextMenuEventArgs args)
		{
		}

		private static void AddOnCell(GridControl gc, DefaultContextMenuEventArgs args)
		{
		}
	}
	internal class DragAndDropHelper
	{
		private const double DraggingStartDistance = 10.0;

		private readonly GridControl _gc;

		private GridControlCell _mouseDownCellControl;

		private Point _mouseDownPosition;

		public DragAndDropHelper(GridControl gc)
		{
			if (gc == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("gc");
			}
			_gc = gc;
		}

		public void DoPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControlCell cellControl = e.GetCellControl(_gc);
			if (cellControl != null && cellControl.Cell != null && (!cellControl.IsInEditMode || CellDraggingAnchor.IsInDraggingAnchor(e.OriginalSource)))
			{
				bool flag = false;
				if (cellControl.Cell.UIType == CellUIType.CheckBox || (cellControl.Cell.ValueType == DataRow.NullableBooleanType && cellControl.Cell.UIType == CellUIType.Unknown))
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				if (!flag && new DataCellInfo(cellControl.Cell.Row, cellControl.Cell.Column).IsSelectedInGrid(_gc))
				{
					if (!cellControl.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
					{
						cellControl.Focus();
					}
					if (cellControl.Cell.AllowDrag)
					{
						e.Handled = true;
						_mouseDownCellControl = cellControl;
						_mouseDownPosition = e.GetPosition(_gc);
						return;
					}
				}
			}
			_mouseDownCellControl = null;
		}

		public void DoPreviewMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (Mouse.LeftButton == MouseButtonState.Pressed && _mouseDownCellControl != null && (e.GetPosition(_gc) - _mouseDownPosition).Length >= 10.0)
			{
				_mouseDownCellControl = null;
				DataObject dataObject = new DataObject();
				_gc.OnDataDragging(dataObject);
				DragDrop.DoDragDrop(_gc, dataObject, DragDropEffects.Copy);
			}
		}

		public void DoPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_mouseDownCellControl == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			IDataCell cell = _mouseDownCellControl.Cell;
			if (cell != null)
			{
				DataCellInfo dataCellInfo = new DataCellInfo(cell.Row, cell.Column);
				bool flag = false;
				if (!((_gc.SelectionUnit != DataGridSelectionUnit.FullRow) ? (_gc.SelectedCells.FirstOrDefault() == dataCellInfo && _gc.SelectedCells.Count() == 1) : (_gc.SelectedRows.FirstOrDefault() == dataCellInfo.Row && _gc.SelectedRows.Count() == 1)))
				{
					_gc.InternalSelectionHelper.LockSelectionChanged();
					_gc.DeselectAll();
					if (_gc.SelectionUnit == DataGridSelectionUnit.FullRow)
					{
						_gc.SelectRow(cell.Row);
					}
					else
					{
						_gc.SelectCell(dataCellInfo);
					}
					_gc.InternalSelectionHelper.UnlockSelectionChanged(raiseEvent: true);
					_gc.CurrentCell = dataCellInfo;
				}
			}
			_mouseDownCellControl = null;
		}

		public static NumberSelectionInfo GetDropTargetInfo(IList<IList<string>> table, GridControlCell spotCell, GridControl gc)
		{
			_ = gc.DataSource;
			int selectedColumnsCount = 0;
			if (table.Count > 0)
			{
				selectedColumnsCount = table.Max((IList<string> row) => row.Count);
			}
			if (spotCell == null || spotCell.Cell == null)
			{
				return new NumberSelectionInfo
				{
					StartRowIndex = Math.Max(0, gc.BoundRows.Count() - 1),
					StartColumnIndex = 0,
					SelectedRowsCount = table.Count,
					SelectedColumnsCount = selectedColumnsCount
				};
			}
			return new NumberSelectionInfo
			{
				StartRowIndex = gc.BoundRows.ToList().IndexOf(spotCell.Cell.Row),
				StartColumnIndex = spotCell.Cell.Column.LogicalDisplayIndex,
				SelectedRowsCount = table.Count,
				SelectedColumnsCount = selectedColumnsCount
			};
		}

		public string CreateDraggingText()
		{
			ObservableCollection<DataRow> rows = _gc.DataSource.Rows;
			_ = _gc.DataSource.Columns;
			int? num = null;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			foreach (DataCellInfo totalOrderedSelectedCell in _gc.TotalOrderedSelectedCells)
			{
				DataRow row = totalOrderedSelectedCell.Row;
				DataColumn column = totalOrderedSelectedCell.Column;
				int num2 = rows.IndexOf(row);
				object cellValue = _gc.InternalCellValueHelper.GetCellValue(row, column);
				string value = ((cellValue == null) ? string.Empty : cellValue.ToString());
				if (!num.HasValue)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(value);
				}
				else if (num.Value == num2)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append('\t').Append(value);
				}
				else
				{
					stringBuilder.AppendLine().Append(value);
				}
				num = num2;
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}
	}
	internal class FilterHelper
	{
		private class OperandsCollection : ObservableCollection<string>
		{
			private const int CountingPoint1 = 100;

			private const int CountingPoint2 = 1000;

			private const int MaxMillisecondsInOneCycle = 400;

			private readonly GridControl _gc;

			private readonly DataColumn _column;

			private bool _stopFlag;

			private int? _index;

			private List<DataRow> _countingRows;

			public OperandsCollection(GridControl gc, DataColumn column)
			{
				_gc = gc;
				_column = column;
				_index = 0;
				BuildCollection(column);
			}

			public void Stop()
			{
				if (_index.HasValue)
				{
					_stopFlag = true;
				}
			}

			public void Restart()
			{
				if (_index.HasValue && _stopFlag)
				{
					_stopFlag = false;
					GenerateOperandStepByStep();
				}
			}

			private void BuildCollection(DataColumn column)
			{
				if (column.DefaultDataType == DataRow.NullableBooleanType || column.DefaultDataType == DataRow.BooleanType)
				{
					AddItem("True");
					AddItem("False");
					return;
				}
				_countingRows = GetCountingRowsForColumn(column).ToList();
				bool flag = _countingRows.Count > 1000;
				if (flag || _countingRows.Count == _gc.DataSource.Rows.Count)
				{
					IList<string> list = _gc.DataSource.OnFilterOperandsQueried(column);
					if (list != null)
					{
						foreach (string item in list)
						{
							AddItem(item);
						}
						return;
					}
				}
				if (column.CellsItemsSource != null && flag)
				{
					foreach (string item2 in (from object x in column.CellsItemsSource
						select x.ToString()).ToList())
					{
						AddItem(item2);
					}
					return;
				}
				if (_countingRows.Count <= 100)
				{
					foreach (DataRow countingRow in _countingRows)
					{
						IDataCell dataCell = countingRow[column];
						if (dataCell != null)
						{
							string valueAsString = dataCell.ValueAsString;
							if (!Contains(valueAsString))
							{
								AddItem(valueAsString);
							}
						}
					}
					return;
				}
				_index = 0;
				_gc.DataSource.EnsureAllRowsData();
				GenerateOperandStepByStep();
			}

			private void GenerateOperandStepByStep()
			{
				if (_stopFlag || !_index.HasValue)
				{
					return;
				}
				Stopwatch stopwatch = new Stopwatch();
				stopwatch.Start();
				int num = 0;
				while (true)
				{
					int value = _index.Value;
					if (value >= _countingRows.Count)
					{
						return;
					}
					IDataCell dataCell = _countingRows[value][_column];
					if (dataCell != null)
					{
						string valueAsString = dataCell.ValueAsString;
						if (!Contains(valueAsString))
						{
							AddItem(valueAsString);
						}
					}
					_index = (value += 1);
					if (stopwatch.ElapsedMilliseconds >= 400)
					{
						break;
					}
					num++;
				}
				stopwatch.Stop();
				_gc.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(GenerateOperandStepByStep), DispatcherPriority.Background);
			}

			private void AddItem(string filterItem)
			{
				if (filterItem != string.Empty)
				{
					Add(filterItem);
				}
			}

			private IEnumerable<DataRow> GetCountingRowsForColumn(DataColumn target)
			{
				if (_gc.DataSource == null)
				{
					return new DataRow[0];
				}
				if (target == null)
				{
					return _gc.BoundRows;
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(target.Filter.SelectedOperand))
				{
					return _gc.BoundRows;
				}
				List<KeyValuePair<DataColumn, string>> list = (from column in _gc.DataSource.Columns
					let operand = column.Filter.SelectedOperand
					where !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(operand) && column != target
					select new KeyValuePair<DataColumn, string>(column, operand)).ToList();
				if (list.Count == 0)
				{
					return _gc.DataSource.Rows;
				}
				List<DataRow> list2 = new List<DataRow>();
				foreach (DataRow row in _gc.DataSource.Rows)
				{
					bool flag = true;
					foreach (KeyValuePair<DataColumn, string> item in list)
					{
						if (!_gc.DataSource.CanCellPassFilter(new DataCellInfo(row, item.Key), item.Value, item.Key.Filter.SelectedOperator))
						{
							flag = false;
							break;
						}
					}
					if (flag)
					{
						list2.Add(row);
					}
				}
				return list2;
			}
		}

		private readonly GridControl _gc;

		public bool HasFiltering
		{
			get
			{
				if (_gc.DataSource != null)
				{
					return _gc.DataSource.Columns.FirstOrDefault((DataColumn x) => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(x.Filter.SelectedOperand)) != null;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public bool IsFilterChangedUpdateLocked { get; private set; }

		public FilterHelper(GridControl gc)
		{
			if (gc == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("gc");
			}
			_gc = gc;
		}

		public IEnumerable<string> GetColumnFilterOperands(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (_gc.DataSource == null)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.ErrorSourceIsEmpty);
			}
			return new OperandsCollection(_gc, column);
		}

		public void OnColumnFilterDropdownClosed(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (column.Filter.AvailableOperands is OperandsCollection operandsCollection)
			{
				operandsCollection.Stop();
			}
		}

		public void OnColumnFilterDropdownReopened(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (column.Filter.AvailableOperands is OperandsCollection operandsCollection)
			{
				operandsCollection.Restart();
			}
		}

		public void ClearColumnsFilterOperands()
		{
			ClearFilterings(forceUpdate: false, resetCurrentOperand: false);
		}

		public void ClearFilterings(bool forceUpdate, bool resetCurrentOperand)
		{
			IsFilterChangedUpdateLocked = true;
			if (_gc.DataSource != null)
			{
				foreach (DataColumn column in _gc.DataSource.Columns)
				{
					if (resetCurrentOperand)
					{
						column.Filter.SelectedOperand = null;
					}
					column.Filter.ResetAvailableOperands();
				}
			}
			IsFilterChangedUpdateLocked = false;
			if (forceUpdate && _gc.DataSource != null)
			{
				_gc.DataSource.OnFilteringChanged();
			}
		}

		public void ResetFilterOperands()
		{
			if (_gc.DataSource == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (DataColumn column in _gc.DataSource.Columns)
			{
				column.Filter.ResetAvailableOperands();
			}
		}

		public void DoDefaultFiltering()
		{
			if (_gc.DataGrid == null || _gc.DataSource == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			Cursor overrideCursor = Mouse.OverrideCursor;
			try
			{
				Mouse.OverrideCursor = Cursors.Wait;
				_gc.InternalSelectionHelper.ResetSelection();
				if (_gc.SortingInfo.SortedItems.Count > 0)
				{
					_gc.InternalSortHelper.DoDefaultSorting();
					return;
				}
				IList<DataRow> filteredRows = GetFilteredRows();
				if (filteredRows != null)
				{
					_gc.DirectlySetItemsSource(filteredRows);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				Mouse.OverrideCursor = overrideCursor;
			}
		}

		public IList<DataRow> GetFilteredRows()
		{
			if (_gc.DataSource == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			List<KeyValuePair<DataColumn, string>> list = (from column in _gc.DataSource.Columns
				let operand = column.Filter.SelectedOperand
				where !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(operand)
				select new KeyValuePair<DataColumn, string>(column, operand)).ToList();
			foreach (DataRow row in _gc.DataSource.Rows)
			{
				row.IsFilteredOut = false;
			}
			if (list.Count == 0)
			{
				return _gc.DataSource.Rows.ToList();
			}
			List<DataRow> list2 = new List<DataRow>();
			foreach (DataRow row2 in _gc.DataSource.Rows)
			{
				bool flag = true;
				foreach (KeyValuePair<DataColumn, string> item in list)
				{
					if (!_gc.DataSource.CanCellPassFilter(new DataCellInfo(row2, item.Key), item.Value, item.Key.Filter.SelectedOperator))
					{
						flag = false;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					list2.Add(row2);
				}
				else
				{
					row2.IsFilteredOut = true;
				}
			}
			return list2;
		}

		public bool CanCellPassFilter(DataCellInfo cell, string filterOperand, DataColumnFilterOperator filterOperator)
		{
			if (cell.Data != null)
			{
				return CanPassFilter(cell.Data.Value, cell.Data.ValueAsString, filterOperand, filterOperator, cell.Column.ResolvedValueComparer);
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool CanPassFilter(object cellValue, string cellValueAsString, string filterOperand, DataColumnFilterOperator filterOperator, IComparer<object> comparer)
		{
			if (filterOperand.Equals(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridControl_ColumnFilter_Blanks))
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(cellValueAsString))
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (cellValue == null)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(cellValue.ToString()))
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			if (cellValue == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			string text = cellValueAsString ?? cellValue.ToString();
			switch (filterOperator)
			{
			case DataColumnFilterOperator.Equal:
				if (!string.Equals(text, filterOperand, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					return comparer.Compare(cellValue, filterOperand) == 0;
				}
				return true;
			case DataColumnFilterOperator.NotEqual:
				if (string.Equals(text, filterOperand, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					return false;
				}
				return comparer.Compare(cellValue, filterOperand) != 0;
			case DataColumnFilterOperator.GreaterThan:
				return comparer.Compare(cellValue, filterOperand) > 0;
			case DataColumnFilterOperator.GreaterThanOrEqual:
				if (!string.Equals(text, filterOperand, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					return comparer.Compare(cellValue, filterOperand) >= 0;
				}
				return true;
			case DataColumnFilterOperator.LessThan:
				return comparer.Compare(cellValue, filterOperand) < 0;
			case DataColumnFilterOperator.LessThanOrEqual:
				if (!string.Equals(text, filterOperand, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					return comparer.Compare(cellValue, filterOperand) <= 0;
				}
				return true;
			case DataColumnFilterOperator.StartsWith:
				return text.StartsWith(filterOperand, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase);
			case DataColumnFilterOperator.EndsWith:
				return text.EndsWith(filterOperand, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase);
			case DataColumnFilterOperator.Contains:
				return text.ToUpper().Contains(filterOperand.ToUpper());
			case DataColumnFilterOperator.NotStartsWith:
				return !text.StartsWith(filterOperand, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase);
			case DataColumnFilterOperator.NotEndsWith:
				return !text.EndsWith(filterOperand, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase);
			case DataColumnFilterOperator.NotContains:
				return !text.ToUpper().Contains(filterOperand.ToUpper());
			default:
				return false;
			}
		}

		public static IEnumerable<DataColumnFilterOperator> GetDefaultFilterOperators(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (column == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("column");
			}
			List<DataColumnFilterOperator> list = new List<DataColumnFilterOperator>();
			if (column.DefaultDataType == DataRow.NullableBooleanType || column.DefaultDataType == DataRow.BooleanType)
			{
				list.Add(DataColumnFilterOperator.Equal);
			}
			else if (column.DefaultDataType == DataRow.Int16Type || column.DefaultDataType == DataRow.Int32Type || column.DefaultDataType == DataRow.Int64Type || column.DefaultDataType == DataRow.NullableIntType)
			{
				list.Add(DataColumnFilterOperator.Equal);
				list.Add(DataColumnFilterOperator.NotEqual);
				list.Add(DataColumnFilterOperator.GreaterThan);
				list.Add(DataColumnFilterOperator.GreaterThanOrEqual);
				list.Add(DataColumnFilterOperator.LessThan);
				list.Add(DataColumnFilterOperator.LessThanOrEqual);
			}
			else if (column.DefaultDataType == DataRow.DoubleType || column.DefaultDataType == DataRow.FloatType || column.DefaultDataType == DataRow.DecimalType || column.DefaultDataType == DataRow.NullableDoubleType)
			{
				list.Add(DataColumnFilterOperator.Equal);
				list.Add(DataColumnFilterOperator.NotEqual);
				list.Add(DataColumnFilterOperator.GreaterThan);
				list.Add(DataColumnFilterOperator.GreaterThanOrEqual);
				list.Add(DataColumnFilterOperator.LessThan);
				list.Add(DataColumnFilterOperator.LessThanOrEqual);
			}
			else
			{
				list.Add(DataColumnFilterOperator.Equal);
				list.Add(DataColumnFilterOperator.NotEqual);
				list.Add(DataColumnFilterOperator.Contains);
				list.Add(DataColumnFilterOperator.NotContains);
				list.Add(DataColumnFilterOperator.StartsWith);
				list.Add(DataColumnFilterOperator.NotStartsWith);
				list.Add(DataColumnFilterOperator.EndsWith);
				list.Add(DataColumnFilterOperator.NotEndsWith);
			}
			return list;
		}

		public static ImageSource GetFilterOperatorImage(DataColumnFilterOperator fo)
		{
			return fo switch
			{
				DataColumnFilterOperator.Equal => ImageSourceHelper.EqualTo, 
				DataColumnFilterOperator.NotEqual => ImageSourceHelper.NotEqualTo, 
				DataColumnFilterOperator.GreaterThan => ImageSourceHelper.GreaterThan, 
				DataColumnFilterOperator.GreaterThanOrEqual => ImageSourceHelper.GreaterThanOrEqualTo, 
				DataColumnFilterOperator.LessThan => ImageSourceHelper.LessThan, 
				DataColumnFilterOperator.LessThanOrEqual => ImageSourceHelper.LessThanOrEqualTo, 
				DataColumnFilterOperator.Contains => ImageSourceHelper.Contains, 
				DataColumnFilterOperator.NotContains => ImageSourceHelper.DoesNotContain, 
				DataColumnFilterOperator.StartsWith => ImageSourceHelper.StartsWith, 
				DataColumnFilterOperator.NotStartsWith => ImageSourceHelper.DoesNotStartWith, 
				DataColumnFilterOperator.EndsWith => ImageSourceHelper.EndsWith, 
				DataColumnFilterOperator.NotEndsWith => ImageSourceHelper.DoesNotEndWith, 
				_ => null, 
			};
		}

		public static string GetFilterOperatorText(DataColumnFilterOperator fo)
		{
			return fo switch
			{
				DataColumnFilterOperator.Equal => AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.FilterOperator_Equals, 
				DataColumnFilterOperator.NotEqual => AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.FilterOperator_NotEquals, 
				DataColumnFilterOperator.GreaterThan => AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.FilterOperator_GreaterThan, 
				DataColumnFilterOperator.GreaterThanOrEqual => AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.FilterOperator_GreaterThanOrEqualTo, 
				DataColumnFilterOperator.LessThan => AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.FilterOperator_LessThan, 
				DataColumnFilterOperator.LessThanOrEqual => AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.FilterOperator_LessThanOrEqualTo, 
				DataColumnFilterOperator.Contains => AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.FilterOperator_Contains, 
				DataColumnFilterOperator.NotContains => AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.FilterOperator_DoesNotContain, 
				DataColumnFilterOperator.StartsWith => AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.FilterOperator_StartsWith, 
				DataColumnFilterOperator.NotStartsWith => AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.FilterOperator_DoesNotStartWith, 
				DataColumnFilterOperator.EndsWith => AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.FilterOperator_EndsWith, 
				DataColumnFilterOperator.NotEndsWith => AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.FilterOperator_DoesNotEndWith, 
				_ => null, 
			};
		}
	}
	internal class FreezingHelper
	{
		private readonly GridControl _gc;

		public FreezingHelper(GridControl gc)
		{
			if (gc == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("gc");
			}
			_gc = gc;
		}

		public bool IsColumnFrozen(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (IsReady(column))
			{
				return column.TargetGridColumn.DisplayIndex < _gc.DataGrid.FrozenColumnCount;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void FreezeColumn(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (IsReady(column) && column.TargetGridColumn.DisplayIndex >= _gc.DataGrid.FrozenColumnCount)
			{
				column.IsFrozen = true;
				column.TargetGridColumn.DisplayIndex = _gc.DataGrid.FrozenColumnCount;
				UpdateFrozenColumnCount(_gc.DataGrid.FrozenColumnCount + 1);
			}
		}

		public void UnfreezeColumn(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (IsReady(column) && column.TargetGridColumn.DisplayIndex < _gc.DataGrid.FrozenColumnCount)
			{
				column.IsFrozen = false;
				column.TargetGridColumn.DisplayIndex = Math.Max(_gc.DataGrid.FrozenColumnCount - 1, _gc.DataSource.Columns.IndexOf(column));
				UpdateFrozenColumnCount(_gc.DataGrid.FrozenColumnCount - 1);
			}
		}

		public void Initialize()
		{
			if (_gc.DataSource != null)
			{
				UpdateFrozenColumnCount(_gc.DataSource.Columns.Count((DataColumn x) => x.IsFrozen));
			}
		}

		public void UpdateFrozenColumnCount(int frozenColumnCount)
		{
			if (_gc.DataGrid != null && frozenColumnCount >= 0)
			{
				_gc.DataGrid.FrozenColumnCount = frozenColumnCount;
				UpdateColumnFrozenLine();
			}
		}

		internal void UpdateColumnFrozenLine()
		{
			Border columnFrozenLine = _gc.ColumnFrozenLine;
			GridControlDataGrid dataGrid = _gc.DataGrid;
			if (columnFrozenLine == null || dataGrid == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (dataGrid.Visibility != Visibility.Visible || dataGrid.FrozenColumnCount <= 0)
			{
				columnFrozenLine.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				return;
			}
			double num = (((dataGrid.HeadersVisibility & DataGridHeadersVisibility.Row) == DataGridHeadersVisibility.Row) ? dataGrid.RowHeaderActualWidth : 0.0);
			for (int i = 0; i < dataGrid.FrozenColumnCount; i++)
			{
				DataGridColumn dataGridColumn = dataGrid.ColumnFromDisplayIndex(i);
				if (dataGridColumn.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					num += dataGridColumn.ActualWidth;
				}
			}
			columnFrozenLine.Margin = new Thickness(num - 1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
			columnFrozenLine.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
		}

		private bool IsReady(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (column != null && column.TargetGridColumn != null && _gc.DataGrid != null)
			{
				return _gc.DataSource != null;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public static class GridControlHelper
	{
		public static T GetAncestor<T>(this DependencyObject descendant, DependencyObject upperLimit = null) where T : DependencyObject
		{
			if (descendant != null)
			{
				DependencyObject dependencyObject = descendant;
				while (dependencyObject != null && dependencyObject != upperLimit)
				{
					if (dependencyObject is T result)
					{
						return result;
					}
					DependencyObject dependencyObject2 = null;
					if (dependencyObject is Visual)
					{
						dependencyObject2 = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject);
					}
					if (dependencyObject2 == null)
					{
						dependencyObject2 = LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject);
					}
					dependencyObject = dependencyObject2;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static GridControlCell GetCellControl(this MouseEventArgs e, GridControl gc)
		{
			if (e == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("e");
			}
			if (gc == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("gc");
			}
			return (e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject).GetAncestor<GridControlCell>(gc);
		}

		public static IDataCell GetCellData(this DataGridCellInfo cellInfo)
		{
			if (cellInfo.IsValid && cellInfo.Item != null && cellInfo.Column != null)
			{
				DataRow dataRow = cellInfo.Item as DataRow;
				GridControlColumn gridControlColumn = cellInfo.Column as GridControlColumn;
				if (dataRow != null && gridControlColumn != null)
				{
					return dataRow[gridControlColumn.DataColumn];
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static bool IsPlaceHolder(this DataGridCellInfo cellInfo)
		{
			if (cellInfo.IsValid)
			{
				return cellInfo.Column is PlaceholderColumn;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool IsPlaceHolder(this DataGridColumn column)
		{
			return column is PlaceholderColumn;
		}

		public static DataCellInfo GetDataCellInfo(this DataGridCellInfo cellInfo)
		{
			return cellInfo.GetDataCellInfos().FirstOrDefault();
		}

		public static IEnumerable<DataCellInfo> GetDataCellInfos(this DataGridCellInfo cellInfo)
		{
			if (!(cellInfo.Item is DataRow row))
			{
				yield break;
			}
			foreach (DataColumn dataColumn in cellInfo.GetDataColumns())
			{
				yield return new DataCellInfo(row, dataColumn);
			}
		}

		public static IEnumerable<DataColumn> GetDataColumns(this DataGridCellInfo cellInfo)
		{
			if (!cellInfo.IsValid || cellInfo.Item == null || !(cellInfo.Item is DataRow) || cellInfo.Column == null)
			{
				yield break;
			}
			if (cellInfo.Column is GridControlColumn)
			{
				yield return ((GridControlColumn)cellInfo.Column).DataColumn;
			}
			else
			{
				if (!cellInfo.IsPlaceHolder())
				{
					yield break;
				}
				PlaceholderColumn placeholderColumn = cellInfo.Column as PlaceholderColumn;
				foreach (DataColumn includedColumn in placeholderColumn.GetIncludedColumns())
				{
					yield return includedColumn;
				}
			}
		}

		public static object GetDirectValueAt(DataRow row, DataColumn column)
		{
			if (row == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("row");
			}
			if (column == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("column");
			}
			if (row.DataTable != null && row.DataTable.GridControl != null)
			{
				return row.DataTable.GridControl.InternalCellValueHelper.GetCellValue(row, column);
			}
			if (column.TargetGridColumn != null && column.TargetGridColumn.GridControlOwner != null)
			{
				return column.TargetGridColumn.GridControlOwner.InternalCellValueHelper.GetCellValue(row, column);
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static DataGridRow GetDataGridRow(this DataGridRowHeader header, DataGrid grid)
		{
			if (header == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("header");
			}
			if (grid == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("grid");
			}
			return header.GetAncestor<DataGridRow>(grid);
		}

		public static T BisectionFind<T, C>(this IEnumerable<T> sequenceList, Func<T, C> searchMethod, C target) where T : class where C : IComparable<C>
		{
			if (sequenceList == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("sequenceList");
			}
			if (searchMethod == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("searchMethod");
			}
			List<T> list = sequenceList.ToList();
			if (list.Count <= 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (list.Count == 1)
			{
				T val = list[0];
				if (BisectionCompares(val, searchMethod, target) == 0)
				{
					return val;
				}
				return null;
			}
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = list.Count - 1;
			T val2 = list[0];
			int num3 = BisectionCompares(val2, searchMethod, target);
			if (num3 == 0)
			{
				return val2;
			}
			if (num3 > 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			T val3 = list[num2];
			int num4 = BisectionCompares(val3, searchMethod, target);
			if (num4 == 0)
			{
				return val3;
			}
			if (num4 < 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			while (num <= num2)
			{
				int num5 = (num + num2) / 2;
				T val4 = list[num5];
				int num6 = BisectionCompares(val4, searchMethod, target);
				if (num6 == 0)
				{
					return val4;
				}
				if (num6 > 0)
				{
					num2 = num5 - 1;
				}
				else
				{
					num = num5 + 1;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static int BisectionFindPositionInsert<T, C>(this IList<T> sequenceList, Func<T, C> searchMethod, C target) where C : IComparable<C>
		{
			if (sequenceList == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("sequenceList");
			}
			if (searchMethod == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("searchMethod");
			}
			if (sequenceList.Count <= 0)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			if (sequenceList.Count == 1)
			{
				if (BisectionCompares(sequenceList[0], searchMethod, target) <= 0)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				return 1;
			}
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = sequenceList.Count - 1;
			if (BisectionCompares(sequenceList[num], searchMethod, target) >= 0)
			{
				return num;
			}
			if (BisectionCompares(sequenceList[num2], searchMethod, target) <= 0)
			{
				return num2;
			}
			while (num <= num2)
			{
				int num3 = (num + num2) / 2;
				int num4 = BisectionCompares(sequenceList[num3], searchMethod, target);
				if (num4 == 0)
				{
					return Math.Min(sequenceList.Count, Math.Max(0, num3));
				}
				if (num4 > 0)
				{
					num2 = num3 - 1;
				}
				else
				{
					num = num3 + 1;
				}
			}
			return Math.Min(sequenceList.Count, Math.Max(0, num2));
		}

		private static int BisectionCompares<T, C>(T obj, Func<T, C> searchMethod, C target) where C : IComparable<C>
		{
			C val = searchMethod(obj);
			if (val == null)
			{
				if (target == null)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				return -1;
			}
			return val.CompareTo(target);
		}
	}
	internal class HeaderColumnSpanHelper
	{
		private readonly GridControl _gc;

		private Dictionary<DataColumn, List<DataColumn>> _columnSpanGroups;

		private Dictionary<DataColumn, DataColumn> _slaverToLeaders;

		public bool HasColumnSpan { get; private set; }

		public HeaderColumnSpanHelper(GridControl gc)
		{
			if (gc == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("gc");
			}
			_gc = gc;
		}

		public void PrepareColumnSpanInfomation(bool hasColumnSpanHeaderSetting)
		{
			_columnSpanGroups = null;
			HasColumnSpan = false;
			if (!hasColumnSpanHeaderSetting)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_gc.DataSource != null)
			{
				List<DataColumn> list = _gc.DataSource.Columns.Where((DataColumn x) => x.Visibility == Visibility.Visible).ToList();
				for (int num = 0; num < list.Count; num++)
				{
					DataColumn dataColumn = list[num];
					if (dataColumn.HeaderColumnSpan <= 1)
					{
						continue;
					}
					int num2 = Math.Min(list.Count - 1 - num, dataColumn.HeaderColumnSpan - 1);
					if (num2 > 0)
					{
						HasColumnSpan = true;
						dataColumn.CanChangeVisibility = false;
						dataColumn.CanSorting = false;
						if (_columnSpanGroups == null)
						{
							_columnSpanGroups = new Dictionary<DataColumn, List<DataColumn>>();
						}
						if (_slaverToLeaders == null)
						{
							_slaverToLeaders = new Dictionary<DataColumn, DataColumn>();
						}
						List<DataColumn> list2 = new List<DataColumn>();
						for (int num3 = 0; num3 < num2; num3++)
						{
							DataColumn dataColumn2 = list[num + 1 + num3];
							dataColumn2.CanChangeVisibility = false;
							dataColumn2.CanSorting = false;
							list2.Add(dataColumn2);
							_slaverToLeaders.Add(dataColumn2, dataColumn);
						}
						_columnSpanGroups.Add(dataColumn, list2);
						num += num2;
					}
				}
			}
			if (HasColumnSpan)
			{
				_gc.AllowColumnReorder = false;
				_gc.AllowRecordFiltering = false;
				_gc.AllowColumnFreeze = false;
				_gc.AllowColumnChooser = false;
			}
		}

		public bool IsColumnSpanLeader(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (HasColumnSpan && _columnSpanGroups.ContainsKey(column))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool IsColumnSpanSlaver(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (HasColumnSpan && _slaverToLeaders.ContainsKey(column))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool IsLastColumnSpanSlaver(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (IsColumnSpanSlaver(column))
			{
				return _columnSpanGroups[_slaverToLeaders[column]].LastOrDefault() == column;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void ProcessColumnSpanHeaderWidth(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (column == null || !HasColumnSpan || _slaverToLeaders == null || _columnSpanGroups == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			DataColumn dataColumn = null;
			if (_columnSpanGroups.ContainsKey(column))
			{
				dataColumn = column;
			}
			else if (_slaverToLeaders.ContainsKey(column))
			{
				dataColumn = _slaverToLeaders[column];
			}
			if (dataColumn == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<DataColumn> list = _columnSpanGroups[dataColumn];
			DataColumn dataColumn2 = list[list.Count - 1];
			if (dataColumn2.HeaderControl != null)
			{
				double num = dataColumn.TargetGridColumn.ActualWidth;
				for (int i = 0; i < list.Count - 1; i++)
				{
					num += list[i].TargetGridColumn.ActualWidth;
				}
				dataColumn2.HeaderControl.Margin = new Thickness(0.0 - num, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
			}
		}

		public DataColumn GetColumnSpanLeader(DataColumn slaver)
		{
			return _slaverToLeaders[slaver];
		}

		public IList<DataColumn> GetColumnSpanSlavers(DataColumn leader)
		{
			return _columnSpanGroups[leader];
		}
	}
	internal class HeaderControlDragAndDropHelper
	{
		private const double GridLineThumbWidth = 4.0;

		private const double SplitterDraggingMinimumLength = 4.0;

		private readonly DataColumnHeaderControl _headerControl;

		private bool _isDraggingSplitter;

		private bool _isDownAtFilterIcon;

		private Point _mouseDownPosition;

		private DataColumn DataColumnOwner => _headerControl.Column;

		private GridControlColumn GridControlColumnOwner => DataColumnOwner.TargetGridColumn;

		private GridControl GridControlOwner => GridControlColumnOwner.GridControlOwner;

		private bool IsReady
		{
			get
			{
				if (DataColumnOwner != null)
				{
					return GridControlColumnOwner != null;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public bool IsLastTimeSelected { get; private set; }

		public HeaderControlDragAndDropHelper(DataColumnHeaderControl headerControl)
		{
			if (headerControl == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("headerControl");
			}
			_headerControl = headerControl;
		}

		public void OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.ClickCount > 1 || !IsReady || !IsMouseOnSelectionArea(e))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (IsMouseOnSplitter(e))
			{
				_headerControl.CaptureMouse();
				_isDraggingSplitter = true;
				_mouseDownPosition = e.GetPosition(_headerControl);
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			else if (IsMouseOnFilterIcon(e))
			{
				_isDownAtFilterIcon = true;
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			else
			{
				if (!GridControlOwner.AllowSelectColumn)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (!Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.LeftCtrl) && !Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.RightCtrl) && !Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.LeftShift) && !Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.RightShift))
				{
					SetLastSelectedFlag();
				}
				else if (GetLastSelectedColumn() == null)
				{
					SetLastSelectedFlag();
				}
				DataColumn dataColumnOwner = DataColumnOwner;
				GridControl gridControlOwner = GridControlOwner;
				bool isColumnSelected = dataColumnOwner.IsColumnSelected;
				gridControlOwner.InternalSelectionHelper.LockSelectionChanged();
				if (Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.LeftCtrl) || Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.RightCtrl))
				{
					if (gridControlOwner.InternalSelectionHelper.IsColumnSelected(dataColumnOwner))
					{
						gridControlOwner.InternalSelectionHelper.DeselectColumn(dataColumnOwner);
					}
					else
					{
						gridControlOwner.InternalSelectionHelper.SelectColumn(dataColumnOwner);
						SetLastSelectedFlag();
						FocusOnTopCell(dataColumnOwner);
					}
				}
				else if (Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.LeftShift) || Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.RightShift))
				{
					DataColumn lastSelectedColumn = GetLastSelectedColumn();
					if (lastSelectedColumn == null)
					{
						gridControlOwner.InternalSelectionHelper.SelectColumn(dataColumnOwner);
					}
					else
					{
						SelectColumnsBetween(lastSelectedColumn, dataColumnOwner);
					}
					FocusOnTopCell(dataColumnOwner);
				}
				else
				{
					gridControlOwner.InternalSelectionHelper.DeselectAll();
					gridControlOwner.InternalSelectionHelper.SelectColumn(dataColumnOwner);
					FocusOnTopCell(dataColumnOwner);
				}
				gridControlOwner.InternalSelectionHelper.UnlockSelectionChanged(raiseEvent: true);
				if (!isColumnSelected || !gridControlOwner.AllowColumnReorder)
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private void FocusOnTopCell(DataColumn column)
		{
			GridControlOwner.InternalSelectionHelper.FocusOnTopFirstCell(column);
		}

		public void OnPreviewMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.LeftButton == MouseButtonState.Released)
			{
				if (!IsMouseOnSelectionArea(e))
				{
					_headerControl.Cursor = Cursors.Arrow;
				}
				if (IsMouseOnSplitter(e))
				{
					_headerControl.Cursor = Cursors.SizeWE;
				}
				else if (IsMouseOnFilterIcon(e))
				{
					_headerControl.Cursor = Cursors.Hand;
				}
				else
				{
					_headerControl.Cursor = Cursors.Arrow;
				}
			}
			else if (_isDraggingSplitter)
			{
				_headerControl.Cursor = Cursors.SizeWE;
			}
			else
			{
				DataColumn lastSelectedColumn = GetLastSelectedColumn();
				if (lastSelectedColumn != null && DataColumnOwner != null)
				{
					SelectColumnsBetween(lastSelectedColumn, DataColumnOwner);
				}
			}
		}

		public void OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!IsReady)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_isDraggingSplitter)
			{
				double length = e.GetPosition(_headerControl).X - _mouseDownPosition.X;
				DoDragAndDropSplitter(length);
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			else if (_isDownAtFilterIcon)
			{
				if (IsMouseOnFilterIcon(e))
				{
					new DataColumnFilterPopup(DataColumnOwner)
					{
						PlacementTarget = _headerControl,
						Placement = PlacementMode.MousePoint,
						MinWidth = GridControlColumnOwner.ActualWidth,
						IsOpen = true
					};
				}
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			Reset();
		}

		public void OnPreviewMouseRightButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (DataColumnOwner != null && GridControlColumnOwner != null && GridControlOwner != null && !GridControlOwner.SelectedColumns.Contains(DataColumnOwner))
			{
				GridControlOwner.DeselectAll();
				GridControlOwner.SelectColumn(DataColumnOwner);
			}
		}

		public void OnPreviewMouseDoubleClick(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (IsReady && IsMouseOnSelectionArea(e) && IsMouseOnSplitter(e))
			{
				DataColumn columnToAutoSize = GetColumnToAutoSize(e);
				if (columnToAutoSize != null && columnToAutoSize.TargetGridColumn != null)
				{
					columnToAutoSize.TargetGridColumn.Width = new DataGridLength(0.0, DataGridLengthUnitType.Auto);
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private DataColumn GetColumnToAutoSize(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			Point position = e.GetPosition(_headerControl);
			if (position.X <= 4.0 && position.X <= _headerControl.ActualWidth / 2.0)
			{
				return GetLeftColumnToDrag();
			}
			return DataColumnOwner;
		}

		private void SelectColumnsBetween(DataColumn fromColumn, DataColumn toColumn)
		{
			int logicalDisplayIndex = fromColumn.LogicalDisplayIndex;
			int logicalDisplayIndex2 = toColumn.LogicalDisplayIndex;
			int num = Math.Min(logicalDisplayIndex, logicalDisplayIndex2);
			int num2 = Math.Max(logicalDisplayIndex, logicalDisplayIndex2);
			GridControlOwner.InternalSelectionHelper.LockSelectionChanged();
			foreach (DataColumn column in GridControlOwner.DataSource.Columns)
			{
				int logicalDisplayIndex3 = column.LogicalDisplayIndex;
				if (logicalDisplayIndex3 >= num && logicalDisplayIndex3 <= num2)
				{
					GridControlOwner.InternalSelectionHelper.SelectColumn(column);
				}
			}
			GridControlOwner.InternalSelectionHelper.UnlockSelectionChanged(raiseEvent: true);
		}

		private void DoDragAndDropSplitter(double length)
		{
			if (Math.Abs(length) < 4.0)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_mouseDownPosition.X <= 4.0 && _mouseDownPosition.X <= _headerControl.ActualWidth / 2.0)
			{
				DataColumn leftColumnToDrag = GetLeftColumnToDrag();
				if (leftColumnToDrag != null)
				{
					if (!GridControlOwner.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.HasColumnSpan)
					{
						UpdateColumnWithAddtionalWidth(leftColumnToDrag, length);
					}
					else if (!GridControlOwner.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.IsColumnSpanLeader(leftColumnToDrag) && !GridControlOwner.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.IsColumnSpanSlaver(leftColumnToDrag))
					{
						UpdateColumnWithAddtionalWidth(leftColumnToDrag, length);
					}
					else
					{
						TryShareAdditionalColumnWidth(leftColumnToDrag, length);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (!GridControlOwner.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.HasColumnSpan)
			{
				UpdateColumnWithAddtionalWidth(DataColumnOwner, length);
			}
			else if (!GridControlOwner.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.IsColumnSpanLeader(DataColumnOwner) && !GridControlOwner.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.IsColumnSpanSlaver(DataColumnOwner))
			{
				UpdateColumnWithAddtionalWidth(DataColumnOwner, length);
			}
			else
			{
				TryShareAdditionalColumnWidth(DataColumnOwner, length);
			}
		}

		private DataColumn GetLeftColumnToDrag()
		{
			if (!GridControlOwner.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.HasColumnSpan)
			{
				return GetColumnAt(-1);
			}
			if (GridControlOwner.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.IsColumnSpanLeader(DataColumnOwner))
			{
				return GetColumnAt(-1);
			}
			if (!GridControlOwner.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.IsColumnSpanSlaver(DataColumnOwner))
			{
				return GetColumnAt(-1);
			}
			DataColumn columnSpanLeader = GridControlOwner.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.GetColumnSpanLeader(DataColumnOwner);
			return GetColumnAt(columnSpanLeader, -1);
		}

		private void UpdateColumnWithAddtionalWidth(DataColumn column, double additionalWidth)
		{
			if (column.TargetGridColumn != null)
			{
				double num = additionalWidth;
				if (num <= 0.0 - column.TargetGridColumn.ActualWidth)
				{
					num = 0.0 - column.TargetGridColumn.ActualWidth;
				}
				column.TargetGridColumn.Width = new DataGridLength(column.TargetGridColumn.ActualWidth + num);
			}
		}

		private void TryShareAdditionalColumnWidth(DataColumn slaverColumn, double additionalWidth)
		{
			HeaderColumnSpanHelper internalHeaderColumnSpanHelper = GridControlOwner.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper;
			if (internalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.IsColumnSpanSlaver(slaverColumn))
			{
				DataColumn columnSpanLeader = internalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.GetColumnSpanLeader(slaverColumn);
				IList<DataColumn> columnSpanSlavers = internalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.GetColumnSpanSlavers(columnSpanLeader);
				List<DataColumn> list = new List<DataColumn>();
				list.Add(columnSpanLeader);
				list.AddRange(columnSpanSlavers);
				double additionalWidth2 = additionalWidth / (double)list.Count;
				{
					foreach (DataColumn item in list)
					{
						UpdateColumnWithAddtionalWidth(item, additionalWidth2);
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			UpdateColumnWithAddtionalWidth(slaverColumn, additionalWidth);
		}

		private void Reset()
		{
			if (_headerControl.IsMouseCaptured)
			{
				_headerControl.ReleaseMouseCapture();
			}
			_isDraggingSplitter = false;
			_isDownAtFilterIcon = false;
		}

		private DataColumn GetLastSelectedColumn()
		{
			if (GridControlOwner.LastSelectedColumnIndex >= 0 && GridControlOwner.DataSource.Columns.Count > GridControlOwner.LastSelectedColumnIndex)
			{
				DataColumn dataColumn = GridControlOwner.DataSource.Columns[GridControlOwner.LastSelectedColumnIndex];
				if (dataColumn.IsColumnSelected)
				{
					return dataColumn;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private void SetLastSelectedFlag()
		{
			GridControlOwner.LastSelectedColumnIndex = DataColumnOwner.Index;
		}

		private DataColumn GetColumnAt(int offset)
		{
			return GetColumnAt(DataColumnOwner, offset);
		}

		private DataColumn GetColumnAt(DataColumn baseColumn, int offset)
		{
			int displayIndex = baseColumn.DisplayIndex + offset;
			return GridControlOwner.ColumnFromDisplayIndex(displayIndex);
		}

		private bool IsMouseOnSplitter(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(_headerControl) as FrameworkElement;
			if (frameworkElement == null)
			{
				frameworkElement = _headerControl;
			}
			Point position = e.GetPosition(frameworkElement);
			if (IsReady && position.X >= 0.0 && position.X <= frameworkElement.ActualWidth && position.Y >= 0.0 && position.Y <= frameworkElement.ActualHeight && ((position.X < 4.0 && DataColumnOwner.LogicalDisplayIndex > 0) || position.X > frameworkElement.ActualWidth - 4.0))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool IsMouseOnFilterIcon(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (DataColumnOwner != null && DataColumnOwner.Filter.Visibility == Visibility.Collapsed && GridControlOwner != null && GridControlOwner.AllowRecordFiltering)
			{
				Point position = e.GetPosition(_headerControl);
				if (position.X <= _headerControl.ActualWidth - 4.0 && position.X >= _headerControl.ActualWidth - 18.0 && position.Y >= _headerControl.ActualHeight / 2.0 - 7.0 && position.Y <= _headerControl.ActualHeight / 2.0 + 7.0)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool IsMouseOnSelectionArea(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if ((e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject).GetAncestor<DataColumnFilterControl>(_headerControl) != null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if ((e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject).GetAncestor<ValueEditor>(_headerControl) != null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if ((e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject).GetAncestor<ComboBox>(_headerControl) != null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if ((e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject).GetAncestor<TextBox>(_headerControl) != null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			Control ancestor = (e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject).GetAncestor<Control>(_headerControl);
			if (ancestor != null && ancestor != _headerControl && ancestor.Focusable)
			{
				return false;
			}
			AdornerDecorator ancestor2 = (e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject).GetAncestor<AdornerDecorator>(_headerControl);
			if (ancestor2 != null && ancestor2.Parent is Decorator reference && VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(reference) is FrameworkElement frameworkElement && frameworkElement.Parent is Popup)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}
	}
	internal static class HeaderControlHelper
	{
		private static PathGeometry _sortAscendingGeometry;

		private static PathGeometry _sortDescendingGeometry;

		public static Size GetSizeToRenderText(DataColumnHeaderControl control, Size availableSize)
		{
			if (control.Column != null)
			{
				string header = control.Column.Header;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(header))
				{
					FormattedText formattedText = new FormattedText(header, Thread.CurrentThread.CurrentCulture, FlowDirection.LeftToRight, new Typeface(control.FontFamily, control.FontStyle, control.FontWeight, control.FontStretch), control.FontSize, control.Foreground);
					if (!double.IsInfinity(availableSize.Width))
					{
						formattedText.MaxTextWidth = Math.Max(0.0, availableSize.Width - 4.0);
					}
					return new Size(Math.Floor(formattedText.Width + 10.0), Math.Floor(formattedText.Height + 14.0));
				}
			}
			return new Size(0.0, 0.0);
		}

		public static void RenderText(DataColumnHeaderControl control, DrawingContext dc)
		{
			if (control.Column != null)
			{
				string header = control.Column.Header;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(header))
				{
					FormattedText formattedText = new FormattedText(header, Thread.CurrentThread.CurrentCulture, FlowDirection.LeftToRight, new Typeface(control.FontFamily, control.FontStyle, control.FontWeight, control.FontStretch), control.FontSize, control.Foreground);
					formattedText.MaxTextWidth = Math.Max(0.0, control.ActualWidth - 4.0);
					formattedText.TextAlignment = TextAlignment.Center;
					double num = 0.0;
					num = ((control.Column.HeaderTextVerticalAlignment == VerticalAlignment.Top) ? 2.0 : ((control.Column.HeaderTextVerticalAlignment != VerticalAlignment.Bottom) ? ((control.ActualHeight - formattedText.Height) / 2.0) : (control.ActualHeight - formattedText.Height - 2.0)));
					Point origin = new Point(0.0, num);
					dc.DrawText(formattedText, origin);
				}
			}
		}

		public static void RenderBackground(DataColumnHeaderControl control, DrawingContext dc)
		{
			if (control.IsHovered && control.MouseOverBrush != null)
			{
				dc.DrawRectangle(control.MouseOverBrush, new Pen(control.BorderBrush, 1.0), new Rect(control.RenderSize));
			}
			else if (control.Background != null)
			{
				dc.DrawRectangle(control.Background, new Pen(control.BorderBrush, 1.0), new Rect(control.RenderSize));
			}
			if (control.Column != null && control.Column.HasSelection && control.ColumnSelectionBrush != null && control.ActualWidth > 8.0 && control.ActualHeight > 4.0)
			{
				dc.DrawRectangle(control.ColumnSelectionBrush, null, new Rect(4.0, 2.0, control.ActualWidth - 8.0, control.ActualHeight - 4.0));
			}
		}

		public static void RenderSortDirectionFlag(DataColumnHeaderControl control, DrawingContext dc)
		{
			if (control.Column != null && control.Column.TargetGridColumn != null && control.ActualWidth > 14.0 && control.ActualHeight > 10.0)
			{
				ListSortDirection? sortDirection = control.Column.TargetGridColumn.GridControlOwner.SortingInfo.GetSortDirection(control.Column);
				if (sortDirection.HasValue)
				{
					TranslateTransform translateTransform = new TranslateTransform((control.ActualWidth - 8.0) * 0.5, 1.0);
					translateTransform.Freeze();
					dc.PushTransform(translateTransform);
					dc.DrawGeometry(Brushes.LightBlue, null, GetSortGeometry(sortDirection.Value));
					dc.Pop();
				}
			}
		}

		public static void RenderFilterFlag(DataColumnHeaderControl control, DrawingContext dc)
		{
			if (control.Column != null && control.Column.TargetGridColumn != null && control.Column.Filter.Visibility != Visibility.Visible && control.Column.TargetGridColumn.GridControlOwner.AllowRecordFiltering && control.ActualWidth > 40.0 && control.ActualHeight > 20.0)
			{
				ImageSource imageSource = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(control.Column.Filter.SelectedOperand) ? ImageSourceHelper.Filter : ImageSourceHelper.FilterWithValue);
				dc.DrawImage(imageSource, new Rect(control.ActualWidth - 18.0, control.ActualHeight / 2.0 - 7.0, 14.0, 14.0));
			}
		}

		private static Geometry GetSortGeometry(ListSortDirection sortDirection)
		{
			if (sortDirection == ListSortDirection.Ascending)
			{
				if (_sortAscendingGeometry == null)
				{
					PathFigure pathFigure = new PathFigure();
					pathFigure.StartPoint = new Point(0.0, 4.0);
					LineSegment lineSegment = new LineSegment(new Point(4.0, 0.0), isStroked: false);
					lineSegment.Freeze();
					pathFigure.Segments.Add(lineSegment);
					lineSegment = new LineSegment(new Point(8.0, 4.0), isStroked: false);
					lineSegment.Freeze();
					pathFigure.Segments.Add(lineSegment);
					pathFigure.IsClosed = true;
					pathFigure.Freeze();
					_sortAscendingGeometry = new PathGeometry();
					_sortAscendingGeometry.Figures.Add(pathFigure);
					_sortAscendingGeometry.Freeze();
				}
				return _sortAscendingGeometry;
			}
			if (_sortDescendingGeometry == null)
			{
				PathFigure pathFigure2 = new PathFigure();
				pathFigure2.StartPoint = new Point(0.0, 0.0);
				LineSegment lineSegment2 = new LineSegment(new Point(8.0, 0.0), isStroked: false);
				lineSegment2.Freeze();
				pathFigure2.Segments.Add(lineSegment2);
				lineSegment2 = new LineSegment(new Point(4.0, 4.0), isStroked: false);
				lineSegment2.Freeze();
				pathFigure2.Segments.Add(lineSegment2);
				pathFigure2.IsClosed = true;
				pathFigure2.Freeze();
				_sortDescendingGeometry = new PathGeometry();
				_sortDescendingGeometry.Figures.Add(pathFigure2);
				_sortDescendingGeometry.Freeze();
			}
			return _sortDescendingGeometry;
		}
	}
	public static class ImageSourceHelper
	{
		private static readonly Dictionary<string, ImageSource> _imageSources = new Dictionary<string, ImageSource>();

		public static readonly ImageSource EqualTo = GetCustomImageSource("Filter_Equals.png");

		public static readonly ImageSource NotEqualTo = GetCustomImageSource("Filter_NotEquals.png");

		public static readonly ImageSource GreaterThan = GetCustomImageSource("Filter_GreaterThan.png");

		public static readonly ImageSource GreaterThanOrEqualTo = GetCustomImageSource("Filter_GreaterThanOrEqualTo.png");

		public static readonly ImageSource LessThan = GetCustomImageSource("Filter_LessThan.png");

		public static readonly ImageSource LessThanOrEqualTo = GetCustomImageSource("Filter_LessThanOrEqualTo.png");

		public static readonly ImageSource StartsWith = GetCustomImageSource("Filter_StartsWith.png");

		public static readonly ImageSource DoesNotStartWith = GetCustomImageSource("Filter_DoesNotStartWith.png");

		public static readonly ImageSource EndsWith = GetCustomImageSource("Filter_EndsWith.png");

		public static readonly ImageSource DoesNotEndWith = GetCustomImageSource("Filter_DoesNotEndWith.png");

		public static readonly ImageSource Contains = GetCustomImageSource("Filter_Contains.png");

		public static readonly ImageSource DoesNotContain = GetCustomImageSource("Filter_DoesNotContain.png");

		public static readonly ImageSource Filter = GetCustomImageSource("Filter.png");

		public static readonly ImageSource FilterWithValue = GetCustomImageSource("FilterWithValue.png");

		public static readonly ImageSource FilterClearButton = GetCustomImageSource("FilterClearButton.png");

		public static readonly ImageSource FieldChooser16 = GetCustomImageSource("FieldChooser16.ico");

		public static readonly ImageSource SortAscend16 = GetCustomImageSource("SortAscend16.ico");

		public static readonly ImageSource SortClear16 = GetCustomImageSource("SortClear16.ico");

		public static readonly ImageSource SortDescend16 = GetCustomImageSource("SortDescend16.ico");

		public static readonly ImageSource RecordsFreezeColumns16 = GetCustomImageSource("RecordsFreezeColumns16.ico");

		public static readonly ImageSource FileNew32 = GetCustomImageSource("FileNew32.ico");

		public static readonly ImageSource FileSave16 = GetCustomImageSource("FileSave16.ico");

		public static readonly ImageSource Copy16 = GetCustomImageSource("Copy16.ico");

		public static readonly ImageSource Paste16 = GetCustomImageSource("Paste16.ico");

		private static ImageSource GetCustomImageSource(string imageName)
		{
			if (!_imageSources.ContainsKey(imageName))
			{
				string fullName = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().FullName;
				string uriString = "pack://application:,,,/" + fullName + ";component/Images/" + imageName;
				BitmapImage bitmapImage = new BitmapImage();
				bitmapImage.BeginInit();
				bitmapImage.UriSource = new Uri(uriString, UriKind.Absolute);
				bitmapImage.EndInit();
				bitmapImage.Freeze();
				_imageSources[imageName] = bitmapImage;
			}
			return _imageSources[imageName];
		}
	}
	internal static class KeyboardAndMouseHelper
	{
		public static void DoPreviewKeyDown(GridControl gc, KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.A && Keyboard.Modifiers == ModifierKeys.Control)
			{
				gc.SelectAll();
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			else
			{
				if (ShouldSkipKey(e.Key) || !gc.BoundRows.Any())
				{
					return;
				}
				GridControlCell ancestor = (e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject).GetAncestor<GridControlCell>(gc);
				if (ancestor == null || (!ancestor.IsInEditMode && (e.Key == Key.Home || e.Key == Key.End)))
				{
					return;
				}
				if (e.Key == Key.Return)
				{
					if (ancestor.IsKeyboardFocusWithin && ancestor.IsInEditMode)
					{
						if (ancestor.IsDropDownOpen)
						{
							return;
						}
						int num = gc.BoundRows.Count();
						bool flag = false;
						if (ancestor.Cell != null && gc.DataSource != null)
						{
							flag = ancestor.Cell.Row == gc.BoundRows.LastOrDefault();
						}
						if (flag)
						{
							ancestor.EndEditModeIfPossible(commitChange: true);
						}
						ancestor.Dispatcher.Invoke((Action<bool, int>)delegate(bool oldIsLast, int oldCount)
						{
							if (!oldIsLast)
							{
								EnterToCell(gc, 1, 0, allowRecycle: true);
							}
							else if (gc.DataSource.Rows.Count == oldCount + 1)
							{
								EnterToCell(gc, 1, 0, allowRecycle: true);
							}
							else
							{
								DataCellInfo currentCell = gc.CurrentCell;
								MoveToCell(gc, currentCell.Row, currentCell.Column);
							}
						}, DispatcherPriority.Loaded, flag, num);
						e.Handled = true;
					}
					else
					{
						e.Handled = EnterToCell(gc, 1, 0, allowRecycle: true);
					}
				}
				else if (e.Key == Key.Tab)
				{
					if (Keyboard.Modifiers == ModifierKeys.Shift)
					{
						e.Handled = EnterToCell(gc, 0, -1, allowRecycle: false);
					}
					else
					{
						e.Handled = EnterToCell(gc, 0, 1, allowRecycle: false);
					}
				}
				else if (e.Key == Key.Up)
				{
					e.Handled = EnterToCell(gc, -1, 0, allowRecycle: false);
				}
				else if (e.Key == Key.Down)
				{
					e.Handled = EnterToCell(gc, 1, 0, allowRecycle: false);
				}
				else if (e.Key == Key.Escape)
				{
					ancestor.EndEditModeIfPossible(commitChange: true);
				}
				else if (e.Key == Key.F2)
				{
					if (ancestor.BeginEditModeIfPossible(syncTemplateImmediately: true))
					{
						e.Handled = true;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					ancestor.BeginEditModeIfPossible(syncTemplateImmediately: true);
					if (ancestor.IsKeyboardFocused || !ancestor.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
					{
						ancestor.TryFocusInternalEditor();
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public static bool DoPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(GridControl gc, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			return e.GetCellControl(gc)?.ProcessMouseDownEventForGroup(e) ?? false;
		}

		public static void DoPreviewMouseRightButtonDown(GridControl gc, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControlCell cellControl = e.GetCellControl(gc);
			if (cellControl == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool isInEditMode = cellControl.IsInEditMode;
			if (gc.SelectionUnit != DataGridSelectionUnit.FullRow)
			{
				DataCellInfo dataCellInfo = new DataGridCellInfo(cellControl).GetDataCellInfo();
				if (!dataCellInfo.IsSelectedInGrid(gc))
				{
					gc.SelectCell(dataCellInfo, deselectOthers: true, setCurrent: true);
				}
				else
				{
					gc.CurrentCell = dataCellInfo;
				}
			}
			if (!isInEditMode && cellControl.IsInEditMode)
			{
				cellControl.EndEditModeIfPossible(commitChange: false);
			}
		}

		public static void DoMouseDoubleClick(GridControl gc, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (gc.SortingMode == SortingMode.NotAllowed)
			{
				return;
			}
			DataColumnHeaderControl ancestor = (e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject).GetAncestor<DataColumnHeaderControl>(gc);
			if (ancestor == null || !ancestor.DragAndDropHelper.IsMouseOnSelectionArea(e))
			{
				return;
			}
			DataColumn column = ancestor.Column;
			if (column.CanSorting)
			{
				ListSortDirection? sortDirection = gc.SortingInfo.GetSortDirection(column);
				if (Keyboard.Modifiers != ModifierKeys.Control || gc.SortingMode == SortingMode.AllowSingleColumnSorting)
				{
					gc.SortingInfo.ClearSorts();
				}
				if (!sortDirection.HasValue)
				{
					gc.SortingInfo.SetSort(column, ListSortDirection.Ascending);
				}
				else if (sortDirection.Value == ListSortDirection.Ascending)
				{
					gc.SortingInfo.SetSort(column, ListSortDirection.Descending);
				}
				else
				{
					gc.SortingInfo.SetSort(column, ListSortDirection.Ascending);
				}
				gc.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					gc.InternalSelectionHelper.FocusOnTopFirstCell(column);
				}, DispatcherPriority.DataBind);
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		public static void DoPreviewMouseWheel(GridControl gc, MouseWheelEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!(e.OriginalSource is Visual) || !gc.IsAncestorOf(e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (Keyboard.Modifiers == ModifierKeys.Control)
			{
				if (e.Delta > 0)
				{
					gc.ZoomFactor *= 1.25;
				}
				else
				{
					gc.ZoomFactor *= 0.8;
				}
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			else if (Keyboard.Modifiers == ModifierKeys.Shift)
			{
				if (gc.InternalScrollHelper.ScrollViewer != null)
				{
					double horizontalOffset = gc.InternalScrollHelper.ScrollViewer.HorizontalOffset;
					if (e.Delta > 0)
					{
						gc.InternalScrollHelper.ScrollHorizontally(left: true);
					}
					else
					{
						gc.InternalScrollHelper.ScrollHorizontally(left: false);
					}
					if (gc.InternalScrollHelper.ScrollViewer.HorizontalOffset != horizontalOffset)
					{
						e.Handled = true;
					}
				}
			}
			else if (Keyboard.Modifiers == ModifierKeys.None && gc.InternalScrollHelper.ScrollViewer != null)
			{
				double verticalOffset = gc.InternalScrollHelper.ScrollViewer.VerticalOffset;
				if (e.Delta > 0)
				{
					gc.InternalScrollHelper.ScrollVertically(up: true);
				}
				else
				{
					gc.InternalScrollHelper.ScrollVertically(up: false);
				}
				if (gc.InternalScrollHelper.ScrollViewer.VerticalOffset != verticalOffset)
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public static void DoPreviewMouseDoubleClick(GridControl gc, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControlCell cellControl = e.GetCellControl(gc);
			if (cellControl != null && !cellControl.IsInEditMode && !cellControl.IsEditModeCombo)
			{
				cellControl.BeginEditModeIfPossible(syncTemplateImmediately: true);
			}
		}

		private static bool EnterToCell(GridControl gc, int rowAddition, int columnAddition, bool allowRecycle)
		{
			if (gc == null || gc.DataSource == null || gc.DataGrid == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (gc.CurrentCell.IsEmpty)
			{
				return false;
			}
			DataGridCellInfo currentCell = gc.DataGrid.CurrentCell;
			_ = gc.DataSource;
			DataRow row = gc.CurrentCell.Row;
			switch (rowAddition)
			{
			case 1:
			{
				DataRow row3 = GetRow(gc, row, farward: true, allowRecycle);
				DataGridColumn nextNavigationColumn2 = GetNextNavigationColumn(gc, currentCell, row3, forward: true, allowRecycle);
				if (!gc.SkipReadonlyCellsWhenNavigation)
				{
					return MoveToCell(gc, row3, nextNavigationColumn2);
				}
				DataRow dataRow4 = row3;
				DataGridColumn dataGridColumn3 = nextNavigationColumn2;
				while (row3 != null && nextNavigationColumn2 != null)
				{
					IDataCell dataCell4 = row3[(nextNavigationColumn2 as GridControlColumn).DataColumn];
					if (dataCell4 != null && !dataCell4.IsReadOnly)
					{
						return MoveToCell(gc, row3, nextNavigationColumn2);
					}
					row3 = GetRow(gc, row3, farward: true, allowRecycle);
					nextNavigationColumn2 = GetNextNavigationColumn(gc, new DataGridCellInfo(row3, nextNavigationColumn2), row3, forward: true, allowRecycle);
					if (row3 == dataRow4 && nextNavigationColumn2 == dataGridColumn3)
					{
						return MoveToCell(gc, row3, nextNavigationColumn2);
					}
				}
				return MoveToCell(gc, row3, nextNavigationColumn2);
			}
			case -1:
			{
				DataRow row2 = GetRow(gc, row, farward: false, allowRecycle);
				DataGridColumn nextNavigationColumn = GetNextNavigationColumn(gc, currentCell, row2, forward: false, allowRecycle);
				if (!gc.SkipReadonlyCellsWhenNavigation)
				{
					return MoveToCell(gc, row2, nextNavigationColumn);
				}
				DataRow dataRow3 = row2;
				DataGridColumn dataGridColumn2 = nextNavigationColumn;
				while (row2 != null && nextNavigationColumn != null)
				{
					IDataCell dataCell3 = row2[(nextNavigationColumn as GridControlColumn).DataColumn];
					if (dataCell3 != null && !dataCell3.IsReadOnly)
					{
						return MoveToCell(gc, row2, nextNavigationColumn);
					}
					row2 = GetRow(gc, row2, farward: true, allowRecycle);
					nextNavigationColumn = GetNextNavigationColumn(gc, new DataGridCellInfo(row2, nextNavigationColumn), row2, forward: false, allowRecycle);
					if (row2 == dataRow3 && nextNavigationColumn == dataGridColumn2)
					{
						return MoveToCell(gc, row2, nextNavigationColumn);
					}
				}
				return MoveToCell(gc, row2, nextNavigationColumn);
			}
			default:
				switch (columnAddition)
				{
				case 1:
				{
					DataColumn column = GetColumn(gc, gc.CurrentCell.Column, farward: true, allowRecycle: true);
					DataRow nextNavigationRow2 = GetNextNavigationRow(gc, gc.CurrentCell, column, forward: true, allowRecycle);
					if (!gc.SkipReadonlyCellsWhenNavigation)
					{
						return MoveToCell(gc, nextNavigationRow2, column);
					}
					DataRow dataRow2 = nextNavigationRow2;
					DataColumn dataColumn = column;
					while (nextNavigationRow2 != null && column != null)
					{
						IDataCell dataCell2 = nextNavigationRow2[column];
						if (dataCell2 != null && !dataCell2.IsReadOnly)
						{
							return MoveToCell(gc, nextNavigationRow2, column);
						}
						column = GetColumn(gc, column, farward: true, allowRecycle: true);
						nextNavigationRow2 = GetNextNavigationRow(gc, new DataCellInfo(dataCell2.Row, dataCell2.Column), column, forward: true, allowRecycle);
						if (nextNavigationRow2 == dataRow2 && column == dataColumn)
						{
							return MoveToCell(gc, nextNavigationRow2, column);
						}
					}
					return false;
				}
				case -1:
				{
					DataGridColumn gridColumn = GetGridColumn(gc, currentCell.Column, farward: false, allowRecycle: true);
					DataRow nextNavigationRow = GetNextNavigationRow(gc, currentCell, gridColumn, forward: false, allowRecycle);
					if (!gc.SkipReadonlyCellsWhenNavigation)
					{
						return MoveToCell(gc, nextNavigationRow, gridColumn);
					}
					DataRow dataRow = nextNavigationRow;
					DataGridColumn dataGridColumn = gridColumn;
					while (nextNavigationRow != null && gridColumn != null)
					{
						IDataCell dataCell = nextNavigationRow[(gridColumn as GridControlColumn).DataColumn];
						if (dataCell != null && !dataCell.IsReadOnly)
						{
							return MoveToCell(gc, nextNavigationRow, gridColumn);
						}
						gridColumn = GetGridColumn(gc, gridColumn, farward: false, allowRecycle: true);
						nextNavigationRow = GetNextNavigationRow(gc, new DataGridCellInfo(dataCell.Row, dataCell.Column.TargetGridColumn), gridColumn, forward: false, allowRecycle);
						if (nextNavigationRow == dataRow && gridColumn == dataGridColumn)
						{
							return MoveToCell(gc, nextNavigationRow, gridColumn);
						}
					}
					return false;
				}
				default:
					return false;
				}
			}
		}

		private static DataGridColumn GetNextNavigationColumn(GridControl gc, DataGridCellInfo basedCell, DataRow row, bool forward, bool allowRecycle)
		{
			if (forward && row == gc.DataGrid.ItemsSource.FirstOrDefault((DataRow x) => x.ResolvedVisibility == Visibility.Visible))
			{
				return GetGridColumn(gc, basedCell.Column, forward, allowRecycle);
			}
			if (!forward && row == gc.DataGrid.ItemsSource.LastOrDefault((DataRow x) => x.ResolvedVisibility == Visibility.Visible))
			{
				return GetGridColumn(gc, basedCell.Column, farward: false, allowRecycle);
			}
			return basedCell.Column;
		}

		private static DataColumn GetNextNavigationColumn(GridControl gc, DataCellInfo basedCell, DataRow row, bool forward, bool allowRecycle)
		{
			if (forward && row == gc.DataGrid.ItemsSource.FirstOrDefault((DataRow x) => x.ResolvedVisibility == Visibility.Visible))
			{
				return GetColumn(gc, basedCell.Column, forward, allowRecycle);
			}
			if (!forward && row == gc.DataGrid.ItemsSource.LastOrDefault((DataRow x) => x.ResolvedVisibility == Visibility.Visible))
			{
				return GetColumn(gc, basedCell.Column, farward: false, allowRecycle);
			}
			return basedCell.Column;
		}

		private static DataRow GetNextNavigationRow(GridControl gc, DataCellInfo basedCell, DataColumn column, bool forward, bool allowRecycle)
		{
			if (forward && column.LogicalDisplayIndex <= basedCell.Column.LogicalDisplayIndex)
			{
				return GetRow(gc, basedCell.Row, farward: true, allowRecycle);
			}
			if (!forward && column.LogicalDisplayIndex >= basedCell.Column.LogicalDisplayIndex)
			{
				return GetRow(gc, basedCell.Row, farward: false, allowRecycle);
			}
			return basedCell.Row;
		}

		private static DataRow GetNextNavigationRow(GridControl gc, DataGridCellInfo basedCell, DataGridColumn column, bool forward, bool allowRecycle)
		{
			if (forward && column.DisplayIndex <= basedCell.Column.DisplayIndex)
			{
				return GetRow(gc, basedCell.Item as DataRow, farward: true, allowRecycle);
			}
			if (!forward && column.DisplayIndex >= basedCell.Column.DisplayIndex)
			{
				return GetRow(gc, basedCell.Item as DataRow, farward: false, allowRecycle);
			}
			return basedCell.Item as DataRow;
		}

		private static DataRow GetRow(GridControl gc, DataRow basedRow, bool farward, bool allowRecycle)
		{
			DataRow dataRow = null;
			DataRow dataRow2 = null;
			IEnumerable<DataRow> enumerable = gc.DataGrid.ItemsSource.Where((DataRow x) => x.ResolvedVisibility == Visibility.Visible);
			foreach (DataRow item in enumerable)
			{
				if (dataRow == null)
				{
					dataRow = item;
				}
				if (!farward && item == basedRow)
				{
					if (dataRow2 != null)
					{
						return dataRow2;
					}
					if (allowRecycle)
					{
						return enumerable.LastOrDefault();
					}
				}
				if (farward && dataRow2 == basedRow)
				{
					return item;
				}
				dataRow2 = item;
			}
			if (farward && dataRow2 == basedRow && allowRecycle)
			{
				return dataRow;
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static DataGridColumn GetGridColumn(GridControl gc, DataGridColumn gridColumn, bool farward, bool allowRecycle)
		{
			int displayIndex = gridColumn.DisplayIndex;
			int count = gc.DataGrid.Columns.Count;
			if (farward)
			{
				for (int i = displayIndex + 1; i < count; i++)
				{
					DataGridColumn dataGridColumn = gc.DataGrid.ColumnFromDisplayIndex(i);
					if (dataGridColumn.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
					{
						return dataGridColumn;
					}
				}
				if (allowRecycle)
				{
					for (int j = 0; j < displayIndex; j++)
					{
						DataGridColumn dataGridColumn2 = gc.DataGrid.ColumnFromDisplayIndex(j);
						if (dataGridColumn2.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
						{
							return dataGridColumn2;
						}
					}
				}
				return gridColumn;
			}
			for (int num = displayIndex - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				DataGridColumn dataGridColumn3 = gc.DataGrid.ColumnFromDisplayIndex(num);
				if (dataGridColumn3.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					return dataGridColumn3;
				}
			}
			if (allowRecycle)
			{
				for (int num2 = count - 1; num2 > displayIndex; num2--)
				{
					DataGridColumn dataGridColumn4 = gc.DataGrid.ColumnFromDisplayIndex(num2);
					if (dataGridColumn4.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
					{
						return dataGridColumn4;
					}
				}
			}
			return gridColumn;
		}

		private static DataColumn GetColumn(GridControl gc, DataColumn column, bool farward, bool allowRecycle)
		{
			int logicalDisplayIndex = column.LogicalDisplayIndex;
			int count = gc.DataGrid.Columns.Count;
			if (farward)
			{
				for (int i = logicalDisplayIndex + 1; i < count; i++)
				{
					DataColumn dataColumn = gc.ColumnFromDisplayIndex(i);
					if (dataColumn.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
					{
						return dataColumn;
					}
				}
				if (allowRecycle)
				{
					for (int j = 0; j < logicalDisplayIndex; j++)
					{
