						DataColumn dataColumn2 = gc.ColumnFromDisplayIndex(j);
						if (dataColumn2.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
						{
							return dataColumn2;
						}
					}
				}
				return column;
			}
			for (int num = logicalDisplayIndex - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				DataColumn dataColumn3 = gc.ColumnFromDisplayIndex(num);
				if (dataColumn3.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					return dataColumn3;
				}
			}
			if (allowRecycle)
			{
				for (int num2 = count - 1; num2 > logicalDisplayIndex; num2--)
				{
					DataColumn dataColumn4 = gc.ColumnFromDisplayIndex(num2);
					if (dataColumn4.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
					{
						return dataColumn4;
					}
				}
			}
			return column;
		}

		private static bool MoveToCell(GridControl gc, DataRow row, DataGridColumn column)
		{
			if (row != null && column != null)
			{
				DataGridCellInfo dataGridCellInfo = new DataGridCellInfo(row, column);
				gc.DataGrid.CurrentCell = dataGridCellInfo;
				if (gc.SelectionUnit != DataGridSelectionUnit.FullRow)
				{
					gc.InternalSelectionHelper.LockSelectionChanged();
					try
					{
						gc.DataGrid.SelectedCells.Clear();
						gc.DataGrid.SelectedCells.Add(dataGridCellInfo);
					}
					finally
					{
						gc.InternalSelectionHelper.UnlockSelectionChanged(raiseEvent: true);
					}
					DataCellInfo dataCellInfo = dataGridCellInfo.GetDataCellInfo();
					if (!dataCellInfo.IsEmpty && dataCellInfo.Data != null)
					{
						GridControlCell hostControl = dataCellInfo.Data.HostControl;
						if (hostControl != null && !hostControl.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
						{
							hostControl.Focus();
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					gc.DataGrid.SelectedItem = row;
				}
				return true;
			}
			_ = gc.CurrentCell;
			if (gc.CurrentCell.Data != null)
			{
				GridControlCell gcc = gc.CurrentCell.Data.HostControl;
				if (gcc != null && gcc.IsKeyboardFocusWithin && gcc.IsInEditMode)
				{
					gcc.EndEditModeIfPossible(commitChange: true, delegate
					{
						gcc.Focus();
					});
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private static bool MoveToCell(GridControl gc, DataRow row, DataColumn column)
		{
			if (row != null && column != null)
			{
				DataCellInfo cell = (gc.CurrentCell = new DataCellInfo(row, column));
				if (gc.SelectionUnit != DataGridSelectionUnit.FullRow)
				{
					gc.SelectCell(cell, deselectOthers: true);
					if (!cell.IsEmpty && cell.Data != null)
					{
						GridControlCell hostControl = cell.Data.HostControl;
						if (hostControl != null && !hostControl.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
						{
							hostControl.Focus();
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					gc.DataGrid.SelectedItem = row;
				}
				return true;
			}
			_ = gc.CurrentCell;
			if (gc.CurrentCell.Data != null)
			{
				GridControlCell gcc = gc.CurrentCell.Data.HostControl;
				if (gcc != null && gcc.IsKeyboardFocusWithin && gcc.IsInEditMode)
				{
					gcc.EndEditModeIfPossible(commitChange: true, delegate
					{
						gcc.Focus();
					});
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private static bool ShouldSkipKey(Key key)
		{
			switch (key)
			{
			case Key.Pause:
			case Key.Capital:
			case Key.Space:
			case Key.Prior:
			case Key.Next:
			case Key.Left:
			case Key.Right:
			case Key.Snapshot:
			case Key.Insert:
			case Key.Delete:
			case Key.F1:
			case Key.F3:
			case Key.F4:
			case Key.F5:
			case Key.F6:
			case Key.F7:
			case Key.F8:
			case Key.F9:
			case Key.F10:
			case Key.F11:
			case Key.F12:
			case Key.NumLock:
			case Key.Scroll:
			case Key.LeftShift:
			case Key.RightShift:
			case Key.LeftCtrl:
			case Key.RightCtrl:
			case Key.LeftAlt:
			case Key.RightAlt:
			case Key.System:
				return true;
			default:
				if (Keyboard.Modifiers == ModifierKeys.Control)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (Keyboard.Modifiers == ModifierKeys.Shift && (key == Key.Up || key == Key.Down))
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}
	}
	internal class LayoutPersistenceHelper
	{
		private readonly GridControl _gc;

		private DataTableLayout _cachedSettings;

		private LayoutOption _cachedOption;

		private bool CanApplySettings
		{
			get
			{
				if (_gc.DataGrid != null)
				{
					return _gc.DataSource != null;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public LayoutPersistenceHelper(GridControl gc)
		{
			if (gc == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("gc");
			}
			_gc = gc;
		}

		public string SaveLayoutSettings(LayoutOption option)
		{
			if (_gc.DataSource != null && _gc.DataGrid != null)
			{
				return XamlWriter.Save(InternalSaveLayoutSettings(option));
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void LoadLayoutSettings(string settings, LayoutOption option)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(settings))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				if (XamlReader.Parse(settings) is DataTableLayout settings2)
				{
					InternalLoadLayoutSettings(settings2, option);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		public void FlushCachedLayoutSettings()
		{
			if (CanApplySettings && _cachedSettings != null)
			{
				ApplyLayoutSettings(_cachedSettings, _cachedOption);
				_cachedSettings = null;
			}
		}

		private DataTableLayout InternalSaveLayoutSettings(LayoutOption option)
		{
			DataTableLayout dataTableLayout = new DataTableLayout
			{
				FilterHeaderVisibility = _gc.FilterHeaderVisibility,
				FrozenColumnCount = _gc.DataGrid.FrozenColumnCount
			};
			if (_gc.InternalColumnsVirtualizationHelper.UseVirtualizationLoading)
			{
				return dataTableLayout;
			}
			DataTable dataSource = _gc.DataSource;
			if (option.HasFlag(LayoutOption.Selection))
			{
				List<DataColumn> list = _gc.SelectedColumns.ToList();
				if (list.Count > 0)
				{
					foreach (DataColumn item in list)
					{
						dataTableLayout.SelectedColumns.Add(dataSource.Columns.IndexOf(item));
					}
				}
				List<DataRow> list2 = _gc.SelectedRows.ToList();
				if (list2.Count > 0)
				{
					foreach (DataRow item2 in list2)
					{
						dataTableLayout.SelectedRows.Add(dataSource.Rows.IndexOf(item2));
					}
				}
				List<DataCellInfo> list3 = _gc.SelectedCells.ToList();
				if (list3.Count > 0)
				{
					DataCellInfo currentCell = _gc.CurrentCell;
					foreach (DataCellInfo item3 in list3)
					{
						int rowIndex = dataSource.Rows.IndexOf(item3.Row);
						int columnIndex = dataSource.Columns.IndexOf(item3.Column);
						dataTableLayout.SelectedCells.Add(new DataCellLayout
						{
							RowIndex = rowIndex,
							ColumnIndex = columnIndex,
							IsSelected = true,
							IsCurrentFocused = (item3.Column == currentCell.Column && item3.Row == currentCell.Row)
						});
					}
				}
			}
			if (option.HasFlag(LayoutOption.SortedColumns))
			{
				foreach (DataColumnSortingItem sortedItem in _gc.SortingInfo.SortedItems)
				{
					dataTableLayout.SortedColumns.Add(new DataColumnSortingLayout
					{
						ColumnIndex = dataSource.Columns.IndexOf(sortedItem.Column),
						SortDirection = sortedItem.SortDirection
					});
				}
			}
			double defaultColumnWidth = _gc.DefaultColumnWidth;
			foreach (DataGridColumn column in _gc.DataGrid.Columns)
			{
				if (!(column is GridControlColumn { DataColumn: var dataColumn } gridControlColumn))
				{
					continue;
				}
				int num = dataSource.Columns.IndexOf(dataColumn);
				int displayIndex = gridControlColumn.DisplayIndex;
				Visibility visibility = gridControlColumn.Visibility;
				DataColumnFilterOperator selectedOperator = dataColumn.Filter.SelectedOperator;
				string selectedOperand = dataColumn.Filter.SelectedOperand;
				double actualWidth = gridControlColumn.ActualWidth;
				bool flag = !gridControlColumn.VirtualizingWidth.HasValue && !GridControlColumn.AreClose(gridControlColumn.OriginalWidth ?? defaultColumnWidth, actualWidth);
				if (displayIndex != num || visibility != gridControlColumn.OriginalVisibility || !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(selectedOperand) || flag)
				{
					DataColumnLayout dataColumnLayout = new DataColumnLayout
					{
						Index = num,
						Visibility = visibility,
						FilterSelectedOperator = selectedOperator,
						FilterSelectedOperand = selectedOperand
					};
					if (displayIndex != num && displayIndex != 9999999)
					{
						dataColumnLayout.DisplayIndex = displayIndex;
					}
					if (flag)
					{
						dataColumnLayout.RenderedWidth = actualWidth;
					}
					dataTableLayout.ColumnsLayout.Add(dataColumnLayout);
				}
			}
			return dataTableLayout;
		}

		private void InternalLoadLayoutSettings(DataTableLayout settings, LayoutOption option)
		{
			if (CanApplySettings)
			{
				ApplyLayoutSettings(settings, option);
				_cachedSettings = null;
			}
			else
			{
				_cachedSettings = settings;
			}
			_cachedOption = option;
		}

		private void ApplyLayoutSettings(DataTableLayout settings, LayoutOption option)
		{
			if (option.HasFlag(LayoutOption.FilterHeaderVisibility) && _gc.DataSource != null && _gc.DataSource.Columns.Count > 0)
			{
				_gc.FilterHeaderVisibility = settings.FilterHeaderVisibility;
				_gc.SyncColumnsFilterVisibility();
			}
			if (_gc.InternalColumnsVirtualizationHelper.UseVirtualizationLoading)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			foreach (DataColumnLayout item in settings.ColumnsLayout.OrderBy((DataColumnLayout x) => x.DisplayIndex))
			{
				DataColumn dataColumn = FindColumnByIndex(item.Index);
				if (dataColumn == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (option.HasFlag(LayoutOption.ColumnDisplayIndex) && item.DisplayIndex != 9999999 && item.DisplayIndex < _gc.DataSource.Columns.Count)
				{
					dataColumn.DisplayIndex = item.DisplayIndex;
				}
				if (option.HasFlag(LayoutOption.ColumnVisibility) && dataColumn.Visibility != item.Visibility)
				{
					dataColumn.Visibility = item.Visibility;
				}
				if (option.HasFlag(LayoutOption.ColumnFilter))
				{
					dataColumn.Filter.SelectedOperator = item.FilterSelectedOperator;
					dataColumn.Filter.SelectedOperand = item.FilterSelectedOperand;
				}
				if (option.HasFlag(LayoutOption.ColumnWidth) && item.RenderedWidth.HasValue)
				{
					if (!flag)
					{
						flag = true;
					}
					dataColumn.Width = item.RenderedWidth.Value;
				}
			}
			if (option.HasFlag(LayoutOption.ColumnWidth) && flag)
			{
				foreach (DataGridColumn column in _gc.DataGrid.Columns)
				{
					if (column is GridControlColumn gridControlColumn)
					{
						if (gridControlColumn.DataColumn.Width.HasValue)
						{
							gridControlColumn.Width = new DataGridLength(gridControlColumn.DataColumn.Width.Value);
						}
						else
						{
							gridControlColumn.Width = default(DataGridLength);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (option.HasFlag(LayoutOption.SortedColumns) && settings.SortedColumns.Count > 0)
			{
				_gc.SortingInfo.ClearSorts();
				foreach (DataColumnSortingLayout sortedColumn in settings.SortedColumns)
				{
					DataColumn dataColumn2 = FindColumnByIndex(sortedColumn.ColumnIndex);
					if (dataColumn2 != null)
					{
						_gc.SortingInfo.SetSort(dataColumn2, sortedColumn.SortDirection);
					}
				}
			}
			_gc.InternalFreezingHelper.UpdateFrozenColumnCount(settings.FrozenColumnCount);
			_gc.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action<DataTableLayout>(ReloadSelections), DispatcherPriority.Loaded, settings);
		}

		private void ReloadSelections(DataTableLayout settings)
		{
			if (settings.SelectedColumns.Count == 0 && settings.SelectedRows.Count == 0 && settings.SelectedCells.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			_gc.InternalSelectionHelper.LockSelectionChanged();
			_gc.DeselectAll();
			if (settings.SelectedColumns.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (int selectedColumn in settings.SelectedColumns)
				{
					DataColumn dataColumn = FindColumnByIndex(selectedColumn);
					if (dataColumn != null)
					{
						_gc.SelectColumn(dataColumn);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (settings.SelectedRows.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (int selectedRow in settings.SelectedRows)
				{
					DataRow dataRow = FindRowByIndex(selectedRow);
					if (dataRow != null)
					{
						_gc.SelectRow(dataRow);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (settings.SelectedCells.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (DataCellLayout selectedCell in settings.SelectedCells)
				{
					DataColumn dataColumn2 = FindColumnByIndex(selectedCell.ColumnIndex);
					DataRow dataRow2 = FindRowByIndex(selectedCell.RowIndex);
					if (dataColumn2 != null && dataRow2 != null)
					{
						_gc.SelectCell(new DataCellInfo(dataRow2, dataColumn2));
					}
				}
			}
			_gc.InternalSelectionHelper.UnlockSelectionChanged(raiseEvent: true);
		}

		private DataColumn FindColumnByIndex(int columnIndex)
		{
			if (columnIndex < _gc.DataSource.Columns.Count)
			{
				return _gc.DataSource.Columns[columnIndex];
			}
			return null;
		}

		private DataRow FindRowByIndex(int rowIndex)
		{
			if (rowIndex < 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (rowIndex < _gc.DataSource.Rows.Count)
			{
				return _gc.DataSource.Rows[rowIndex];
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	internal class SelectionHelper
	{
		private class SelectionPersister
		{
			private int _lockFlag;

			private List<int> _persistedRows;

			private List<int> _persistedColumns;

			private List<(int Row, int Column)> _persistedCells;

			private SelectionHelper Helper { get; }

			public SelectionPersister(SelectionHelper helper)
			{
				Helper = helper ?? throw new ArgumentNullException("helper");
			}

			public void SaveSelection()
			{
				if (Helper.IsReady)
				{
					if (_lockFlag == 0)
					{
						SaveSelectionInternal();
					}
					_lockFlag++;
				}
			}

			public void RestoreSelection(Lazy<bool> raiseSelectionChanged)
			{
				if (Helper.IsReady)
				{
					if (_lockFlag > 0)
					{
						_lockFlag--;
					}
					if (_lockFlag == 0)
					{
						RestoreSelectionInternal(raiseSelectionChanged);
					}
				}
			}

			private void SaveSelectionInternal()
			{
				Helper.LockSelectionChanged();
				IEnumerable<DataRow> itemsSource = Helper._gc.DataGrid.ItemsSource;
				if (!itemsSource.Any() || (Helper.DataGrid.SelectedItems.Count <= 0 && Helper.DataGrid.SelectedCells.Count <= 0 && !Helper.SelectedColumns.Any()))
				{
					return;
				}
				_persistedRows = new List<int>();
				_persistedColumns = new List<int>();
				_persistedCells = new List<(int, int)>();
				List<object> list = itemsSource.Cast<object>().ToList();
				foreach (object selectedItem in Helper.DataGrid.SelectedItems)
				{
					int num = list.IndexOf(selectedItem);
					if (num >= 0)
					{
						_persistedRows.Add(num);
					}
				}
				foreach (DataColumn selectedColumn in Helper.SelectedColumns)
				{
					_persistedColumns.Add(selectedColumn.LogicalDisplayIndex);
				}
				foreach (DataGridCellInfo selectedCell in Helper.DataGrid.SelectedCells)
				{
					int num2 = list.IndexOf(selectedCell.Item);
					if (num2 >= 0)
					{
						_persistedCells.Add((num2, selectedCell.Column.DisplayIndex));
					}
				}
			}

			private void RestoreSelectionInternal(Lazy<bool> raiseSelectionChanged)
			{
				if (!HasPersistedSelection())
				{
					Helper.UnlockSelectionChanged(raiseEvent: false);
					return;
				}
				IEnumerable<DataRow> itemsSource = Helper._gc.DataGrid.ItemsSource;
				if (!itemsSource.Any())
				{
					Helper.UnlockSelectionChanged(raiseSelectionChanged.Value);
					return;
				}
				List<DataRow> list = itemsSource.Cast<DataRow>().ToList();
				int count = list.Count;
				ReadOnlyCollection<DataColumn> columns = Helper._gc.DataSource.Columns;
				Helper.DeselectAll();
				if (_persistedRows != null)
				{
					foreach (int persistedRow in _persistedRows)
					{
						if (persistedRow >= 0 && persistedRow < count)
						{
							Helper.SelectRow(list[persistedRow]);
						}
					}
					_persistedRows = null;
				}
				if (_persistedColumns != null)
				{
					foreach (int columnIndex in _persistedColumns)
					{
						DataColumn dataColumn = columns.FirstOrDefault((DataColumn x) => x.LogicalDisplayIndex == columnIndex);
						if (dataColumn != null)
						{
							Helper.SelectColumn(dataColumn);
						}
					}
					_persistedColumns = null;
				}
				if (_persistedCells != null)
				{
					foreach (var cellIndex in _persistedCells)
					{
						if (cellIndex.Row >= 0 && cellIndex.Row < count)
						{
							DataRow dataRow = list[cellIndex.Row];
							DataColumn dataColumn2 = columns.FirstOrDefault((DataColumn x) => x.LogicalDisplayIndex == cellIndex.Column);
							if (dataRow != null && dataColumn2 != null)
							{
								Helper.SelectCell(new DataCellInfo(dataRow, dataColumn2));
							}
						}
					}
					_persistedCells = null;
				}
				Helper.UnlockSelectionChanged(raiseSelectionChanged.Value);
			}

			private bool HasPersistedSelection()
			{
				if (_persistedRows != null && _persistedRows.Count > 0)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (_persistedColumns != null && _persistedColumns.Count > 0)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (_persistedCells != null && _persistedCells.Count > 0)
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private readonly GridControl _gc;

		private int _internalSuppressSelectionChangedEvent;

		private SelectionPersister _persister;

		private GridControlDataGrid DataGrid => _gc.DataGrid;

		private SelectionPersister Persister => _persister ?? (_persister = new SelectionPersister(this));

		public bool IsReady
		{
			get
			{
				if (_gc.DataSource != null)
				{
					return DataGrid != null;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public bool HasSelection
		{
			get
			{
				if (IsReady)
				{
					if (_gc.DataSource.Rows.Count == 0 || _gc.DataSource.Columns.Count == 0)
					{
						return false;
					}
					if (!_gc.DataSource.Rows.Any((DataRow x) => x.ResolvedVisibility == Visibility.Visible))
					{
						return false;
					}
					if (!_gc.DataSource.Columns.Any((DataColumn x) => x.Visibility == Visibility.Visible))
					{
						return false;
					}
					if (DataGrid.SelectedItems != null && DataGrid.SelectedItems.OfType<DataRow>().Any((DataRow x) => !x.IsFakeRow))
					{
						return true;
					}
					if (SelectedCells.Any())
					{
						return true;
					}
					foreach (DataGridColumn column in _gc.DataGrid.Columns)
					{
						if (column.Visibility == Visibility.Visible && IsDataGridCoumnSelected(column))
						{
							return true;
						}
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<DataRow> SelectedRows
		{
			get
			{
				if (!IsReady)
				{
					yield break;
				}
				if (IsAllSelected)
				{
					foreach (DataRow boundRow in _gc.BoundRows)
					{
						yield return boundRow;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (DataGrid.SelectedItems == null)
					{
						yield break;
					}
					foreach (DataRow selectedItem in DataGrid.SelectedItems)
					{
						if (!selectedItem.IsFakeRow)
						{
							yield return selectedItem;
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<DataColumn> SelectedColumns
		{
			get
			{
				if (!IsReady)
				{
					yield break;
				}
				foreach (DataGridColumn column in _gc.DataGrid.Columns)
				{
					if (column.Visibility != Visibility.Visible)
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (column is PlaceholderColumn placeholderColumn)
					{
						if (!placeholderColumn.IsColumnSelected)
						{
							continue;
						}
						foreach (DataColumn includedColumn in placeholderColumn.GetIncludedColumns())
						{
							yield return includedColumn;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						DataColumn dataColumn = ((GridControlColumn)column).DataColumn;
						if (dataColumn.IsColumnSelected)
						{
							yield return dataColumn;
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<DataCellInfo> SelectedCells
		{
			get
			{
				if (!IsReady)
				{
					yield break;
				}
				foreach (DataGridCellInfo selectedCell in DataGrid.SelectedCells)
				{
					if (selectedCell.Column == null || selectedCell.Column.Visibility != Visibility.Visible || !(selectedCell.Item is DataRow { IsFakeRow: false }))
					{
						continue;
					}
					foreach (DataCellInfo dataCellInfo in selectedCell.GetDataCellInfos())
					{
						yield return dataCellInfo;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<DataCellInfo> OrderedSelectedCells
		{
			get
			{
				if (!IsReady)
				{
					yield break;
				}
				IList<DataRow> rows = _gc.BoundRows.ToList();
				int columnsCount = _gc.DataSource.Columns.Count;
				IEnumerable<DataCellInfo> enumerable = from <>h__TransparentIdentifier0 in SelectedCells.Select(delegate(DataCellInfo x)
					{
						IList<DataRow> list = rows;
						DataCellInfo dataCellInfo = x;
						int num = list.IndexOf(dataCellInfo.Row) * columnsCount;
						dataCellInfo = x;
						return new
						{
							x = x,
							w = num + dataCellInfo.Column.LogicalDisplayIndex
						};
					})
					orderby <>h__TransparentIdentifier0.w
					select <>h__TransparentIdentifier0.x;
				foreach (DataCellInfo item in enumerable)
				{
					yield return item;
				}
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<DataCellInfo> TotalSelectedCells
		{
			get
			{
				if (!IsReady)
				{
					yield break;
				}
				foreach (DataColumn column in SelectedColumns)
				{
					foreach (DataRow boundRow in _gc.BoundRows)
					{
						if (boundRow.ResolvedVisibility == Visibility.Visible)
						{
							yield return new DataCellInfo(boundRow, column);
						}
					}
				}
				List<DataRow> selectedRows = SelectedRows.ToList();
				foreach (DataRow row in selectedRows)
				{
					foreach (DataColumn column2 in _gc.DataSource.Columns)
					{
						if (!column2.IsColumnSelected && column2.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
						{
							yield return new DataCellInfo(row, column2);
						}
					}
				}
				foreach (DataCellInfo selectedCell in SelectedCells)
				{
					if (!selectedRows.Contains(selectedCell.Row) && !selectedCell.Column.IsColumnSelected)
					{
						yield return selectedCell;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<DataCellInfo> TotalOrderedSelectedCells
		{
			get
			{
				if (!IsReady)
				{
					yield break;
				}
				IList<DataRow> rows = _gc.BoundRows.ToList();
				int columnsCount = _gc.DataSource.Columns.Count;
				IEnumerable<DataCellInfo> enumerable = from <>h__TransparentIdentifier0 in TotalSelectedCells.Select(delegate(DataCellInfo x)
					{
						IList<DataRow> list = rows;
						DataCellInfo dataCellInfo = x;
						int num = list.IndexOf(dataCellInfo.Row) * columnsCount;
						dataCellInfo = x;
						return new
						{
							x = x,
							w = num + dataCellInfo.Column.LogicalDisplayIndex
						};
					})
					orderby <>h__TransparentIdentifier0.w
					select <>h__TransparentIdentifier0.x;
				foreach (DataCellInfo item in enumerable)
				{
					yield return item;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsAllSelected
		{
			get
			{
				if (IsReady)
				{
					foreach (DataGridColumn column in _gc.DataGrid.Columns)
					{
						if (column.Visibility == Visibility.Visible && !IsDataGridCoumnSelected(column))
						{
							return false;
						}
					}
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public bool ResolvedSuppressSelectionChangedEvent
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_gc.SupressSelectionChangedEvent)
				{
					return _internalSuppressSelectionChangedEvent > 0;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public SelectionHelper(GridControl gc)
		{
			if (gc == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("gc");
			}
			_gc = gc;
		}

		public void LockSelectionChanged()
		{
			_internalSuppressSelectionChangedEvent++;
		}

		public void UnlockSelectionChanged(bool raiseEvent)
		{
			_internalSuppressSelectionChangedEvent--;
			if (raiseEvent && !ResolvedSuppressSelectionChangedEvent)
			{
				_gc.TryInvokeSelectionChanged();
			}
		}

		public void ResetSelection()
		{
			if (IsReady)
			{
				DeselectAll();
			}
		}

		public void SelectAll()
		{
			if (!IsReady)
			{
				return;
			}
			LockSelectionChanged();
			bool raiseEvent = false;
			foreach (DataGridColumn column in _gc.DataGrid.Columns)
			{
				if (column is PlaceholderColumn placeholderColumn)
				{
					if (!placeholderColumn.IsColumnSelected)
					{
						placeholderColumn.IsColumnSelected = true;
						raiseEvent = true;
					}
					continue;
				}
				DataColumn dataColumn = ((GridControlColumn)column).DataColumn;
				if (!dataColumn.IsColumnSelected)
				{
					dataColumn.IsColumnSelected = true;
					raiseEvent = true;
				}
			}
			UnlockSelectionChanged(raiseEvent);
		}

		public void DeselectAll()
		{
			if (IsReady)
			{
				bool hasSelection = HasSelection;
				if (hasSelection)
				{
					LockSelectionChanged();
					DataGrid.UnselectAllCells();
					DataGrid.UnselectAll();
				}
				RemoveColumnSelectionFlagsSilently();
				if (hasSelection)
				{
					UnlockSelectionChanged(raiseEvent: true);
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsColumnSelected(DataColumn column)
		{
			return column.IsColumnSelected;
		}

		private bool IsColumnSelectedInternal(DataColumn column)
		{
			return column.IsColumnSelected;
		}

		public void SelectColumn(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (!IsReady)
			{
				return;
			}
			LockSelectionChanged();
			foreach (DataColumn item in GetColumnsInSameGroup(column))
			{
				SelectColumnInternal(item);
			}
			UnlockSelectionChanged(raiseEvent: true);
		}

		private void SelectColumnInternal(DataColumn column)
		{
			column.IsColumnSelected = true;
		}

		public void DeselectColumn(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (!IsReady)
			{
				return;
			}
			LockSelectionChanged();
			foreach (DataColumn item in GetColumnsInSameGroup(column))
			{
				DeselectColumnInternal(item);
			}
			UnlockSelectionChanged(raiseEvent: true);
		}

		public void RemoveColumnSelectionFlagsSilently()
		{
			if (!IsReady)
			{
				return;
			}
			LockSelectionChanged();
			foreach (DataGridColumn column in _gc.DataGrid.Columns)
			{
				if (column is PlaceholderColumn placeholderColumn)
				{
					placeholderColumn.IsColumnSelected = false;
				}
				else
				{
					((GridControlColumn)column).DataColumn.IsColumnSelected = false;
				}
			}
			UnlockSelectionChanged(raiseEvent: false);
		}

		private void DeselectColumnInternal(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (!IsColumnSelectedInternal(column))
			{
				return;
			}
			List<DataGridCellInfo> list = DataGrid.SelectedCells.Where(delegate(DataGridCellInfo x)
			{
				DataGridCellInfo dataGridCellInfo = x;
				return ((GridControlColumn)dataGridCellInfo.Column).DataColumn == column;
			}).ToList();
			if (_gc.SelectionUnit != DataGridSelectionUnit.FullRow)
			{
				foreach (DataGridCellInfo item in list)
				{
					DataGrid.SelectedCells.Remove(item);
				}
			}
			column.IsColumnSelected = false;
		}

		public bool IsRowSelected(DataRow row)
		{
			if (IsReady)
			{
				return DataGrid.SelectedItems.Contains(row);
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void SelectRow(DataRow row)
		{
			if (IsReady && !IsRowSelected(row))
			{
				if (_gc.SelectionMode == DataGridSelectionMode.Single)
				{
					DataGrid.SelectedItem = row;
				}
				else
				{
					DataGrid.SelectedItems.Add(row);
				}
			}
		}

		public void DeselectRow(DataRow row)
		{
			if (IsReady && IsRowSelected(row))
			{
				if (_gc.SelectionMode == DataGridSelectionMode.Single)
				{
					DataGrid.SelectedItem = null;
				}
				else
				{
					DataGrid.SelectedItems.Remove(row);
				}
			}
		}

		public void SelectCell(DataCellInfo cell)
		{
			if (IsReady && cell.Row != null && cell.Column != null && cell.Column.TargetGridColumn != null && _gc.SelectionUnit != DataGridSelectionUnit.FullRow)
			{
				DataGridCellInfo item = new DataGridCellInfo(cell.Row, cell.Column.TargetGridColumn);
				if (!DataGrid.SelectedCells.Contains(item))
				{
					DataGrid.SelectedCells.Add(item);
				}
			}
		}

		public void DeselectCell(DataCellInfo cell)
		{
			if (IsReady && cell.Row != null && cell.Column != null && cell.Column.TargetGridColumn != null && _gc.SelectionUnit != DataGridSelectionUnit.FullRow)
			{
				DataGridCellInfo item = new DataGridCellInfo(cell.Row, cell.Column.TargetGridColumn);
				if (DataGrid.SelectedCells.Contains(item))
				{
					DataGrid.SelectedCells.Remove(item);
				}
			}
		}

		public void FocusOnTopFirstCell()
		{
			if (IsReady && _gc.DataGrid.Columns.Count > 0)
			{
				GridControlColumn gridControlColumn = _gc.DataGrid.ColumnFromDisplayIndex(0) as GridControlColumn;
				FocusOnTopFirstCell(gridControlColumn.DataColumn);
			}
		}

		public void FocusOnTopFirstCell(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (!IsReady || column == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			IEnumerable<DataRow> viewportRows = _gc.ViewportRows;
			if (viewportRows == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			DataRow dataRow = viewportRows.FirstOrDefault();
			if (dataRow != null)
			{
				DataCellInfo currentCell = new DataCellInfo(dataRow, column);
				_gc.CurrentCell = currentCell;
				if (currentCell.Data != null && currentCell.Data.HostControl != null && !currentCell.Data.HostControl.IsFocused)
				{
					currentCell.Data.HostControl.Focus();
				}
			}
		}

		public void UpdateRowAndColumnSelectionStatus()
		{
			if (!IsReady)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			if (_gc.DataGrid.SelectedItems.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (DataGridColumn column in _gc.DataGrid.Columns)
				{
					SetColumnHasSelectionFlag(column, hasSelection: true);
				}
				flag = true;
			}
			bool flag2 = false;
			if (SelectedColumns.Any())
			{
				foreach (DataRow row in _gc.DataSource.Rows)
				{
					row.HasSelection = true;
				}
				flag2 = true;
			}
			if (flag && flag2)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				foreach (DataGridColumn column2 in _gc.DataGrid.Columns)
				{
					SetColumnHasSelectionFlag(column2, hasSelection: false);
				}
				foreach (DataColumn selectedColumn in SelectedColumns)
				{
					selectedColumn.HasSelection = true;
				}
			}
			if (!flag2)
			{
				foreach (DataRow row2 in _gc.DataSource.Rows)
				{
					row2.HasSelection = false;
				}
				foreach (DataRow selectedItem in _gc.DataGrid.SelectedItems)
				{
					selectedItem.HasSelection = true;
				}
			}
			foreach (DataGridCellInfo selectedCell in _gc.DataGrid.SelectedCells)
			{
				if (selectedCell.Item != null && selectedCell.Column != null && !_gc.DataGrid.SelectedItems.Contains(selectedCell.Item))
				{
					if (!flag2)
					{
						(selectedCell.Item as DataRow).HasSelection = true;
					}
					if (!flag)
					{
						SetColumnHasSelectionFlag(selectedCell.Column, hasSelection: true);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void SetColumnHasSelectionFlag(DataGridColumn dgc, bool hasSelection)
		{
			if (dgc is PlaceholderColumn placeholderColumn)
			{
				placeholderColumn.HasSelection = hasSelection;
			}
			else
			{
				((GridControlColumn)dgc).DataColumn.HasSelection = hasSelection;
			}
		}

		private bool IsDataGridCoumnSelected(DataGridColumn dgc)
		{
			if (dgc.IsPlaceHolder())
			{
				return ((PlaceholderColumn)dgc).IsColumnSelected;
			}
			return ((GridControlColumn)dgc).DataColumn.IsColumnSelected;
		}

		private List<DataColumn> GetColumnsInSameGroup(DataColumn column)
		{
			List<DataColumn> list = new List<DataColumn>();
			if (_gc.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.IsColumnSpanLeader(column))
			{
				IList<DataColumn> columnSpanSlavers = _gc.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.GetColumnSpanSlavers(column);
				list.Add(column);
				list.AddRange(columnSpanSlavers);
			}
			else if (_gc.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.IsColumnSpanSlaver(column))
			{
				DataColumn columnSpanLeader = _gc.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.GetColumnSpanLeader(column);
				IList<DataColumn> columnSpanSlavers2 = _gc.InternalHeaderColumnSpanHelper.GetColumnSpanSlavers(columnSpanLeader);
				list.Add(columnSpanLeader);
				list.AddRange(columnSpanSlavers2);
			}
			else
			{
				list.Add(column);
			}
			return list;
		}

		public void SaveSelection()
		{
			Persister.SaveSelection();
		}

		public void RestoreSelection(bool raiseSelectionChanged)
		{
			Persister.RestoreSelection(new Lazy<bool>(() => raiseSelectionChanged));
		}

		public void RestoreSelection(Lazy<bool> raiseSelectionChanged)
		{
			Persister.RestoreSelection(raiseSelectionChanged);
		}
	}
	internal class SortingHelper
	{
		private readonly GridControl _gridControl;

		private GridControlSortingInfo _sortingInfo;

		private bool _invalidateSortingFlag;

		public GridControlSortingInfo SortingInfo
		{
			get
			{
				if (_sortingInfo == null)
				{
					_sortingInfo = new GridControlSortingInfo(_gridControl);
				}
				return _sortingInfo;
			}
		}

		public SortingHelper(GridControl gridControl)
		{
			if (gridControl == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("gridControl");
			}
			_gridControl = gridControl;
		}

		public void InvalidateSorting()
		{
			if (_invalidateSortingFlag)
			{
				return;
			}
			_invalidateSortingFlag = true;
			_gridControl.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				if (_gridControl.DataSource != null && SortingInfo.SortedItems.FirstOrDefault((DataColumnSortingItem x) => !_gridControl.DataSource.Columns.Contains(x.Column)) != null)
				{
					SortingInfo.ClearSortsWithoutEvents();
				}
				GridControlDataSortingEventArgs e = new GridControlDataSortingEventArgs(_gridControl, SortingInfo.SortedItems, GridControl.DataSortingEvent, _gridControl);
				_gridControl.OnDataSorting(e);
				if (!e.Handled)
				{
					DoDefaultSorting();
				}
				_invalidateSortingFlag = false;
			});
		}

		public void DoDefaultSorting()
		{
			if (_invalidateSortingFlag)
			{
				_invalidateSortingFlag = false;
			}
			if (_gridControl.DataGrid == null || _gridControl.DataSource == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			IList<DataRow> filteredRows = _gridControl.InternalFilterHelper.GetFilteredRows();
			if (filteredRows == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (SortingInfo.SortedItems.Count == 0)
			{
				CheckAndUpdateItemsSource(filteredRows);
				return;
			}
			DataColumnSortingItem firstItem = SortingInfo.SortedItems[0];
			DataRow dataRow = filteredRows.LastOrDefault();
			DataRow dataRow2 = ((dataRow != null && dataRow.IsNewlyAdded) ? dataRow : null);
			IList<DataRow> list = filteredRows;
			if (dataRow2 != null)
			{
				list.Remove(dataRow2);
			}
			IOrderedEnumerable<DataRow> orderedEnumerable = null;
			orderedEnumerable = ((firstItem.SortDirection != ListSortDirection.Ascending) ? list.OrderByDescending((DataRow x) => _gridControl.InternalCellValueHelper.GetCellValue(x, firstItem.Column), firstItem.Column.ResolvedValueComparer) : list.OrderBy((DataRow x) => _gridControl.InternalCellValueHelper.GetCellValue(x, firstItem.Column), firstItem.Column.ResolvedValueComparer));
			for (int num = 1; num < SortingInfo.SortedItems.Count; num++)
			{
				DataColumnSortingItem sortedItem = SortingInfo.SortedItems[num];
				orderedEnumerable = ((sortedItem.SortDirection != ListSortDirection.Ascending) ? orderedEnumerable.ThenByDescending((DataRow x) => _gridControl.InternalCellValueHelper.GetCellValue(x, sortedItem.Column), sortedItem.Column.ResolvedValueComparer) : orderedEnumerable.ThenBy((DataRow x) => _gridControl.InternalCellValueHelper.GetCellValue(x, sortedItem.Column), sortedItem.Column.ResolvedValueComparer));
			}
			if (dataRow2 == null)
			{
				CheckAndUpdateItemsSource(orderedEnumerable);
				return;
			}
			CheckAndUpdateItemsSource(orderedEnumerable.Union(new DataRow[1] { dataRow2 }));
		}

		private void CheckAndUpdateItemsSource(IEnumerable<DataRow> newRows)
		{
			if (!_gridControl.BoundRows.SequenceEqual(newRows))
			{
				_gridControl.DirectlySetItemsSource(newRows);
			}
		}
	}
	public class GenericObjectComparer : IComparer<object>
	{
		public static readonly GenericObjectComparer Default = new GenericObjectComparer();

		protected GenericObjectComparer()
		{
		}

		public int Compare(object x, object y)
		{
			return InternalCompare(x, y);
		}

		private static int InternalCompare(object x, object y)
		{
			if (x == DBNull.Value)
			{
				x = null;
			}
			if (y == DBNull.Value)
			{
				y = null;
			}
			if (x == null && y == null)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			if (x == null)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			if (y == null)
			{
				return 1;
			}
			Type type = x.GetType();
			Type type2 = y.GetType();
			if (type == type2 && type is IComparable && type != DataRow.StringType)
			{
				return ((IComparable)x).CompareTo(y);
			}
			if (IsNumberType(type) && IsNumberType(type2))
			{
				double num = double.Parse(x.ToString());
				double value = double.Parse(y.ToString());
				return num.CompareTo(value);
			}
			if (type == DataRow.StringType || type2 == DataRow.StringType)
			{
				double result = 0.0;
				double result2 = 0.0;
				if (double.TryParse(x.ToString(), out result) && double.TryParse(y.ToString(), out result2))
				{
					return result.CompareTo(result2);
				}
			}
			return x.ToString().CompareTo(y.ToString());
		}

		private static bool IsNumberType(Type type)
		{
			if (!(type == DataRow.Int32Type) && !(type == DataRow.DoubleType) && !(type == DataRow.Int64Type) && !(type == DataRow.Int16Type) && !(type == DataRow.FloatType))
			{
				return type == DataRow.DecimalType;
			}
			return true;
		}
	}
	internal class ScrollHelper
	{
		private const string GridControlHorizontalScrollBar = "PART_HorizontalScrollBar";

		private const string GridControlVerticalScrollBar = "PART_VerticalScrollBar";

		private const string DataGridScrollViewer = "DG_ScrollViewer";

		private const int StartDeferHorizontalScrollPoint = 100;

		private readonly GridControl _gc;

		private ScrollBar _horizontalScrollBar;

		private ScrollBar _verticalScrollBar;

		private ScrollViewer _scrollViewer;

		private bool _loaded;

		private bool? _skipMouseWhellProcess;

		public ScrollViewer ScrollViewer => _scrollViewer;

		public ScrollHelper(GridControl gc)
		{
			if (gc == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("gc");
			}
			_gc = gc;
		}

		public void InitializeScrollBars()
		{
			if (_gc.DataGrid != null)
			{
				_gc.DataGrid.Loaded += DataGrid_Loaded;
			}
		}

		public void ScrollVertically(bool up)
		{
			if (_loaded && _scrollViewer != null)
			{
				if (up)
				{
					ScrollDataGridToVerticalOffset(_scrollViewer.VerticalOffset - 1.0);
				}
				else
				{
					ScrollDataGridToVerticalOffset(_scrollViewer.VerticalOffset + 1.0);
				}
			}
		}

		public void ScrollHorizontally(bool left)
		{
			if (_loaded && _scrollViewer != null)
			{
				if (left)
				{
					MoveHorizontallyByColumns(-1);
				}
				else
				{
					MoveHorizontallyByColumns(1);
				}
			}
		}

		public void OnDataGridApplyTemplate()
		{
			_horizontalScrollBar = _gc.Template.FindName("PART_HorizontalScrollBar", _gc) as ScrollBar;
			_verticalScrollBar = _gc.Template.FindName("PART_VerticalScrollBar", _gc) as ScrollBar;
			_scrollViewer = _gc.DataGrid.Template.FindName("DG_ScrollViewer", _gc.DataGrid) as ScrollViewer;
			if (_scrollViewer != null)
			{
				_scrollViewer.ApplyTemplate();
				if (_scrollViewer.Template.FindName("PART_ColumnHeadersPresenter", _scrollViewer) is DataGridColumnHeadersPresenter dataGridColumnHeadersPresenter)
				{
					dataGridColumnHeadersPresenter.ItemsPanel = GridControlDataGridCellsPanel.HeaderItemsPanelTemplate;
				}
				if (_horizontalScrollBar != null)
				{
					SetBinding(_scrollViewer, ScrollViewer.ScrollableWidthProperty, _horizontalScrollBar, RangeBase.MaximumProperty);
					SetBinding(_scrollViewer, ScrollViewer.ViewportWidthProperty, _horizontalScrollBar, ScrollBar.ViewportSizeProperty);
					_horizontalScrollBar.SetBinding(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, new MultiBinding
					{
						Bindings = 
						{
							(BindingBase)new Binding
							{
								Source = _gc,
								Path = new PropertyPath(GridControl.HorizontalScrollBarVisibilityProperty)
							},
							(BindingBase)new Binding
							{
								RelativeSource = RelativeSource.Self,
								Path = new PropertyPath(UIElement.IsEnabledProperty)
							}
						},
						Converter = ScrollBarVisibilityToVisibilityConverter.Horizontal,
						Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
					});
					_horizontalScrollBar.Scroll += _horizontalScrollBar_Scroll;
				}
				if (_verticalScrollBar != null)
				{
					SetBinding(_scrollViewer, ScrollViewer.ScrollableHeightProperty, _verticalScrollBar, RangeBase.MaximumProperty);
					SetBinding(_scrollViewer, ScrollViewer.ViewportHeightProperty, _verticalScrollBar, ScrollBar.ViewportSizeProperty);
					_verticalScrollBar.SetBinding(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, new MultiBinding
					{
						Bindings = 
						{
							(BindingBase)new Binding
							{
								Source = _gc,
								Path = new PropertyPath(GridControl.VerticalScrollBarVisibilityProperty)
							},
							(BindingBase)new Binding
							{
								RelativeSource = RelativeSource.Self,
								Path = new PropertyPath(UIElement.IsEnabledProperty)
							},
							(BindingBase)new Binding
							{
								Source = _gc,
								Path = new PropertyPath(FrameworkElement.ActualHeightProperty)
							},
							(BindingBase)new Binding
							{
								Source = _gc,
								Path = new PropertyPath(GridControl.FullDisplayDesiredHeightProperty)
							}
						},
						Converter = ScrollBarVisibilityToVisibilityConverter.Vertical,
						Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
					});
					_verticalScrollBar.Scroll += _verticalScrollBar_Scroll;
				}
				_scrollViewer.ScrollChanged += _scrollViewer_ScrollChanged;
				if (VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(_scrollViewer) > 0 && VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(_scrollViewer, 0) is Grid grid)
				{
					grid.MouseWheel += ScrollViewerInnerGrid_MouseWheel;
					_scrollViewer.AddHandler(UIElement.MouseWheelEvent, new MouseWheelEventHandler(ScrollViewer_OnMouseWheelThunk), handledEventsToo: true);
				}
			}
		}

		private void DataGrid_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_loaded)
			{
				_loaded = true;
			}
		}

		private void ScrollViewer_OnMouseWheelThunk(object sender, MouseWheelEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_skipMouseWhellProcess.HasValue)
			{
				if (e.Handled)
				{
					e.Handled = false;
				}
				_skipMouseWhellProcess = null;
			}
		}

		private void ScrollViewerInnerGrid_MouseWheel(object sender, MouseWheelEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!e.Handled)
			{
				_skipMouseWhellProcess = true;
			}
		}

		private void _horizontalScrollBar_Scroll(object sender, ScrollEventArgs e)
		{
			switch (e.ScrollEventType)
			{
			case ScrollEventType.LargeDecrement:
				MoveHorizontallyByColumns(-3);
				break;
			case ScrollEventType.LargeIncrement:
				MoveHorizontallyByColumns(3);
				break;
			case ScrollEventType.SmallDecrement:
				MoveHorizontallyByColumns(-1);
				break;
			case ScrollEventType.SmallIncrement:
				MoveHorizontallyByColumns(1);
				break;
			case ScrollEventType.ThumbTrack:
				if (!_gc.IsHorizontalScrollDeferred && !_gc.InternalColumnsVirtualizationHelper.UseVirtualizationLoading && _gc.DataSource.Columns.Count < 100)
				{
					ScrollDataGridToHorizontalOffset(e.NewValue);
				}
				break;
			default:
				ScrollDataGridToHorizontalOffset(e.NewValue);
				break;
			}
		}

		private void _verticalScrollBar_Scroll(object sender, ScrollEventArgs e)
		{
			switch (e.ScrollEventType)
			{
			case ScrollEventType.LargeDecrement:
				ScrollDataGridToVerticalOffset(_scrollViewer.VerticalOffset - _scrollViewer.ViewportHeight + 1.0);
				break;
			case ScrollEventType.LargeIncrement:
				ScrollDataGridToVerticalOffset(_scrollViewer.VerticalOffset + _scrollViewer.ViewportHeight - 1.0);
				break;
			case ScrollEventType.ThumbTrack:
				if (!_gc.IsVerticalScrollDeferred)
				{
					ScrollDataGridToVerticalOffset(e.NewValue);
				}
				break;
			default:
				ScrollDataGridToVerticalOffset(e.NewValue);
				break;
			}
		}

		private void _scrollViewer_ScrollChanged(object sender, ScrollChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.OriginalSource == sender)
			{
				_horizontalScrollBar.Value = e.HorizontalOffset;
				_verticalScrollBar.Value = e.VerticalOffset;
				if (e.HorizontalChange != 0.0 || e.ViewportWidthChange != 0.0)
				{
					_gc.OnHorizontalScrollChanged();
				}
				_gc.OnScrollChanged(new GridControlScrollChangedEventArgs(e, GridControl.ScrollChangedEvent, _gc));
				if (e.VerticalChange != 0.0 && _gc.VerticalScrollUpdateLayout)
				{
					InvalidateScrollContent();
				}
			}
		}

		private void ScrollDataGridToVerticalOffset(double verticalOffset)
		{
			try
			{
				_scrollViewer.ScrollToVerticalOffset(verticalOffset);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		private void ScrollDataGridToHorizontalOffset(double horizontalOffset)
		{
			try
			{
				_scrollViewer.ScrollToHorizontalOffset(horizontalOffset);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		private void MoveHorizontallyByColumns(int columns)
		{
			double horizontalOffset = _scrollViewer.HorizontalOffset;
			if (columns > 0)
			{
				int num = 0;
				double num2 = 0.0;
				int frozenColumnCount = _gc.DataGrid.FrozenColumnCount;
				int num3 = _gc.DataGrid.Columns.Count((DataGridColumn x) => x.Visibility == Visibility.Visible);
				for (int num4 = frozenColumnCount; num4 < num3; num4++)
				{
					DataGridColumn dataGridColumn = _gc.DataGrid.ColumnFromDisplayIndex(num4);
					num2 += dataGridColumn.ActualWidth;
					if (num2 > horizontalOffset)
					{
						num++;
					}
					if (num >= columns)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
				ScrollDataGridToHorizontalOffset(num2);
			}
			else
			{
				if (columns >= 0)
				{
					return;
				}
				int num5 = 0;
				double num6 = 0.0;
				int frozenColumnCount2 = _gc.DataGrid.FrozenColumnCount;
				int num7 = _gc.DataGrid.Columns.Count((DataGridColumn x) => x.Visibility == Visibility.Visible);
				for (int num8 = frozenColumnCount2; num8 < num7; num8++)
				{
					DataGridColumn dataGridColumn2 = _gc.DataGrid.ColumnFromDisplayIndex(num8);
					num6 += dataGridColumn2.ActualWidth;
					if (num6 >= horizontalOffset)
					{
						num5 = num8;
						break;
					}
				}
				int num9 = 0;
				for (int num10 = num5; num10 >= frozenColumnCount2; num10--)
				{
					DataGridColumn dataGridColumn3 = _gc.DataGrid.ColumnFromDisplayIndex(num10);
					num6 -= dataGridColumn3.ActualWidth;
					num9++;
					if (num9 >= -columns)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
				ScrollDataGridToHorizontalOffset(num6);
			}
		}

		private void InvalidateScrollContent()
		{
			if (_scrollViewer.Content is FrameworkElement { Margin: var margin } frameworkElement)
			{
				if (margin.Right == 0.0)
				{
					frameworkElement.Margin = new Thickness(0.0, 0.0, 0.01, 0.0);
				}
				else if (frameworkElement.Margin.Right > 0.0)
				{
					frameworkElement.Margin = new Thickness(0.0, 0.0, -0.01, 0.0);
				}
				else
				{
					frameworkElement.Margin = new Thickness(0.0);
				}
			}
		}

		private static void SetBinding(DependencyObject source, DependencyProperty sourceProperty, DependencyObject target, DependencyProperty targetProperty, BindingMode mode = BindingMode.OneWay)
		{
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(target, targetProperty, new Binding
			{
				Source = source,
				Path = new PropertyPath(sourceProperty),
				Mode = mode
			});
		}
	}
	internal class TabControlHelper
	{
		private const string PART_TabControl = "PART_TabControl";

		private readonly GridControlPanel _panel;

		private TabControl _tabControl;

		private bool inUpdate;

		public TabControlHelper(GridControlPanel panel)
		{
			if (panel == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("panel");
			}
			_panel = panel;
		}

		public void InitializeOnTemplateApplied()
		{
			if (_panel.Template != null)
			{
				_tabControl = _panel.Template.FindName("PART_TabControl", _panel) as TabControl;
				if (_tabControl != null)
				{
					_tabControl.SelectionChanged += _tabControl_SelectionChanged;
					_tabControl.MouseRightButtonDown += _tabControl_MouseRightButtonDown;
					_tabControl.ContextMenuOpening += _tabControl_ContextMenuOpening;
					_panel.GridControls.CollectionChanged += GridControls_CollectionChanged;
					_panel.ActiveGridControlChanged += _panel_ActiveGridControlChanged;
					UpdateTabControl();
				}
			}
		}

		private void _tabControl_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			GridControl grid = FindGridControl(_tabControl.SelectedItem as TabItem);
			if (grid == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (grid.IsDummyForAdd)
			{
				if (inUpdate)
				{
					_tabControl.SelectedItem = ((_tabControl.Items.Count > 1) ? _tabControl.Items[0] : null);
					return;
				}
				grid.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					object selectedItem = _tabControl.SelectedItem;
					grid.AddFromDummy();
					if (selectedItem == _tabControl.SelectedItem)
					{
						TabItem tabItem = FindTabItem(_panel.ActiveGridControl);
						if (tabItem != null && tabItem != selectedItem)
						{
							_tabControl.SelectedItem = tabItem;
						}
						else
						{
							_tabControl.SelectedItem = ((_tabControl.Items.Count > 1) ? _tabControl.Items[0] : null);
						}
					}
				});
			}
			else
			{
				_panel.ActiveGridControl = grid;
			}
		}

		private void _tabControl_MouseRightButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			TabItem ancestor = (e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject).GetAncestor<TabItem>(_tabControl);
			if (ancestor != null)
			{
				GridControl gridControl = FindGridControl(ancestor);
				if (gridControl != null && !gridControl.IsDummyForAdd)
				{
					_panel.ActiveGridControl = gridControl;
				}
			}
		}

		private void _tabControl_ContextMenuOpening(object sender, ContextMenuEventArgs e)
		{
			if ((e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject).GetAncestor<TabItem>(_tabControl) == null || _panel.HeaderContextMenu == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (object item in (IEnumerable)_panel.HeaderContextMenu.Items)
			{
				if (item is MenuItem { CommandTarget: null } menuItem)
				{
					menuItem.CommandTarget = _panel;
				}
			}
			_panel.HeaderContextMenu.IsOpen = true;
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private void GridControls_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateTabControl();
		}

		private void _panel_ActiveGridControlChanged(object sender, RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<GridControl> e)
		{
			TabItem tabItem = FindTabItem(_panel.ActiveGridControl);
			if (tabItem != null)
			{
				_tabControl.SelectedItem = tabItem;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateTabControl()
		{
			TabItem tabItem = _tabControl.Items.Cast<TabItem>().FirstOrDefault((TabItem x) => FindGridControl(x).IsDummyForAdd);
			GridControl gridControl = ((tabItem == null) ? null : FindGridControl(tabItem));
			List<GridControl> list = (from TabItem x in _tabControl.Items
				select FindGridControl(x)).ToList();
			List<GridControl> list2 = list.Except(_panel.GridControls).ToList();
			if (gridControl != null && !list2.Contains(gridControl))
			{
				list2.Add(gridControl);
			}
			else
			{
				gridControl = null;
			}
			List<GridControl> list3 = _panel.GridControls.Except(list).ToList();
			if (gridControl != null && !list3.Contains(gridControl))
			{
				list3.Add(gridControl);
			}
			inUpdate = true;
			foreach (GridControl item in list2)
			{
				_tabControl.Items.Remove(FindTabItem(item));
			}
			foreach (GridControl item2 in list3)
			{
				_tabControl.Items.Add(CreateTabItem(item2));
			}
			inUpdate = false;
		}

		private TabItem CreateTabItem(GridControl grid)
		{
			TabItem tabItem = new TabItem();
			tabItem.Content = grid;
			tabItem.SetBinding(HeaderedContentControl.HeaderProperty, new Binding
			{
				Source = grid,
				Path = new PropertyPath(GridControl.TitleProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			tabItem.SetBinding(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, new Binding
			{
				Source = grid,
				Path = new PropertyPath(UIElement.VisibilityProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			return tabItem;
		}

		private GridControl FindGridControl(TabItem item)
		{
			if (item != null)
			{
				return item.Content as GridControl;
			}
			return null;
		}

		private TabItem FindTabItem(GridControl grid)
		{
			if (grid != null)
			{
				return grid.Parent as TabItem;
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public static class TimingDebugger
	{
		private class TimingItem
		{
			public string Tag { get; private set; }

			public DateTime Time { get; private set; }

			public bool IsEnd { get; private set; }

			public TimingItem(string tag, bool isEnd)
			{
				Tag = tag;
				IsEnd = isEnd;
				Time = DateTime.Now;
			}
		}

		private static List<TimingItem> _timings;

		private static List<TimingItem> Timings
		{
			get
			{
				if (_timings == null)
				{
					_timings = new List<TimingItem>();
				}
				return _timings;
			}
		}

		private static bool IsTurnedOn => false;

		public static void BeingTimingBlock(string tag)
		{
			if (IsTurnedOn)
			{
				Timings.Add(new TimingItem(tag, isEnd: false));
			}
		}

		public static void EndTimingBlock()
		{
			if (IsTurnedOn)
			{
				Timings.Add(new TimingItem(null, isEnd: true));
			}
		}

		public static void Reset()
		{
			if (IsTurnedOn)
			{
				Timings.Clear();
			}
		}

		public static void RegisterTimingReportKey(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			if (IsTurnedOn && element != null)
			{
				element.PreviewKeyDown += element_PreviewKeyDown;
				element.Unloaded += element_Unloaded;
			}
		}

		private static void element_Unloaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is FrameworkElement frameworkElement)
			{
				frameworkElement.PreviewKeyDown -= element_PreviewKeyDown;
				frameworkElement.Unloaded -= element_Unloaded;
			}
		}

		private static void element_PreviewKeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key != Key.F8 || Keyboard.Modifiers != ModifierKeys.Control)
			{
				return;
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			Stack<TimingItem> stack = new Stack<TimingItem>();
			TimingItem timingItem = null;
			foreach (TimingItem timing in _timings)
			{
				if (!timing.IsEnd)
				{
					for (int i = 0; i < stack.Count; i++)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append('\t');
					}
					stringBuilder.Append("BEGIN ").Append(timing.Tag).Append(" @ ")
						.Append(timing.Time.ToString("hh:mm:ss fff"));
					stack.Push(timing);
				}
				else if (stack.Count > 0)
				{
					TimingItem timingItem2 = stack.Pop();
					double totalMilliseconds = (timing.Time - timingItem2.Time).TotalMilliseconds;
					for (int j = 0; j < stack.Count; j++)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append('\t');
					}
					stringBuilder.Append("End ").Append(timing.Tag).Append(" @ ")
						.Append(timing.Time.ToString("hh:mm:ss fff"))
						.Append(", Total ms: ")
						.Append(totalMilliseconds);
				}
				if (timingItem != null && (timing.Time - timingItem.Time).TotalMilliseconds > 10.0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append("\t\t !!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!BE CAREFUL!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!");
				}
				timingItem = timing;
				stringBuilder.AppendLine();
			}
			TextBox content = new TextBox
			{
				Text = stringBuilder.ToString(),
				AcceptsReturn = true,
				VerticalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Auto,
				HorizontalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Auto,
				IsReadOnly = true
			};
			Window window = new Window();
			window.Content = content;
			window.Owner = Window.GetWindow(sender as UIElement);
			window.Show();
		}
	}
}
namespace AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Filter
{
	public class DataColumnFilterControl : Control
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty SelectedOperatorProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SelectedOperandProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AvailableOperatorsProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AvailableOperandsProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsOperandDropdownOpenProperty;

		public static readonly RoutedCommand OperatorSelectCommand;

		public static readonly RoutedCommand FilterClearCommand;

		private DataColumn _parentColumn;

		private Button _operatorButton;

		private ComboBox _operandComboBox;

		public DataColumnFilterOperator SelectedOperator
		{
			get
			{
				return (DataColumnFilterOperator)GetValue(SelectedOperatorProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(SelectedOperatorProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public string SelectedOperand
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(SelectedOperandProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(SelectedOperandProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<DataColumnFilterOperator> AvailableOperators
		{
			get
			{
				return (IEnumerable<DataColumnFilterOperator>)GetValue(AvailableOperatorsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(AvailableOperatorsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<string> AvailableOperands
		{
			get
			{
				return (IEnumerable<string>)GetValue(AvailableOperandsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(AvailableOperandsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsOperandDropdownOpen
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsOperandDropdownOpenProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsOperandDropdownOpenProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private DataColumn ParentColumn
		{
			get
			{
				if (_parentColumn == null)
				{
					DataGridColumnHeader ancestor = this.GetAncestor<DataGridColumnHeader>();
					if (ancestor != null && ancestor.Column is GridControlColumn gridControlColumn)
					{
						_parentColumn = gridControlColumn.DataColumn;
					}
				}
				return _parentColumn;
			}
		}

		static DataColumnFilterControl()
		{
			SelectedOperatorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SelectedOperator", typeof(DataColumnFilterOperator), typeof(DataColumnFilterControl));
			SelectedOperandProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SelectedOperand", typeof(string), typeof(DataColumnFilterControl));
			AvailableOperatorsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AvailableOperators", typeof(IEnumerable<DataColumnFilterOperator>), typeof(DataColumnFilterControl));
			AvailableOperandsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AvailableOperands", typeof(IEnumerable<string>), typeof(DataColumnFilterControl));
			IsOperandDropdownOpenProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsOperandDropdownOpen", typeof(bool), typeof(DataColumnFilterControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnIsOperandDropdownOpenPropertyChanged));
			OperatorSelectCommand = new RoutedCommand("OperatorSelectionCommand", typeof(DataColumnFilter));
			FilterClearCommand = new RoutedCommand("FilterClearCommand", typeof(DataColumnFilter));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(DataColumnFilterControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(DataColumnFilterControl)));
			Control.IsTabStopProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(DataColumnFilterControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(DataColumnFilterControl), new CommandBinding(OperatorSelectCommand, OperatorSelectCommand_Executed, OperatorSelectCommand_CanExecute));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(DataColumnFilterControl), new CommandBinding(FilterClearCommand, FilterClearCommand_Executed, FilterClearCommand_CanExecute));
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			_operatorButton = GetTemplateChild("PART_Operator") as Button;
			_operandComboBox = GetTemplateChild("PART_ComboBox") as ComboBox;
			if (_operandComboBox != null)
			{
				_operandComboBox.SelectionChanged += _operandComboBox_SelectionChanged;
			}
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
		}

		private void _operandComboBox_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		private void ApplySelection()
		{
			if (_operandComboBox != null)
			{
				_operandComboBox.GetBindingExpression(ComboBox.TextProperty)?.UpdateSource();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewKeyDown(KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Return)
			{
				ApplySelection();
			}
			base.OnPreviewKeyDown(e);
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsOperandDropdownOpenChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			if (ParentColumn != null)
			{
				ParentColumn.Filter.UpdateAvailableOperands();
				UpdateComoboBox(_operandComboBox);
				if (newValue)
				{
					ParentColumn.TargetGridColumn.GridControlOwner.InternalFilterHelper.OnColumnFilterDropdownReopened(ParentColumn);
				}
				else
				{
					ParentColumn.TargetGridColumn.GridControlOwner.InternalFilterHelper.OnColumnFilterDropdownClosed(ParentColumn);
				}
			}
			if (oldValue && !newValue)
			{
				ApplySelection();
			}
		}

		private void UpdateComoboBox(ComboBox box)
		{
			if (box != null)
			{
				string text = box.Text;
				box.ItemsSource = AvailableOperands;
				box.Text = text;
			}
		}

		private void OpenOperatorPopup()
		{
			if (AvailableOperators == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			ContextMenu contextMenu = new ContextMenu
			{
				PlacementTarget = _operatorButton,
				Placement = PlacementMode.Bottom
			};
			foreach (DataColumnFilterOperator availableOperator in AvailableOperators)
			{
				MenuItem menuItem = new MenuItem
				{
					Icon = new Image
					{
						Source = FilterHelper.GetFilterOperatorImage(availableOperator)
					},
					Header = FilterHelper.GetFilterOperatorText(availableOperator),
					Tag = availableOperator
				};
				menuItem.Click += mi_Click;
				contextMenu.Items.Add(menuItem);
			}
			contextMenu.IsOpen = true;
		}

		private void mi_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			DataColumnFilterOperator selectedOperator = (DataColumnFilterOperator)(sender as MenuItem).Tag;
			SelectedOperator = selectedOperator;
		}

		private static void OnIsOperandDropdownOpenPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataColumnFilterControl).OnIsOperandDropdownOpenChanged((bool)e.OldValue, (bool)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OperatorSelectCommand_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			(sender as DataColumnFilterControl).OpenOperatorPopup();
		}

		private static void OperatorSelectCommand_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = true;
		}

		private static void FilterClearCommand_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			(sender as DataColumnFilterControl).SelectedOperand = null;
		}

		private static void FilterClearCommand_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			DataColumnFilterControl dataColumnFilterControl = sender as DataColumnFilterControl;
			e.CanExecute = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(dataColumnFilterControl.SelectedOperand);
		}
	}
	public enum DataColumnFilterOperator
	{
		Equal,
		NotEqual,
		GreaterThan,
		GreaterThanOrEqual,
		LessThan,
		LessThanOrEqual,
		StartsWith,
		NotStartsWith,
		EndsWith,
		NotEndsWith,
		Contains,
		NotContains
	}
	public class DataColumnFilter : NotifiableObjectBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty SelectedOperatorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SelectedOperator", typeof(DataColumnFilterOperator), typeof(DataColumnFilter), new PropertyMetadata(DataColumnFilterOperator.Equal, OnSelectedOperatorPropertyChanged));

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey AvailableOperatorsPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("AvailableOperators", typeof(IEnumerable<DataColumnFilterOperator>), typeof(DataColumnFilter), new PropertyMetadata());

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AvailableOperatorsProperty = AvailableOperatorsPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SelectedOperandProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SelectedOperand", typeof(string), typeof(DataColumnFilter), new PropertyMetadata(OnSelectedOperandPropertyChanged));

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey AvailableOperandsPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("AvailableOperands", typeof(IEnumerable<string>), typeof(DataColumnFilter), new PropertyMetadata());

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AvailableOperandsProperty = AvailableOperandsPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty VisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Visibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(DataColumnFilter), new PropertyMetadata(Visibility.Collapsed, OnVisibilityPropertyChanged));

		private bool _invalidateOperandsFlag;

		private bool _suppressNotification;

		public DataColumn Column { get; private set; }

		public DataColumnFilterOperator SelectedOperator
		{
			get
			{
				return (DataColumnFilterOperator)GetValue(SelectedOperatorProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(SelectedOperatorProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<DataColumnFilterOperator> AvailableOperators
		{
			get
			{
				return (IEnumerable<DataColumnFilterOperator>)GetValue(AvailableOperatorsProperty);
			}
			private set
			{
				SetValue(AvailableOperatorsPropertyKey, value);
			}
		}

		public string SelectedOperand
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(SelectedOperandProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(SelectedOperandProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<string> AvailableOperands
		{
			get
			{
				return (IEnumerable<string>)GetValue(AvailableOperandsProperty);
			}
			private set
			{
				SetValue(AvailableOperandsPropertyKey, value);
			}
		}

		public Visibility Visibility
		{
			get
			{
				return (Visibility)GetValue(VisibilityProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(VisibilityProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public object ResolvedValidSelectedOperand => DataColumn.ConvertStringToObject(SelectedOperand, Column.DefaultDataType);

		public DataColumnFilter(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (column == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("column");
			}
			Column = column;
		}

		public void UpdateAvailableOperands()
		{
			if ((AvailableOperands == null || _invalidateOperandsFlag) && Column.TargetGridColumn != null && Column.TargetGridColumn.GridControlOwner != null)
			{
				AvailableOperands = Column.TargetGridColumn.GridControlOwner.InternalFilterHelper.GetColumnFilterOperands(Column);
				_invalidateOperandsFlag = false;
			}
		}

		public void ResetAvailableOperands()
		{
			_invalidateOperandsFlag = true;
		}

		public void SuppressFilterChangeNotification()
		{
			_suppressNotification = true;
		}

		public void FreeFilterChangeNotification(bool notifyChange)
		{
			_suppressNotification = false;
			if (notifyChange)
			{
				NotifyFilterChanged();
			}
		}

		private void NotifyFilterChanged()
		{
			if (!_suppressNotification)
			{
				GridControlColumn targetGridColumn = Column.TargetGridColumn;
				if (targetGridColumn != null && targetGridColumn.GridControlOwner != null && !targetGridColumn.GridControlOwner.InternalFilterHelper.IsFilterChangedUpdateLocked && targetGridColumn.GridControlOwner.DataSource != null)
				{
					targetGridColumn.GridControlOwner.DataSource.OnFilteringChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnSelectedOperatorChanged(DataColumnFilterOperator oldValue, DataColumnFilterOperator newValue)
		{
			NotifyFilterChanged();
		}

		private void OnSelectedOperandChanged(string oldValue, string newValue)
		{
			if (Column.HeaderControl != null)
			{
				Column.HeaderControl.InvalidateVisual();
			}
			NotifyFilterChanged();
		}

		private void OnVisibilityChanged(Visibility oldValue, Visibility newValue)
		{
			if (newValue == Visibility.Visible && AvailableOperators == null)
			{
				IEnumerable<DataColumnFilterOperator> defaultFilterOperators = FilterHelper.GetDefaultFilterOperators(Column);
				FilterOperatorsQueriedEventArgs e = new FilterOperatorsQueriedEventArgs(Column, defaultFilterOperators, GridControl.FilterOperatorsQueriedEvent, Column.TargetGridColumn.GridControlOwner);
				Column.TargetGridColumn.GridControlOwner.OnFilterOperatorsQueried(e);
				AvailableOperators = e.Operators;
			}
			Column.InvalidateHeaderControlTemplate();
		}

		private static void OnSelectedOperatorPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataColumnFilter).OnSelectedOperatorChanged((DataColumnFilterOperator)e.OldValue, (DataColumnFilterOperator)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnSelectedOperandPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataColumnFilter).OnSelectedOperandChanged((string)e.OldValue, (string)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnVisibilityPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataColumnFilter).OnVisibilityChanged((Visibility)e.OldValue, (Visibility)e.NewValue);
		}
	}
	public class DataColumnFilterPopup : Popup, IComponentConnector
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty SourceOperandsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SourceOperands", typeof(IEnumerable<string>), typeof(DataColumnFilterPopup), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnSourceOperandsPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SelectedFilterItemProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SelectedFilterItem", typeof(string), typeof(DataColumnFilterPopup));

		private readonly string _oldSelectedOperand;

		private readonly DataColumnFilterOperator _oldSelectedOperator;

		internal ListBox theListBox;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public IEnumerable<string> SourceOperands
		{
			get
			{
				return (IEnumerable<string>)GetValue(SourceOperandsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(SourceOperandsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public string SelectedFilterItem
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(SelectedFilterItemProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(SelectedFilterItemProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public DataColumn Column { get; private set; }

		public ObservableCollection<string> FilterItems { get; private set; }

		public DataColumnFilterPopup(DataColumn column)
		{
			if (column == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("column");
			}
			Column = column;
			FilterItems = new ObservableCollection<string>();
			Column.Filter.UpdateAvailableOperands();
			SourceOperands = Column.Filter.AvailableOperands;
			SelectedFilterItem = (_oldSelectedOperand = column.Filter.SelectedOperand);
			_oldSelectedOperator = column.Filter.SelectedOperator;
			InitializeComponent();
		}

		protected override void OnClosed(System.EventArgs e)
		{
			NotifyFilterDropdownClosed();
			base.OnClosed(e);
		}

		private void NotifyFilterDropdownClosed()
		{
			if (Column.TargetGridColumn != null && Column.TargetGridColumn.GridControlOwner != null)
			{
				Column.TargetGridColumn.GridControlOwner.InternalFilterHelper.OnColumnFilterDropdownClosed(Column);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			ListBoxItem ancestor = (e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject).GetAncestor<ListBoxItem>(theListBox);
			if (ancestor != null)
			{
				base.IsOpen = false;
				string text = ancestor.Content as string;
				if (!string.Equals(SelectedFilterItem, text, StringComparison.CurrentCulture))
				{
					SelectedFilterItem = text;
					Column.Filter.SuppressFilterChangeNotification();
					if (string.Equals(SelectedFilterItem, AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridControl_ColumnFilter_All, StringComparison.InvariantCulture))
					{
						Column.Filter.SelectedOperand = null;
					}
					else
					{
						Column.Filter.SelectedOperand = SelectedFilterItem;
					}
					Column.Filter.SelectedOperator = DataColumnFilterOperator.Equal;
					bool notifyChange = !string.Equals(_oldSelectedOperand, SelectedFilterItem) || _oldSelectedOperator != DataColumnFilterOperator.Equal;
					Column.Filter.FreeFilterChangeNotification(notifyChange);
				}
				NotifyFilterDropdownClosed();
			}
			base.OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
		}

		protected virtual void OnSourceOperandsChanged(IEnumerable<string> oldValue, IEnumerable<string> newValue)
		{
			if (oldValue is INotifyCollectionChanged notifyCollectionChanged)
			{
				notifyCollectionChanged.CollectionChanged -= NotifyCollectionChanged_CollectionChanged;
			}
			UpdateFilterItems();
			if (newValue is INotifyCollectionChanged notifyCollectionChanged2)
			{
				notifyCollectionChanged2.CollectionChanged += NotifyCollectionChanged_CollectionChanged;
			}
		}

		private void NotifyCollectionChanged_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateFilterItems();
		}

		private void UpdateFilterItems()
		{
			FilterItems.Clear();
			if (SourceOperands == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			FilterItems.Add(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridControl_ColumnFilter_All);
			if (SourceOperands.Contains(null) || SourceOperands.Contains(string.Empty))
			{
				FilterItems.Add(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.GridControl_ColumnFilter_Blanks);
			}
			foreach (string sourceOperand in SourceOperands)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sourceOperand))
				{
					FilterItems.Add(sourceOperand);
				}
			}
		}

		private static void OnSourceOperandsPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as DataColumnFilterPopup).OnSourceOperandsChanged((IEnumerable<string>)e.OldValue, (IEnumerable<string>)e.NewValue);
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls;component/filter/datacolumnfilterpopup.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			if (connectionId == 1)
			{
				theListBox = (ListBox)target;
			}
			else
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public class CellValuePastingEventArgs : System.EventArgs
	{
		private bool _tried;

		private object _pastingValue;

		public IDataCell Cell { get; private set; }

		public string PastingText { get; private set; }

		public bool Handled { get; set; }

		public bool IsLastRowInClipboard { get; private set; }

		public object PastingValue
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_tried)
				{
					_pastingValue = ConvertTextToValue();
					_tried = true;
				}
				return _pastingValue;
			}
		}

		public CellValuePastingEventArgs(IDataCell cell, string pastngText, bool isLastRowInClipboard = false)
		{
			if (cell == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("cell");
			}
			Cell = cell;
			PastingText = pastngText;
			IsLastRowInClipboard = isLastRowInClipboard;
		}

		private object ConvertTextToValue()
		{
			Type valueType = Cell.ValueType;
			if (valueType == typeof(double))
			{
				double result = 0.0;
				if (double.TryParse(PastingText, out result))
				{
					return result;
				}
				return null;
			}
			if (valueType == typeof(int))
			{
				int result2 = 0;
				if (int.TryParse(PastingText, out result2))
				{
					return result2;
				}
				return null;
			}
			if (valueType == typeof(bool))
			{
				bool result3 = false;
				if (bool.TryParse(PastingText, out result3))
				{
					return result3;
				}
				return false;
			}
			return PastingText;
		}
	}

		}
	}

	public class FilterOperatorsQueriedEventArgs : RoutedEventArgs
	{
		public DataColumn Column { get; private set; }

		public IList<DataColumnFilterOperator> Operators { get; private set; }

		public FilterOperatorsQueriedEventArgs(DataColumn column, IEnumerable<DataColumnFilterOperator> operators, RoutedEvent routedEvent, object source)
			: base(routedEvent, source)
		{
			if (column == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("column");
			}
			Column = column;
			if (operators == null)
			{
				Operators = new List<DataColumnFilterOperator>();
			}
			else
			{
				Operators = operators.ToList();
			}
		}
	}
