	public class GridControlCopyEventArgs : GridControlEventArgs
	{
		public Dictionary<string, object> ClipboardContents { get; private set; }

		public Dictionary<DataColumn, string> ColumnPrefixes { get; private set; }

		public Func<DataColumn, string> ColumnHeaderTextFunc { get; set; }

		public bool ResolvedIncludeHeader
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.GridControl.ClipboardCopyMode == GridControlClipboardCopyMode.IncludeHeader)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (base.GridControl.ClipboardCopyMode == GridControlClipboardCopyMode.IncludeHeaderOnlyWhenColumnSelected && base.GridControl.SelectedColumns.FirstOrDefault() != null)
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public bool IsAllSelected => base.GridControl.InternalSelectionHelper.IsAllSelected;

		public GridControlCopyEventArgs(GridControl gridControl, RoutedEvent routedEvent, object source)
			: base(gridControl, routedEvent, source)
		{
			ClipboardContents = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			ColumnPrefixes = new Dictionary<DataColumn, string>();
		}

		public string GenerateTextToCopy(List<IGridCellData> cellData = null, bool? includeHeader = null, bool removeLastRow = false)
		{
			return GridClipboardHelper.GenerateTextToCopy(base.GridControl.DataSource, base.GridControl.SelectedRows, base.GridControl.SelectedColumns, base.GridControl.SelectedCells, includeHeader ?? ResolvedIncludeHeader, ColumnHeaderTextFunc, ColumnPrefixes, cellData, removeLastRow);
		}

		public string GenerateTextToCopyAndRemoveEmptyRowsColumns()
		{
			return GridClipboardHelper.GenerateTextToCopyAndRemoveEmptyRowsColumns(base.GridControl.DataSource, base.GridControl.SelectedRows, base.GridControl.SelectedColumns, base.GridControl.SelectedCells, ResolvedIncludeHeader, ColumnHeaderTextFunc, ColumnPrefixes);
		}

		public static List<DataColumn> GetOrderedIncludedColumns(DataTable table, List<DataRow> selectedRows, List<DataColumn> selectedColumns, List<DataCellInfo> selectedCells)
		{
			return GridClipboardHelper.GetOrderedIncludedColumns(table, selectedRows, selectedColumns, selectedCells);
		}

		public static List<DataRow> GetOrderedIncludedRows(DataTable table, List<DataRow> selectedRows, List<DataColumn> selectedColumns, List<DataCellInfo> selectedCells)
		{
			return GridClipboardHelper.GetOrderedIncludedRows(table, selectedRows, selectedColumns, selectedCells);
		}

		internal void SendToClipboard()
		{
			if (ClipboardContents.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			DataObject data = new DataObject();
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, object> clipboardContent in ClipboardContents)
			{
				if (clipboardContent.Value != null)
				{
					data.SetData(DataFormats.GetDataFormat(clipboardContent.Key).Name, clipboardContent.Value);
				}
			}
			TimesExecution.TryExecute(delegate
			{
				Clipboard.SetDataObject(data, copy: true);
			});
		}
	}

		}

		public GridControlScrollChangedEventArgs(double extentHeight, double extentHeightChange, double extentWidth, double extentWidthChange, double horizontalChange, double horizontalOffset, double verticalChange, double verticalOffset, double viewportHeight, double viewportHeightChange, double viewportWidth, double viewportWidthChange, RoutedEvent routedEvent, object source)

			ViewportWidthChange = viewportWidthChange;

		}
	}

	public class DefaultContextMenuEventArgs : RoutedEventArgs
	{
		private bool _sourceResolved;

		private DataGridColumnHeader _sourceColumnHeader;

		private DataGridRowHeader _sourceRowHeader;

		private DataGridCell _sourceCell;

		private DataGrid _sourceGrid;

		private ScrollBar _sourceScrollBar;

		public GridControl GridControl { get; private set; }

		public ContextMenu DefaultContextMenu { get; private set; }

		public RoutedEventArgs OriginalEventArgs { get; private set; }

		public bool IsOpenFromHeaderPrefixArea
		{
			get
			{
				if (!IsOpenFromColumnHeader && !IsOpenFromRowHeader && !IsOpenFromGridCell)
				{
					return !IsOpenFromBlankArea;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public bool IsOpenFromColumnHeader => SourceColumnHeader != null;

		public bool IsOpenFromRowHeader => SourceRowHeader != null;

		public bool IsOpenFromGridCell => SourceCell != null;

		public bool IsOpenFromBlankArea => SourceGrid == null;

		public bool IsOpenFromScrollBar => SourceScrollBar != null;

		public DataGridColumnHeader SourceColumnHeader => ResolveSourceElement(ref _sourceColumnHeader);

		public DataGridRowHeader SourceRowHeader => ResolveSourceElement(ref _sourceRowHeader);

		public DataGridCell SourceCell => ResolveSourceElement(ref _sourceCell);

		public DataRow SourceRow
		{
			get
			{
				if (SourceRowHeader != null)
				{
					return SourceRowHeader.DataContext as DataRow;
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		public DataColumn SourceColumn
		{
			get
			{
				if (SourceColumnHeader != null && SourceColumnHeader.Column is GridControlColumn gridControlColumn)
				{
					return gridControlColumn.DataColumn;
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		public ScrollBar SourceScrollBar => ResolveSourceElement(ref _sourceScrollBar);

		private DataGrid SourceGrid
		{
			get
			{
				if (_sourceGrid == null)
				{
					_sourceGrid = (OriginalEventArgs.OriginalSource as DependencyObject).GetAncestor<DataGrid>(GridControl);
				}
				return _sourceGrid;
			}
		}

		public DefaultContextMenuEventArgs(GridControl gridControl, ContextMenu defaultContextMenu, RoutedEventArgs originalEventArgs, RoutedEvent routedEvent, object source)
			: base(routedEvent, source)
		{
			if (gridControl == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("gridControl");
			}
			if (defaultContextMenu == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("defaultContextMenu");
			}
			if (originalEventArgs == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("originalEventArgs");
			}
			GridControl = gridControl;
			DefaultContextMenu = defaultContextMenu;
			OriginalEventArgs = originalEventArgs;
		}

		private T ResolveSourceElement<T>(ref T sourceElement) where T : DependencyObject
		{
			if (!_sourceResolved)
			{
				sourceElement = (OriginalEventArgs.OriginalSource as DependencyObject).GetAncestor<T>(GridControl);
				_sourceResolved = sourceElement != null;
			}
			return sourceElement;
		}
	}

namespace AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Converters

	public class ComboColumnHeaderItemTooltipConverter : IMultiValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (values == null || values.Length != 2)
			{
				return DependencyProperty.UnsetValue;
			}
			if (values[0] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue || values[1] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
			{
				return DependencyProperty.UnsetValue;
			}
			string text = values[0] as string;
			DataColumn dataColumn = values[1] as DataColumn;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && dataColumn != null)
			{
				Dictionary<string, string> headerItemsTooltips = dataColumn.HeaderItemsTooltips;
				if (headerItemsTooltips != null && headerItemsTooltips.ContainsKey(text))
				{
					return headerItemsTooltips[text];
				}
			}
			return DependencyProperty.UnsetValue;
		}

		public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public class ComboColumnHeaderItemTooltipVisibilityConverter : IMultiValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (values == null || values.Length != 2)
			{
				return DependencyProperty.UnsetValue;
			}
			if (values[0] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue || values[1] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
			{
				return DependencyProperty.UnsetValue;
			}
			string text = values[0] as string;
			DataColumn dataColumn = values[1] as DataColumn;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && dataColumn != null)
			{
				Dictionary<string, string> headerItemsTooltips = dataColumn.HeaderItemsTooltips;
				if (headerItemsTooltips != null && headerItemsTooltips.ContainsKey(text) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(headerItemsTooltips[text]))
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
			}
			return Visibility.Collapsed;
		}

		public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public class GroupingStateToImageBrushConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value != null && value != DependencyProperty.UnsetValue && value is RowGroupingState rowGroupingState)
			{
				switch (rowGroupingState)
				{
				case RowGroupingState.Expanded:
					return CellControlTemplates.MinusSigh;
				case RowGroupingState.Collapsed:
					return CellControlTemplates.PlusSigh;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public class GroupingStateToRowFontConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public string Type { get; set; }

		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value != null && value is RowGroupingState && (RowGroupingState)value != RowGroupingState.None)
			{
				if (string.Equals(Type, "Bold", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					return FontWeights.Bold;
				}
				if (string.Equals(Type, "Italic", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					return FontStyles.Italic;
				}
			}
			return DependencyProperty.UnsetValue;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}

	public class ScrollBarVisibilityToVisibilityConverter : IMultiValueConverter
	{
		public static readonly ScrollBarVisibilityToVisibilityConverter Horizontal = new ScrollBarVisibilityToVisibilityConverter
		{
			Flag = 0
		};

		public static readonly ScrollBarVisibilityToVisibilityConverter Vertical = new ScrollBarVisibilityToVisibilityConverter
		{
			Flag = 1
		};

		private int Flag { get; set; }

		public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (Flag == 0)
			{
				return GetHorizontalScrollBarVisibility(values);
			}
			if (Flag == 1)
			{
				return GetVerticalScrollBarVisibility(values);
			}
			return Visibility.Collapsed;
		}

		public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		private Visibility GetHorizontalScrollBarVisibility(object[] values)
		{
			if (values != null && values.Length == 2 && values[0] is ScrollBarVisibility && values[1] is bool)
			{
				ScrollBarVisibility scrollBarVisibility = (ScrollBarVisibility)values[0];
				bool flag = (bool)values[1];
				switch (scrollBarVisibility)
				{
				case ScrollBarVisibility.Visible:
					return Visibility.Visible;
				case ScrollBarVisibility.Hidden:
					return Visibility.Hidden;
				case ScrollBarVisibility.Auto:
					if (flag)
					{
						return Visibility.Visible;
					}
					return Visibility.Collapsed;
				default:
					return Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
			}
			return Visibility.Collapsed;
		}

		private Visibility GetVerticalScrollBarVisibility(object[] values)
		{
			if (values != null && values.Length == 4 && values[0] is ScrollBarVisibility && values[1] is bool && values[2] is double && values[3] is double)
			{
				ScrollBarVisibility scrollBarVisibility = (ScrollBarVisibility)values[0];
				bool flag = (bool)values[1];
				double num = (double)values[2];
				double num2 = (double)values[3];
				switch (scrollBarVisibility)
				{
				case ScrollBarVisibility.Visible:
					return Visibility.Visible;
				case ScrollBarVisibility.Hidden:
					return Visibility.Hidden;
				case ScrollBarVisibility.Auto:
					if (flag)
					{
						return Visibility.Visible;
					}
					if (num2 > num)
					{
						return Visibility.Visible;
					}
					return Visibility.Collapsed;
				default:
					return Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
			}
			return Visibility.Collapsed;
		}
	}
	public class DoubleToTextConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value != null && value != DependencyProperty.UnsetValue && value is double num)
			{
				return num.ToString();
			}
			return null;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value != null && value != DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
			{
				string text = value as string;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					double result = 0.0;
					if (double.TryParse(text, out result))
					{
						return result;
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public class BooleanInvertConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			bool? flag = value as bool?;
			if (flag.HasValue)
			{
				return !flag.Value;
			}
			return DependencyProperty.UnsetValue;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	[ValueConversion(typeof(bool), typeof(Visibility))]
	public class BooleanToVisibilityInverseConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value != null && value != DependencyProperty.UnsetValue && value is bool)
			{
				return ((bool)value) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
			}
			return Visibility.Collapsed;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}

	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value != null && value != DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
			{
				return Visibility.Visible;
			}
			return Visibility.Collapsed;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}

namespace AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Bindings
{
	internal class BindingDataCellWrapper : DependencyObject
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty DataProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Data", typeof(object), typeof(BindingDataCellWrapper), new UIPropertyMetadata(OnDataPropertyChanged));

		private bool _bindingSetup;

		private bool _isSettingBinding;

		public object Data
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(DataProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DataProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public BindingDataRow Row { get; private set; }

		public BindingDataColumn Column { get; private set; }

		public object CellValue
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_bindingSetup)
				{
					ResetBinding();
				}
				return Data;
			}
		}

		public BindingDataCellWrapper(BindingDataRow row, BindingDataColumn column)
		{
			if (row == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("row");
			}
			if (column == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("column");
			}
			Row = row;
			Column = column;
		}

		public void ResetBinding()
		{
			_bindingSetup = false;
			_isSettingBinding = true;
			if (Row.DataItem != null)
			{
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, DataProperty, Column.GenerateBinding(Row.DataItem));
			}
			_isSettingBinding = false;
			_bindingSetup = true;
		}

		protected virtual void OnDataChanged(object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			if (_isSettingBinding)
			{
				return;
			}
			IDataCell dataCell = Row[Column];
			if (dataCell != null)
			{
				if (newValue != null && dataCell.ValueType != DataRow.StringType && newValue is string)
				{
					dataCell.Parse((string)newValue);
				}
				else
				{
					dataCell.Value = newValue;
				}
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual bool OnCellValueChanged(object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			if (!_isSettingBinding)
			{
				Binding binding = BindingOperations.GetBinding(this, DataProperty);
				if (binding != null && binding.Mode == BindingMode.TwoWay)
				{
					object data = Data;
					Data = newValue;
					return !object.Equals(data, Data);
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		private string ChangeStringToDecimal(string baseString)
		{
			if (decimal.TryParse(baseString.ToString(), NumberStyles.Any, null, out var result))
			{
				return result.ToString();
			}
			return baseString;
		}

		private static void OnDataPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as BindingDataCellWrapper).OnDataChanged(e.OldValue, e.NewValue);
		}
	}
	public class BindingDataColumn : DataColumn
	{
		private readonly string _bindingPath1;

		private readonly List<object> _bindingParameters1;

		private readonly IValueConverter _bindingConverter1;

		private readonly object _bindingConverterParameter1;

		public Binding Binding { get; }

		public string BindingPath { get; }

		public BindingMode BindingMode { get; set; }

		public BindingDataColumn(Binding binding)
		{
			Binding = binding ?? throw new ArgumentNullException("binding");
			_bindingPath1 = binding.Path.Path;
			_bindingParameters1 = binding.Path.PathParameters.ToList();
			_bindingConverter1 = binding.Converter;
			_bindingConverterParameter1 = binding.ConverterParameter;
			BindingMode = binding.Mode;
		}

		public BindingDataColumn(string bindingPath)
		{
			BindingPath = bindingPath ?? throw new ArgumentNullException("bindingPath");
			BindingMode = BindingMode.OneWay;
		}

		internal Binding GenerateBinding(object bindingSource)
		{
			if (Binding != null)
			{
				return new Binding
				{
					Source = bindingSource,
					Path = new PropertyPath(_bindingPath1, _bindingParameters1.ToArray()),
					Converter = _bindingConverter1,
					ConverterParameter = _bindingConverterParameter1,
					Mode = BindingMode
				};
			}
			return new Binding
			{
				Source = bindingSource,
				Path = new PropertyPath(BindingPath),
				Mode = BindingMode
			};
		}

		private static PropertyPath ClonePropertyPath(PropertyPath path)
		{
			return new PropertyPath(path.Path, path.PathParameters.ToArray());
		}
	}
	public class BindingDataRow : DataRow
	{
		private readonly Dictionary<DataColumn, BindingDataCellWrapper> _wrappers = new Dictionary<DataColumn, BindingDataCellWrapper>();

		private object _dataItem;

		public object DataItem
		{
			get
			{
				return _dataItem;
			}
			set
			{
				object dataItem = _dataItem;
				if (dataItem != value)
				{
					_dataItem = value;
					OnDataItemChanged(dataItem, value);
				}
			}
		}

		public BindingDataRow(DataTable parentDataTable)
			: base(parentDataTable)
		{
		}

		public object GetBindingValue(BindingDataColumn column)
		{
			if (column == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("column");
			}
			BindingDataCellWrapper bindingDataCellWrapper = null;
			bindingDataCellWrapper = (_wrappers.ContainsKey(column) ? _wrappers[column] : (_wrappers[column] = new BindingDataCellWrapper(this, column)));
			return bindingDataCellWrapper.CellValue;
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnCellValueChanged(IDataCell cell, object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			BindingDataColumn bindingDataColumn = cell.Column as BindingDataColumn;
			if (!_wrappers.ContainsKey(bindingDataColumn))
			{
				GetBindingValue(bindingDataColumn);
			}
			if (_wrappers.TryGetValue(cell.Column, out var value) && !value.OnCellValueChanged(oldValue, newValue))
			{
				ClearData(cell.Column);
				RaiseIndexerPropertyChanged();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnDataItemChanged(object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			foreach (KeyValuePair<DataColumn, BindingDataCellWrapper> wrapper in _wrappers)
			{
				wrapper.Value.ResetBinding();
			}
		}
	}
	public class BindingDataTable : DataTable
	{
		private IEnumerable _itemsSource;

		public IEnumerable ItemsSource
		{
			get
			{
				return _itemsSource;
			}
			set
			{
				IEnumerable itemsSource = _itemsSource;
				if (itemsSource != value)
				{
					InternalUpdateProperty("ItemsSource", ref _itemsSource, value);
					OnItemsSourceChanged(itemsSource, _itemsSource);
				}
			}
		}

		public BindingDataTable(IEnumerable<BindingDataColumn> columns)
			: base(columns)
		{
			if (columns == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("columns");
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemsSourceChanged(IEnumerable oldValue, IEnumerable newValue)
		{
			if (oldValue is INotifyCollectionChanged notifyCollectionChanged)
			{
				notifyCollectionChanged.CollectionChanged -= ItemsSource_CollectionChanged;
			}
			if (newValue is INotifyCollectionChanged notifyCollectionChanged2)
			{
				notifyCollectionChanged2.CollectionChanged += ItemsSource_CollectionChanged;
			}
			ResetRows();
		}

		private void ItemsSource_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			switch (e.Action)
			{
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset:
				ResetRows();
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add:
				if (e.NewItems != null && e.NewItems.Count > 0)
				{
					BindingDataRow bindingDataRow = CreateRow() as BindingDataRow;
					bindingDataRow.DataItem = e.NewItems[0];
					base.Rows.Insert(e.NewStartingIndex, bindingDataRow);
				}
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Remove:
				base.Rows.RemoveAt(e.OldStartingIndex);
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Move:
				base.Rows.Move(e.OldStartingIndex, e.NewStartingIndex);
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace:
				if (e.NewItems != null && e.NewItems.Count > 0)
				{
					((BindingDataRow)base.Rows[e.OldStartingIndex]).DataItem = e.NewItems[0];
				}
				break;
			}
		}

		protected override IDataCell OnCellValueQueried(DataRow row, DataColumn column)
		{
			return DataRow.CreateDataCell(GetFinalCellValue(row, column), isReadOnly: false, null, CellUIType.Unknown, null, null, column.DefaultDataType);
		}

		protected internal override object GetDirectCellValue(DataRow row, DataColumn column)
		{
			return GetFinalCellValue(row, column);
		}

		private object GetFinalCellValue(DataRow row, DataColumn column)
		{
			BindingDataRow bindingDataRow = row as BindingDataRow;
			BindingDataColumn bindingDataColumn = column as BindingDataColumn;
			if (bindingDataRow != null && bindingDataColumn != null)
			{
				if (bindingDataRow.DataItem == null)
				{
					bindingDataRow.DataItem = GetRowDataItem(bindingDataRow);
				}
				return bindingDataRow.GetBindingValue(bindingDataColumn);
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected override void OnCellValueChanged(IDataCell cell, object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			if (cell.Row is BindingDataRow bindingDataRow)
			{
				bindingDataRow.OnCellValueChanged(cell, oldValue, newValue);
			}
			base.OnCellValueChanged(cell, oldValue, newValue);
		}

		public override DataRow CreateRow()
		{
			return new BindingDataRow(this);
		}

		private object GetRowDataItem(BindingDataRow row)
		{
			if (ItemsSource == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			int num = base.Rows.IndexOf(row);
			if (ItemsSource is IList list)
			{
				return list[num];
			}
			int num2 = 0;
			foreach (object item in ItemsSource)
			{
				if (num == num2)
				{
					return item;
				}
				num2++;
			}
			return null;
		}

		private void ResetRows()
		{
			base.Rows.Clear();
			if (ItemsSource == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (ItemsSource is IList list)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < list.Count; i++)
				{
					BindingDataRow bindingDataRow = CreateRow() as BindingDataRow;
					bindingDataRow.DataItem = list[i];
					base.Rows.Add(bindingDataRow);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				foreach (object item in ItemsSource)
				{
					BindingDataRow bindingDataRow2 = CreateRow() as BindingDataRow;
					bindingDataRow2.DataItem = item;
					base.Rows.Add(bindingDataRow2);
				}
			}
			if (base.GridControl != null)
			{
				base.GridControl.InternalSortHelper.InvalidateSorting();
			}
		}
	}
}
namespace AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.AutomationPeers
{
	internal static class AutomationPeerHelpers
	{
		private static bool? _useCustomAutomationPeers;

		internal static bool UseCustomAutomationPeers
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_useCustomAutomationPeers.HasValue)
				{
					try
					{
						string environmentVariable = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("AT_ENABLECODEDUITESTING");
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(environmentVariable))
						{
							_useCustomAutomationPeers = string.Equals(environmentVariable, "1", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
						}
						else
						{
							_useCustomAutomationPeers = false;
						}
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
				}
				return _useCustomAutomationPeers == true;
			}
			set
			{
				_useCustomAutomationPeers = value;
			}
		}

		internal static string UIACommandPrefix => "!#UIA:";

		internal static bool IsUIACommand(string data)
		{
			return string.Compare(data, 0, UIACommandPrefix, 0, UIACommandPrefix.Length, StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0;
		}

		internal static string TryProcessUIACommand(string data, Func<string, string, string> commandProcessor)
		{
			string result = null;
			try
			{
				string[] array = data.Substring(UIACommandPrefix.Length).Split(new char[1] { ':' }, 2);
				if (array != null && array.Length != 0)
				{
					string arg = array[0];
					string arg2 = string.Empty;
					if (array.Length > 1)
					{
						arg2 = array[1];
					}
					result = commandProcessor(arg, arg2);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static T GetVisualChild<T>(Visual parent) where T : Visual
		{
			T val = null;
			int childrenCount = VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(parent);
			for (int i = 0; i < childrenCount; i++)
			{
				Visual visual = (Visual)VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(parent, i);
				val = visual as T;
				if (val == null)
				{
					val = GetVisualChild<T>(visual);
				}
				if (val != null)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			return val;
		}
	}
	public class GridControlAutomationPeer : FrameworkElementAutomationPeer, IValueProvider
	{
		private string _lastCommandResult;

		public bool IsReadOnly => false;

		public string Value
		{
			get
			{
				string lastCommandResult = _lastCommandResult;
				_lastCommandResult = null;
				return lastCommandResult;
			}
		}

		public GridControlAutomationPeer(GridControl owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
		}

		protected override AutomationControlType GetAutomationControlTypeCore()
		{
			return AutomationControlType.Custom;
		}

		protected override string GetClassNameCore()
		{
			return base.Owner.GetType().Name;
		}

		protected override string GetNameCore()
		{
			return (base.Owner as GridControl).Name;
		}

		protected override string GetAutomationIdCore()
		{
			return (base.Owner as GridControl).Name;
		}

		public override object GetPattern(PatternInterface patternInterface)
		{
			if (patternInterface == PatternInterface.Value)
			{
				return this;
			}
			return base.GetPattern(patternInterface);
		}

		public void SetValue(string value)
		{
			if (AutomationPeerHelpers.IsUIACommand(value))
			{
				_lastCommandResult = AutomationPeerHelpers.TryProcessUIACommand(value, ProcessUIACommand);
			}
		}

		protected virtual string ProcessUIACommand(string commandKey, string commandData)
		{
			if (commandKey.ToUpperInvariant() == "ENABLECODEDUITESTING")
			{
				AutomationPeerHelpers.UseCustomAutomationPeers = true;
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public class GridControlCellAutomationPeer : FrameworkElementAutomationPeer, IValueProvider
	{
		public bool IsReadOnly
		{
			get
			{
				if (!(base.Owner is GridControlCell { Cell: not null } gridControlCell))
				{
					return true;
				}
				return gridControlCell.Cell.IsReadOnly;
			}
		}

		public string Value
		{
			get
			{
				if (!(base.Owner is GridControlCell { Cell: not null } gridControlCell))
				{
					return null;
				}
				return gridControlCell.Cell.ValueAsString;
			}
		}

		public GridControlCellAutomationPeer(GridControlCell owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
		}

		protected override AutomationControlType GetAutomationControlTypeCore()
		{
			return AutomationControlType.Custom;
		}

		protected override string GetClassNameCore()
		{
			return base.Owner.GetType().Name;
		}

		protected override string GetAutomationIdCore()
		{
			string automationIdCore = base.GetAutomationIdCore();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(automationIdCore))
			{
				return automationIdCore;
			}
			return (((base.Owner is GridControlCell gridControlCell) ? gridControlCell.GridColumn : null)?.DataColumn)?.Name;
		}

		protected override string GetNameCore()
		{
			return Value;
		}

		protected override bool IsOffscreenCore()
		{
			return base.IsOffscreenCore();
		}

		public override object GetPattern(PatternInterface patternInterface)
		{
			object obj = null;
			if (patternInterface == PatternInterface.Value)
			{
				return this;
			}
			if (obj == null)
			{
				obj = base.GetPattern(patternInterface);
			}
			return obj;
		}

		public void SetValue(string value)
		{
			if (!(base.Owner is GridControlCell { Cell: not null } gridControlCell))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				gridControlCell.Cell.Parse(value);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}
	}
	public class GridControlDataGridAutomationPeer : FrameworkElementAutomationPeer, IItemContainerProvider, ITableProvider, IGridProvider, ISelectionProvider
	{
		private List<GridControlDGItemAutomationPeer> _cachedChildren;

		public RowOrColumnMajor RowOrColumnMajor => RowOrColumnMajor.RowMajor;

		public int ColumnCount => (base.Owner as DataGrid).Columns.Count;

		public int RowCount => (base.Owner as DataGrid).Items.Count;

		public bool CanSelectMultiple => (base.Owner as DataGrid).SelectionMode == DataGridSelectionMode.Extended;

		public bool IsSelectionRequired => false;

		public GridControlDataGridAutomationPeer(GridControlDataGrid owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
			if (owner == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("owner");
			}
		}

		protected virtual bool IsPropertySupportedByControlForFindItem(int propertyId)
		{
			if (AutomationElementIdentifiers.NameProperty.Id != propertyId && AutomationElementIdentifiers.AutomationIdProperty.Id != propertyId && AutomationElementIdentifiers.ControlTypeProperty.Id != propertyId)
			{
				return SelectionItemPatternIdentifiers.IsSelectedProperty.Id == propertyId;
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected object GetSupportedPropertyValue(GridControlDGItemAutomationPeer itemPeer, int propertyId)
		{
			object result = null;
			if (AutomationElementIdentifiers.NameProperty.Id == propertyId)
			{
				result = itemPeer.GetName();
			}
			else if (AutomationElementIdentifiers.AutomationIdProperty.Id == propertyId)
			{
				result = itemPeer.GetAutomationId();
			}
			else if (AutomationElementIdentifiers.ControlTypeProperty.Id == propertyId)
			{
				result = itemPeer.GetAutomationControlType();
			}
			else if (SelectionItemPatternIdentifiers.IsSelectedProperty.Id == propertyId && itemPeer.GetPattern(PatternInterface.SelectionItem) is ISelectionItemProvider selectionItemProvider)
			{
				result = selectionItemProvider.IsSelected;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private GridControlDGItemAutomationPeer CreateItemAutomationPeer(object item)
		{
			return new GridControlDGItemAutomationPeer(this, item);
		}

		private GridControlDGItemAutomationPeer FindOrCreateItemAutomationPeer(object item)
		{
			GridControlDGItemAutomationPeer gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer = null;
			if (_cachedChildren != null)
			{
				gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer = _cachedChildren.FirstOrDefault((GridControlDGItemAutomationPeer p) => object.Equals(item, p.Item));
			}
			if (gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer == null)
			{
				gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer = CreateItemAutomationPeer(item);
			}
			return gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer;
		}

		public GridControlDGItemAutomationPeer GetExistingPeerByItem(object item)
		{
			GridControlDGItemAutomationPeer result = null;
			GetChildrenCore();
			if (_cachedChildren != null)
			{
				result = _cachedChildren.FirstOrDefault((GridControlDGItemAutomationPeer p) => object.Equals(item, p.Item));
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected override AutomationControlType GetAutomationControlTypeCore()
		{
			return AutomationControlType.DataGrid;
		}

		protected override List<AutomationPeer> GetChildrenCore()
		{
			DataGrid obj = base.Owner as DataGrid;
			List<AutomationPeer> list = new List<AutomationPeer>();
			DataGridColumnHeadersPresenter visualChild = AutomationPeerHelpers.GetVisualChild<DataGridColumnHeadersPresenter>(obj);
			if (visualChild != null && visualChild.IsVisible)
			{
				AutomationPeer automationPeer = UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement(visualChild);
				if (automationPeer != null)
				{
					list.Add(automationPeer);
				}
			}
			List<GridControlDGItemAutomationPeer> cachedChildren = _cachedChildren;
			_cachedChildren = new List<GridControlDGItemAutomationPeer>();
			foreach (object item in (IEnumerable)obj.Items)
			{
				GridControlDGItemAutomationPeer gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer = null;
				if (cachedChildren != null)
				{
					gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer = cachedChildren.FirstOrDefault((GridControlDGItemAutomationPeer p) => p.Item == item);
				}
				if (gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer == null)
				{
					gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer = CreateItemAutomationPeer(item);
				}
				_cachedChildren.Add(gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer);
				list.Add(gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer);
			}
			return list;
		}

		public override object GetPattern(PatternInterface patternInterface)
		{
			if (patternInterface != PatternInterface.Scroll && patternInterface == PatternInterface.ItemContainer)
			{
				return this;
			}
			return base.GetPattern(patternInterface);
		}

		public IRawElementProviderSimple FindItemByProperty(IRawElementProviderSimple startAfter, int propertyId, object value)
		{
			ResetChildrenCache();
			if (propertyId != 0 && !IsPropertySupportedByControlForFindItem(propertyId))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("PropertyNotSupported");
			}
			ItemCollection items = (base.Owner as DataGrid).Items;
			if (items != null && items.Count > 0)
			{
				GridControlDGItemAutomationPeer gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer = null;
				if (startAfter != null)
				{
					gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer = PeerFromProvider(startAfter) as GridControlDGItemAutomationPeer;
					if (gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer == null)
					{
						return null;
					}
				}
				int num = 0;
				if (gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer != null)
				{
					if (gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer.Item == null)
					{
						throw new InvalidOperationException(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.InvalidStartItem);
					}
					num = items.IndexOf(gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer.Item) + 1;
					if (num <= 0 || num == items.Count)
					{
						return null;
					}
				}
				if (propertyId == 0)
				{
					for (int i = num; i < items.Count; i++)
					{
						if (items.IndexOf(items[i]) == i)
						{
							return ProviderFromPeer(FindOrCreateItemAutomationPeer(items[i]));
						}
					}
				}
				for (int j = num; j < items.Count; j++)
				{
					object obj = null;
					GridControlDGItemAutomationPeer gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer2 = FindOrCreateItemAutomationPeer(items[j]);
					if (gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer2 != null)
					{
						try
						{
							obj = GetSupportedPropertyValue(gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer2, propertyId);
						}
						catch (ElementNotAvailableException)
						{
							continue;
						}
						catch (Exception)
						{
						}
						if ((value == null && obj == null) || (value != null && obj != null && value.Equals(obj)))
						{
							return ProviderFromPeer(gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer2);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected AutomationPeer GetColumnHeaderAutomationPeer(DataGridColumn column)
		{
			return null;
		}

		public IRawElementProviderSimple[] GetColumnHeaders()
		{
			DataGrid dataGrid = base.Owner as DataGrid;
			if ((dataGrid.HeadersVisibility & DataGridHeadersVisibility.Column) != DataGridHeadersVisibility.Column)
			{
				return null;
			}
			List<IRawElementProviderSimple> list = new List<IRawElementProviderSimple>();
			foreach (DataGridColumn column in dataGrid.Columns)
			{
				AutomationPeer columnHeaderAutomationPeer = GetColumnHeaderAutomationPeer(column);
				if (columnHeaderAutomationPeer != null)
				{
					list.Add(ProviderFromPeer(columnHeaderAutomationPeer));
				}
			}
			if (list.Count <= 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return list.ToArray();
		}

		public IRawElementProviderSimple[] GetRowHeaders()
		{
			DataGrid dataGrid = base.Owner as DataGrid;
			if ((dataGrid.HeadersVisibility & DataGridHeadersVisibility.Row) != DataGridHeadersVisibility.Row)
			{
				return null;
			}
			List<IRawElementProviderSimple> list = new List<IRawElementProviderSimple>();
			foreach (object item in (IEnumerable)dataGrid.Items)
			{
				AutomationPeer rowHeaderAutomationPeer = FindOrCreateItemAutomationPeer(item).RowHeaderAutomationPeer;
				if (rowHeaderAutomationPeer != null)
				{
					list.Add(ProviderFromPeer(rowHeaderAutomationPeer));
				}
			}
			if (list.Count <= 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return list.ToArray();
		}

		public IRawElementProviderSimple GetItem(int row, int column)
		{
			if (row < 0 || row >= RowCount)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (column < 0 || column >= ColumnCount)
			{
				return null;
			}
			DataGrid obj = base.Owner as DataGrid;
			object item = obj.Items[row];
			DataGridColumn column2 = obj.Columns[column];
			obj.ScrollIntoView(item, column2);
			obj.UpdateLayout();
			GridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer gridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer = FindOrCreateItemAutomationPeer(item)?.FindOrCreateItemAutomationPeer(column2);
			if (gridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return ProviderFromPeer(gridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer);
		}

		public IRawElementProviderSimple[] GetSelection()
		{
			DataGrid dataGrid = base.Owner as DataGrid;
			List<IRawElementProviderSimple> list = new List<IRawElementProviderSimple>();
			DataGridSelectionUnit selectionUnit = dataGrid.SelectionUnit;
			if ((selectionUnit == DataGridSelectionUnit.FullRow || selectionUnit == DataGridSelectionUnit.CellOrRowHeader) && dataGrid.SelectedCells != null)
			{
				foreach (DataGridCellInfo selectedCell in dataGrid.SelectedCells)
				{
					GridControlDGItemAutomationPeer gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer = FindOrCreateItemAutomationPeer(selectedCell.Item);
					if (gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer != null)
					{
						list.Add(ProviderFromPeer(gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer));
					}
				}
			}
			if ((selectionUnit == DataGridSelectionUnit.Cell || selectionUnit == DataGridSelectionUnit.CellOrRowHeader) && dataGrid.SelectedCells != null)
			{
				foreach (DataGridCellInfo selectedCell2 in dataGrid.SelectedCells)
				{
					GridControlDGItemAutomationPeer gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer2 = FindOrCreateItemAutomationPeer(selectedCell2.Item);
					if (gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer2 != null)
					{
						GridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer gridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer = gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer2.FindOrCreateItemAutomationPeer(selectedCell2.Column);
						if (gridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer != null)
						{
							list.Add(ProviderFromPeer(gridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer));
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return list.ToArray();
		}
	}
	public class GridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer : AutomationPeer, ITableItemProvider, IGridItemProvider, IInvokeProvider, IScrollItemProvider, ISelectionItemProvider, IValueProvider, IVirtualizedItemProvider
	{
		private readonly object _item;

		private readonly DataGridColumn _column;

		private readonly DataGrid _dataGrid;

		public DataGridColumn GridColumn => _column;

		private DataGridCell OwningCell
		{
			get
			{
				int num = OwningDataGrid.Columns.IndexOf(GridColumn);
				if (num < 0)
				{
					return null;
				}
				if (!(OwningDataGrid.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(_item) is DataGridRow row))
				{
					return null;
				}
				DataGridCellsPresenter cellsPresenter = GridControlDGRowAutomationPeer.GetCellsPresenter(row);
				if (cellsPresenter == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return cellsPresenter.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromIndex(num) as DataGridCell;
			}
		}

		private AutomationPeer OwningCellPeer
		{
			get
			{
				AutomationPeer automationPeer = null;
				DataGridCell owningCell = OwningCell;
				if (owningCell != null)
				{
					automationPeer = UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement(owningCell);
					automationPeer.EventsSource = this;
				}
				return automationPeer;
			}
		}

		private IValueProvider OwningCellValueProvider
		{
			get
			{
				AutomationPeer owningCellPeer = OwningCellPeer;
				if (owningCellPeer == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return owningCellPeer.GetPattern(PatternInterface.Value) as IValueProvider;
			}
		}

		private DataGrid OwningDataGrid => _dataGrid;

		private AutomationPeer OwningItemPeer
		{
			get
			{
				if (OwningDataGrid != null && UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement(OwningDataGrid) is GridControlDataGridAutomationPeer gridControlDataGridAutomationPeer)
				{
					return gridControlDataGridAutomationPeer.GetExistingPeerByItem(_item);
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		private bool IsCellSelectionUnit
		{
			get
			{
				if (OwningDataGrid == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (OwningDataGrid.SelectionUnit != DataGridSelectionUnit.Cell)
				{
					return OwningDataGrid.SelectionUnit == DataGridSelectionUnit.CellOrRowHeader;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public int Column => OwningDataGrid.Columns.IndexOf(_column);

		public int ColumnSpan => 1;

		public IRawElementProviderSimple ContainingGrid
		{
			get
			{
				AutomationPeer automationPeer = UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement(OwningDataGrid);
				if (automationPeer == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return ProviderFromPeer(automationPeer);
			}
		}

		public int Row => OwningDataGrid.Items.IndexOf(_item);

		public int RowSpan => 1;

		public bool IsSelected
		{
			get
			{
				DataGridCellInfo item = new DataGridCellInfo(_item, _column);
				return OwningDataGrid.SelectedCells.Contains(item);
			}
		}

		public IRawElementProviderSimple SelectionContainer => ContainingGrid;

		public bool IsReadOnly => OwningCellValueProvider?.IsReadOnly ?? true;

		public string Value => OwningCellValueProvider?.Value;

		public GridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer(DataGrid dataGrid, DataGridColumn column, object item)
		{
			_item = item;
			_column = column;
			_dataGrid = dataGrid;
		}

		private void ThrowElementNotAvailableException()
		{
		}

		protected override string GetAcceleratorKeyCore()
		{
			AutomationPeer owningCellPeer = OwningCellPeer;
			if (owningCellPeer != null)
			{
				return owningCellPeer.GetAcceleratorKey();
			}
			ThrowElementNotAvailableException();
			return string.Empty;
		}

		protected override string GetAccessKeyCore()
		{
			AutomationPeer owningCellPeer = OwningCellPeer;
			if (owningCellPeer != null)
			{
				return owningCellPeer.GetAccessKey();
			}
			ThrowElementNotAvailableException();
			return string.Empty;
		}

		protected override AutomationControlType GetAutomationControlTypeCore()
		{
			return AutomationControlType.Custom;
		}

		protected override string GetAutomationIdCore()
		{
			AutomationPeer owningCellPeer = OwningCellPeer;
			if (owningCellPeer != null)
			{
				return owningCellPeer.GetAutomationId();
			}
			ThrowElementNotAvailableException();
			return string.Empty;
		}

		protected override Rect GetBoundingRectangleCore()
		{
			AutomationPeer owningCellPeer = OwningCellPeer;
			if (owningCellPeer != null)
			{
				return owningCellPeer.GetBoundingRectangle();
			}
			ThrowElementNotAvailableException();
			return default(Rect);
		}

		protected override List<AutomationPeer> GetChildrenCore()
		{

			{
				ThrowElementNotAvailableException();
			}

		public override object GetPattern(PatternInterface patternInterface)
		{
			switch (patternInterface)
			{
			case PatternInterface.Invoke:
				if (OwningDataGrid.IsReadOnly || _column.IsReadOnly)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return this;
			case PatternInterface.Value:
				return this;
			case PatternInterface.ScrollItem:
			case PatternInterface.GridItem:
				return this;
			case PatternInterface.TableItem:
				return this;
			case PatternInterface.VirtualizedItem:
				if (OwningCellPeer == null)
				{
					return this;
				}
				if (OwningItemPeer == null)
				{
					return this;
				}
				return null;
			case PatternInterface.SelectionItem:
				if (IsCellSelectionUnit)
				{
					return this;
				}
				return null;
			default:
				return null;
			}
		}

		public IRawElementProviderSimple[] GetColumnHeaderItems()
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		public IRawElementProviderSimple[] GetRowHeaderItems()
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		public void Invoke()
		{
			if (OwningDataGrid.IsReadOnly || _column.IsReadOnly)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			if (OwningCell == null)
			{
				OwningDataGrid.ScrollIntoView(_item, _column);
			}
			DataGridCell owningCell = OwningCell;
			if (owningCell != null)
			{
				if (!owningCell.IsEditing)
				{
					if (!owningCell.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
					{
						owningCell.Focus();
					}
					Select();
					flag = OwningDataGrid.BeginEdit();
				}
				else
				{
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.ErrorInvokeFaile);
			}
		}

		public void ScrollIntoView()
		{
			OwningDataGrid.ScrollIntoView(_item, _column);
		}

		public void AddToSelection()
		{
			if (!IsCellSelectionUnit)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.ErrorCannotSelectSingleCells);
			}
			DataGridCellInfo item = new DataGridCellInfo(_item, _column);
			if (!OwningDataGrid.SelectedCells.Contains(item))
			{
				if (OwningDataGrid.SelectionMode == DataGridSelectionMode.Single && OwningDataGrid.SelectedCells.Count > 0)
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException();
				}
				OwningDataGrid.SelectedCells.Add(item);
			}
		}

		public void RemoveFromSelection()
		{
			if (!IsCellSelectionUnit)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.ErrorCannotSelectSingleCells);
			}
			DataGridCellInfo item = new DataGridCellInfo(_item, _column);
			if (OwningDataGrid.SelectedCells.Contains(item))
			{
				OwningDataGrid.SelectedCells.Remove(item);
			}
		}

		public void Select()
		{
			if (!IsCellSelectionUnit)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.ErrorCannotSelectSingleCells);
			}
			OwningDataGrid.SelectedCells.Clear();
			OwningDataGrid.SelectedCells.Add(new DataGridCellInfo(_item, _column));
		}

		public void SetValue(string value)
		{
			(OwningCellValueProvider ?? throw new InvalidOperationException(AspenTech.Engineering.GridControls.Properties.Resources.ErrorCellIsReadOnly)).SetValue(value);
		}

		public void Realize()
		{
			OwningDataGrid.ScrollIntoView(_item, _column);
		}
	}
	public class GridControlDGItemAutomationPeer : AutomationPeer, IVirtualizedItemProvider, IItemContainerProvider
	{
		private readonly object _item;

		private readonly GridControlDataGridAutomationPeer _dataGridPeer;

		private List<GridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer> _cachedChildren;

		public object Item => _item;

		public AutomationPeer RowHeaderAutomationPeer
		{
			get
			{
				throw new NotImplementedException();
			}
		}

		public GridControlDGItemAutomationPeer(GridControlDataGridAutomationPeer dataGridPeer, object item)
		{
			if (dataGridPeer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("dataGridPeer");
			}
			_item = item;
			_dataGridPeer = dataGridPeer;
		}

		protected GridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer CreateItemAutomationPeer(DataGridColumn column)
		{
			return new GridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer(_dataGridPeer.Owner as DataGrid, column, _item);
		}

		public GridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer FindOrCreateItemAutomationPeer(DataGridColumn column)
		{
			GridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer gridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer = null;
			if (_cachedChildren != null)
			{
				gridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer = _cachedChildren.FirstOrDefault((GridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer p) => column == p.GridColumn);
			}
			if (gridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer == null)
			{
				gridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer = CreateItemAutomationPeer(column);
			}
			return gridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer;
		}

		public UIElement GetItemContainer()
		{
			return (_dataGridPeer.Owner as DataGrid).ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(Item) as UIElement;
		}

		public virtual AutomationPeer GetItemContainerPeer()
		{
			UIElement itemContainer = GetItemContainer();
			if (itemContainer == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			AutomationPeer automationPeer = UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement(itemContainer);
			if (automationPeer == null)
			{
				automationPeer = ((!(itemContainer is FrameworkElement)) ? new UIElementAutomationPeer(itemContainer) : new FrameworkElementAutomationPeer((FrameworkElement)itemContainer));
			}
			if (automationPeer is GridControlDGRowAutomationPeer)
			{
				automationPeer.EventsSource = this;
			}
			return automationPeer;
		}

		private void ThrowElementNotAvailableException()
		{
		}

		protected override string GetAcceleratorKeyCore()

			}
			return GetCellItemPeers();

		internal List<AutomationPeer> GetCellItemPeers()
		{
			List<AutomationPeer> list = new List<AutomationPeer>();
			List<GridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer> list2 = new List<GridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer>();
			foreach (DataGridColumn column in (_dataGridPeer.Owner as DataGrid).Columns)
			{
				GridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer gridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer = FindOrCreateItemAutomationPeer(column);
				if (gridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer != null)
				{
					list.Add(gridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer);
					list2.Add(gridControlDGItemCellAutomationPeer);
				}
			}
			_cachedChildren = list2;
			return list;
		}

			AutomationPeer itemContainerPeer = GetItemContainerPeer();

		public void Realize()
		{
			DataGrid dataGrid = _dataGridPeer.Owner as DataGrid;
			if (dataGrid.ItemContainerGenerator.Status == GeneratorStatus.ContainersGenerated)
			{
				if (dataGrid.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(Item) is FrameworkElement frameworkElement)
				{
					frameworkElement.BringIntoView();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Loaded, new Action(BringItemIntoView));
			}
		}

		private void BringItemIntoView()
		{
			if ((_dataGridPeer.Owner as DataGrid).ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(Item) is FrameworkElement frameworkElement)
			{
				frameworkElement.BringIntoView();
			}
		}

		public IRawElementProviderSimple FindItemByProperty(IRawElementProviderSimple startAfter, int propertyId, object value)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public class GridControlDGRowAutomationPeer : FrameworkElementAutomationPeer
	{
		private DataGridRow OwningDataGridRow => (DataGridRow)base.Owner;

		private DataGridCellsPresenter CellsPresenter => GetCellsPresenter(OwningDataGridRow);

		internal AutomationPeer DetailsPresenterAutomationPeer
		{
			get
			{
				DataGridDetailsPresenter visualChild = AutomationPeerHelpers.GetVisualChild<DataGridDetailsPresenter>(OwningDataGridRow);
				if (visualChild != null)
				{
					return UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement(visualChild);
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		internal AutomationPeer RowHeaderAutomationPeer
		{
			get
			{
				DataGridRowHeader visualChild = AutomationPeerHelpers.GetVisualChild<DataGridRowHeader>(OwningDataGridRow);
				if (visualChild != null)
				{
					return UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement(visualChild);
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		public GridControlDGRowAutomationPeer(GridControlDataGridRow owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
		}

		protected override AutomationControlType GetAutomationControlTypeCore()
		{
			return AutomationControlType.DataItem;
		}

		public static DataGridCellsPresenter GetCellsPresenter(DataGridRow row)
		{
			return AutomationPeerHelpers.GetVisualChild<DataGridCellsPresenter>(row);
		}

		protected override string GetClassNameCore()
		{
			return base.Owner.GetType().Name;
		}

		protected override bool IsOffscreenCore()
		{
			if (base.Owner.IsVisible)
			{
				Rect rect = CalculateVisibleBoundingRect();
				if (!AreClose(rect.Height, 0.0))
				{
					return AreClose(rect.Width, 0.0);
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		private static bool AreClose(double valueA, double valueB)
		{
			return Math.Abs(valueA - valueB) < double.Epsilon;
		}

		internal Rect CalculateVisibleBoundingRect()
		{
			Rect empty = Rect.Empty;
			empty = new Rect(base.Owner.RenderSize);
			Visual visual = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(base.Owner) as Visual;
			while (visual != null && empty != Rect.Empty && empty.Height != 0.0 && empty.Width != 0.0)
			{
				Geometry clip = VisualTreeHelper.GetClip(visual);
				if (clip != null)
				{
					GeneralTransform inverse = base.Owner.TransformToAncestor(visual).Inverse;
					if (inverse != null)
					{
						Rect bounds = clip.Bounds;
						bounds = inverse.TransformBounds(bounds);
						empty.Intersect(bounds);
					}
					else
					{
						empty = Rect.Empty;
					}
				}
				visual = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(visual) as Visual;
			}
			return empty;
		}

		protected override List<AutomationPeer> GetChildrenCore()
		{
			if (CellsPresenter == null)
			{
				return base.GetChildrenCore();
			}
			List<AutomationPeer> list = new List<AutomationPeer>(3);
			AutomationPeer rowHeaderAutomationPeer = RowHeaderAutomationPeer;
			if (rowHeaderAutomationPeer != null)
			{
				list.Add(rowHeaderAutomationPeer);
			}
			if (base.EventsSource is GridControlDGItemAutomationPeer gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer)
			{
				list.AddRange(gridControlDGItemAutomationPeer.GetCellItemPeers());
			}
			AutomationPeer detailsPresenterAutomationPeer = DetailsPresenterAutomationPeer;
			if (detailsPresenterAutomationPeer != null)
			{
				list.Add(detailsPresenterAutomationPeer);
			}
			return list;
		}
	}
}
